This extends the case $α>1$ of [Fawzi, Kochanowski, Rouzé, Van Himbeeck, CMP 2026] and the unconditional results of [Devetak, Junge, King, Ruskai, CMP 2006]. Beyond tensor products, we establish chain rules for $\tilde H^\uparrow_α$, $α\in[\frac12,1)$, for compositions of possibly signaling channels, extending upon the smooth max-entropy chain rule used in device-independent entanglement certification to the whole interval $α\in[1/2,1)$.

</details>


### [79] [Accelerated search for global extrema in structured quantum signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04024)

Abstract: Extreme values of a physical system are rare by definition, yet they can govern some of its most consequential and anomalous behavior. Their sparsity makes them particularly costly to identify in large data sets. Quantum computation offers a potential way to reduce this cost. The challenge is that quantum amplitudes cannot simply be read, copied, compared, or marked using conventional search oracles. Through a nonlinear amplitude transformation, the algorithm identifies and certifies a global extremum while exponentially reducing the effective search space for structured signals. The resulting relation contains a port-to-port scattering matrix and a Langevin-noise contribution from material absorption that together preserve the bosonic output commutation relations. For embedded emitters, the low-saturation and single-excitation scattering response is determined by Green's functions connecting the ports and emitters while retaining the non-Markovianity of the photonic environment. Abstract: A small excitation gap makes a quantum dot a sensitive probe of a weak electric field, but the charge detector that reads the probe can also excite it. We ask how much of this gap enhancement survives continuous readout, for two interacting electrons tuned through a confinement-driven crossover in a gate-defined quantum dot, using a weak-coupling, stationary two-level model. The answer depends on the energy structure of the detector, not only on its noise. A quantum point contact measures more strongly at higher bias, but once the bias energy exceeds the gap it also excites the probe. When this back-action dominates the probe's own relaxation, the optimised mean-current information rate grows only as the inverse of the gap. A sensor quantum dot with a narrow level instead reaches full measurement strength at a bias below the gap, where, for a non-interacting detector and to second order in the coupling, it cannot excite the probe at zero temperature. This energy-filtered readout keeps the inverse-square gap dependence of the quantum Fisher information, down to a gap set by the detector's level width and the electron temperature. Through such a detector the interacting pair gives about three times the information rate of a single electron with the same gap and detector, compared with 1.6-1.7 in the quantum Fisher information, for both contact and Coulomb-type interactions. These different gap dependences, and the different optimal biases of the two detectors, can be tested by bias and gate sweeps on suitably tuned charge-sensed double dots.

</details>

Abstract: Simulating open-system dynamics on gate-based quantum computers offers a route to exploring dissipative quantum phenomena through programmable quantum processing. Collision models provide a direct and flexible framework by representing the environment through sequential system--ancilla collisions. At finite temperatures, however, they typically require repeated preparation of thermal ancillas, adding circuit overhead and gate errors. Here, we show that, for a broad class of $U(1)$-symmetric system--ancilla interactions, finite-temperature dynamics can instead be reconstructed from simulations with pure ancillas in orthogonal basis states. The target temperature enters only through classical post-processing of the resulting pure-branch data, allowing the same data to be reused for different temperatures without additional quantum processing. Bypassing explicit thermal-state preparation reduces ancilla-preparation overhead and preparation-induced errors.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the Wigner--Smith time-delay matrix of a chaotic cavity with broken time-reversal symmetry, a finite number $M$ of equivalent open channels, and a uniform tunnel barrier of reflection probability $R$. Exact random-matrix results for nonideal coupling are known at the level of the joint matrix distribution, whereas explicit finite-channel formulas for Schur moments are much less developed. We show that every Schur moment of the nonideal Wigner--Smith matrix factorizes into its ideal-coupling inverse-Laguerre value and a barrier factor given by a circular unitary ensemble (CUE) average of a Schur polynomial. A dual-Cauchy generating function reduces the latter to a Toeplitz determinant with a rational symbol, placing the barrier problem within the classical theory of exact CUE characteristic-polynomial ratios.
  For one-row partitions, specializing the exact CUE ratio formula gives a closed generating function for all barrier coefficients and therefore all finite delay-time moments in the allowed range $n\le M$. The exact ratio formula decomposes into a sector visible to the semiclassical expansion and an additional contribution proportional to $R^{M+1}$, which is invisible to every algebraic order in $1/M$. For the second moment, this contribution exactly reproduces the exponentially small term in the known random-matrix expression for $\langleτ_W^2\rangle$ that is absent from the semiclassical expansion. We also derive an exact finite sum for all one-column partitions and an exact complement symmetry for Schur averages inside the $M\times M$ square. The new contribution therefore lies not in the underlying CUE ratio identities, which are classical, but in combining them with the exact nonideal Wigner--Smith mapping and extracting the resulting finite-channel time-delay formulas and corrections to semiclassical Schur-moment conjectures.

</details>

Motivation: 耗散量子算法受经典MCMC启发，通过模拟Lindbladian支配的耗散过程制备量子态。但其性能受限于跳跃算子的串行实现，导致电路深度过高，难以在近期量子设备上实用。

Abstract: Inspired by classical Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods, dissipative quantum algorithms are an emerging class of methods for preparing quantum states on a quantum computer. They operate by simulating a dissipative process governed by a mathematical operator known as the Lindbladian. The performance of these algorithms can be limited by the sequential implementation of the Lindbladian's constituent jump operators, which leads to high circuit depth. To address this issue, this work analyzes a parallelization scheme to implement jump operators simultaneously. We leverage the structure of geometric locality to show that individual jump operators remain spatially localized. This exponentially reduces the circuit depth per time step, resulting in an overall polynomial improvement. Numerical experiments demonstrate a 53x reduction in query depth for a 1D benchmark system of 100 qubits. The reduced circuit depth makes dissipative algorithms more practical for near-term quantum computing.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子核方法的训练成本主要来自Gram矩阵构建的二次缩放（O(N^2)）以及单个核值估计的测量开销。作者希望寻找一种资源高效的运行区间，在近-term量子硬件约束下降低训练非变分量子核模型的资源需求。

Abstract: Quantum kernel methods are promising for near-term quantum machine learning, but their training costs are driven by the quadratic scaling of Gram-matrix construction and the measurement cost of estimating individual kernel values. In this work, we investigate one-class anomaly detection as a resource-efficient operating regime for training non-variational quantum kernel models. Using the ULB Credit Card Fraud Detection dataset, we evaluate three quantum feature maps across training-set sizes and qubit counts. Power-law saturation models reveal strong diminishing returns with increasing training data, with 500-sample models retaining approximately 90--93\% of their fitted asymptotic average precision (AP). We further observe no severe exponential concentration among the evaluated feature maps over the investigated qubit range, thereby avoiding the exponentially increasing measurement requirements associated with that regime. Finally, Nyström approximation reduces kernel construction from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(Nm)$ evaluations, with QSVR retaining 94--97\% of its exact-kernel AP using only five landmarks. For ZZ-QSVR, downsampling from 4,000 to 500 training samples and applying a five-landmark approximation decreases the number of required kernel evaluations from approximately $8.0$ million to $2,485$, a reduction of more than 3,200-fold, while AP decreases from $0.812$ to $0.737$. Under the minimum IonQ pricing assumption considered here, this corresponds to an illustrative reduction in quantum kernel training cost from approximately \$1.34 billion to \$417{,}480. These results demonstrate that training-set reduction and low-rank approximation can substantially reduce the resources required to train non-variational quantum kernel anomaly detectors while retaining meaningful anomaly-discrimination performance.

</details>


### [87] [Symmetry resolved entanglement in Schwinger pair production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04513)
*Wajid Joyia,S. M. Al-Marzoug,Amaria Javed*

TL;DR: 本文研究Schwinger效应产生的模式对纠缠的电荷分辨结构，将约化密度矩阵的纠缠按U(1)电荷分解到各电荷扇区，并对纯双分划约化应用对称分辨纠缠熵、对混合粒子-反粒子对应用电荷不平衡分辨负性度。

Method: 利用量子电动力学的U(1)电荷，将动量模式约化密度矩阵的纠缠分解到各个电荷扇区；对纯双分划约化采用对称分辨纠缠熵，对混合粒子-反粒子对采用电荷不平衡分辨负性度。在恒定电场下推导每个模式配对的闭式结果。

Abstract: We study the charge resolved structure of the pairwise mode entanglement produced by the Schwinger effect. Using the $U(1)$ electric charge of quantum electrodynamics, we decompose the entanglement of the reduced density matrices of momentum modes into individual charge sectors, applying the symmetry resolved entanglement entropy to the pure bipartition reductions and the charge imbalance resolved negativity to the mixed particle-antiparticle pair. For a constant electric field we obtain closed form results for every mode pairing, with all field dependence controlled by the single pair production probability $|β|^2=e^{-πμ}$. For the particle, particle and antiparticle antiparticle pairs the entanglement entropy within each charge sector is field independent, so that the field acts only by redistributing statistical weight among the sectors; for the mixed particle antiparticle pair the populated sector additionally carries a field dependent entropy. The vacuum created pair is the richest case: its resolved negativity is distributed over three imbalance sectors and is transported from one sector to the next as the field increases, the sectors contributing equally only at the critical field. The entanglement across a fixed momentum bipartition, by contrast, is frozen at its initial value because pair creation conserves the sector charge. Symmetry resolution thus refines the total entanglement measures into a sector resolved picture, exposing where in charge space the entanglement of the Schwinger mechanism resides and how the electric field moves it, information beyond that contained in the total measures or in particle number distributions.

</details>


### [88] [How to distinguish chaos from integrability using OTOC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04197)

<details>

Result: 算符纯度晚期饱和值随系统尺寸的标度行为能够稳健地区分混沌与可积系统；Mazur界提供了明确的标度估计并解释了局域性的作用；一维自旋链数值模拟支持了上述发现。

Conclusion: 算符纯度的标度行为可作为区分混沌与可积多体系统的可靠判据，超越OTOC的普适性局限。

Abstract: The decay of out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs) to a small residual value signals the scrambling of quantum information. This behavior is generic: it occurs in both chaotic and interacting integrable many-body systems, and therefore cannot by itself distinguish between them. In this paper we analytically study the late-time saturation value of a closely related observable, operator purity, and show that its scaling with system size robustly distinguishes chaotic from integrable systems. Our approach is based on the Mazur bound, which provides concrete scaling estimates and makes explicit why the locality of integrable charges matters. We support our findings with numerical simulations of one-dimensional spin chains.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a distinct route for extending classical atomistic spin dynamics into the quantum regime for arbitrary spin $s$, based on a variational product-state \textit{ansatz} that includes both pure and mixed local states. By construction, intersite entanglement -- expected to remain negligible even on very short time scales in many real materials -- is excluded, while local quantum effects are retained through the nonclassical structure of $s\geq 1$ density operators, and nonlinear dynamics arise from both the mean-field interdependence of the local states and the state-dependent dissipative terms. This framework clarifies the variational and geometric origin of the inequivalence between quantum Landau-Lifshitz and Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert dynamics, provides a criterion for their equivalence under a common time rescaling, establishes connections with the classical theory through two qualitatively distinct limits ($s\to\infty$ and a large number of quantum spins $N$), and allows realistic $\mathcal{O}(N)$ simulations of quantum spin dynamics in materials.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将PDC光源视为黑盒，产生具有可区分信号场和闲频场的混合多模高斯态。基于此框架推导态纯度的下界，该下界可通过模式未分辨（mode-unresolved）测量在实验上确定，无需对非线性器件及其周围光学网络建立详细模型。

Result: 所提框架能够给出态纯度的下界，并可通过模式未分辨测量实验确定，实现对实际压缩光源质量的整体评估，为优化面向先进量子应用的光源提供实用基准。

Abstract: Reliable verification of squeezed-light sources is essential for photonic quantum technologies. High-dimensional quantum optical networks demand single-mode, pure squeezed states which, in principle, can be generated through careful engineering of parametric down-conversion-based (PDC) sources. However, due to the imperfections of optical components, the generated state is never pure and commonly contains internal mode structure. Existing characterisation techniques, based on either joint spectral intensity or normalized second-order correlation functions, work under the assumption that the probed state is pure, and therefore can result in incomplete or misleading assessment of the source quality. Here, we develop a device-agnostic characterisation framework for PDC-based sources with distinguishable signal and idler fields. Treating the source as a black box producing a mixed multimode Gaussian state, we find a lower bound for the state purity, which can be experimentally determined via mode-unresolved measurements. Our approach enables a holistic assessment of realistic squeezed-light source quality without requiring a detailed model of the nonlinear device and its surrounding optical network, providing practical benchmarks for optimizing sources for state-of-the-art quantum applications.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费电子在尺寸、功耗和噪声方面对经典传感方法形成约束，量子传感被视为提升传感能力的实用途径，因此需要评估其近期可落地性。

Method: 采用平台—应用分析框架，概述通用量子传感协议，讨论与消费电子相关的四类关键平台：氮空位（NV）色心、原子蒸气池、里德堡原子传感器和光子量子传感器；并结合导航定位、医疗健康与可穿戴、射频与无线系统、物联网（IoT）及成像等案例进行分析。

Result: 通过案例研究，识别出量子传感可提供的具体工程优势，其作用方式主要是升级混合经典—量子系统中的特定测量组件，而非整体替代现有器件。

Conclusion: 量子传感近期最清晰的机会集中在弱场探测、紧凑型射频接收以及在噪声环境下保持稳健的传感；其在消费电子中的定位更可能是对经典系统的测量模块进行增强。

Abstract: Quantum sensing is emerging as a practical means of improving sensing capabilities in consumer electronics, particularly where size, power, and noise constrain classical approaches. In this paper, we reviewed the near-term applicability of quantum sensing using a platform-and-application framework. Within this framework, we outlined the general sensing protocol and discussed the working of the four key quantum sensing platforms relevant to consumer electronics: nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers, atomic vapor cells, Rydberg atomic sensors, and photonic quantum sensors. Through case studies involving navigation and positioning, healthcare and wearables, radio-frequency (RF) and wireless systems, the Internet of Things (IoT), and imaging, we examined the tangible engineering advantages offered by quantum sensing. Rather than replacing devices outright, quantum sensing is more likely to earn its place in the near-term by upgrading specific measurement components within hybrid classical-quantum systems. Right now, the clearest opportunities are in weak-field detection, compact RF reception, and sensing that remains robust in noise.

</details>

Abstract: The manipulation of non-Abelian anyons is a foundational ingredient for topological quantum computing and underlies topologically protected quantum memories. Here we analyze a quantum-impurity-based protocol for braiding non-Abelian anyons. Exploiting mobile impurities with addressable internal spin states, we provide a concrete protocol to experimentally measure the matrix elements of the $R$ and $F$ matrices that characterize the non-Abelian statistics of anyons. In particular, focusing on the case of Ising and Fibonacci anyons, we illustrate how the anyonic nature of the excitations emerges directly in the Ramsey interferometer we propose. Building on this framework, we further introduce a conventional-to-topological qubit encoder to transfer quantum information from conventional flying qubits into a topologically protected quantum memory.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自2009年提出的有限能量容量问题，确立反向相干信息速率的最优性，并揭示能量约束如何重塑可达速率与逆定理界。

Method: 核心工具为“扇区隐形传态仿真”（sector teleportation simulation），将输入能量约束转移至共享纠缠资源，从而在一般自适应协议的逆定理论证中保持约束。

Result: 四个容量在透射率 η、平均光子数 N 下均为 g(N)-g((1-η)N)；对每个 N>0，强逆阈值等于无约束值 -log₂(1-η)；对硬总光子数截断的并行协议，同一公式满足指数强逆。

Conclusion: 建立了自适应玻色通信的统一有限能量框架，表明能量约束根本性地改变了可达速率与逆定理界。

Abstract: We determine the two-way quantum, entanglement-distribution, private, and secret-key capacities of the pure-loss bosonic channel under an unconditional mean transmitted-photon-number constraint. For transmissivity $η$ and mean photon number $N$, all four capacities coincide and are given by $g(N)-g((1-η)N)$, where $g$ is the entropy of a thermal mode. This resolves the longstanding finite-energy capacity problem by establishing the optimality of the reverse-coherent-information rate introduced in 2009. Our central tool is sector teleportation simulation, which we develop specifically to transfer the input-energy constraint to the shared entanglement resource, thereby preserving the constraint throughout converse arguments for general adaptive protocols. For every $N>0$, we further show that the corresponding strong-converse thresholds coincide with the unconstrained value $-\log_2(1-η)$. For parallel protocols with a hard total-photon-number cutoff, the same finite-energy capacity formula instead satisfies an exponential strong converse. Together, these results establish a unified finite-energy framework for adaptive bosonic communication and reveal how energy constraints fundamentally reshape both attainable rates and converse bounds.

</details>

*Jinkai Liu,Xian He,Jizhou He,Jianhui Wang*

TL;DR: 通过随机化开环控制测量方位角相位，可在不改变事件率、极角和单次布居转移概率的前提下消除稳态相干，使有效布居转移率达到理论上限，从而提升三能级量子制冷机的制冷功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倾斜的非选择性测量虽能关闭三能级量子制冷机的上跃迁，但会生成相干，相干反馈到布居并抑制制冷流。因此需要寻找一种控制方案，在保留直接布居转移的同时消除有害相干。

Method: 对测量方位角相位α施加随机化开环控制，在每个事件上从概率分布P(α)独立采样，同时固定事件率、极角和单次布居转移概率。直接布居转移与相位无关，而相干源带有因子e^{-iα}。通过精确消去稳态相干，导出有效布居转移率k_eff[P]满足0≤k_eff[P]≤A_m，其中A_m为裸直接混合率。

Result: 当固定测量耦合布居与相干时，上界在且仅在⟨e^{iα}⟩_P=0时达到。最小实现使用两个等权重、相差π的相位，使布居-相干源及其回流在生成元层面消失，而完整直接混合保留。由于在制冷窗口内冷流随k_eff严格增加，该条件在所考虑的独立随机相位分布下最大化稳态制冷功率。对一组代表性参数，相位循环使固定间隙制冷功率相对固定相位测量提高约32%，同时提取冷热与供给测量能量之比保持不变。

Conclusion: 随机化相位控制（尤其两相位相差π的等权方案）可消除相干对布居的反馈，使有效布居转移率达到裸混合率上限，从而在不牺牲能量效率比的前提下显著增强量子制冷机的制冷功率。

Abstract: A tilted nonselective measurement can close the upper transition of a three-level quantum refrigerator, but it also generates coherence that feeds back on the populations and suppresses the cooling current. We formulate randomized open-loop control of the measurement azimuthal phase \(α\), sampled independently at each event from a probability distribution \(P(α)\), while keeping the event rate, polar angle, and single-event population-transfer probability fixed. Direct population transfer is phase independent, whereas the coherence source carries the factor \(e^{-iα}\). Exact elimination of the stationary coherence gives \(0\leq k_{\rm eff}[P]\leq A_m\), where \(k_{\rm eff}[P]\) is the effective population-transfer rate between the upper levels and \(A_m\) is the bare direct mixing rate. When the fixed measurement couples populations and coherence, the upper bound is attained if and only if \(\langle e^{iα}\rangle_P=0\), where \(\langle\cdot\rangle_P\) denotes an average over the phase distribution. The minimal realization uses two equally weighted phases separated by \(π\): the population-to-coherence source and its return to the populations then vanish at the generator level, while the full direct mixing remains. Because the cold current increases strictly with \(k_{\rm eff}\) in the refrigeration window, the same condition maximizes the stationary cooling power over the independent random-phase distributions considered here. For one representative parameter set, phase cycling increases the fixed-gap cooling power by about \(32\%\) relative to a fixed-phase measurement, while leaving the ratio of extracted cold heat to supplied measurement energy unchanged.

</details>

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对微扰深强耦合区间的开放量子Rabi模型，推导出Lindblad形式的量子主方程，并验证其与Bloch-Redfield方程的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在深强耦合区间，Rabi模型能谱接近简并且近似谐振，需要建立适用于该开放系统的Lindblad型主方程以刻画耗散动力学。

Method: 在Schrieffer-Wolff变换框架下，结合secular近似与singular-coupling近似，推导开放量子Rabi模型的Lindblad主方程，并与Bloch-Redfield方程对比检验。

Result: 所推导的Lindblad方程在若干可观测量的时间演化与稳态性质上与Bloch-Redfield方程高度吻合，并在极限情形下获得解析结果。

Abstract: We derive a quantum master equation in Lindblad form for the open quantum Rabi model in the perturbative deep strong coupling regime, where the spectrum is close to degenerate and harmonic. The Lindblad form is obtained by combining secular and singular-coupling approximations, working in a Schrieffer-Wolff transformed frame. We benchmark our equation against a Bloch-Redfield one, analyzing the time-evolution and steady state properties of several observables and find excellent agreement. Analytical results are obtained in limiting cases.

</details>


### [96] [Q-MERGE: Parallelising Quantum State Preparation for Large-Scale Classical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04247)
*Archie Butterworth,Jens Renders,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Q-MERGE状态制备框架，将大目标态分割为M个n量子比特片段并行制备后相干合并，以可调电路深度-比特数权衡实现高效振幅编码，在真实超声数据集上保真度较直接制备提升七个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理经典数据依赖将经典数据集映射到量子态振幅上，但大规模振幅编码态的制备是量子计算的主要瓶颈，现有方法在比特数与电路深度上难以兼顾。

Method: Q-MERGE将大目标态划分为M个n量子比特片段，各片段独立并行制备，再通过SELECT-SWAP操作与测量相干合并为单一振幅编码态。该方法对片段级制备技术不敏感，可复用现有方法；借助中电路测量与制备寄存器复用，将所需辅助比特从O(Mn)降至O(n)。

Result: 在真实128×256超声数据集上，Q-MERGE不保真度仅1.066×10⁻⁸，而相同底层方法的直接制备为3.413×10⁻¹，提升七个数量级；在Quantinuum H2离子阱量子计算机上完成实验验证，并用shadow-overlap层析验证制备态；对Haar随机态的数值分析显示M达10⁷片段仍保持稳定成功率。

Conclusion: Q-MERGE通过分而治之的策略有效缓解振幅编码态制备瓶颈，提供电路深度与量子比特数的可调权衡，并具备向大规模经典数据集编码的可扩展性。

Abstract: Quantum processing of classical data fundamentally relies on efficiently mapping classical datasets onto the amplitudes of quantum states. Preparing large amplitude-encoded states, however, remains a major bottleneck in quantum computing. In this paper, we introduce a novel state-preparation framework we call Q-MERGE, that addresses this bottleneck by partitioning a large target state into $M$ $n$-qubit segments, preparing these segments independently and in parallel, and then coherently combining them into a single amplitude-encoded state using SELECT-SWAP operations and measurement. Q-MERGE is agnostic to the segment-level preparation method, allowing existing techniques to be applied to smaller subproblems while providing a tunable trade-off between circuit depth and qubit count. With mid-circuit measurement and preparation-register reuse, the required ancilla qubits can be reduced from $\mathcal{O}(Mn)$ to $\mathcal{O}(n)$. Applied to a real-world \(128\times256\) ultrasound dataset, Q-MERGE achieves an infidelity of $1.066\times10^{-8}$, compared with $3.413\times10^{-1}$ for direct preparation using the same underlying method, a seven-order of magnitude improvement. We demonstrate its feasibility experimentally on the Quantinuum System Model H2 trapped-ion quantum computer and validate the prepared state using shadow-overlap tomography. Numerical analysis of Haar-random states indicates consistent success probability for up to $M=10^7$ segments, supporting the scalability of Q-MERGE for encoding massive classical datasets.

</details>


### [97] [Amenable groups with nearly exponential sofic profile, and quantum channels that need nearly linear memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04271)
*Seth Douglas,Nidhal Mghirbi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了一个有限呈现的初等可均群，其某有限片段的 sofic profile 为 exp(r^{1+o(1)})，并由此导出 3V 群及一个 C^873 上量子信道的相关性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究可均群离有限有多远：用有限置换模仿可均群的有限片段需要多少点，即 Cornulier 的 sofic profile。目标是逼近计数方法所能检测的上限。

Method: 构造自带灯架结构（lamplighter），灯放在构型而非点上：Houghton 群移动三条射线的点，有限支撑仿射映射作用于其二元构型，每个构型上放置一个 S_3 拷贝。使 N 个点承载 2^N 盏灯，而任意两灯可通过每步至多涉及三点的移动聚到一起。

Result: 得到 sofic profile 为 exp(r^{1+o(1)}) 的有限呈现初等可均群；该群嵌入 Brin 群 3V，故 3V 也有近乎指数 profile 的有限片段。还给出 C^873 上一个量子信道：使用 n 次约需 n/B 量子比特内存（可花 B 位纯度），精确设备在 polylog 因子内达到该界；最优交换率下最少内存约 sqrt(n)。该信道可因子化且位于有限最大混浴信道的闭包中，但任何以精度 u 模仿它的此类浴需维度 exp(u^{-1+o(1)})，而其最小 Stinespring 膨胀可用维度 130 的纯环境精确实现。

Conclusion: 核心是一个单轮定理：设备使用一次后，其内存携带该群的近似表示，从而把群的组合性质转化为信道的记忆-纯度权衡。

Abstract: How far from finite can an amenable group be? Every finite piece of an amenable group can be imitated by permutations of a finite set, and Cornulier's sofic profile counts how many points such an imitation needs at accuracy $1/r$. We build a finitely presented elementary amenable group with a finite piece whose profile is $\exp(r^{1+o(1)})$, close to the most our counting method can ever detect. The construction is a lamplighter with its lamps on configurations rather than on points. Houghton's group moves the points of three rays, finitely supported affine maps act on their binary configurations, and a copy of $S_3$ sits on every configuration. Then $N$ points carry $2^N$ lamps, yet any two lamps can be brought together by moves that each involve at most three points. The group embeds in Brin's group $3V$, so $3V$ too has a finite piece of nearly exponential profile. The group also gives an explicit quantum channel on $\mathbb C^{873}$ that is cheap to use once and expensive to use many times. A device that applies it $n$ times, releasing each output before the next input arrives, needs about $n/B$ qubits of memory when it may spend $B$ bits of purity, and exact devices achieve this up to polylogarithmic factors. With exchange at the optimal rate, the least memory is about $\sqrt n$, attained with purity of the same order. The channel is factorizable and lies in the closure of channels with finite maximally mixed baths, yet any such bath that imitates it to accuracy $u$ needs dimension $\exp(u^{-1+o(1)})$, although its minimal Stinespring dilation implements it exactly with a pure environment of dimension $130$. The engine is a one-round theorem: after a single use, the memory of any such device carries an approximate representation of the group.

</details>


### [98] [Polynomial Separation between One-Way and Two-Way LOCC in Discrimination of Maximally Entangled States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04288)
*Youngrong Lim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 单向LOCC在局域维度$d\ge4$时无法区分某些正交最大纠缠态；本文证明加入一次反馈（$A\to B\to A$协议）后，任意$k$个两两正交最大纠缠态在$d\ge Ck^4[\log(2k)]^\nu$时可完美区分，从而给出单向与双向LOCC之间的多项式分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知单向LOCC（one-way LOCC）存在局限性：在每个局域维度$d\ge4$下，单向LOCC无法区分四个正交最大纠缠态。这促使作者研究一次反馈消息（即$A\to B\to A$协议）能否显著提升可完美区分的候选态数量，并刻画单向与双向LOCC之间的能力差距。

Method: 论文构造并分析了一个$A\to B\to A$协议。在该协议中，Alice在保持态之间精确正交性的同时，降低Bob各条件子空间之间的重叠；随后Bob将候选集合缩减为单元素或一个正交对，并细化其测量结果，使Alice能够最终完成区分。作者据此推导出维度$d$与候选态数量$k$之间满足$d\ge Ck^4[\log(2k)]^\nu$（$C,\nu>0$为普适常数）时的完美区分条件。

Result: 对$\mathbb C^d\otimes\mathbb C^d$中任意$k$个两两正交最大纠缠态，当$d\ge Ck^4[\log(2k)]^\nu$时，可用$A\to B\to A$协议完美区分。单次反馈消息将可始终区分的候选态数量从常数提升到$\Omega\!\left(d^{1/4}/(\log d)^{\nu/4}\right)$，由此建立了单向LOCC与双向LOCC之间的多项式分离。作为推论，在充分大的所有局域维度下，九份拷贝即可对任意正交最大纠缠态集合实现完美双向LOCC区分。

Conclusion: 一次反馈消息足以带来单向与双向LOCC之间的多项式级区分能力分离，显著扩展了可完美区分的正交最大纠缠态规模；并且九份拷贝在足够大的局域维度下即可保证双向LOCC完美区分任意正交最大纠缠态集合。

Abstract: One-way local operations and classical communication (LOCC) can fail to distinguish four orthogonal maximally entangled states in every local dimension $d\ge4$. We show that every set of $k$ pairwise orthogonal maximally entangled states in $\mathbb C^d\otimes\mathbb C^d$ is perfectly distinguishable by an $A\to B\to A$ protocol whenever $d\ge Ck^4[\log(2k)]^ν$, for universal constants $C,ν>0$. A single feedback message therefore raises the number of candidates that can always be distinguished from a constant to $Ω\!\left(d^{1/4}/(\log d)^{ν/4}\right)$, establishing a polynomial separation between one-way and two-way LOCC. In our protocol, Alice preserves exact orthogonality while reducing overlaps between Bob's conditional subspaces. Bob reduces the candidates to a singleton or an orthogonal pair and refines his outcome so that Alice can complete the discrimination. As a corollary, nine copies suffice for perfect two-way LOCC discrimination of any orthogonal maximally entangled set in all sufficiently large local dimensions.

</details>


### [99] [The cost of each side condition in a gauged logical measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04306)
*Shuoming An,Fusheng Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文逐项检验 Williamson–Yoder 关于 gauging 逻辑测量容错保证的四个前提条件，证明其权重不等价：首末轮完美性决定时间距离，去掉后故障距离塌缩为 1；扩张条件不决定结果；轮数紧性依赖于比较规则。两类分量在形式化证明助手中精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Williamson–Yoder 已证明 gauging 逻辑测量的两个容错分量（码距离与故障隐藏轮数）均不低于码距离，但该保证依赖四个被整体接受、未经分别评估的前提条件。作者希望确定这些条件是否同等重要，即是否存在决定性的条件与冗余条件，从而明确容错阈值的真实来源，并为阈值估计提供可利用的精确数值。

Method: 在探测器比较相邻轮次的模型下，逐一放松四个前提条件：(1) 去掉首末轮完美的要求，观察故障距离变化；(2) 在同一组量子比特上构造两条均处于 covered regime 之外的扩张路径，比较其距离；(3) 区分"比较轮次即全部规则"与完整协议，检验轮数紧性。对 gauged bivariate bicycle 码与 Bacon–Shor 测量进行精确计算，并在证明助手中形式化验证。

Result: 首末轮完美的要求看似记账性质，实则承载时间部分：去掉它后，对所有码与轮数，故障距离（未被察觉却翻转读出的最小故障权重）塌缩为 1，完整协议在所测量实例上崩溃。扩张条件（不存在被封闭的小量子比特集）不决定结果：同一组量子比特上两条均在 covered regime 之外的路径分别给出距离 1 和 2。轮数在"比较轮次即全部规则"时是紧的，完整协议则提前一轮达到该距离。两类分量在 gauged bivariate bicycle 码和 Bacon–Shor 测量上均精确算出。

Conclusion: 四个前提条件权重不同：首末轮完美性是时间容错的决定性条件，扩张条件并非结果的决定因素，轮数紧性取决于探测规则。因此容错保证不能把四个条件当作不可分割的整体；阈值估计可基于分别定价后的精确距离数值，前提条件是可以被单独评估与量化的。

Abstract: A logical measurement reads out protected information, and the noise it survives sets how much a quantum computation can absorb. Gauging builds one from a graph on the measured qubits, one extra qubit on each edge, turning a global operator into local parity checks. Its tolerance splits in two: the distance of the code left behind, and the rounds a fault can hide in. Williamson and Yoder proved both components stay at or above the code's distance. The guarantee rests on four side conditions taken as a package, none of them priced. Here we show they are not worth the same: the demand that the first and last rounds be perfect, which reads like bookkeeping, carries the temporal part. Dropping it flattens the fault distance, the least weight of a fault that passes unseen and flips the readout, to one for every code and round count, in a model whose detectors compare adjacent rounds; the full protocol collapses on the instance measured. The expansion condition, that no small set of qubits be sealed off, does not decide the outcome: two paths on the same qubits, both outside the covered regime, give distances one and two. The round count is tight where comparing rounds is the whole rule; the full protocol reaches the distance a round early. Both components are computed exactly on a gauged bivariate bicycle code and a Bacon--Shor measurement, inside a proof assistant. Threshold estimates consume such numbers; side conditions can be priced.

</details>


### [100] [Generalized Dunkl Quantum Systems with Energy-Dependent Interactions: Exact Solvability and Thermodynamic Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04322)
*B. Hamil,R. Boudjouraf,A. Benchikha,M. Merad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含能量相关相互作用的广义Dunkl–Schrödinger方程描述的一类精确可解量子系统，引入双参数Dunkl算子扩展，导出修正Heisenberg代数与守恒流，求解能量相关谐振子的能谱与波函数，并分析其热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展精确可解量子模型的范围，特别是将Dunkl变形（反射对称）与能量相关相互作用相结合，以探究对能谱结构和热力学行为的调控能力。

Method: 引入双参数Dunkl微分算子扩展（包含传统反射项与额外的导数-反射耦合项），推导修正Heisenberg代数、连续性方程、守恒概率流与广义归一化条件；对能量相关谐振子求解定态方程，用连带Laguerre多项式表示本征函数与能谱；利用Euler–Maclaurin求和公式构造正则配分函数并推导热力学量。

Result: 获得能量相关谐振子的精确本征函数与能谱解析表达式；观察到宇称分裂、非线性能谱及非均匀能级间距，同时保持精确可解性；得到配分函数、内能、熵与比热的解析表达式，并给出数值分析结果。

Conclusion: 广义Dunkl变形和能量相关相互作用可有效调控系统的能谱与热力学特性，从而扩展了具有反射对称性的精确可解量子模型类别。

Abstract: This work investigates a class of exactly solvable quantum systems described by a generalized Dunkl--Schrödinger equation with energy-dependent interactions. A two-parameter extension of the Dunkl differential operator is introduced, incorporating both the conventional reflection contribution and an additional derivative-reflection coupling. The resulting formalism leads to a modified Heisenberg algebra, from which the continuity equation, conserved probability current, and generalized normalization condition are derived for energy-dependent potentials. As an application, an energy-dependent harmonic oscillator is studied in detail. Exact analytical expressions for the eigenfunctions and energy spectrum are obtained in terms of associated Laguerre polynomials. The combined effects of the Wigner parameter, the deformation parameter, and the energy-dependence parameter are shown to produce parity splitting, nonlinear energy spectra, and nonuniform level spacing while preserving exact solvability. The thermodynamic properties of the model are then examined through the canonical partition function using the Euler--Maclaurin summation formula. Analytical expressions for the partition function, internal energy, entropy, and specific heat are derived and analyzed numerically. The results demonstrate that the generalized Dunkl deformation and energy-dependent interaction provide effective control over both the spectral and thermal characteristics of the system, extending the class of exactly solvable quantum models with reflection symmetry.

</details>


### [101] [A Cheat-Sensitive Primitive for Destination-Private Quantum Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04359)
*S. F. E. Oliviero,S. Onofri,V. Giovannetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"两次调用"量子路由原语，让用户Alice在与多个客户端之一建立纠缠链路的同时，通过相干编码请求测试提供方Bob是否读取了目的地址，并刻画了完美通过极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中中间节点负责分发纠缠，但这些节点可能获知（读取）被连接的用户身份，从而威胁路由隐私。作者希望验证：路由请求本身能否携带一个物理性的作弊检测机制。

Method: 采用量子隐私查询中的隐序（hidden-order）机制构造最小路由原语：一次调用包含直接请求，另一次调用包含目标地址与空地址的相干叠加，Alice保密二者的顺序。被选中的客户端持有一个私有匹配量子比特（private matching qubit），在与有利的Bell测量结果配合下恢复相干测试态，因此Alice无需Bob再做任何操作即可检测返回的查询。

Result: 协议表明路由请求可携带物理作弊检测，并能以非零概率检测出一种显式的目的地址读取攻击。作者明确承认局限：尚未给出定量的信息—扰动权衡、可组合安全性，也未实现对任意攻击下未测量输出的认证。此外刻画了精确的完美通过极限：在该极限下Bob的残余态对私有匹配态平均后与目的地址无关。

Conclusion: 该工作是对"路由请求内含物理作弊检验"这一概念的原理性验证，属于有限主张的初步构造，而非完整安全的路由协议；后续需建立定量权衡与可组合安全性。

Abstract: Quantum networks will rely on intermediate nodes to distribute entanglement, but the same nodes may learn which users are being connected. We introduce a minimal two-call routing primitive in which a user, Alice, establishes an entangled link with one of several clients while testing whether the provider, Bob, has disturbed a coherently encoded request. The construction adapts the hidden-order mechanism of quantum private queries: one call contains a direct request, the other a coherent superposition of the desired and null addresses, and Alice keeps their order secret. A private matching qubit held by the selected client restores the coherent test state after a favorable Bell-measurement outcome, so that Alice can test the returned query without any further operation by Bob. The claim is deliberately limited. The protocol shows that a routing request can carry a physical cheating test, and it detects an explicit destination-reading attack with nonzero probability. It does not yet provide a quantitative information-disturbance tradeoff, composable security, or certification of an unmeasured output against arbitrary attacks. We also characterize the exact perfect-pass limit, in which Bob's residual state, averaged over the private matching states, is independent of the destination.

</details>


### [102] [Structural Conditions for Distributed Quantum Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04393)
*Sabina Drăgoi,María Gragera Garcés,Lirandë Pira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以"可负担的编织成本、切割后仍存的经典困难性、变分电路可分辨的梯度"三项要求为框架，系统分析电路切割下的分布式量子优势，并将其转化为筛选准则应用于十八类电路族，指出有限局域深度、有界接口电路是三项要求可共存的情形（困难性仅为最坏情况），并在IBM Nighthawk处理器上对两个场扰动环面码片做单门编织验证（最多142自旋，98量子比特内可分辨接触关联）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路切割可在比电路更小的处理器上运行量子计算，代价是经典"编织"成本随切割门数指数增长。既有工作多关注保持该成本可负担，但作者指出这既非充分也非优势来源：经典上容易的子电路被编织后依然经典容易。因此需要明确分布式量子优势的成立条件，并给出可操作的判别标准。

Method: 1）提出分布式量子优势的三项必要条件：可负担的编织、切割后仍保留的经典困难性、以及变分电路情形下可分辨的梯度；2）在给定假设与预算下证明编织成本的标度行为；3）将三项要求转化为筛选准则，对十八类电路族逐一评估；4）以经典可验证的原理性实验：在IBM Nighthawk处理器上编织连接两个场扰动环面码片的单门。

Result: 理论上证明：当子电路在"有界接口"之后增长时编织成本保持可负担，而当固定宽度的子电路数量增多时编织成本指数上升。筛选十八类电路族后，识别出有限局域深度且有界接口的电路是三项要求可能共存的情形，但其困难性仅在最坏情况下成立。实验上实现了连接两个场扰动环面码片（最多142自旋）的单门编织，并确认在最多98量子比特范围内重构能分辨出超过独立执行的接触关联。

Conclusion: 电路切割的可负担性不足以带来优势，必须以"可负担编织＋切割后困难性＋可分辨梯度"三重条件共同筛选。有限局域深度、有界接口电路是当前最有希望满足三条件的候选，但其困难性证据局限于最坏情况；单门编织实验提供了经典可验证的原理性展示，但尚不构成分布式量子优势的完整证明。

Abstract: Circuit cutting runs a quantum computation on processors smaller than the circuit, at the price of classical knitting whose cost grows exponentially with the number of cut gates. Keeping this cost affordable is necessary but not sufficient for an advantage, since knitting classically easy subcircuits is itself classically easy. We formulate three requirements for distributed quantum advantage under a stated budget: affordable knitting, classical difficulty that survives the cut, and, for variational circuits, resolvable gradients. We prove that under stated assumptions knitting stays affordable when subcircuits grow behind a bounded interface and becomes exponentially expensive when fixed-width subcircuits multiply. We turn the requirements into screening criteria and apply them to eighteen circuit families. We identify finite local-depth circuits with bounded interfaces as a setting in which the requirements can coexist, with hardness established only in the worst case. As a classically verifiable proof of principle, we knit one gate joining two field-perturbed toric-code patches on an IBM Nighthawk processor for up to 142 spins, and confirm that the reconstruction can at least resolve the contact correlation beyond independent execution for up to 98 qubits.

</details>


### [103] [Imaginarity as a resource in Shor's algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04443)
*Linlin Ye,Chuanfa Wu,Zhaoqi Wu,Shao-Ming Fei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子信道虚数性(imaginarity)视角研究Shor序查找算法的序贯版本,将过程分解为实对角因子与剩余相位信道,建立剩余虚数性与单块成功概率的定量联系,给出理想与含噪情形下成功概率的精确表达式及上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清Shor算法中量子信道虚数性所扮演的角色,并为理想与含噪的序贯量子算法提供更精细的量子资源行为视角。

Method: 固定模乘酉算子的本征分支与目标输出路径,将序贯Shor序查找过程分解为实对角因子和剩余相位信道;针对理想剩余相位信道比较三种迹范数诱导的虚数性度量,并按分支概率加权汇总;进一步推广到含噪剩余相位信道进行分析。

Result: 理想剩余相位信道的三种迹范数诱导虚数性度量彼此相等;在良好输出路径上,剩余虚数性越小则单块成功概率越高;结合分支概率得到序贯序查找协议的理想成功概率精确值,以及以剩余相位信道虚数性表示的上下界;对含噪情形亦得到含噪成功概率的上下界。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了信道虚数性在Shor算法中的作用,为理想及含噪序贯量子算法中的量子资源行为提供了精细化认识。

Abstract: We investigate the sequential version of Shor's order-finding algorithm from the perspective of the imaginarity of quantum channels. By fixing an eigenbranch of the modular multiplication unitary and a target output path, the process is effectively decomposed into a real diagonal factor and a residual phase channel. We find that for the ideal residual phase channel, the three trace-norm induced imaginarity measures are all equal. On good output paths, a smaller residual imaginarity corresponds to a higher single-block success probability. By combining the branch-wise probabilities, we obtain the exact ideal success probability of the sequential order-finding protocol and derive lower and upper bounds in terms of residual phase-channel imaginarity. We further extend the analysis to noisy residual phase channels, and obtain the lower and upper bounds on the noisy success probability. These results clarify the role of imaginarity of channels in Shor's algorithm and provide a refined perspective on quantum-resource behavior in ideal and noisy sequential quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [104] [Optimal Interferometer Geometry for Gravitationally Induced Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04471)
*Alireza Maleki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究引力诱导的量子纠缠，证明干涉仪的几何构型对纠缠生成起决定性作用，几何优化可显著增强引力相位移动并提升探测前景，同时指出既往方案中的标准近似会严重损害预测精度与实验可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力理论是现代物理的核心难题，但普朗克尺度的不可及性使直接实验验证极为困难。近期提议通过两个大质量粒子间的引力诱导量子纠缠来获取引力量子性的经验证据，然而如何提升信号强度与实验可行性仍待解决。

Method: 采用量子干涉测量技术，分析干涉仪几何构型对引力诱导纠缠生成的影响，并对既往方案中使用的标准近似进行评估。

Result: 干涉仪的几何构型对纠缠生成具有决定性作用；几何优化能大幅增强引力诱导的相位移动，从而改善引力介导纠缠的实验探测前景。

Conclusion: 几何优化为探测引力量子性质提供了可行路径，同时既往方案中的标准近似可能严重限制预测精度并损害实验可行性，需予以修正。

Abstract: The quest for a quantum theory of gravity remains a central challenge in modern physics, driven by the goal of unifying general relativity and quantum mechanics. Direct experimental access to quantum gravitational effects is notoriously difficult due to the inaccessibility of the Planck scale. Recent proposals, however, suggest that gravitationally induced quantum entanglement between two massive particles, realized through advanced quantum interferometric techniques, could provide empirical evidence for the quantum nature of gravity. We demonstrate that the geometrical configuration of an interferometer plays a decisive role in entanglement generation. Our analysis reveals that geometric optimization can substantially enhance the gravitationally induced phase shift, thereby improving the prospects for experimental detection of gravity-mediated entanglement. Moreover, we show that standard approximations employed in prior proposals may severely limit predictive accuracy and compromise experimental feasibility.

</details>


### [105] [The collapse time of weak measurement on $N$ entangled sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04556)
*Truong-Son P. Van,David W. Snoke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定在PRA 111, 032217 (2025)模型中，N个备选态初始叠加下局域弱测量坍缩到单一占据格点所需时间：等初始占据概率、固定置信阈值1−δ时，证明T_N/ln N→1/2（L¹收敛），时间以单探测器特征测量时间τ_m为单位；并将渐近稳定定理与有限测量联系：首个达到1−δ的格点即最终结果，条件概率恰为1−δ；纠正了此前的双对数渐近标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对局域弱测量在N个备选态初始叠加中坍缩到单一占据格点所需时间的标度问题，此前工作提出了双对数渐近标度，本文旨在给出严格结果并纠正该标度。

Method: 在等初始占据概率与固定置信阈值1−δ条件下，分析以单探测器特征测量时间τ_m为单位的坍缩时间T_N，采用L¹意义下的渐近分析；并将该工作的渐近稳定定理与有限测量过程相衔接，考察首个达到1−δ的格点与最终结果之间的关系。

Result: 证明了T_N/ln N→1/2（在L¹意义下）；首个达到1−δ的格点即最终结果，且条件概率恰为1−δ；同时指出有限范围数值拟合无法确定渐近常数修正或精确的有限N规律。

Conclusion: 局域弱测量的坍缩时间随N呈对数标度，系数为1/2；置信阈值1−δ与最终结果的判定条件概率精确对应；纠正了此前双对数渐近标度的结论。

Abstract: We determine the time required for local weak measurements to collapse to one occupied site in an initial superposition of $N$ alternatives in the model of PRA 111, 032217 (2025). For equal initial occupation probabilities and a fixed confidence threshold $1-δ$, we prove $T_N/\ln N\to1/2$ in $L^1$, with time measured in units of the characteristic measurement time $τ_m$ of each detector. We also connect the asymptotic stabilization theorem of that work to a finite measurement: the first site to reach $1-δ$ is the eventual outcome with conditional probability exactly $1-δ$. The result corrects the previously proposed double-logarithmic asymptotic scaling. The finite-range numerical fit does not determine a constant asymptotic correction or a precise finite-$N$ law.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Submodular Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04560)
*Yonggang Jiang,Xiaoming Sun,Penghui Yao,Zekun Ye,Jialin Zhang,Zhijie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非负子模函数最大化的量子查询复杂度，涵盖无约束情形以及单调函数的基数约束情形。在精确可逆数字值Oracle模型下，无约束算法以 O(log n) 查询达到 (1/2-ε) 近似，与经典 Ω(n/log n) 下界形成指数分离；基数约束算法以 Õ(min{√n, n/k}) 查询达到 (1-1/e-ε) 近似，并在特定参数下实现指数分离，同时给出了相应的量子下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模函数最大化在机器学习、影响力最大化等领域应用广泛，但经典查询复杂度下界较高。作者希望探究量子计算能否在查询复杂度上带来加速，特别是无约束与基数约束两种设定下，能否突破经典算法的查询下界，并明确量子加速的适用边界。

Method: 在精确可逆数字值Oracle模型下设计量子算法：1）无约束情形，构造期望 (1/2-ε)-近似算法，仅需 O_ε(log n) 查询；2）基数约束单调函数情形，设计有界误差量子算法，使用 Õ_ε(min{√n, n/k}) 查询达到 (1-1/e-ε) 近似。此外通过量子下界证明，刻画超过特定近似阈值所需的查询复杂度。

Result: 无约束：量子算法 O_ε(log n) 查询达 (1/2-ε) 近似，而经典算法达到高于 1/4 的固定期望比率需 Ω(n/log n) 查询，形成指数分离。基数约束：量子算法 Õ_ε(min{√n, n/k}) 查询达 (1-1/e-ε) 近似；k=o(n) 时相对经典算法至多对数因子下达到至少二次加速；k=cn（c<1-1/e-ε 固定有理数）时查询复杂度降至 O_{ε,c}(log n)，与经典 Ω(n/log n) 下界形成指数分离。下界：无约束超过 1/2+ε 需 exp(Ω(ε²n)) 查询；k/n≤ε 时超过 1-1/e+ε 需 exp(Ω(ε²k)) 查询。

Conclusion: 量子计算在非负子模函数最大化上可带来显著查询复杂度优势：无约束与部分基数约束情形下实现指数分离，一般 k 情形实现二次加速。但所证明的下界表明，在特定近似阈值（无约束 1/2、约束 1-1/e）之外，量子计算无法提供指数加速，界定了量子优势的适用范围。

Abstract: We study the quantum query complexity of maximizing a non-negative submodular function, considering both the unconstrained setting and, for monotone functions, a cardinality constraint $k$ on an $n$-element ground set. In the exact reversible digital value-oracle model, our unconstrained algorithm achieves an expected $(1/2-\varepsilon)$-approximation using only $O_\varepsilon(\log n)$ queries. In contrast, any classical randomized algorithm that attains a fixed expected ratio above $1/4$ requires $Ω(n/\log n)$ queries (Li, Feldman, Kazemi, and Karbasi, 2022), establishing an exponential separation in query complexity. For cardinality-constrained maximization, we give a bounded-error quantum algorithm that achieves a $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)$-approximation using $\widetilde O_\varepsilon(\min\{\sqrt n,n/k\})$ queries. When $k=o(n)$, our algorithm achieves at least a quadratic speedup up to logarithmic factors over classical randomized algorithms (Mirzasoleiman, Badanidiyuru, Karbasi, Vondrák, and Krause, 2015; Peng and Rubinstein, 2025). Moreover, when $k=cn$ for any fixed rational $c<1-1/e-\varepsilon$, the query complexity reduces to $O_{\varepsilon,c}(\log n)$, yielding an exponential separation from the classical $Ω(n/\log n)$ lower bound (Li, Feldman, Kazemi, and Karbasi, 2022). We further prove quantum lower bounds of $\exp(Ω(\varepsilon^2n))$ queries for achieving a ratio beyond $1/2+\varepsilon$ without constraints, and $\exp(Ω(\varepsilon^2k))$ queries for exceeding $1-1/e+\varepsilon$ when $k/n\le\varepsilon$. These barriers demonstrate that quantum computation offers no exponential speedup at these approximation thresholds.

</details>


### [107] [Geometry-Controlled Squeezed-Waveguide Enabled Quantum Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04562)
*Luis D. Zambrano-Palma,Yusef Maleki,M. Suhail Zubairy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究波导中多发射体量子电池在压缩真空库驱动下的稳态性能，揭示波导几何位置如何通过调控非经典关联来控制电池的非被动性（ergotropy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用非经典资源提升量子电池性能是量子技术的重要目标；本文旨在探明一维波导几何结构对多发射体电池性能及热力学特性的调控机制。

Method: 考虑耦合到一维波导、由宽带压缩真空库驱动的多发射体电池，通过分析单、双、三发射体情形下集体与相位敏感耦合（依赖于发射体位置）对稳态关联的影响，研究几何对非被动性及ergotropy的调控。

Result: 单发射体：压缩库注入有限能量但态保持被动，ergotropy为零；双发射体：相位敏感关联产生相干并可将稳态驱入完全非被动区；三发射体：集体热动力学已支持有限ergotropy，压缩进一步提升可提取能量。由于集体与相位敏感耦合依赖位置，几何可直接控制非经典关联与稳态非被动性；ergotropy可被强烈调制而储能变化微弱，双发射体下仅靠几何即可在被动稳态与全部能量可幺正提取之间切换。

Conclusion: 波导几何是调控压缩驱动多发射体量子电池非被动性与ergotropy的有效手段，可在储能基本不变的情况下实现可提取功的可控切换。

Abstract: Harnessed nonclassical resources to enhance the performance of quantum batteries is an important goal of quantum technology. In this work, we investigate multi-emitter batteries coupled to a one-dimensional waveguide driven by a broadband squeezed-vacuum reservoir, and determine how the waveguide geometry controls the battery performance and and their thermodynamic features. We show that for a single emitter, the squeezed reservoir deposits finite energy while the state remains passive, yielding zero ergotropy. For two emitters, however, phase-sensitive reservoir correlations generate coherence and can drive the steady state into a fully nonpassive regime. For three emitters, collective thermal dynamics already supports finite ergotropy, while squeezing further increases the extractable component of the stored energy. Crucially, because the collective and phase-sensitive couplings depend on the emitter positions, geometry provides direct control over the nonclassical correlations and, consequently, over the nonpassivity of the steady state. The ergotropy can therefore be strongly modulated while the stored energy changes only weakly, and in the two-emitter configuration geometry alone can switch the battery between a passive steady state and one in which all stored energy is unitarily extractable.

</details>


### [108] [Decoy-state optical quantum information processing with coherent states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04581)
*Wenyuan Wang,H. F. Chau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于相位随机化相干态与阈值探测器，结合诱骗态后处理与线性回归方法，实现单光子源和光子数分辨探测器的模拟，显著提升Fock态统计预测精度并大幅减少探测资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效高速单光子源与光子数分辨（PNR）探测器在量子信息处理中至关重要，但迄今仍难以实现，因此需要一种利用易获得的相干光源和阈值探测器来等效模拟这些器件的方案。

Method: 采用相位随机化相干态与阈值探测器，结合诱骗态后处理来模拟单光子源和PNR探测器；提出一种简单线性回归方法，通过相干态探针的可观测量统计及随机/结构化采样预测Fock态统计，替代传统的线性规划与笛卡尔积网格采样。

Result: 相比传统诱骗态后处理，该方法精度大幅提升且探针资源显著减少；对固定Hilbert空间维度的未知酉电路尤为有效，例如仅用32组输入/输出探针设置即可准确模拟线性光学中的4光子干涉，用256组探针模拟6光子干涉；同时适用于耗散、非线性等非酉量子信道。

Conclusion: 该方法可利用易获得的相干光源和阈值探测器完成多种任务，如小规模线性光学量子计算电路表征以及量子传感/计量器件表征，具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Photonic qubits play an important role in quantum information processing. However, to date, efficient high-speed single photon sources and photon number resolving (PNR) detectors are still difficult to make. Here, we use phase-randomized coherent states and threshold detectors, combined with decoy-state post-processing, to simulate single photon sources and PNR detectors and perform various quantum information processing tasks. We propose a simple linear regression method to predict Fock state statistics based on observable statistics from coherent-state probes, combined with random/structured sampling of probe settings. Compared with traditional decoy-state post-processing based on linear programming and Cartesian product grid sampling, our method enables drastically improved accuracy and much reduced probe resources. It works particularly well for unknown unitary circuits of interest with fixed-size Hilbert space dimensions. For instance, it can accurately simulate 4-photon interference in linear optics with just 32 input/output probe settings and 6-photon interference with 256 probe settings. Moreover, it also works for non-unitary (such as lossy, nonlinear, or arbitrary) quantum channels. This enables a wide variety of applications that can be implemented with easily attainable coherent light sources and threshold detectors, such as characterization of small-scale linear optical quantum computing circuits or quantum sensing/metrology devices.

</details>


### [109] [Variational Quantum Attention for Molecular Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04588)
*Yu-Cheng Lin,Yu-Chao Hsu,Tai-Yue Li,Nan-Yow Chen,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出边感知变分量子注意力机制（QGAT），将接收原子、邻近原子与连接键共同编码为量子注意力状态，用于分子图学习。在五项分子性质与生物活性预测任务中性能与GATv2相当，在BBBP上跨六种电路拟设一致提升；并在Verubecestat BACE1抑制剂系列中取得更高Spearman相关性与符合SAR的归因。电路消融表明性能依赖电路设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测是计算药物发现的核心，图神经网络通过消息传递学习邻近原子环境的权重，但变分量子电路如何改变分子图中的注意力行为尚不明确，缺乏对其准确性之外（如归因行为）的系统比较。

Method: 提出边感知变分量子注意力机制（QGAT）：注意力状态由接收原子、邻近原子与连接键三者共同决定，以变分量子电路参数化注意力打分器，并与匹配的经典打分器（GATv2）进行对照。在五项分子性质与生物活性预测任务上评测，覆盖六种电路拟设，并进行电路消融与归因分析。

Result: QGAT相对GATv2具竞争力，在BBBP上跨全部六种电路拟设一致提升；在Verubecestat BACE1抑制剂系列中，QGAT的Spearman相关性高于GATv2，并对若干与已报道SAR一致的结构变化赋予正向归因；电路消融显示性能依赖电路设计；两种注意力机制在结构相关的BACE1类似物上呈现不同的预测与归因行为。

Conclusion: 变分量子注意力可作为可行的分子注意力参数化替代方案，其行为与匹配的经典打分器不同，且具有电路与化学体系依赖性；这些差异能否在更广的化学系列与靶点上一致泛化，仍需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction is central to computational drug discovery, where graph neural networks learn to weight neighboring atomic environments during message passing. Yet it remains unclear how variational quantum circuits alter learned attention behavior in molecular graphs. We introduce an edge-aware variational quantum attention mechanism for molecular graph learning, in which the receiving atom, neighboring atom, and connecting bond jointly determine the quantum attention state. Across five molecular property and bioactivity prediction tasks, QGAT achieves competitive performance relative to GATv2, with a consistent improvement on BBBP across all six evaluated circuit ansatzes. We further compare how the quantum and classical attention scores weight molecular structure beyond accuracy. In the Verubecestat BACE1 inhibitor series, QGAT achieves a higher Spearman correlation than GATv2 and assigns positive attributions to several structural changes consistent with reported structure-activity relationships (SARs). This case study shows that the two attention mechanisms can exhibit different prediction and attribution behavior across structurally related BACE1 analogues, while broader validation is required to determine how consistently these differences generalize across chemical series and targets. Circuit ablations further show that performance depends on the circuit design. Together, these results show that variational quantum attention can serve as a viable alternative molecular attention parameterization while inducing circuit- and chemistry-dependent behavior distinct from a matched classical scorer.

</details>


### [110] [Efficient Quantum State Identity Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04597)
*Shih-Han Hung,Ming-Hsien Tsai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子态身份问题：给定n个未知纯态的副本，判断是否所有对的保真度≥1-ε，或存在某对保真度≤ε。对任意固定ε∈(0,1/4)，提出两种量子算法，分别优化电路深度与样本复杂度，并证明匹配的样本复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题放宽了此前工作中的"全同或正交"承诺，即不再要求任意一对态要么完全相同、要么完全正交，而是允许保真度在ε与1-ε之间的容忍区间。动机在于使量子态身份判别更贴近实际场景，并在电路深度与样本复杂度之间提供可权衡的算法方案，同时刻画该松弛问题的样本复杂度极限。

Method: 第一种算法采用并行交换测试（parallel swap tests），实现常数电路深度，每个输入态消耗O(log(n/δ)log(2/δ))个副本，成功概率至少1-δ。第二种算法基于Schur变换，通过将Jucys–Murphy元素与Schur采样相关联的分析，将样本复杂度降至O(log(n/δ))。此外，利用该分析证明任意失败概率≤δ≤1/2-c（c为任意正常数）的量子算法所需样本数下界为Ω(log(n/δ))。

Result: 对任意固定ε∈(0,1/4)，常数深度算法以O(log(n/δ)log(2/δ))样本/态成功；Schur变换算法将样本复杂度降至O(log(n/δ))；并证明匹配下界Ω(log(n/δ))，故第二种算法在n与δ两方面均达到最优依赖（ε固定时）。

Conclusion: 该工作在放宽的量子态身份问题上给出了深度与样本复杂度互补的两种算法，并通过Jucys–Murphy元素与Schur采样的联系确立最优样本复杂度下界，表明O(log(n/δ))为固定ε时的紧确样本复杂度。

Abstract: We study the following quantum state identity problem: given access to copies of $n$ unknown pure quantum states, the goal is to determine whether every pair has fidelity at least $1-\varepsilon$ or some pair has fidelity at most $\varepsilon$. This problem relaxes the identical-or-orthogonal promise considered in previous work. For any fixed overlap parameter $\varepsilon\in(0,1/4)$, we present two quantum algorithms with complementary guarantees on circuit depth and sample complexity. The first runs in constant depth using parallel swap tests and takes $O(\log(n/δ)\log(2/δ))$ copies of each input state to succeed with probability at least $1-δ$. The second uses the Schur transform to reduce the sample complexity to $O(\log(n/δ))$, through an analysis relating Jucys--Murphy elements to Schur sampling. We also prove a matching sample complexity lower bound of $Ω(\log(n/δ))$ for any quantum algorithm with failure probability at most $δ\le 1/2-c$, for any constant $c>0$, establishing that the second algorithm achieves the optimal dependence on both $n$ and $δ$ for fixed $\varepsilon$.

</details>


### [111] [Multi-Yoked Surface Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04613)
*Yuga Hirai,Yasunari Suzuki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广yoked surface code至多级级联，提出multi-yoked surface codes。通过引入chain codes外层码和分层yoking区间的可移动工作区patch分配方案，构造三级yoked surface code（[[256,166,8]]外层码），在SI1000噪声模型下验证其逻辑错误率随内码距离指数抑制，teraquop区密度较未yoked表面码提升约4–4.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面码是容错量子计算的主流方案，具备平面最近邻连接、高阈值和高效解码等优势，但其编码率低，在teraquop区每个逻辑量子比特需约千个物理量子比特。已有yoked surface codes通过在其上叠加高密度奇偶校验码可将密度提升2–3倍，但单层级联提升有限，需要进一步推广以获取更高编码密度。

Method: 提出multi-yoked surface codes，实现多级级联。两项核心构造：（1）chain codes外层码族，通过链接seed code的副本构建，该seed code能检测下一级码的块错误；这些码利用下层全部逻辑量子比特，在少数级联层级内即可实现高编码率。（2）patch分配方案，采用可移动工作区（movable workspaces）经lattice surgery执行yoking校验，并设计分层yoking区间，使低层校验频繁执行、高层校验较少执行，从而避免单一大型外层码所需的长yoking区间。以三级yoked surface code（[[256,166,8]]外层码）为基准，在SI1000噪声模型下利用表面码解码器的软信息进行数值评估。

Result: 逻辑错误率随内码距离呈指数抑制。在teraquop区，三级yoked surface code的密度约为未yoked表面码的4至4.5倍，优于此前yoked surface codes的2–3倍提升。

Conclusion: 多级级联结合chain codes与分层yoking区间设计，可在保持平面最近邻连接的前提下显著提高表面码编码密度，为teraquop区容错量子计算提供更高效的编码方案。

Abstract: The surface code is the leading candidate for fault-tolerant quantum computation owing to its planar nearest-neighbor connectivity, high threshold, and efficient decoding, but its low encoding rate demands on the order of a thousand physical qubits per logical qubit in the teraquop regime. Yoked surface codes improve the density by a factor of two or three by concatenating a high-density parity check code on top of the surface code while retaining planar nearest-neighbor connectivity. In this work, we generalize this idea to multiple levels of concatenation and introduce multi-yoked surface codes. Our construction rests on two new ingredients. First, we propose a family of outer codes called chain codes, built by chaining copies of a seed code that detects the block errors of the code one level below; the resulting codes use all logical qubits of the lower level and achieve a high encoding rate within a few concatenation levels. Second, we design a patch allocation in which movable workspaces perform the yoking checks by lattice surgery with a hierarchy of yoking intervals, so that lower-level checks are performed frequently while higher-level checks are performed less often, which avoids the long yoking intervals that a single large-distance outer code would require. As a benchmark, we construct a three-yoked surface code with a $[[256,166,8]]$ outer code and evaluate it numerically under the SI1000 noise model using soft information from the surface code decoder. The logical error rate is suppressed exponentially in the inner code distance, and in the teraquop regime the three-yoked surface code is about 4 to 4.5$\times$ denser than the unyoked surface code.

</details>


### [112] [Exact Clifford Optimality for Two-Sided Locally Randomized Classical Shadows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04621)
*Xiaotian Nie,Tao Zhang,Yadong Wu,Linghui Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究经典阴影框架下测量多体关联的统计代价，证明两体集体泡利旋转电路在特定测量架构中达到最小平方阴影范数，并将最优前因子从2改进至4/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠测量可降低学习多体关联的统计代价。作者希望在经典阴影方案中寻找最优的可控幺正电路，以最小化测量Pauli串所需的采样成本，并验证已有工作中指数基的最优性。

Method: 针对作用在指定k量子比特区域、具有全支撑的Pauli串，采用如下测量架构：可控幺正两侧各加一层独立均匀随机的乘积单比特Clifford层，随后进行计算基读出。在该架构下，解析求解所有Clifford选择下的平方阴影范数最小值。

Result: 证明由两个集体Pauli旋转构成的电路U_*对所有k≥1均达到平方阴影范数的精确最小值：奇数k为4·9^k/(3·5^k+3^k−6)，偶数k为4·9^k/(3·5^k−3^k+2)。渐近最优值为(4/3)·(9/5)^k；对1≤k≤6，给出精确有理证书，证明该最优值在整个U(2^k)（含非Clifford幺正）上成立。

Conclusion: 该结果确立了Wu等人的指数基(9/5)^k为最优，并将其前因子从2改进为4/3；有限尺寸结果支持如下猜想：在该测量架构下，U_*对任意k在所有幺正变换中都是最优的。

Abstract: Entangling measurements can reduce the statistical cost of learning many-body correlations. We study Pauli strings with full support on a specified $k$-qubit region using classical shadows with two independent, uniformly random product single-qubit Clifford layers around a controllable unitary acting on that region, followed by computational-basis readout. In this architecture, we prove that a circuit $U_*$ of two collective Pauli rotations attains the exact minimum squared shadow norm over all Clifford choices of the controllable unitary for every $k\geq1$, namely $C_k^{\rm opt}=4\cdot 9^k/(3\cdot 5^k+3^k-6)$ for odd $k$ and $C_k^{\rm opt}=4\cdot 9^k/(3\cdot 5^k-3^k+2)$ for even $k$. The asymptotic optimum $(4/3)\cdot(9/5)^k$ establishes Wu et al.'s exponential base as optimal and improves their prefactor from $2$ to $4/3$. For $1\leq k\leq6$, exact rational certificates prove that the same optimum holds over the entire unitary group $\mathrm U(2^k)$, including all non-Clifford unitaries, within the same measurement architecture. These finite-size results motivate the conjecture that $U_*$ is optimal over all unitaries for arbitrary $k$ within this architecture.

</details>


### [113] [Bounding Two-Way Average Communication Cost of Simulating Quantum Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04648)
*Kai-Siang Chen,Gelo Noel M. Tabia,Bo-An Tsai,Swati Kumari,Yeong-Cherng Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从非局域博弈出发，推导了输入平均通信代价的下界，适用于变长、完全交互的双向协议，并在并行博弈上给出显式有限量子关联的精确模拟代价下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell非局域关联无法由无通信的局域隐变量模型复现，因此经典通信代价成为非局域性的自然量化指标。但确定精确模拟任意给定非局域关联所需的最小通信量（尤其在平均代价设定下）仍具挑战性。

Method: 利用非局域博弈推导输入平均通信代价下界，允许变长、完全交互的双向协议；将界应用于并行博弈；并构建由确定关联组成的下界层级与配合选定通信代价的上界。

Result: 对n个并行Magic-Square获胜关联证明下界n log2(3/2)比特，并给出渐近达到该率的单向协议；对最大化赢得n份CHSH博弈的量子关联，得到精确模拟的输入平均下界约0.04627n比特。

Conclusion: 所提下界层级与互补上界在最终层可恢复精确输入平均代价；若在较低层已一致，其公共值即为精确输入平均代价，无需考察所有通信策略。

Abstract: Bell nonlocal correlations cannot be reproduced by local hidden-variable models without communication, making classical communication cost a natural quantitative measure of nonlocality. Although finite communication always suffices to simulate any correlation in a fixed finite Bell scenario, determining the minimum amount needed to (exactly) simulate any given nonlocal correlation remains challenging, especially in the average-cost setting. Here, we derive lower bounds on input-average communication cost from nonlocal games, allowing variable-length, fully interactive two-way protocols. When applied to parallel games, these bounds yield explicit finite quantum correlations for which the exact simulation cost exceeds any prescribed finite input-average communication budget. For $n$ parallel Magic-Square-winning correlations, we prove a lower bound of $n\log_2(3/2)$ bits and give a one-way communication protocol that attains this rate asymptotically. For the quantum correlation maximally winning the $n$-copy of the Clauser--Horne--Shimony--Holt nonlocal game, we obtain an input-average lower bound of approximately $0.04627n$ bits for exact simulation. Finally, we formulate a hierarchy of lower bounds and complementary upper bounds using deterministic correlations up to chosen communication costs. Both recover the exact input-average cost at their final levels, and if the bounds agree at lower levels, their common value yields the exact input-average cost without needing to consider all communication strategies.

</details>


### [114] [Correlations decide a shallow-circuit advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04666)
*Zijian Gong,Zhaobin Lyu,Jingjing Hu,Dengfeng Li,Shuoming An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅用经典样本即可认证"量子电路采样优于浅层经典电路"的检验方法，其关键在于经典类别的单比特与成对关联模式而非距离度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机被宣称能产生经典计算机无法复现的样本，其中最强的论点涉及常数深度量子电路与浅层经典电路的对比。然而此前没有高效检验方法能仅凭经典样本认证这一论断，因此需要设计一种可认证该类量子优势的统计检验。

Method: 检验基于一个不等式：任何采样机器与目标分布的距离不超过其误标比例加上"串半部分"（标签之外的比特）偏离均匀分布的程度。作者用Lean 4证明助手机器验证了一个塌缩定理，该定理将第二项限制为每次设定采样器随机输入比特的一个设置。作者分析了四种自然检验（均被证明失败）以及第五种检验——读取相同的单比特与成对关联。对标签检验证明了其最优性，并在有界钉扎余数类上证明了第五检验的可靠性。

Result: 四种自然的检验方法被证明失败；第五种基于关联的检验能捕获前者无法触及的远离目标构造，并在少输入门类上精确成立。标签检验在其容差内被证明为样本最优（至多相差对数因子）；第五检验在有界钉扎余数类（成对均匀采样器，其余数即串权重模素数由少数种子钉扎，且种子共享有界）上是可靠的。实验受限于43量子比特纠缠资源态（保真度接近0.99），而非读出环节。

Conclusion: 该工作表明经典样本认证的关键在于关联模式而非距离，并留下两个待解决的开放问题，其中第一个问题的肯定回答将把保证扩展到完整类别。

Abstract: Quantum computers are claimed to produce samples ordinary classical computers cannot reproduce. The sharpest such claim known pits constant-depth quantum circuits, given one entangled state per run, against shallow classical circuits of few-input gates and limited randomness. No efficient test was known that certifies such a claim from classical samples alone. Here we show that what such a test must read is decided by the classical class's pattern of single-bit and pair correlations, not by a distance. The test rests on one inequality: any machine producing the samples sits no further from the target than the fraction it mislabels plus the deviation of its string half, the bits beyond the label, from uniform. A collapse theorem, machine-checked in the Lean~4 proof assistant, confines that second term, one setting of the sampler's random input bits at a time. Four natural checks provably fail; a fifth, reading those same correlations, catches a far-from-target construction they cannot reach, and is exact on the class of few-input gates. The label test is proven and sample-optimal in its own tolerance up to a logarithmic factor; the fifth check is sound, not only effective, on the bounded pinned-residue class, pairwise-uniform samplers whose residue, the string's weight modulo the prime, is pinned by few seeds and whose seed sharing is bounded. What binds an experiment is the $43$-qubit entangled resource state at fidelity near $0.99$, not the readout. What remains are two named open problems; a positive answer to the first would extend the guarantee to the full class.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum-State Oblivious Transfer over Quantum Erasure Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04667)
*Remi A. Chou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究量子擦除信道上的量子态不经意传输，在信息论安全下给出有限码长逆定理与可达界，诚实但好奇情形两者匹配，恶意情形结合交互哈希与信道输出检测，两种情形容量相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子擦除信道上的量子态不经意传输（quantum-state oblivious transfer）在信息论安全保证下的根本极限，涵盖诚实但好奇与恶意两类对手，此前缺乏该信道下有限码长的紧致刻画。

Method: 模型设定：Alice持有两个量子系统，Bob欲恢复其中之一；双方可独立使用信道并进行认证的公开经典通信，且无预先共享纠缠。分别分析诚实但好奇与恶意行为。通过建立有限码长逆定理与可达性界；恶意情形构造结合交互哈希（interactive hashing）与“Bob是否收到选定信道输出”的检测，并把每个输入分散到多个量子比特上，使擦除能对不诚实接收者隐藏整个输入，即使输入与外部参考系纠缠。

Result: 在诚实但好奇情形下，有限码长逆定理与可达界相互匹配；针对恶意情形给出相应构造；并推导固定误差渐近展开，证明恶意与诚实但好奇两种情形具有相同容量。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了量子擦除信道上量子态不经意传输的有限码长性能与渐近容量，表明恶意与诚实但好奇设置在容量上无差异，为后续协议设计提供理论基准。

Abstract: We study quantum-state oblivious transfer over the quantum erasure channel under information-theoretic security guarantees. Specifically, Alice holds two quantum systems, Bob wishes to recover one of them, and the parties have access to independent channel uses and authenticated public classical communication, with no initial shared entanglement. We consider both honest-but-curious and malicious behavior. We establish finite-blocklength converse and achievability bounds, which match in the honest-but-curious setting. The malicious setting construction combines interactive hashing with tests of whether Bob received selected channel outputs, and distributes each input across several qubits so that erasures conceal an entire input from a dishonest receiver, even when the inputs are entangled with an external reference. We also derive fixed-error asymptotic expansions and show that both malicious and honest-but-curious settings have the same capacity.

</details>


### [116] [Stabilizer codes over general phase spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04694)
*Sayan Chakraborty,Victor V. Albert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了由Qudit Pauli、振子位移和平面转子位移构成的稳定子码理论，涵盖构造、逻辑算子、距离、最优解码、重量枚举及有限能量码字。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一并推广Pauli码、Qudit码与GKP码等稳定子码，探索不能通过广义Clifford变换分解为独立子系统的混合系统码（振子-转子、转子-Qudit、振子-转子-Qubit及基于Golay码的振子-Qubit码）。

Method: 考虑稳定子群在量子相空间中构成广义格（保证有限逻辑维度）；借助Rieffel的非交换几何结果，并将其解释为与GKP码的级联/解级联配合格论结果；由辛对偶格导出逻辑算子解析形式，由格对称性确定Clifford-Gaussian门，将伴随子空间组织为向量丛。

Result: 构造出不可经广义Clifford变换分解的振子-转子、转子-Qudit、振子-转子-Qubit码，以及由部分提升Golay码（对Golay码一半坐标做Construction A）得到的振子-Qubit码；证明码维度等于稳定子格的余体积；给出距离、最优解码与量子重量枚举，并恢复Pauli、Qudit、GKP版本及已知MacWilliams恒等式；构造有限能量码字，其编码在逆阻尼参数上指数小误差意义下为等距。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了离散与连续变量稳定子码，提供了混合量子系统的格论构造工具与解码/枚举理论，并给出满足有限能量等距编码的实用码字构造。

Abstract: We develop a theory of stabilizer codes whose stabilizer groups consist of commuting qudit Paulis, oscillator displacements, and/or planar-rotor displacements. We consider codes whose stabilizer groups form generalized lattices in quantum phase space, a condition that guarantees a finite logical dimension. We construct oscillator-rotor, rotor-qudit, and oscillator-rotor-qubit codes that cannot be decomposed into separate subsystems by generalized Clifford transformations. We also introduce an oscillator-qubit code arising from a partially lifted Golay code, where Construction A is applied to half of the Golay code coordinates. We derive analytical forms of logical operators from the symplectic dual lattice, determine Clifford-Gaussian gates from lattice symmetries, show that the code dimension equals the covolume of the stabilizer lattice, and organize the syndrome subspaces into a vector bundle. Our technique builds on earlier non-commutative geometric results by Rieffel, but it can often be interpreted simply as concatenating a given stabilizer code with a Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code, applying conventional lattice-theoretic results, and unconcatenating. We formulate distances, optimal decoding, and quantum weight enumerators, recovering the Pauli, qudit, and GKP versions together with known MacWilliams identities. We construct finite-energy codewords whose encoding is an isometry up to an error exponentially small in the inverse damping parameter.

</details>


### [117] [Sum-of-squares compilation of matrix-model dynamics for NISQ and fault-tolerant hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04731)
*Ayanendu Dutta,Nabanita Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将 SU(N) 矩阵模型势 V 精确分解为对称化平方对易子项之和，分别用 Hadamard（NISQ）与 T（容错）两类门实现平方运算，绕开 parity network 的门类下界，把 Trotter 步代价从 O(d²N⁴Q⁴) 降至 Θ(d²N³Q³) 双比特门 / Θ(d²N³Q²) T 门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BFSS/BMN 矩阵模型势的 Trotter 编译此前以相位多项式形式实现，需 O(d²N⁴Q⁴) 次旋转，接近其门类（{CNOT,CZ,CPhase,R_ZZ,SWAP,X,R_z}，含 ancilla）的下界：Walsh 支撑 m_min 同时下界旋转数与双比特门数，所构 parity network 仅达 1.01–1.10 m_min。要突破必须引入该类之外的门，故需新编译方案。

Method: 基于 V 的精确分解 V = 平方对易子项之和，为每项各构造一个门类外的实现：NISQ 路线用 Hadamard 把每个平方项写入 Fourier 寄存器并在该处平方（Θ(d²N³Q³) 双比特门）；容错路线用可逆算术，每个平方项注入一次相位梯度（Θ(d²N³Q²) T 门，无任意角旋转）。两条电路均实际构建并验证，并做同算术但无分解的消融实验。

Result: 原生 R_ZZ 下，NISQ 电路自 Q=6（SU(2)）、Q=2（SU(5)）起双比特门数低于所有 parity network；oracle 除最小点外全面优于精度匹配的 Hamming-weight-phasing 基线，T 计数最高 11.9×，等宽下表面码时空最高 25.5×。消融（同算术无分解）代价最高多 21×，且在 17 点中 15 点劣于该基线，说明增益来自分解而非算术。两构造均可延续至含费米子的 mini-BMN 超对称变形；态矢量模拟器上完整 Trotter 演化与精确乘积公式吻合至 2×10⁻¹²。

Conclusion: Walsh 支撑给出的门类下界表明相位多项式路线已近最优，必须换门类；把 V 精确分解为平方对易子并配合 Hadamard 平方或可逆算术相位注入，可在 NISQ 与容错两种体制下分别获得 Θ(d²N³Q³) 与 Θ(d²N³Q²) 的代价降低，且结果由消融、基线对比与模拟精度共同佐证。

Abstract: The SU$(N)$ matrix-model potential $V=-\tfrac{g^2}{4}\sum_{I,J}Tr[X_I,X_J]^2$, the interaction of the BFSS/BMN family, whose thermal large-$N$ states are dual to black holes, is diagonal in the coordinate basis and is compiled in the literature as a phase polynomial with $O(d^2N^4Q^4)$ rotations per Trotter step. That cost is close to optimal for its gate class: we show that for a generic Trotter step the Walsh support $m_{\min}$ lower-bounds both the rotation count and the two-qubit count of every circuit over $\{CNOT,CZ,CPhase,R_{ZZ},SWAP,X,R_z\}$, ancillas included, and we build a parity network attaining $1.01$--$1.10\,m_{\min}$. Improvement therefore requires a gate outside that class, and the exact factorisation of $V$ into a sum of squared commutator entries supplies one in each era. For NISQ, Hadamard: each squared entry is added into a Fourier register and squared there, $Θ(d^2N^3Q^3)$ two-qubit gates. For fault tolerance, $T$: reversible arithmetic with one phase-gradient injection per squared entry, $Θ(d^2N^3Q^2)$ T gates and no arbitrary-angle rotation. Both are built and verified. With a native $R_{ZZ}$ the NISQ circuit uses fewer two-qubit gates than every parity network from $Q=6$ at SU(2) and $Q=2$ at SU(5); the oracle beats a precision-matched Hamming-weight-phasing incumbent at every point built except the smallest, by up to $11.9\times$ in T count and, at equal width, $25.5\times$ in surface-code spacetime. An ablation with the same arithmetic but no factorisation costs up to $21\times$ more and loses to that incumbent at $15$ of $17$ points, so the factorisation, not the arithmetic, produces the win. Both constructions survive the supersymmetric deformation to mini-BMN with fermions, and complete Trotter evolutions on a statevector simulator reproduce the exact product formula to $2\times10^{-12}$.

</details>


### [118] [Topology-independent quantum advantage in communication networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04734)
*Ankush Pandit,V. N. S. Meghanath Ashtakala,Leon George Padayatty,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"拓扑无关量子优势"概念，指量子协议在相同通信约束下，无论经典通信资源如何在各方间分配、采用何种通信拓扑，均能胜过所有经典协议，即使各方共享无限经典随机性。作者通过三方相等任务展示该现象，建立一般框架并完整刻画最小三方场景下的经典关联多面体，其面不等式揭示了多个拓扑无关量子优势实例，并将框架推广至四方网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信在多种信息处理任务中优于经典通信，但此类优势通常建立在固定通信网络之上。作者希望突破固定网络的限制，研究一种对通信拓扑无依赖、更稳健的量子优势形式。

Method: 1) 提出拓扑无关量子优势的定义，要求量子协议在相同通信约束下胜过所有经典协议，不论经典通信资源分配方式与拓扑结构，即使允许无限经典随机性；2) 通过三方相等任务（两个发送方接收三值经典输入，无输入接收方判断输入是否相等）验证该现象；3) 建立拓扑无关通信的一般框架，完整刻画最小三方场景下的经典关联多面体及其面不等式；4) 将框架扩展至四方网络，刻画相关经典通信结构并识别具有量子优势的通信任务。

Result: 1) 在三方相等任务中证实了拓扑无关量子优势的存在；2) 完整刻画了最小三方场景的经典关联多面体，其面不等式给出多个拓扑无关量子优势实例；3) 其中一个不等式可实现非酉量子信道的半设备无关认证，另一个可在无任何网络配置先验假设下认证顺序量子网络拓扑；4) 在四方网络中识别出对所有可行经典拓扑均具量子优势的通信任务。

Conclusion: 研究结果确立了拓扑无关性作为通信网络中一种真实且稳健的量子优势形式。

Abstract: Quantum communication can outperform classical communication in a variety of information-processing tasks, but such advantages are typically established for a fixed communication network. Here we introduce topology-independent quantum advantage, in which a quantum protocol outperforms every classical protocol subject to the same communication constraint, irrespective of how the classical communication resource is distributed among the parties or which communication topology is employed, even when the parties may share unlimited classical randomness. We first demonstrate this phenomenon through a simple three-party equality task: two senders receive ternary classical inputs, while an input-free receiver determines whether the inputs are equal. We then formulate a general framework for topology-independent communication and completely characterize the corresponding classical correlation polytope in the minimal three-party scenario. Its facet inequalities reveal several instances of topology-independent quantum advantage. Remarkably, one of these inequalities enables the semi-device-independent certification of a nonunitary quantum channel, while another certifies a sequential quantum network topology without any prior assumption about the network configuration. Finally, we extend the framework to four-party networks, characterize the relevant classical communication structures, and identify a communication task exhibiting quantum advantages against all admissible classical topologies. Our results establish topology independence as a genuine and robust form of quantum advantage in communication networks.

</details>


### [119] [Record consistency under repeated readout and apparatus composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04788)
*D. M. Theshan N. Weerasinghe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究"无信号约束仅约束边缘概率、不足以刻画装置在重复读出/网络组合/复用下是否保持联合记录律"这一问题，通过条件记录判据与四个显式模型给出可分辨条件与定量界，并以830万条模拟历史验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的无信号（no-signalling）约束通常只作用于孤立的边缘结果概率，无法判定装置在重复读出、网络组合或复用等扩展场景下是否仍保持其可及记录的联合分布。因此需要找出：在什么额外条件下，边缘无信号才能可靠地推广到更丰富的记录与干预结构。

Method: 提出一个"条件记录判据"作为统一分析工具，并构造四个显式模型：(1) 缠绕柯西（wrapped-Cauchy）回溯因果源；(2) 有限角带宽假设下的定量泄漏界；(3) 特定双源 Bell 结点，分析所需修正矩阵的非负秩；(4) 高斯控制器（含单态加共享经典噪声）。配合精确的噪声—分布改变权衡推导、有限资源障碍的证明及其正有限控制器近似构造，并用830万条独立模拟历史做数值检验。

Result: 对缠绕柯西回溯源：两个条件独立连续读出下的保护强制响应与角度无关；无需连续性时三个读出即足够；有限角带宽给出定量泄漏上界，而仅连续性不提供一致稳定性。在指定双源 Bell 结点，所需修正矩阵在每个偶数均匀角网格上具有满非负秩。高斯控制器可消除分支质量依赖，却留下可区分的联合记录，并给出精确的噪声与定律改变权衡。固定单态加共享经典噪声可复现变宽度的孤立结果族，但保留式与刷新式控制器产生不同的时间统计。同时证明了有限资源障碍，并构造出正有限控制器近似。

Conclusion: 结果表明：把装置扩展到更丰富记录与干预时，边缘无信号必须附加特定条件（如条件独立性、连续性/有限带宽、非负秩结构、控制器类型与有限资源约束等）才具有扩展有效性；这些条件与模型专属预测均得到大规模数值模拟支持。

Abstract: No-signalling constraints on isolated outcome probabilities do not determine whether an apparatus preserves the joint law of its accessible records under repeated readout, network composition, or reuse. We study these extensions through a conditional-record criterion and four explicit models. For a wrapped-Cauchy retrocausal source, protection under two conditionally independent continuous readouts forces an angle-independent response; three readouts suffice without continuity. A finite angular-bandwidth assumption gives a quantitative leakage bound, whereas continuity alone gives no uniform stability. At a specified two-source Bell junction, the required correction matrix has full nonnegative rank on every even uniform angular grid. A Gaussian controller can cancel branch-mass dependence while leaving a distinguishable joint record, for which we derive exact noise and law-change tradeoffs. Finally, a fixed singlet with shared classical noise reproduces the variable-width isolated outcome family, but retained and refreshed controllers generate different temporal statistics. We prove a finite-resource obstruction and construct positive finite-controller approximations. Numerical studies comprising 8.3 million independent simulated histories support the model-specific predictions. Together, the results identify conditions that must accompany marginal no-signalling when extending an apparatus to richer records and interventions.

</details>


### [120] [Competing pathways in polaritonic chemistry and general harmonic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04817)
*Jonas Vinther,Leonardo A. Cunha,Johannes Flick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了通用量子动力学框架，通过张量网络HEOM方法计算振动态间概率流，揭示振动强耦合如何通过改变局域跃迁来重分配多步反应网络的反应通量与分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验表明分子与腔模的强光-物质耦合能显著改变化学反应速率与产率，但已有多种机制无法给出关于振动强耦合如何改变反应动力学的清晰微观理解，尤其缺乏对多步/竞争反应路径的定量分析工具。

Method: 采用通用量子动力学框架分析极化激元化学中的竞争反应路径，考虑具有多个相关跃迁频率的反应坐标与一般谐振子环境；扩展层级运动方程(HEOM)的张量网络表述以计算振动态间概率流，使用有效谱密度的简洁表述并将马尔可夫浴模受控压缩为系统级跃迁算符。

Result: 可对态-态概率流进行分解，揭示腔诱导的单个跃迁变化如何在多步反应网络中传播；发现这些局域修正可改变动力学瓶颈、选择性地在竞争路径间重分配反应通量，从而在不可逆条件下改变分支比，而在可逆区不显著影响平衡产率；对于反应坐标、旁观者模与腔环境耦合的情形，发现源自杂化衰减通道干涉的、结构化的非对称速率分布。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解振动强耦合下路径特异性化学反应性提供了机制性框架。

Abstract: Recent experiments suggest that strong light-matter coupling between molecules and cavity modes can significantly modify chemical reaction rates and yields. Despite multiple proposed mechanisms, a clear microscopic understanding of how vibrational strong coupling alters reaction kinetics remains incomplete. Here, we employ a general quantum-dynamical framework for analyzing competing reaction pathways in polaritonic chemistry by considering reaction coordinates with multiple relevant transition frequencies and general harmonic environments. We extend on a tensor-network formulation of the hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) method to compute probability currents between vibrational states, leveraging a concise formulation for effective spectral densities and a controlled compression of Markovian bath modes into system-level jump operators. This allows us to decompose the resulting state-to-state probability currents, revealing how cavity-induced changes in individual transitions propagate through multistep reaction networks. We find that these local modifications can alter kinetic bottlenecks and selectively redistribute reaction flux between competing pathways, changing branching ratios under irreversible conditions without substantially affecting the equilibrium yield in the reversible regime.
