Finally, for coupled reaction-coordinate, spectator-mode, and cavity environments, we find structured, asymmetric rate profiles arising from interference between hybridized decay channels. Together, these results provide a mechanistic framework for understanding pathway-specific chemical reactivity under vibrational strong coupling.

</details>


### [121] [Sidewall spacer passivated epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions for superconducting qubit applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04820)
*Koki Honda,Daiki Kurihara,Hiroki Kutsuma,Hirotaka Terai,Taro Yamashita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了侧壁间隔层钝化的外延NbN/AlN/NbN约瑟夫森结制备工艺，减少非晶介质体积与工艺复杂度，实现了高质量结与可扩展平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外延NbN/AlN/NbN结因晶体隧道势垒和NbN高转变温度，是替代传统Al/AlOx/Al结的潜力方案，但其集成到量子电路通常需复杂工艺并引入非晶夹层介质，会降低量子比特相干时间。

Method: 采用侧壁间隔层钝化的外延NbN/AlN/NbN结制备工艺，减少非晶介质体积与工艺复杂度；通过改变AlN隧道势垒厚度调控临界电流密度，并用缓冲氢氟酸处理前后对比电流-电压特性评估湿法刻蚀影响。

Result: 制备的结具有约5.6 mV的大能隙电压和低亚能隙漏电流；在0.3至10^4 A/cm^2的宽临界电流密度范围内获得高质量结；缓冲氢氟酸处理前后IV特性对比表明湿法刻蚀未劣化电学特性。

Conclusion: 结果确立了外延约瑟夫森结的可扩展制备工艺，为高相干氮化物基超导量子比特提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: Epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions are promising alternatives to conventional Al/AlO$_x$/Al junctions for superconducting qubits due to their crystalline tunnel barriers and the high transition temperature of NbN. However, their integration into quantum circuits typically requires complex fabrication processes involving amorphous interlayer dielectrics, which can degrade qubit coherence times. To address these issues, we developed sidewall spacer passivated epitaxial NbN/AlN/NbN junctions using a fabrication process that reduces both the volume of amorphous dielectric material and fabrication complexity. The fabricated junctions exhibited a large gap voltage of approximately 5.6 mV and low subgap leakage currents. By varying the thickness of the AlN tunnel barrier, high-quality junctions were fabricated over a wide range of critical current densities ${\it J}_c$, from 0.3 to 10$^4$ A/cm$^2$. In addition, the comparison of the current-voltage characteristics before and after buffered hydrofluoric acid treatment indicated that the wet etching process did not degrade the electrical characteristics of the junctions. These results establish a scalable fabrication process for epitaxial Josephson junctions and provide a promising platform for high-coherence nitride-based superconducting qubits.

</details>


### [122] [Epitaxial NbN-junction-based self-shunted superconducting flux qubit with high anharmonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04821)
*Tokunoshin Uchida,Shingo Nozaki,Daiki Kurihara,Hiroki Kutsuma,Shotaro Shirai,Atsushi Noguchi,Hirotaka Terai,Taro Yamashita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用全外延生长的 NbN/AlN/NbN 约瑟夫森结，实现了一种自分流超导磁通量子比特（SSFQ），兼具超紧凑、强非谐性与长寿命特性，为可扩展超导量子比特提供了新选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电容分流（C-shunt）磁通量子比特依赖大面积并联电容，导致体积大、难以扩展；需要一种超紧凑、强非谐且长寿命的量子比特方案。

Method: 采用全外延生长的 NbN/AlN/NbN 约瑟夫森结构建自分流超导磁通量子比特（SSFQ），不依赖大面积并联电容器，并对其非谐性、占位面积与寿命进行测量评估。

Result: NbN 结 SSFQ 实现了 797 MHz 的高非谐性；相比带大分流电容的 C-shunt 磁通量子比特，占位面积大幅减小；寿命达到 13–25 μs，与 NbN 基 C-shunt 磁通量子比特及 Al 基磁通量子比特（结面积小 10 倍以上）相当或更高。

Conclusion: 大面积外延 NbN 结不仅适用于 SSFQ，也可用于合并元件 transmon；该工作为可扩展量子比特及大规模超导量子计算机的材料平台提供了新选择。

Abstract: We report a demonstration of a self-shunted superconducting flux qubit (SSFQ) as an ultracompact, strongly anharmonic, and long-lifetime qubit by using the full epitaxially grown NbN/AlN/NbN Josephson junctions. The NbN-junction-based SSFQ achieved a high anharmonicity of 797 MHz as well as drastically reduced footprint of the qubit compared to capacitively shunted (C-shunt) flux qubits with a large shunt capacitor. The lifetime of the SSFQ reached 13 - 25 $μ$s, which is comparable to or relatively higher than that of NbN-based C-shunt flux qubits and Al-based flux qubits with more than 10 times smaller junctions. This indicates that the large epitaxial NbN-based junction can be adopted for SSFQ as well as merged-element transmon with the relatively large junctions. The results presented in this work will offer a new choice of the scalable qubits as well as the material platform toward the realization of large-scale superconducting quantum computers.

</details>


### [123] [Entanglement-of-formation potentials for feedback-assisted classical communication and randomness distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04831)
*Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展"形成纠缠势"（entanglement-of-formation potentials），为反馈辅助经典通信与单向随机性蒸馏给出更紧的容量与有限码长上界，并揭示双向随机性蒸馏可严格超越单向速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道的经典反馈容量与单向共享随机性蒸馏容量缺乏统一、可计算的紧界；Winter-Yang 的混合凸包（potential capacity）表达式虽有潜力，但缺少操作化的摊还（amortization）证明与具体信道评估，且单向与双向随机性蒸馏的能力差异尚不清晰。

Method: 为有限维量子信道（无初始共享纠缠）建立精确的摊还定理，将"消息信息 + 剩余纠缠"的增量加以界定；由此导出基于混合凸包表达的经典反馈容量上界，并将态版本用于单向随机性蒸馏的有限码长分析。同时对标志混合信道（无噪 + 纠缠破缺）求容量，对去极化信道改进已知逆定理，对振幅阻尼信道精确求值。此外构造两轮弱测量协议比较单向与双向蒸馏。

Result: 摊还定理证明了基于混合凸包表达的经典反馈容量上界；确定了无噪与纠缠破缺信道标志混合的容量，改进了已发表的去极化信道逆定理，并对振幅阻尼信道精确计算新界。态版本给出单向经典通信下净共享随机性的有限码长上界，振幅阻尼 Choi 态提供精确基准，满秩广义振幅阻尼 Choi 态展示了有效改进。两轮弱测量协议严格超越每个非平凡 qubit 各向同性态的精确单向速率，并在首次解码中保留量子边信息、在反向方向额外提取随机性。

Conclusion: 单向态势并非一般的双向逆定理；保留的量子关联使双向蒸馏能超越单向极限。这些结果建立了以纠缠为基础的统一框架，用于获得更紧的通信与随机性界。

Abstract: We develop entanglement-of-formation potentials for feedback-assisted classical communication and one-way randomness distillation. For a finite-dimensional quantum channel without initial shared entanglement, an exact amortization theorem bounds the increase of message information plus entanglement remaining across the communicating laboratories. This proves a classical-feedback capacity bound using a mixed-convex-roof expression previously studied by Winter and Yang in the context of potential capacities. We determine the capacity of flagged mixtures of noiseless and entanglement-breaking channels, improve a published depolarizing-channel converse, and evaluate the new bound exactly for the amplitude damping channel. A state analogue gives a finite-blocklength upper bound on the net shared randomness obtainable with one-way classical communication. Amplitude-damping Choi states provide exact benchmarks, while a full-rank generalized amplitude-damping Choi state demonstrates a useful improvement where we do not evaluate the operational rate exactly. We also distinguish one-way from two-way randomness distillation: a two-round weak-measurement protocol strictly exceeds the exact one-way rate of every nontrivial qubit isotropic state. The protocol preserves quantum side information during its first decoding and extracts additional randomness in the reverse direction. This shows why the one-way state potential is not a general two-way converse. Together, these results establish a common entanglement-based approach to sharper communication and randomness bounds, while revealing how retained quantum correlations enable two-way distillation to surpass one-way limits.

</details>


### [124] [Quantum codes in the Lee metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04834)
*Jinkang Guo,Yiqiu Han,Aranya Chakraborty,Shubham P. Jain,Tianhao Liu,Victor V. Albert,Andrew Lucas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Lee度量的量子纠错编码框架，用于建模离散小位移噪声，并证明大q系统中Lee距离受O(n)上界限制，同时构造了具自纠正性质的二维量子码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散小位移噪声（如核自旋噪声）近似表现为低权重X/Z型Pauli位移，与连续变量系统中的小相空间位移类似，这种结构由Lee度量刻画，而经典Lee度量编码理论尚缺乏量子对应，因此需要发展量子Lee度量编码理论。

Method: 针对Z_q上的稳定子码（任意q），建立联合与分离Lee度量的形式化框架；对量子比特，联合度量将Y错误计为两次；研究Clifford门下的Lee权重扩散，并通过Gray映射将Z_4上的码量子比特化；分析量子CSS Lee-LDPC码的Lee距离；在局域Metropolis动力学下研究经典q元螺旋重复码及其超图积构造的二维量子码。

Result: 1）对量子比特，联合Lee度量码可用比Hamming度量码更少的物理量子比特检测并纠正X和Z错误；2）通过Gray映射得到具两重横向非量子比特Clifford门的码；3）证明n个qudit上的量子CSS Lee-LDPC码Lee距离不超过O(n)，且对q一致成立，表明大q内部Hilbert空间无法用于构造高Lee距离码；4）经典q元螺旋重复码在固定温度下具有指数长记忆时间（q足够大且随系统长度增长），可理解为有限温度下局域一维模型中的自发对称破缺；5）其超图积给出具Z错误自纠正的局域二维量子码。

Conclusion: 该框架将经典Lee度量编码理论推广至量子情形，揭示了大规模qudit系统在Lee距离上的根本限制，并借助螺旋重复码的超图积为构造具自纠正性质的局域二维量子码提供了新途径，其中X错误的自纠正仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum coding framework for discrete small-shift noise, in which errors on qudits are modeled as low-weight $X$- and $Z$-type Pauli shifts, analogous to small phase-space displacements in continuous-variable systems. This structure is approximately respected by nuclear-spin noise and captured by the Lee metric, motivating a quantum extension of classical Lee-metric coding theory. We develop such a formalism for stabilizer codes over $\mathbb{Z}_q$ for arbitrary $q$, using joint and separate Lee metrics for the $X$- and $Z$-components of errors. For qubits, the joint metric counts $Y$ errors twice and can yield codes that detect and correct $X$ and $Z$ errors with fewer physical qubits than codes designed for the conventional Hamming metric. We discuss Lee-weight spreading under Clifford gates and qubitize codes over $\mathbb{Z}_4$ via the Gray map, finding codes with two-fold transversal non-qubit-Clifford gates. For quantum CSS Lee-LDPC codes on $n$ qudits, we prove that the Lee distance cannot exceed $O(n)$, uniformly in $q$, demonstrating an unexpected obstruction to using the large internal Hilbert space of a large-$q$ qudit to make high-Lee-distance codes. Under local Metropolis dynamics, certain classical $q$-ary ``helical repetition codes'' have exponentially long memory times at fixed temperature for sufficiently large $q$ (that grows with system length), which can be understood as spontaneous symmetry breaking at finite temperature, even in local one-dimensional models. Hypergraph products of these helical repetition codes provide local two-dimensional quantum codes that inherit self-correction for $Z$ errors, but self-correction for $X$ errors remains an open question.

</details>


### [125] [Long-lived Coherent Phonons Reveal Competing Thermal and Many-Body Dynamics in $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04863)
*Xuanchao Zhang,Dehao Yuan,Junhua Zhou,Vandana Tiwari,Fulu Zheng,Ajay Jha,Hong-Guang Duan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用约5飞秒宽带瞬态光栅光谱研究铁电α-In₂Se₃中光激发后量子相干性的演化，发现约104 cm⁻¹主导声子模式在10–300 K频率几乎不变但相干性被抑制，且随激发能量增加出现从相干晶格运动向非相干多体态的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光激发后量子相干性的命运是非平衡凝聚态物理的核心问题，尤其在电子、结构及多体能量尺度强烈耦合的低维固体中。α-In₂Se₃作为极性范德瓦尔斯半导体是研究该耦合的理想平台，但其相干晶格动力学与载流子密度、热涨落之间的独立关系尚不清楚。

Method: 采用约5 fs激光脉冲的宽带瞬态光栅光谱技术，在10–300 K温度范围及不同激发能量下测量α-In₂Se₃的相干响应；结合第一性原理计算指认声子模式，并用量子动力学模拟再现瞬态光栅振荡。

Result: 光激发触发以约104 cm⁻¹模式为主导的显著振荡，可持续数皮秒。10–300 K变温测量显示该模式频率几乎不变，而相干性逐渐被抑制，从而将晶格坐标与热激活退相干区分开。改变激发能量揭示出明显交叉：增强光激发逐步修饰并阻尼相干响应。第一性原理将主导振荡指认为101.47 cm⁻¹的Γ点光学声子，涉及In-Se集体位移；量子动力学模拟再现了主要的瞬态光栅振荡。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了α-In₂Se₃作为可独立探测晶格相干性、热涨落与载流子密度依赖相互作用的模型体系，揭示了极性范德瓦尔斯半导体在超快激发后如何从相干晶格运动演化为非相干多体态。

Abstract: The fate of quantum coherence following photoexcitation is a central problem in nonequilibrium condensed-matter physics, especially in low-dimensional solids where electronic, structural and many-body energy scales are strongly coupled. Here, we investigate this interplay in ferroelectric $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ using broadband transient-grating spectroscopy with $\sim$5 fs laser pulses. Photoexcitation launches pronounced oscillations dominated by a $\sim$104 cm$^{-1}$ mode that persists for several picoseconds. Measurements from 10 to 300 K show that its frequency remains nearly unchanged while its coherence is progressively suppressed, distinguishing the lattice coordinate from thermally activated dephasing. Varying excitation energy reveals a distinct crossover in which increasing photoexcitation progressively modifies and damps the coherent response. First-principles calculations assign the dominant oscillation to a 101.47 cm$^{-1}$ $Γ$-point optical phonon involving collective In-Se displacement, while quantum-dynamical simulations reproduce the main transient-grating oscillations. These results establish $α$-In$_{2}$Se$_{3}$ as a model system for independently probing lattice coherence, thermal fluctuations and carrier-density-dependent interactions, revealing how a polar van der Waals semiconductor evolves from coherent lattice motion toward an incoherent many-body state after ultrafast excitation.

</details>


### [126] [Custom Penalty QAOA via Walsh-Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04908)
*Xin Wei LEE,Siong Thye GOH,Hoong Chuin LAU*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出WCP-QAOA框架，用Walsh-Hadamard变换将任意作用于线性约束的罚函数精确展开为Pauli-Z串的加权和，避免松弛变量与量子比特开销，并给出系数求解与截断条件及6量子比特背包问题验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化中的不等式约束通常需引入松弛变量与二次罚项，这会增加量子比特数并扭曲优化景观；而无松弛的定制非线性罚函数难以在QAOA中实现。因此需要一种能直接在量子线路中实现任意罚函数、且不增加量子比特的方法。

Method: 证明Walsh-Hadamard变换（WHT）可将作用于线性约束的罚函数精确表示为Pauli-Z串的加权和，据此提出Walsh定制罚函数QAOA（WCP-QAOA）；在Walsh度k处截断，得到O(n^k)个Pauli项且不改变量子比特数。该截断是罚函数在L²意义下的最优k次逼近，并给出被舍弃Walsh系数满足的条件，以保证截断Hamiltonian仍将每个可行解排在每个不可行解之前。为高效获取系数，提出可在亚指数时间返回任意系数的算法，使固定k下k度Hamiltonian可在n的多项式时间内获得；针对指数罚函数给出WHT的闭合乘积形式及系数闭合表达式，并识别缩短精确谱的结构性捷径。

Result: WHT消除障碍：任意罚函数对线性约束可用Pauli-Z串加权和精确表示；度k截断成本O(n^k)项、量子比特数不变；截断为L²最优k次逼近，且在给定系数条件下保持可行/不可行解排序；任意Walsh系数可在亚指数时间求出，固定k下Hamiltonian构造为n的多项式时间；指数罚函数具有闭合乘积形式与系数闭合表达式；在6量子比特背包问题上测试了WCP-QAOA。

Conclusion: WCP-QAOA通过WHT将无松弛非线性罚函数可行地嵌入QAOA，兼顾量子比特效率与近似最优性，并在小规模背包问题上得到验证，为约束组合优化的量子求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: Inequality constraints in combinatorial optimization are conventionally handled by introducing slack variables and quadratic penalties, which increase the qubit count and distort the optimization landscape. Slack-free formulations instead apply a nonlinear custom penalty function directly to the constraint, which are difficult to be implemented in quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA). We show that the Walsh--Hadamard transform (WHT) removes this obstruction for any penalty function: a penalty applied to a linear constraint is exactly a weighted sum of Pauli-$Z$ strings. We propose this framework as the Walsh custom penalty QAOA (WCP-QAOA). Truncating the expansion at Walsh degree $k$ costs $\mathcal{O}(n^k)$ Pauli terms and leaves the qubit count untouched. The truncation is the optimal degree-$k$ approximation of the penalty in $L^2$, and we give a condition on the discarded Walsh coefficients under which the truncated Hamiltonian still ranks every feasible solution below every infeasible one. Since evaluating a Walsh coefficient from its definition requires all $2^n$ penalty values, we give an algorithm that returns any coefficient in sub-exponential time, so that the degree-$k$ Hamiltonian is obtained in time polynomial in $n$ for fixed $k$. We show that for the exponential penalty, there exists a closed product form of WHT, and hence a closed form expression to calculate the Walsh coefficients. We identify structural shortcuts that shorten the exact spectrum. We also tested WCP-QAOA in solving a 6-qubit knapsack problem.

</details>


### [127] [Pairwise detection, chiral conversion: Resource theory of quantum multistates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04930)
*Yuan-Hong Tao,Yu-Xin Wu,Bing Xie,Lin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子多态在保交换操作（CPO）下的精确资源转换理论：对量子比特，两两Bargmann不变量决定候选信道的奇异值，单个三阶Bargmann相位决定其取向，完全正性给出精确转换的充要判据；对d≥3，结构刚性，转换由各向同性退极化或转置退极化信道实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多态相干性由各组成态是否对易决定，量子比特情形只需两两态交叠即可探测。然而，支配其确定性（精确）转换的规律此前尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在共同的保交换操作（CPO）框架下研究量子多态的资源转换。对量子比特，利用两两Bargmann不变量确定唯一候选信道的奇异值，用单个三阶Bargmann相位确定信道取向，再通过完全正性条件导出精确转换的充要判据。对d≥3，分析其刚性结构，并借助中心化Bargmann不变量判定可转换性。

Result: 量子比特情形下，判据刻画出手性敏感的壳层C_chir，其中具有相同两两几何的目标可仅因三阶不变量的符号不同而转换性不同，由此得到对所有n≥3量子比特多态成立的分阶关系k_detect=2<3=k_convert。该手性敏感壳层恰占奇异值单纯形的三分之一，而镜像反射在体积上阻碍了三分之二的容许保取向变换。对d≥3，每个有资源的转换均由各向同性退极化或转置退极化信道实现，可转换性由阶数至多d²的中心化Bargmann不变量决定。

Conclusion: CPO下量子多态的转换理论对量子比特与d≥3呈现截然不同的图景：量子比特是可探测阶数（2）与可转换阶数（3）分离的手性敏感结构，而高维则表现为刚性、由两类退极化信道支配的结构。该理论以Bargmann不变量为核心，统一刻画了精确转换的充要条件。

Abstract: Basis-independent coherence of a quantum multistate is fixed by whether its constituent states commute, and for qubits it is detected by pairwise state overlaps alone. What governs its deterministic transformation has been open. We give the exact resource-conversion theory of quantum multistates under common commutativity-preserving operations (CPOs). For qubits, pairwise Bargmann invariants determine the singular values of the unique candidate channel, while a single third-order Bargmann phase determines its orientation. Complete positivity then yields a necessary and sufficient criterion for exact conversion. The criterion identifies a chirality-sensitive shell $\mathcal C_{\rm chir}$, within which targets with identical pairwise geometry can differ in convertibility solely by the sign of their cubic invariant. This yields an order separation, $k_{\rm detect}=2<3=k_{\rm convert}$, for qubit multistates of every size $n\geqslant3$. The chirality-sensitive shell occupies exactly one third of the singular-value simplex, while mirror reflection obstructs two thirds of the admissible orientation-preserving transformations by volume. For $d\geqslant3$, by contrast, the structure is rigid: every resourceful conversion is realized by either an isotropic depolarizing or a transpose-depolarizing channel. Convertibility is then decided by centered Bargmann invariants of degree at most $d^2$.

</details>


### [128] [Gap Amplification for Local Hamiltonians with Combinatorial Soundness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04952)
*Mitali Bafna,Quynh T. Nguyen,Tina Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个可行的量子保局部性间隙放大模板，并证明其能放大局部哈密顿量的组合间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子PCP猜想是量子复杂性理论的核心开放问题。经典PCP定理证明中使用的许多工具（如保局部性间隙放大和字母表约化）因量子不可克隆性而缺乏明显的量子对应物，其中保局部性间隙放大尤为关键——它要求在不增加哈密顿量局部性的前提下放大承诺间隙，仅适度增加局部qudit维度。获得这种对局部性的控制对许多经典PCP证明策略至关重要。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于容错计算的新框架来推理量子间隙放大，并借鉴了Bafna--Minzer--Vyas基于高维扩展子的经典PCP、Anshu--Breuckmann--Nguyen的电路到哈密顿量构造，以及量子编码理论的若干近期进展，提出了保局部性间隙放大的模板。

Result: 证明了该过程能够放大局部哈密顿量的组合间隙（combinatorial gap）。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子间隙放大引入了基于容错计算的新框架，揭示了将经典PCP核心要素引入量子情形的路径，并为量子PCP猜想的研究提供了可行方向。

Abstract: The quantum PCP conjecture is one of the major open problems in quantum complexity theory. It has resisted attack in part because many primitives used in the proof of the classical PCP theorem, such as locality-preserving gap amplification and alphabet reduction, have no obvious quantum analogues due to quantum no-cloning. Locality-preserving gap amplification is a procedure that takes as input a local Hamiltonian problem instance and produces a new instance with a larger promise gap, without increasing the locality of the Hamiltonian, and instead moderately increasing its local qudit dimension. Obtaining this kind of control over the locality during gap amplification is critical to the success of many known strategies for proving the classical PCP theorem. In this work, we put forth the first known viable template for quantum locality-preserving gap amplification, and we prove that our procedure amplifies the combinatorial gap of local Hamiltonians. Our work introduces a new framework for reasoning about quantum gap amplification in terms of fault-tolerant computation, and illuminates a route toward importing one of the central ingredients in classical PCPs into the quantum setting. In particular, we build upon ideas from the recent classical PCP of Bafna--Minzer--Vyas based on high-dimensional expanders, and the circuit-to-Hamiltonian construction of Anshu--Breuckmann--Nguyen, in addition to several recent advances in quantum coding theory.

</details>


### [129] [Improved Upper and Lower Bounds for Quantum Convex-Body Volume Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04987)
*Ruizhe Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对高维凸体体积估计问题，在高斯量子成员预言机模型下提出查询复杂度为 $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$ 的量子算法，改进了已有量子上界并扩大了相对经典算法的量子加速，同时给出常数相对误差下 $\Omega(d)$ 的量子查询下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维凸体体积估计是理论计算机科学的基础问题。此前量子上界为 $\widetilde O(d^3+d^{9/4}/\varepsilon)$，而经典最优上界为 $\widetilde O(d^{7/2}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$，量子加速幅度有限；同时已有量子下界仅为 $\Omega(\sqrt d)$，与上界之间存在较大间隙，因此需要更优的量子算法与更强的下界。

Method: 算法融合两个新要素：(1) 基于均匀经典热启动混合界，对量子游走进行输入相关的有效谱隙分析；(2) 在模拟退火框架下，基于经典桥式采样，用单个量子估计器估计归一化比率乘积。下界方面证明常数相对误差下的 $\Omega(d)$ 量子查询下界。

Result: 在给定凸体 $K\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ 的量子成员预言机下，以相对误差 $\varepsilon$ 估计 $\mathrm{vol}(K)$ 需 $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$ 次查询；改进了 Chakrabarti 等（ACM TQC 2023）与 Cornelissen--Hamoudi（SODA 2023）的量子上界，并相对 Jia 等（JACM 2026）经典上界获得更大加速。常数相对误差下的量子查询下界提升至 $\Omega(d)$，优于此前的 $\Omega(\sqrt d)$。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了量子凸体体积估计的复杂度刻画：上界改进至 $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$，下界提升至 $\Omega(d)$，缩小了量子复杂度上下界之间的差距，并增强了相对经典算法的量子优势。

Abstract: Estimating the volume of a high-dimensional convex body is a fundamental problem in theoretical computer science. Given a quantum membership oracle for a convex body $K\subset\mathbb{R}^d$, we show that estimating $\mathrm{vol}(K)$ to relative error $\varepsilon$ takes $\widetilde O(d^{5/2}+d^{3/2}/\varepsilon)$ queries. This improves the previous quantum upper bound $\widetilde O(d^3+d^{9/4}/\varepsilon)$ of Chakrabarti et al. (ACM TQC 2023) and Cornelissen--Hamoudi (SODA 2023), and achieves a larger quantum speedup over the currently best classical upper bound $\widetilde O(d^{7/2}+d^3/\varepsilon^2)$ of Jia et al. (JACM 2026). Our quantum algorithm combines two new ingredients: an input-dependent effective spectral-gap analysis of quantum walks based on uniform classical warm-start mixing bounds, and a single quantum estimator for products of normalizer ratios in simulated annealing based on classical bridge sampling. We also prove an $Ω(d)$ quantum query lower bound for constant relative error, improving the previous $Ω(\sqrt d)$ lower bound.

</details>


### [130] [Blind Remote Implementation of Arbitrary Two-Qubit Unitary Operation via Pre-Shared Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04995)
*Plaban Saha,Binayak S. Choudhury*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预共享纠缠的任意两量子比特幺正操作远程实现协议：Bob持有未知两量子比特态且不离实验室，Alice仅用标定黑盒设备操作，双方均无需操作的经典描述。协议以单量子比特Euler分解与两量子比特Cartan分解为基础，构建单量子比特操作、$R_{zz}(\theta)$及其经局域幺正变换推广至$R_{xx}(\theta)$、$R_{yy}(\theta)$的基本协议，再组合实现任意两量子比特幺正操作，由第三方Charlie确定分解并提供黑盒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现远程量子操作时，通常需要操作参数的经典描述或对未知量子态的掌握；本文旨在让Bob的未知态始终留在其实验室，且Alice无需知晓操作参数，从而提升隐私性与安全性。

Method: 利用预共享纠缠；基于单量子比特幺正的Euler分解$U=R_z(\gamma)R_x(\beta)R_z(\alpha)$与两量子比特幺正的Cartan分解。先用预共享Bell态构建单量子比特远程实现协议；再用单个预共享Bell态实现$R_{zz}(\theta)$，并通过局域幺正变换扩展到$R_{xx}(\theta)$、$R_{yy}(\theta)$；最后组合这些基本协议实现任意两量子比特幺正操作。第三方Charlie在协议开始前确定所需分解并向Alice提供相应黑盒设备。

Result: 构建了单量子比特操作、$R_{zz}(\theta)$、$R_{xx}(\theta)$、$R_{yy}(\theta)$等基本远程实现协议，并可组合用于任意两量子比特幺正操作的远程实现。

Conclusion: 该协议可在Alice无需知道操作参数、Bob未知态不出实验室、双方均不需幺正操作经典描述的条件下实现任意两量子比特幺正操作的远程执行。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for the remote implementation of an arbitrary two-qubit unitary operation using pre-shared entanglement. In our protocol, Bob possesses an unknown two-qubit state, while Alice implements the required operations using calibrated black-box devices. Alice does not need to know the parameters of these operations, and Bob's unknown state never leaves his laboratory. The protocol is based on the Euler decomposition of single-qubit unitary operations and the Cartan decomposition of two-qubit unitary operations. First, we construct protocols for the remote implementation of the single-qubit operation $U = R_z(γ)R_x(β)R_z(α)$ using pre-shared Bell states. We then construct a protocol for the two-qubit operation $R_{zz}(θ)$ using a single pre-shared Bell state and extend it to $R_{xx}(θ)$ and $R_{yy}(θ)$ through local unitary transformations. These elementary protocols are finally combined to implement an arbitrary two-qubit unitary operation. A third party, Charlie, determines the required decomposition and provides Alice with the corresponding black-box devices before the protocol begins. Thus, neither Alice nor Bob requires the classical description of the unitary operation.

</details>


### [131] [The power of time reversal in Hamiltonian property testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04996)
*Richard R. Allen,Matthias C. Caro,Yanlin Chen,Tom Gur,Angus Lowe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了时间反演是哈密顿量性质测试中达到Heisenberg极限精度的必要条件，建立了连续时间量子下界的新Fourier分析方法，并揭示了时间反演作为量子测量精度与算法鲁棒性的核心资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子算法在传感与学习中可达Heisenberg极限（总演化时间O(1/ε)），但在哈密顿量性质测试中，除非能反转哈密顿量下的时间流，现有最佳算法仅达O(1/ε²)。本文旨在证明时间反演对弥合这一精度差距是必要的。

Method: 采用连续时间量子下界的新型Fourier分析方法，该方法对时间反演敏感；针对哈密顿量认证与局域性测试，在从平均到最坏情况的连续误差度量族上建立演化时间下界，并给出匹配的上界。

Result: 证明了哈密顿量认证与局域性测试的演化时间下界；对哈密顿量认证在所有情形（含与不含时间反演）给出匹配上界，表明时间反演的优势持续存在；给出振幅放大与估计需要逆操作的新证明，改进并扩展了Tang和Wright的下界至分数次查询；对无结构搜索，相位不确定性在无逆查询时破坏量子加速，即使预言机固定且相干。

Conclusion: 时间反演被严格确立为支配量子测量精度与量子算法鲁棒性的资源。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms enable Heisenberg-limited scaling across many tasks in sensing and learning, achieving precision $\varepsilon$ using $O(1/\varepsilon)$ total evolution time. However, in Hamiltonian property testing, the best known algorithms have $O(1/\varepsilon^2)$ total evolution time, unless it is possible to $\textit{reverse}$ the flow of time under the Hamiltonian. In this work, we prove that time reversal is necessary to close this gap. We establish evolution time lower bounds for Hamiltonian certification and locality testing, for a continuous family of error metrics ranging from average- to worst-case. We also prove matching upper bounds for Hamiltonian certification in all cases, both with and without time reversal, showing that the advantage from time reversal persists. Our results follow from a new Fourier-analytic approach to quantum lower bounds in continuous time, which is sensitive to time reversal and applicable beyond Hamiltonian property testing. This gives an alternative proof that inverses are required for amplitude amplification and estimation, improving the lower bounds of Tang and Wright and extending to fractional queries. We also show that, for unstructured search, uncertainty in the phase of the oracle destroys the quantum speedup without inverse queries, even when the oracle is fixed and coherent. Our work rigorously establishes time reversal as a resource governing both the precision of quantum measurement and the robustness of quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [132] [Arbitrary-phase CZ gate and remote readout across spatially separated superconducting nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05005)
*Anatoly Kulikov,Josua D. Schär,Alonso Hernández-Antón,Aleksandr Grigorev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在由两个稀释制冷机通过30米低温量子链路连接的超导电路QED双节点网络上实现了跨节点的纠缠门（任意相位CZ门），并利用该操作完成节点A对节点B量子比特的单发读出，无需节点间精确同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算与通信网络需要在空间分离的量子节点间建立完整的门操作集合。一种可行方案是以超导器件作为可存储与处理量子信息的通用量子节点，以飞行微波光子作为通信载体，因此需要实验验证跨节点的纠缠操作。

Method: 构建由两台稀释制冷机（A、B）经30米低温量子链路连接的原始双节点超导电路QED网络；执行双向光子传输协议：A端通信量子比特发射的微波光子在与B端量子比特相互作用后携带依赖于B态的条件相位；通过改变通信光子频率调节光子从远端节点反射时获得的条件相位，从而实现任意相位CZ门；并用该纠缠操作实现A对B的单发读出。

Result: 成功实现跨两个空间分离超导节点的纠缠门，并通过调节通信光子频率实现任意相位CZ门；利用该纠缠操作完成了由节点A对节点B量子比特的单发读出；验证了该方案无需主动节点与远端节点的精确同步，远端读出可在远端节点完全不主动参与的情况下完成。

Conclusion: 该工作为超导电路的量子通信工具箱增添了跨节点纠缠门与远程读出等协议，提供了一种实现基本分布式量子计算操作的途径。

Abstract: Establishing a complete set of gates across spatially separated quantum nodes is fundamental for distributed quantum computation and communication networks. One of the approaches to do so consists of employing a superconducting device as a universal quantum node that is capable of storing and processing quantum information, and traveling microwave photons as means of communication. In this work, we realize an entangling gate across a primitive two-node superconducting circuit QED network consisting of two dilution cryostats, A and B, connected via a 30-m-long cryogenic quantum link. We perform a bidirectional photon transfer protocol, where a microwave photon emitted by the communication qubit at A acquires a conditional phase based on the state of qubit B. We vary the communication photon frequency, hence tuning the conditional phase acquired during the reflection off the remote node, effectively implementing an arbitrary-phase CZ gate. We use this entangling operation to perform single-shot readout of the qubit in node B from node A. An interesting feature of our scheme is that it does not require precise synchronization of the active node with the remote node; in particular, remote readout can be performed without any active participation by the remote node. These protocols add to the quantum communication toolbox with superconducting circuits and provide a way of realizing basic distributed quantum computing operations.

</details>


### [133] [Process tensor simulations of non-Markovian open quantum systems with large uncoupled subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05035)
*Thomas K. Bracht,Moritz Cygorek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出在仅有部分系统态与环境耦合时高效使用过程张量（PT-MPO）方法，并结合泰勒展开将传播数值复杂度从 O(d^6) 降至 O(d^3)，从而可模拟数百个态的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PT-MPO 是计算开放量子系统时间演化与多时关联的有力工具，但后续系统传播的数值代价通常为 O(d^6)，使系统维度超过几十时方法变得不可承受，亟需降低复杂度以支持更大系统。

Method: 利用仅部分系统态与环境耦合的结构特点，减少有效耦合维度；并将 PT-MPO 方法与泰勒展开方法相结合，以压缩传播计算的复杂度。

Result: 将数值复杂度从 O(d^6) 降低至 O(d^3)，实现了对包含数百个态的系统的高效模拟。

Conclusion: 该方法对多模固态腔量子电动力学系统以及通过耦合传感器系统计算频率滤波关联函数等场景尤为适用。

Abstract: Process tensors in matrix product operator representation (PT-MPOs) are a powerful tool to calculate the temporal evolution and multi-time correlations of open quantum systems. While much progress has been made in making the calculation of process tensors for larger system dimensions possible, the numerical cost of the subsequent propagation of the system typically scales as $\mathcal{O}(d^6)$, which renders PT-MPO methods prohibitively expensive for system dimensions above several dozens. Here, we show how PT-MPO methods can be used efficiently when only some of the system states couple to the environment, and how combination with a Taylor expansion method allows for a reduction of the numerical complexity down to $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$. We show that these methods enable efficient simulations of systems with several hundreds of states. This is particularly relevant for solid-state cavity-QED systems with multiple modes, or when calculating frequency-filtered correlation functions by coupling sensor systems to the system of interest.

</details>


### [134] [Onset of a quantum Fisher-Selke sequence in the transverse-field ANNNI model and its bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05057)
*Tuan-Vu Truong,Vu-Quoc-Minh Nguyen,Hoang-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Tien Nguyen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用微扰论和量子蒙特卡罗方法研究横场ANNNI模型，发现量子涨落从经典多相点选出⟨3⟩相，并依次打开⟨23⟩、⟨223⟩、⟨2223⟩等中间相，双层的存在进一步压制这些相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典ANNNI模型在程-point κ=1/2 处存在大量简并基态，量子涨落如何选出特定调制结构（如devil's staircase中间相）是核心问题；同时需理解额外维度（双层）对相图的影响。

Method: 在平方晶格与双层上研究横场ANNNI模型，于经典多相点κ=J₂/J₀=1/2 处，因壁刚性可对每次构型单独做横场g=Γ/J₀的微扰展开至g⁸阶；用随机级数展开量子蒙特卡罗配合热力学积分比较竞争有序态的 Free 能，以规避退火在相变处冻结调制的问题。

Result: 量子涨落在g²阶选出⟨3⟩相（壁旁自旋从虚翻转获得最多能量）；⟨23⟩、⟨223⟩、⟨2223⟩分别在g⁴、g⁶、g⁸阶于⟨3⟩与反相⟨2⟩之间打开，顺序与经典三维模型的Fisher-Selke序列一致。第二层对每个后续相的压制比局部场增强更强。QMC将⟨3⟩楔形区复现到约1%，预注册检验中⟨23⟩在7点中的6点稳定，第7点由事后运行分辨。

Conclusion: 量子涨落按Fisher-Selke序列逐级选出调制相，双层结构抑制中间相稳定性；微扰论与QMC结果一致，支持该序列在量子模型中的有效性。

Abstract: We study the transverse-field axial next-nearest-neighbor Ising (ANNNI) model on the square lattice and on a bilayer. At the classical multiphase point $κ=J_2/J_0=1/2$, every configuration of straight domain walls that separates domains of at least two sites is a ground state. The walls are rigid, so perturbation theory in the transverse field $g=Γ/J_0$ can be carried out for each configuration separately, and we take it to order $g^8$. Quantum fluctuations select $\langle 3\rangle$ ($\uparrow\uparrow\uparrow\downarrow\downarrow\downarrow$) at order $g^2$, because spins next to a wall gain the most energy from virtual flips. The structures $\langle 23\rangle$, $\langle 223\rangle$ and $\langle 2223\rangle$ then open between $\langle 3\rangle$ and the antiphase $\langle 2\rangle$ at orders $g^4$, $g^6$ and $g^8$, in the order of the Fisher-Selke sequence of the classical three-dimensional model. A second layer suppresses each successive phase more strongly, beyond the stiffening of the local fields. We test the two widest phases with stochastic series expansion quantum Monte Carlo, comparing the free energies of the competing ordered states by thermodynamic integration, since annealing freezes in the modulation that forms at the ordering transition. The simulations reproduce the $\langle 3\rangle$ wedge to about 1%, and in a pre-registered test $\langle 23\rangle$ is stable at six of seven points, with the seventh resolved by post-hoc runs.

</details>


### [135] [Phase-controlled recoverable entanglement in accelerated detector registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05079)
*Zhiming Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究四量子比特簇态寄存器中两个探测比特与标量场耦合时的可恢复纠缠，给出最优局域制备相位、解码方案及加速探测器的纠缠衰减与最优时间解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在集体噪声与相对加速（Unruh效应）环境下，如何通过局域制备相位保护并恢复寄存器-参考系统间的纠缠，并刻画探测器间距、加速与热浴对应关系对纠缠寿命的影响。

Method: 以四量子比特簇态为寄存器，两探测比特耦合标量场；分析局域制备相位对初始纠缠与探测比特边缘态的影响；在保持单态与三重态扇区的信道下用固定解码器恢复Bell对；用有限间距加速探测器的精确迹范数公式，在长期弱耦合（secular近似）动力学下分析衰减；将局域响应匹配到惯性热浴；并讨论空间涂抹与有序极限（先弱耦合后小间距）。

Result: 存在最优制备相位，使固定解码器对任何保持单态与三重态扇区的信道均可恢复Bell对；在secular弱耦合加速情形下该相位在每个时刻均最优；小间距导致缓慢衰减，推导出衰减速率与相位依赖振幅；通过匹配惯性热浴得到纠缠对比度的最优时间解析式；测量与条件相位修正可恢复两比特隐形传态资源。

Conclusion: 局域制备相位可统一优化集体噪声与相对加速下的纠缠恢复；纠缠寿命预测在考虑空间涂抹及有序弱耦合、小间距极限后具有明确的微观适用范围。

Abstract: We study recoverable entanglement in a four-qubit cluster-state register whose two detector qubits couple to a scalar field. A local preparation phase leaves the initial reference--register entanglement and detector marginal unchanged, but controls protection under collective noise. At the optimal phase, a fixed decoder recovers a Bell pair after any channel preserving the singlet and triplet sectors. For uniformly accelerated detectors at finite separation, an exact trace-norm formula shows that the same phase is optimal at every time within the secular weak-coupling dynamics. Small separation produces a slow decay, whose rate and phase-dependent amplitude we derive. Matching the local response to an inertial thermal bath gives an analytic optimum time for the entanglement contrast. A detector measurement and conditional phase correction recover a two-qubit teleportation resource. Spatial smearing and an ordered weak-coupling then small-separation limit specify the microscopic scope of the lifetime prediction.

</details>


### [136] [The quantum-classical bipartite universe and the logical emergence of $\hbar$ from classical temporality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05083)
*Vincenzo Chilla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于冯·诺依曼形式体系，提出经典观测环境与量子被观测系统之间的结构逻辑二分框架，证明理论自洽性要求二者服从不同的命题逻辑（环境为布尔逻辑，被观测系统为非布尔逻辑），并在此框架内推导出普朗克常数ℏ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对普遍主义框架（认为单一逻辑可统一描述量子与经典领域）的不足，作者试图从逻辑结构层面重新诠释量子动力学，解决测量问题，并替代需要隐变量和超决定论假设的解释方案。

Method: 采用标准冯·诺依曼形式体系，设定经典观测环境与被观测量子系统的逻辑二分；将理想经典时钟建模为能量谱均匀离散的开放系统，其动力学由GKSL主方程描述，并施加哈密顿量保持可观测的约束；进而由经典时钟的作用-角量对易关系导出ℏ。

Result: 时钟虽由与测量装置的耗散耦合驱动，但存在稳定的确定性解，对应信息守恒的循环动力学；该确定性机制作为逻辑吸引子出现，由测量相互作用固有的布尔结构稳定。ℏ被推导为基本标度参数。该框架与't Hooft元胞自动机形式体系建立精确映射。

Conclusion: 量子动力学（作为潜在的重语境化）与经典动力学（作为时间现实化）的区分，为测量问题提供了新的逻辑进路；该本体论解释无需隐变量与超决定论假设。

Abstract: We present a foundational reinterpretation of quantum dynamics based on the standard von Neumann formalism, positing a structural logical bipartition between a classical observational environment and a quantum observed system. Against universalist frameworks, we show that theoretical consistency requires distinct propositional logics: Boolean logic governs the environment, while non-Boolean logic applies to the observed system. Within this framework, an ideal classical clock is represented as an open system whose energy spectrum is uniformly discretized. The dynamics follow from the Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad (GKSL) master equation under the constraint that the Hamiltonian remains observable. Although the clock's temporal evolution is driven by dissipative coupling to the measurement apparatus, it admits a stable, deterministic solution corresponding to information-preserving cyclic dynamics. This deterministic regime emerges as a logical attractor, stabilized by the Boolean structure inherent to the measurement interaction. Ultimately, $\hbar$ is derived as a fundamental scaling parameter from the classical clock's action-angle commutation relation, revealing that the quantum of action reflects the discrete logical structure underlying classical temporality. Furthermore, this framework establishes an exact mapping with 't Hooft's cellular automaton formalism, offering an alternative ontological interpretation that not require hidden variables and superdeterministic assumptions. The resulting distinction between quantum dynamics (as potential recontextualization) and classical dynamics (as temporal actualization) provides a novel logical approach to the measurement problem.

</details>


### [137] [JAQSI: Just Another Quantum Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05086)
*Melvin Strobl,Maja Franz,Lukas Scheller,Eileen Kuehn,Wolfgang Mauerer,Achim Streit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: JAQSI 是一个基于 JAX 的可微分量子模拟器，统一门级与脉冲级仿真，在多项基准中性能优于同类工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典仿真通过全态检查、可控噪声模型与反向传播支撑量子算法的开发与验证，但现有框架多只提供门级执行、可微脉冲级动力学或优化的硬件执行，缺乏统一方案。

Method: 构建于 JAX 之上的可微分电路模型中融合门级与脉冲级仿真：电路表示为数值输入的纯函数，支持向量化批次与 JIT 编译；单个电路内可自由组合矩阵定义操作、由哈密顿量时间演化合成的门与噪声信道；采用门与信道融合减少状态遍历，使用伴随微分以常数个态矢量计算期望值梯度；脉冲级上将单项哈密顿量下的演化约化为标量脉冲面积积分，并复用缓存的执行计划与已编译求解器。

Result: 与 PennyLane、Qiskit、Qibo、Qulacs、QuTiP 及 dynamiqs 对比：门级在 12 量子比特以内，态矢量、密度矩阵、噪声与梯度仿真性能持平或快一个数量级；超过 18 量子比特时，态矢量与梯度仿真与 PennyLane Lightning 持平；在态矢量与含噪声密度矩阵脉冲基准中至少快 5 倍，即使禁用闭式求解，禁用后运行时间最多增加 1.5 倍。

Conclusion: JAQSI 以单一可微分模型统一门级与脉冲级量子仿真，兼顾灵活性与性能，并作为开源软件发布于 GitHub 与 PyPI。

Abstract: Classical simulation supports the development and validation of quantum algorithms through full-state inspection, controlled noise models and backpropagation, albeit for small quantum systems. Existing frameworks offer gate-level execution, differentiable pulse-level dynamics or optimised hardware execution. Just another quantum simulator (JAQSI) combines gate- and pulse-level simulation in one differentiable circuit model built upon JAX. Circuits are represented as pure functions of numerical inputs which support vectorised batches and JIT compilation. Within a single circuit, matrix-defined operations, gates synthesised from Hamiltonian time evolution and noise channels can be composed freely. For a fast simulation, gate and channel fusion reduces passes over the state, while adjoint differentiation computes expectation-value gradients with a constant number of statevectors. At pulse level, JAQSI reduces evolution under single-term Hamiltonians to a scalar pulse-area integral and reuses cached execution plans and compiled solvers. In a benchmark we compare JAQSI with PennyLane, Qiskit, Qibo, Qulacs, QuTiP and dynamiqs. At gate level, we observe performance either on par or an order of magnitude faster across statevector, density, noise and gradient simulation than the compared simulators up to 12 qubits. Beyond 18 qubits, JAQSI is on par with PennyLane Lightning in both statevector and gradient simulation. In the statevector and noisy density-matrix pulse benchmarks, it is at least 5 times faster than the other simulators, even with its closed-form solve disabled. Disabling the latter increases its runtime by at most a factor of 1.5. We provide JAQSI as open-source software on GitHub and PyPI.

</details>


### [138] [The magic cost of fermionic randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05093)
*Chenfeng Cao,Yifan Tang,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究费米子采样中复现随机矩阵统计所需的非高斯性资源，证明对任意纯半填充输入态，一体缺陷ν必须随系统尺寸线性增长，并在分块乘积情形下给出充要条件与碰撞矩的定量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型量子优势的一个基本资源问题是：复现与量子混沌相关的随机矩阵统计需要多少非高斯性。被动费米子线性光学保持高斯结构，即使Haar随机模式混合也无法对Slater输入实现反集中，因此需要刻画突破这一障碍所需的输入资源。

Method: 引入一体缺陷ν，即自然轨道占据数方差之和，用于度量纯半填充输入态的非高斯性；对4N模式上有限尺寸块的乘积态分析归一化碰撞矩；结合两副本粒子数涨落测量ν；并考察确定性精确制备中非高斯资源模式与保持宇称的局域门的总代价下界。

Result: 对任意纯半填充输入态，ν必须随系统尺寸线性增长；对4N模式上有限尺寸块的乘积，该条件是充分的，归一化碰撞矩渐近为2N/ν，而Porter-Thomas值为2；对四模式乘积，当缺陷密度趋于最大值时精确恢复完整Porter-Thomas矩层级；在平均情形困难性假设下，最大密度附近的近似经典采样仍然困难，且容错随密度增大。两副本粒子数涨落可直接测量ν，该量同时下界确定性精确制备中非高斯资源模式与保持宇称局域门的组合代价。

Conclusion: 费米子随机性具有广泛（extensive）的“魔法”代价，在采样统计、制备资源与实验可及的见证量之间建立了定量联系。

Abstract: Sampling-based quantum advantage raises a basic resource question: how much non-Gaussianity is needed to reproduce the random-matrix statistics often associated with quantum chaos? Passive fermionic linear optics preserves Gaussian structure, and even Haar-random mode mixing fails to anticoncentrate for Slater inputs. We show that overcoming this obstruction requires an extensive input resource. For any pure half-filled input state, the one-body defect $ν$, defined as the summed occupation variance of the natural orbitals, must scale linearly with system size. For products of bounded-size blocks on $4N$ modes, this condition is also sufficient, with normalized collision moment asymptotic to $2N/ν$, compared with the Porter-Thomas value 2. For four-mode products, the full Porter-Thomas moment hierarchy is recovered precisely when the defect density tends to its maximum. Assuming average-case hardness, approximate classical sampling remains hard near maximal density, with an error tolerance that increases with the density. Two-copy number fluctuations measure $ν$ directly, and the same quantity lower-bounds the combined cost of non-Gaussian resource modes and parity-preserving local gates in deterministic exact preparation. Fermionic randomness therefore carries an extensive magic cost, with a quantitative link between sampling statistics, preparation resources, and an experimentally accessible witness.

</details>


### [139] [Training Variational Quantum Algorithms Is NP-Hard, Even Locally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05128)
*Dax Enshan Koh,Triscia Mundo,Iosif Sakos,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：即使只求解VQA训练目标的局部极小值（或其ℓ_p距离逼近，p≥1且距离严格小于π/2）也是强NP难的，即使目标函数可高效经典求值；核心是逼近Hermitian三角多项式的局部极小值强NP难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法（VQA）依赖经典优化训练参数化量子电路，其训练效率决定其实用性。已知全局最小化训练目标为NP难，但通常认为局部极小值更易达到，因此作者探究更弱的局部最优任务是否存在计算可行性，以厘清VQA训练的根本计算极限。

Method: 从复杂度理论出发，将问题归约为逼近Hermitian三角多项式的局部极小值，证明该逼近任务强NP难；随后显式构造量子电路，使其训练目标复现这些困难实例，从而给出到VQA训练的多项式时间归约。硬度覆盖目标可高效经典求值的情形，并扩展到寻找与某局部极小值ℓ_p距离严格小于π/2的参数向量（对所有p≥1）。

Result: 证明寻找VQA训练目标的局部极小值是强NP难的；即使目标函数可高效经典求值亦然；对每个p≥1，寻找距某局部极小值ℓ_p距离严格小于π/2的参数向量仍为强NP难；核心结论为逼近Hermitian三角多项式局部极小值强NP难，并通过显式电路构造实现多项式时间归约。

Conclusion: VQA训练存在根本性计算壁垒：在最坏情况下，即便仅到达局部极小值附近也是困难的，限制了对高效训练的通用保证，说明经典优化在VQA中的可行性面临本质障碍。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) generally rely on classical optimization to train parameterized quantum circuits. This training seeks to minimize an objective function, and its efficiency is central to the practical success of these algorithms. However, globally minimizing such training objectives over the circuit parameters is known to be $\mathsf{NP}$-hard, limiting the prospect of general guarantees for efficient training. In this Letter, we prove that even the weaker task of finding a local minimum of such VQA training objectives is strongly $\mathsf{NP}$-hard, including when the objective admits efficient classical evaluation. Moreover, we show that this hardness persists even for the task of finding a parameter vector within $\ell_p$-distance strictly less than $π/2$ of some local minimizer, for every $p\geq 1$. Our central technical result is that approximating a local minimizer of a Hermitian trigonometric polynomial is strongly $\mathsf{NP}$-hard. By explicitly constructing quantum circuits whose training objectives reproduce these hard instances, we obtain a polynomial-time reduction to VQA training. Our results establish a fundamental computational barrier to variational quantum training: even reaching the vicinity of a local minimum remains hard in the worst case.

</details>


### [140] [The diffusive spectral gap of the qubit eSWAP chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05152)
*Yanying Liang,Haoran Zhu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了开放量子比特eSWAP链的二阶矩哈密顿量具有扩散型谱隙，验证了Li-Zheng-Liu-Jiang-Liu猜想，并刻画了任意连通相互作用图上的基态空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Li、Zheng、Liu、Jiang、Liu提出的关于开放eSWAP链二阶矩哈密顿量扩散谱隙的猜想，并深化对相关量子电路混合/收敛性质的理解。

Method: 结合谱隙分析与代数方法：证明开放eSWAP链二阶矩哈密顿量的扩散谱隙；刻画至少含五个顶点的任意连通相互作用图上的基态空间；建立非归一化完全图谱隙的一致下界；并将结论应用于顺序电路与砌砖电路。

Result: 开放eSWAP链二阶矩哈密顿量具有扩散型谱隙；给出了任意连通相互作用图（≥5顶点）上基态空间的完整刻画；得到非归一化完全图谱隙的一致下界；并证明了周期性链同样具有扩散型谱隙。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了已有猜想，并通过对相互作用图与电路结构的普遍分析，建立了开放eSWAP链及周期链扩散谱隙的统一框架，对量子电路谱性质研究有推广意义。

Abstract: We prove that the second-moment Hamiltonian of the open qubit eSWAP chain has a diffusive spectral gap, resolving a conjecture of Li, Zheng, Liu, Jiang, and Liu. We also characterise the ground space on every connected interaction graph with at least five vertices and prove a uniform lower bound for the unnormalised complete-graph gap. We apply these results to sequential and brickwork circuits and prove that the periodic chain also has a diffusive spectral gap.

</details>


### [141] [On the Completeness of Quantum Mechanics: Sources, Records and Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05153)
*Mustafa Bakr*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在给定源、探测器与统计条件下重建量子表示与Born概率，证明所容许的量子预言具有完备代数封闭性，无需坍缩假设，并指出许多量子悖论源于混淆相干过程与保留记录、概率与实际结果。最后探讨数学整体或源架构是否源自更深层结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应"量子力学能否在不具基础性的前提下保持完备"这一核心问题，试图在不引入坍缩公设的情况下说明量子预言体系的封闭性与自洽性，并澄清常见量子悖论的来源。

Method: 在明确给定的源（source）、探测器（detector）与统计条件（statistical conditions）下，重建量子表示形式与Born概率规则；进而对容许的量子预言进行代数封闭性证明，全程不依赖坍缩公设；并辨析相干过程与保留记录的混淆、概率与实际结果的混淆。

Result: 证明了所容许量子预言的完备代数封闭性，即无需坍缩假设即可实现理论完备；指出大量熟悉量子悖论实为概念混淆的产物，而非物理必然。

Conclusion: 量子力学可以完备而不必基础，完备性可由代数封闭性保证而非坍缩机制；并对数学整体或源架构是否源自更深层结构提出开放性追问。

Abstract: Can quantum mechanics be complete without being fundamental? Under stated source, detector, and statistical conditions, we reconstruct the quantum representation and Born probabilities and prove complete algebraic closure of the admitted quantum predictions without requiring a collapse postulate. We further show that many familiar quantum paradoxes arise from conflating coherent processes with retained records or probabilities with realized outcomes. We also ask whether mathematics as a whole, or the source architecture itself, emerges from a deeper structure.

</details>


### [142] [Error-Transparent Code Switching Between Permutation-Invariant Codes with Transversal Clifford and Icosahedral Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05189)
*Zachary P. Bradshaw*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究两个可置换不变码之间的码切换：一个具有横向Clifford门，另一个含非Clifford二十面体群的横向门。作者证明距离至少3的这类码由至多两个二次方程刻画，且误差几何相同；存在"误差透明"切换酉算子当且仅当两码可由距离3码的连续路径连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 码切换可让两个码的横向门组合实现通用量子计算，但切换操作本身通常非横向，会把单个故障扩散为不可纠正错误。因此需要刻画何种码对之间能存在容错切换，并构造误差透明的切换酉算子。

Method: 聚焦同一组量子比特上的两个可置换不变码，二者均兼容横向X、Z、F=HS†；分析具备该性质的编码器构成的线性空间。证明距离≥3的码由至多两个二次方程截出，其单比特误差态内积相同。对实数编码器，距离3码为单个二次方程的解；在13量子比特上用显式圆周参数化，并沿该圆周构造集体算子形式的切换酉算子。

Result: 距离≥3的码由至多两个二次方程刻画且误差几何一致；误差透明切换酉算子存在当且仅当两码由距离3码连续路径连接。实数情形下距离3码由单个二次方程给出，13量子比特上构成经过两个Clifford码与一个二十面体码的显式圆周。沿圆周构造的切换算子每故障恢复不保真度达机器精度，而两个自然替代方案的平均不保真度为0.13与0.85。对≤29的奇数量子比特数调查了此类切换的存在性。

Conclusion: 存在误差透明码切换的充要条件是两码可由距离3码连续路径连接；13量子比特样例给出显式构造且恢复精度达机器精度，但切换算子合成为物理控制仍待解决，并给出奇数量子比特数的存在性清单。

Abstract: Code switching moves an encoded qubit between two codes whose transversal gates together are universal, but the switching operation itself is usually not transversal and can spread a single fault into an uncorrectable error. We study switching between two permutation-invariant codes on the same qubits, one with transversal Clifford gates and one with transversal gates from the non-Clifford binary icosahedral group. Both are compatible with transversal $X$, $Z$, and $F = HS^\dagger$, and the encoders with this property form a small linear space. We show that its codes of distance at least three are cut out by at most two quadratic equations, and that all of them have the same error geometry in the sense that their single-qubit error states have the same inner products. It follows that a switching unitary commuting with qubit permutations and with the shared transversal gates at every instant, and tolerating one single-qubit fault at an unknown time, exists if and only if the two codes are joined by a continuous path of distance-three codes. Such a switch can be made error transparent, so that a fault at any moment reaches the target code as the same single-qubit error. For real encoders, the distance-three codes are the solutions of a single quadratic equation, and on thirteen qubits, they form an explicit circle through two Clifford codes and an icosahedral code. We construct the switching unitary along this circle as a collective operator, leaving its synthesis into physical control open, and verify that its recovery infidelity per fault is at machine precision, whereas two natural alternatives leave average infidelities of $0.13$ and $0.85$. A survey over odd qubit numbers up to $29$ lists where such switches exist.

</details>


### [143] [Universal and rigorous asymptotic convergence of standard macroscopic quantum electrodynamics to the modified Langevin noise formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05202)
*A. Ciattoni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明标准朗之万噪声形式（LNF）的虚构吸收极限过程具有普适有效性：通过构造MLNF态与可观测量到LNF的映射，严格证明当虚构吸收趋于零时，任意LNF矩阵元精确收敛于对应的MLNF结果，从而涵盖包括任意量子散射过程在内的全部物理预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LNF是处理无界损耗介质宏观量子电动力学的成熟方法，但应用于有限物体时需给周围真空赋予虚构吸收并令其渐近消失。已有对该奇异极限过程的论证主要局限于真空涨落与介质诱导相互作用（Casimir-Polder力、热辐射、量子发射体动力学等），严格排除了涉及外部量子辐射散射的情形。作者希望证明该极限过程在任意量子散射过程下同样成立，弥补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者引入一个适当的映射，将任意MLNF态与可观测量对应到加入虚构真空吸收的LNF中的实体；随后严格证明随着虚构吸收趋于零，通过该映射构造的每一个LNF矩阵元都精确收敛于其MLNF对应量，从而建立LNF对MLNF的渐近收敛性。

Result: 研究表明，LNF极限过程在所有区域内都能重现MLNF的任何物理预言，包括任意量子散射过程；即当虚构吸收趋于零时，经映射构造的LNF矩阵元精确收敛到MLNF原值。

Conclusion: LNF的极限过程具有普适有效性，其适用性不限于真空涨落与介质诱导相互作用，而是涵盖包括外部量子辐射散射在内的全部物理过程；该工作以严格的渐近收敛证明将LNF与完整的MLNF统一起来。

Abstract: The standard Langevin noise formalism (LNF) is a well-established approach for macroscopic quantum electrodynamics in unbounded lossy media, and accordingly, its application to finite objects requires endowing the surrounding vacuum with a fictitious absorption that is taken to asymptotically vanish. Existing justifications for such a singular limiting procedure have been mainly restricted to phenomena governed by vacuum fluctuations and medium-induced interactions (Casimir-Polder forces, thermal radiation, quantum emitter dynamics, etc.), thereby strictly excluding scenarios that involve the scattering of external quantum radiation. Conversely, the modified Langevin noise formalism (MLNF) has recently been established as the complete canonical quantum theory for arbitrary macroscopic systems and electromagnetic processes, entirely bypassing the need for limiting procedures, treating incident quantum radiation fields as independent degrees of freedom, and exactly reducing to the LNF for unbounded media. In this work, we demonstrate that the LNF limiting procedure is universally valid by showing that it recovers any physical prediction of the MLNF across all regimes, including arbitrary quantum scattering processes. Specifically, we introduce a suitable mapping associating any MLNF state and observable with a corresponding entity within the LNF augmented by fictitious vacuum absorption. We then rigorously prove that, as the fictitious absorption tends to zero, every LNF matrix element constructed via this mapping exactly converges to its MLNF original, so that the limiting procedure establishes the asymptotic convergence of the LNF to the MLNF.

</details>


### [144] [Theory of the Edge-of-Chaos Advantage in Quantum Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05213)
*Shuaifan Cao,Wei Xia,Xiaopeng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可严格分析的量子储备池模型，分别刻画其混沌区与可积点的计算能力上限，从而为"混沌边缘最优"这一经验现象提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储备池计算（QRC）的数值研究普遍显示其在混沌边缘（edge of chaos）表现最佳，通常被解释为记忆保持与非线性信息处理之间的平衡；但缺乏严格的理论依据，也缺乏对混沌区与可积点各自失效机制的具体刻画。

Method: 构建一个显式的量子储备池模型并对其进行严格分析：(1) 在混沌区，利用高斯幺正系综（Gaussian unitary ensemble, GUE）统计刻画动力学；(2) 在可积点，将系统描述为自由费米子（free-fermion）模型，分析幅度编码（amplitude encoding）加费米子两点关联的线性读出；并进一步引入相互作用考察其影响。

Result: 混沌区中GUE统计导致记忆快速丢失，学习性能随任务所需存储时间呈指数衰减，从而限制对长程时间依赖的建模能力；在可积点，证明幅度编码配合费米子两点关联的线性读出无法学习一类代表性的非线性任务（该任务耦合当前与过去输入）；引入相互作用可缓解这一阻碍。

Conclusion: 混沌区受限于记忆保持，可积点受限于非线性处理，两类机制互补，共同构成了混沌边缘优势的理论基础，并为面向时间学习的量子储备池设计提供指导。

Abstract: Numerical studies suggest that quantum reservoir computing performs optimally near the edge of chaos, a behavior commonly attributed to a balance between memory retention and nonlinear information processing. Here we develop an explicit quantum reservoir model and rigorously analyze its chaotic regime and integrable point. In the chaotic regime, we show that Gaussian-unitary-ensemble statistics lead to rapid memory loss: learning performance decays exponentially with the storage time required by a temporal task. This decay limits the ability of chaotic reservoirs to learn long-range temporal dependencies. At the integrable point, described by a free-fermion model, we prove that amplitude encoding and a linear readout of fermionic two-point correlations cannot learn a representative class of nonlinear tasks coupling present and past inputs. Introducing interactions alleviates this obstruction. By identifying distinct limitations on memory retention in the chaotic regime and nonlinear processing at the integrable point, our analysis provides a theoretical basis for the observed edge-of-chaos advantage and guides the design of quantum reservoirs for temporal learning.

</details>


### [145] [Cups and Gates II: Operads, twists, and addressable intra-code gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05214)
*Nikolas P. Breuckmann,Margarita Davydova,Jens Niklas Eberhardt,Lucas Ferdinand Hallwirth,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将第一部分中基于上同调不变量构造量子CSS码逻辑门（cup product及copy-cup）的框架大幅推广，引入twist（含码自同构）、高阶twisted上同调运算与相对上同调折叠操作，并实现从Z/2到Z/2^k的系数提升，从而给出可寻址对角逻辑门（如R(2π/2^k)、T门）及超越拓扑码的qLDPC码逻辑门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一部分仅覆盖由cup product及推广给出的copy-cup类逻辑门，构造能力有限，难以实现非Clifford对角门、可寻址门以及在更广泛的qLDPC码上的门。为推动容错量子计算与同伦论的交叉，需要一套更强、可提升系数、可寻址的逻辑门构造框架。

Method: 在cochain上引入E_∞-结构，结合cyclic resolution与surjection operad给出显式构造；通过twist并入码自同构；发展高阶twisted上同调运算（如higher twisted Pontryagin powers）；利用相对上同调中的folding操作；引入系数提升（Z/2→Z/2^k）；并将上述运算与选取上同调类的映射复合以获得可寻址门；整体形式化推广到带合适operadic结构的链复形。

Result: 构造出涵盖所有对角逻辑门（含单比特旋转R(2π/2^k)）的运算集合；给出三环面与四环面上的可寻址T门，以及超越拓扑类的qLDPC码逻辑门；提出此前未见于数学文献的高阶twisted Pontryagin powers等上同调运算。

Conclusion: 该operadic视角将容错量子门构造推广至更广的链复形，可能揭示同伦论与容错量子门之间更多联系，并对同伦论本身具有独立意义。

Abstract: In Part I, we introduced a general framework for constructing logical gates in quantum CSS codes from cohomology invariants, focusing on cup products and their generalizations from topological to qLDPC codes. In particular, we produced a family of $C^{k-1}Z$ logical gates that we called copy-cup on $k$ copies of the same quantum code implemented by finite-depth circuits. Here, we vastly extend this framework by introducing twists incorporating code automorphisms, higher twisted cohomology operations, and folding operations in relative cohomology. The framework includes operations lifting coefficients from $\mathbb{Z}/2$ to $\mathbb{Z}/2^k$, enabling all diagonal logical gates such as single-qubit rotations $\operatorname{R}(2π/2^k)$. Composing these operations with maps that select cohomology classes yields addressable logical gates. Examples include addressable $T$ gates on three- and four-tori and gates in qLDPC codes beyond topological ones. We introduce cohomology operations, such as the higher twisted Pontryagin powers, that to our knowledge have not previously appeared in the mathematics literature. These operations arise from $E_\infty$-structures on cochains, with explicit constructions using cyclic resolutions and the surjection operad. We expect these results to be of independent interest in homotopy theory. Our formalism applies much more broadly to chain complexes carrying suitable operadic structures, and we expect this operadic perspective to uncover further connections between homotopy theory and fault-tolerant quantum gates.

</details>


### [146] [Efficient Fidelity Estimation of General Quantum Resource States via Clifford Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05222)
*Shunta Takahashi,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Bell--coherence fidelity estimation (BCFE)，仅用Clifford门和Pauli测量即达到最优样本复杂度Θ(1/ε)，并在STAR架构中验证其优于DFE约27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保真度估计是评估量子资源态的基本工具。断层成像与直接保真度估计(DFE)在固定相对精度与置信度下最坏情况样本复杂度为O(1/ε²)；直接目标投影可达O(1/ε)，但需非Clifford操作，其可靠性依赖于被基准测试的资源态本身，形成循环依赖。因此需要一种既达到最优样本复杂度又仅依赖Clifford门与Pauli测量的估计方法。

Method: 提出Bell--coherence fidelity estimation(BCFE)：仅使用Clifford门与Pauli测量，样本复杂度为Θ(1/ε)。对任意纯目标态(可多量子比特)均适用，并显式控制单拷贝相干部分的统计误差。随后将BCFE应用于候选早期容错量子计算(early-FTQC)架构STAR中的模拟旋转资源态，并在surface code仿真中与DFE对比，评估电路级噪声下的表现。

Result: BCFE样本复杂度为Θ(1/ε)，达到最优。在surface code仿真中，达到相同不可信度估计标准误差时，BCFE所需资源态数量比DFE最多少约27倍；即使附加Bell测量操作引入电路级噪声，BCFE估计值仍接近参考值。

Conclusion: BCFE为容错量子计算中的资源基准测试提供了一种高效、抗噪的方法，克服了直接目标投影对非Clifford操作的依赖，支持在FTQC体系内进行可扩展的保真度估计。

Abstract: Fidelity estimation is a fundamental tool for assessing quantum resource states across quantum information science. For small infidelity $ε$, tomography and direct fidelity estimation (DFE) have worst-case sample complexity $O(1/ε^2)$ at fixed relative accuracy and confidence. Direct target projections achieve $O(1/ε)$ but generally require non-Clifford operations whose reliability depends on the resource states that are typically the target of benchmarking. Here, we introduce the Bell--coherence fidelity estimation (BCFE) with optimal sample complexity $Θ(1/ε)$ using only Clifford gates and Pauli measurements. We prove that BCFE is applicable to arbitrary pure targets, possibly multi-qubit, with the statistical error of the single-copy coherence part explicitly controlled. To evaluate BCFE in a practical setting, we apply BCFE to analog rotation resource states in the candidate early-FTQC architecture called the Space-Time Efficient Analog Rotations (STAR) architecture. In the simulations using surface codes, BCFE is estimated to require up to approximately $27$ times fewer resource states than DFE for the same standard error in the infidelity estimate, while its estimates remain close to reference values despite circuit-level noise in the additional Bell measurement operations. These results support efficient resource benchmarking within fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [147] [Complete Classification of Reversible Entanglement Transformations of Two Qutrits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05234)
*Wei Song,Xiao-Lan Zong,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了双qutrit态之间的可逆LOCC转换：两态可逆互转当且仅当它们的最小纠缠块在归一化前局部酉等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Alice与Bob通过LOCC将一个共享混合态转换为另一个、并能反向恢复时，究竟哪些东西在转换中保持不变，即刻画可逆转换的不变量。

Method: 考虑单拷贝的精确确定性LOCC变换，允许有限协议及可数协议；利用局部flag可将可分部分替换为局部制备而保留纠缠块；用部分转置的负本征空间与至多三次矩阵零检验求最小保留块。

Result: 证明：两互转纠缠态若非局部酉等价，则必须存在上述“可分部分+纠缠块”分解；两部分可由局部测量区分；纠缠块权重相同，归一化后局部酉等价；每个纠缠态有唯一最小保留块。两态可逆互转当且仅当未归一化块局部酉等价。

Conclusion: 可逆转换被唯一最小纠缠块（及其权重）完全刻画；对负部分转置态，可通过负本征空间和有限次矩阵零检验高效确定该块，给出了双qutrit可逆LOCC转换的完整分类。

Abstract: Suppose Alice and Bob can transform one shared mixed state into another by local operations and classical communication, and can also recover the first state from the second. What can change in such a conversion? We give a complete classification of reversible conversions between two-qutrit states. We consider exact deterministic LOCC transformations of a single copy, allowing finite protocols and the countable protocols specified here. Local flags are known to allow a separable part to be replaced by local preparation while an entangled block is retained. We prove that such a decomposition is necessary for two mutually convertible entangled states that are not locally unitarily equivalent. The two parts are distinguishable by local measurements. The entangled blocks have the same weight and are locally unitarily equivalent after normalization. Each entangled state has a unique smallest retained block. Two entangled states are reversibly convertible if and only if these unnormalized blocks are locally unitarily equivalent. For a state with negative partial transpose, the smallest block can be found using the negative eigenspace of its partial transpose and at most three matrix zero tests.

</details>


### [148] [Origin and classification of fractional dynamical topology in non-Hermitian quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05262)
*Zimiao Zhang,Li Chen,Weilun Jiang,Heng Shen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非厄米两带模型中的动力学量子相变（DQPT），指出动力学拓扑序参量的半整数跳变源于复能谱的不连续性，并与淬火哈密顿量的稳态相图本质关联，同时给出DQPT的分类，并推广至多带与二维模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学量子相变为非平衡临界现象提供基本框架，近年被推广到非厄米系统。半整数跳变的动力学拓扑序参量在厄米与非厄米系统中均可出现，但其在非厄米情形下的起源尚不明确，本文旨在澄清该起源。

Method: 在两带非厄米模型中研究DQPT，分析复能谱的不连续性；将半整数跳变行为与淬火哈密顿量的稳态相图相联系，并对DQPT的发生进行分类；进一步将分析推广到多带哈密顿量与二维模型。

Result: 发现半整数（分数化）跳变起源于复能谱的不连续性，该行为与淬火哈密顿量的稳态相图内在关联；实现了对DQPT发生的分类，并在多带与二维模型中验证了分数化动力学拓扑信号的统一图景。

Conclusion: 非厄米系统中的分数化动力学拓扑特征可由复能谱不连续性与稳态相图统一解释，该机制适用于多带与二维情形，为理解非厄米DQPT提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPT) provide a fundamental framework for characterizing nonequilibrium critical phenomena, and have recently been extended to non-Hermitian systems. Half-integer jumps of the dynamical topological order parameter can arise in both Hermitian and non-Hermitian systems, yet their origin in non-Hermitian settings remains unclear. Here, we investigate DQPTs in a two-band non-Hermitian model and show that these fractional jumps originate from the discontinuity in the complex energy spectrum. We demonstrate that this behavior is intrinsically linked to the steady-state phase diagram of the quench Hamiltonian, and also classify the occurrence of DQPT. We further extend our analysis to the multiband Hamiltonian and the two-dimensional model, providing a unified understanding of fractional dynamical topological signatures.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum n-coloring is undecidable for every n $\ge$ 3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05280)
*Christian Bo Kidmose-Frederiksen,Alfred Leth Nielsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过一个初等归约证明了量子n-染色问题对所有n≥3均不可判定，将原有仅n=3的结果推广到一般情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前仅知道量子3-染色问题是不可判定的，作者希望将这一不可判定性结果推广到所有n≥3的情形。

Method: 构造图变换 G'=(G□K_3)∨K_{n-3}（笛卡尔积后与完全图取联），证明G量子3-可染色当且仅当G'量子n-可染色。

Result: 建立了量子3-可染色与量子n-可染色之间的等价归约，从而将不可判定性从n=3推广到所有n≥3。

Conclusion: 量子n-染色问题对所有n≥3都是不可判定的。

Abstract: The quantum $n$-coloring problem, given graph $G$, asks whether there exists a perfect quantum strategy for the $n$-coloring game of $G$. Previously it was known that quantum $n$-coloring is undecidable only for $n=3$. We extend this to all $n\geq 3$ with one elementary reduction. Letting $G'=(G\square K_3)\lor K_{n-3}$, we show that $G$ is quantum $3$-colorable if and only if $G'$ is quantum $n$-colorable.

</details>


### [150] [Fast Random Compilers for Time-Dependent and Time-Independent Lindbladian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05288)
*Youngjin Seo,Dhrumil Patel,Hyukjoon Kwon,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 qSWIFT 型高阶随机编译器从哈密顿量模拟推广到 Lindblad 动力学，提出时不变与时变情形下的一阶、二阶随机采样算法。二阶方法在时间切片数上达到 ε^{-1/2} 精度依赖，优于一阶 qDRIFT 型的 ε^{-1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机积木（product formula）方法简单灵活，qDRIFT 型随机模拟已扩展到 Lindblad 动力学，但高阶随机编译器主要局限于哈密顿量模拟。作者希望为 Lindblad 模拟构造高阶随机采样算法，以改善精度依赖，并覆盖时不变与时变两类设置。

Method: 方法包括：一阶基于 qDRIFT 型 Lindblad 模拟；针对具有有限局部分解的时不变 Lindblad 情形，引入 qSWIFT 型二阶修正；进一步发展时变连续时间采样算法，包含一阶与二阶构造；并将时变构造推广到局域哈密顿量与耗散生成元的线性组合输入模型，使采样器以局域演化而非完整时变 Lindbladian 表达。由于二阶修正映射未必 CPTP，故用于估计可观测期望值而非确定性制备输出态。

Result: 当时不变情形下 λt ≥ 1（λ 为哈密顿系数与耗散率之和），二阶近似以 r=O(λ²t²/√ε) 个时间切片达到归一化 diamond 范数误差至多 ε，较一阶 qDRIFT 型的 ε^{-1} 精度依赖有所改进。时变情形的二阶构造在时间切片数上亦达到 ε^{-1/2} 精度依赖。

Conclusion: 本文为 Lindblad 模拟建立了系统的随机采样框架，涵盖时不变与时变、一阶与二阶算法，并将方法扩展至线性组合局域生成元输入模型。研究者强调二阶修正映射非 CPTP，因此其合理用途是估计可观测期望值，而非确定性地制备输出态。

Abstract: Randomized product formulas provide simple and flexible methods for quantum simulation. While qDRIFT-type randomized simulation has been extended to Lindbladian dynamics, higher-order randomized compilers have primarily been developed for Hamiltonian simulation. In this work, we develop first- and second-order randomized sampling algorithms for Lindbladian simulation in both the time-independent and time-dependent settings. Building on first-order qDRIFT-type Lindbladian simulation, we introduce a qSWIFT-type second-order correction for time-independent Lindbladians with a finite local decomposition. For $λt \geq 1$, where $λ$ is the sum of the Hamiltonian coefficients and dissipative rates, the resulting approximation achieves normalized diamond-norm error at most $\varepsilon$ using $r=O(λ^2 t^2/\sqrt{\varepsilon})$ time slices, improving the $\varepsilon^{-1}$ precision dependence of the first-order qDRIFT-type approximation. We then develop first- and second-order continuous-time sampling algorithms for general time-dependent Lindbladians, with the second-order construction likewise achieving an $\varepsilon^{-1/2}$ dependence on the target precision in the number of time slices. We further extend the time-dependent construction to a linear-combination input model over local Hamiltonian and dissipative generators, allowing the randomized sampler to be formulated in terms of local evolutions rather than the full time-dependent Lindbladian. Because the second-order corrected maps need not be CPTP, we use them to estimate observable expectation values rather than to deterministically prepare output states.

</details>


### [151] [Demonstration of a Structured Agentic Workflow for Applied Quantum Computing Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05304)
*Dikshant Dulal,Maxence Grandadam,Maciej Koch-Janusz,Mykola Maksymenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QAOS——一种基于非线性异构多智能体工作流图的agentic研究系统，用于量子与计算研发项目，并在量子化学原型任务上验证其优于基线智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子应用研究横跨领域知识、算法、理论物理、器件物理及量子/经典计算方法，所需专业广度与项目复杂度超出单个研究者能力，也对通用AI系统构成挑战。

Method: QAOS以非线性工作流图组织异构多智能体节点，控制上下文与计算资源的流动；将复杂研究项目分解为沙箱化的多智能体任务，在项目图、任务相关方法论与必要验证步骤的约束下执行。

Result: 在量子化学原型任务中（为质子转移问题建立最小模型并用基于采样的量子对角化SQD求解，同时用FCI和DFT等经典参考方法），QAOS给出了可行解；而基线智能体（Claude Sonnet 5 + Claude Code harness）会静默忽略明确指令，且运行间差异巨大。

Conclusion: 结构化agentic系统（如QAOS）能更可靠地完成复杂量子研发任务，克服通用AI智能体忽略指令、结果不稳定的缺陷。

Abstract: Quantum hardware advances now make it practical to explore quantum approaches to scientific problems. Applied quantum computing research sits at the intersection of domain knowledge, algorithms, theoretical physics, device physics, and quantum and classical computational methods. The breadth of expertise required and the inherent project complexity challenge not only any individual researcher, but also generic AI systems. Here, we introduce the Quantum Agentic Operating System (QAOS), a structured agentic research system designed to address this challenge for quantum and computational R&D projects. QAOS is built around a nonlinear workflow graph of heterogeneous multi-agent nodes, with a controlled flow of context and computational resources. It decomposes a complex research project into sandboxed multi-agent tasks executed under constraints from the project graph, task-relevant methodologies, and necessary verification steps. We demonstrate QAOS on a quantum chemistry prototyping task, in which it is asked to set up a minimal model for a proton-transfer problem and to implement its solution using sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD), together with classical reference methods such as FCI (exact diagonalization) and density functional theory (DFT). We compare the QAOS solution to those obtained with baseline agents (Claude Sonnet 5 with the Claude Code harness). In contrast to QAOS, the baseline agents silently ignore explicit instructions, and the runs show large variability.

</details>


### [152] [Lifted surgery for non-Abelian two-block group-algebra codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05307)
*Tushar Pandey*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将"提升手术"（lifted surgery）从阿贝尔群代数码推广到非阿贝尔群的双块群代数码，研究非交换性能否让单个合并码测量更多逻辑算符。作者分类了这些码的块仿射自同构，证明增益至多为最大阿贝尔子群的指数，并给出距离最大为13、增益为2至3的显式码例，以及实验模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 码手术（code surgery）测量量子LDPC码的逻辑算符需要O(d)轮综合。提升手术利用平移对称性，在单个合并码中一次性测量一个逻辑算符轨道，从而摊销该开销，但此前仅适用于阿贝尔群代数码。作者希望探究非阿贝尔群的双块群代数码能否借助非交换性让单个合并码测量比任何阿贝尔对称群更多的逻辑算符。

Method: 1) 将提升手术推广至非阿贝尔群上的双块群代数码；2) 对这些码的块仿射自同构（block-affine automorphisms）进行完整分类，并以此为基准衡量"非阿贝尔增益"；3) 证明增益的普适上界为最大阿贝尔子群的指数；4) 构造并检验具体码例，给出合并距离引理（merged-distance lemma）以保证gadget保持码距；5) 在电路级噪声下使用Relay-BP解码进行模拟。

Result: 在完整块仿射自同构群的基准下，大多数表面上的非阿贝尔增益消失；增益至多为最大阿贝尔子群的指数。找到精确距离最高达13、增益为2的码，其中包括一个合并码即可读出所有逻辑量子比特的码；并给出基于A₄、S₄与SL(2,3)乘积、最佳逻辑增益为3、规模达[[336,26,12]]的码。合并距离引理给出gadget保持码距的简明条件。电路级模拟表明，在可分辨的错误率下，非阿贝尔gadget的可靠性在统计误差内不低于其所替代的阿贝尔gadget，同时所需轮数减少2到3倍。

Conclusion: 非交换性带来的逻辑算符测量增益受到严格限制：相对于完整的块仿射自同构群，增益上限为最大阿贝尔子群的指数，多数非阿贝尔优势并不成立。尽管如此，仍存在确实获得增益2或3的码例，且非阿贝尔gadget在保持码距与可靠性的同时显著减少综合轮数，具有实际价值。

Abstract: Code surgery measures a logical operator of a quantum LDPC code with $O(d)$ rounds of syndrome extraction. Lifted surgery amortises this cost on Abelian group-algebra codes by measuring an orbit of logical operators under a translation symmetry in one merged code. We extend it to two-block group-algebra codes over non-Abelian groups and ask whether non-commutativity lets one merged code measure more operators than any Abelian group of its symmetries. We classify the block-affine automorphisms of these codes and find that, measured against this full group, most apparent non-Abelian gains disappear. We prove that the gain is at most the index of a largest Abelian subgroup. We find codes with exact distance up to $13$ where the gain is two, including codes in which one merged code reads out every logical qubit, and codes over products of $A_4$, $S_4$ and SL(2,3) with gain three for a best logical, up to $[[336,26,12]]$. A merged-distance lemma gives a simple condition for the gadget to preserve the code distance. In circuit-level simulations with Relay-BP decoding, at the error rates we can resolve, the non-Abelian gadget is as reliable as, or more reliable than, the Abelian gadgets it replaces within statistical error, while using two to three times fewer rounds.

</details>


### [153] [Shallow Quantum Circuits for Geometrically Local Lindblad Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05362)
*Zheyu Shen,Yusen Wu,Xiao Yuan,Yukun Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出模拟有限程Lindblad动力学的量子算法，深度O(T polylog(nT/ε))、门数O(nT polylog(nT/ε))，并将HHKL空间分解推广至开放系统；同时给出匹配的最坏情况下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放系统动力学支配量子多体系统的退相干、输运与工程耗散，需高效量子模拟；本文旨在将HHKL空间分解从酉动力学推广到耗散通道，并刻画其复杂度下界。

Method: 针对常维格点上n个格点、时间无关、有限程、局域强度有界的Lindblad动力学（跳跃算符可非对易），保留环境于共享酉膨胀中使系统丢失的信息相干可用，从而使空间修正可逆；证明分解所生成环境态的指数占据界，实现压缩表示与局域因子的相干模拟；将短时演化化为对数直径区域上的常数层并行最近邻门，并借助polylog辅助密度。

Result: 上界：T≥1、金刚石范数误差≤ε时，深度O(T polylog(nT/ε))，门数O(nT polylog(nT/ε))。下界：基于时间无关纯耗散信息输运过程，对含局域量子与经典通信的电路，期望门数Ω(nT)、深度Ω(T)（时间尺度至格点直径），与上界主阶匹配；固定时间且误差足够小时，深度下界Ω(log n/log log n)。

Conclusion: 该工作将HHKL分解成功推广至开放系统，借助共享酉膨胀与环境态占据界实现高效模拟；并用纯耗散过程证明近乎匹配的worst-case下界，表明全局精度可能随格点增大要求更深电路。

Abstract: Open-system dynamics govern decoherence, transport, and engineered dissipation in quantum many-body systems. We propose a quantum algorithm for simulating time-independent, finite-range Lindblad dynamics with bounded local strength on a constant-dimensional lattice of $n$ sites, allowing noncommuting jump operators. For time $T\ge1$ and diamond-norm error at most $ε$, it has depth $O(T\operatorname{polylog}(nT/ε))$ and gate count $O(nT\operatorname{polylog}(nT/ε))$. These results extend the HHKL spatial decomposition to open systems. The main obstacle is that the reversible overlap corrections used for unitary dynamics have no direct analogue for dissipative channels. We overcome this by retaining the environment in a shared unitary dilation, keeping information lost from the system coherently available and making the spatial corrections reversible. We prove exponential occupation bounds for the environmental states generated by the decomposition, allowing compact representation and coherent simulation of local factors on the inputs that arise. The construction reduces each short-time evolution to a constant number of parallel layers on regions of logarithmic diameter, using nearest-neighbour gates and polylogarithmic auxiliary density.
  We also prove unconditional worst-case lower bounds using time-independent, purely dissipative processes that transport quantum information. For circuits with local quantum and classical communication, the lower bounds are $Ω(nT)$ for the expected gate count and $Ω(T)$ for depth on time scales up to the lattice diameter. They match the upper bounds' leading time and volume dependence up to polylogarithmic factors. At fixed time and sufficiently small fixed error, we further prove a depth lower bound of $Ω(\log n/\log\log n)$, showing that global accuracy can require increasing depth as the lattice grows.

</details>


### [154] [Minimax Quantum State Tomography in a Known Trace-Distance Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05374)
*Yonglong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知量子态位于已知参考态 ρ 的迹距离球内的量子态层析问题，针对任意集体测量，确定了极小极大期望风险与置信半径，精确到通用常数，并给出副本复杂度；方法上结合加权迹范数见证的下界与基于纯化过程修改版的谱与集中分析上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化在未知态处于已知参考态迹距离球这一先验约束下的量子态层析最优精度，此前结果通常未同时对维度、中心谱、半径和样本量一致最优，需要确定精确的极小极大风险与副本复杂度，为集体测量下的层析性能提供紧确刻画。

Method: 对任意集体测量分析极小极大风险；下界使用加权迹范数见证，在平方根扰动族中保留所有谱尺度；上界对已有基于纯化的过程进行修改，直接做谱分析与浓度分析。

Result: 定义 Γ=d(Tr√ρ)²+d²R。对 d≥2、0<R≤1、n≥1 个副本，期望风险为 Θ(min{R,√(Γ/n)})；失败概率 0<δ≤1/3 时，极小极大置信半径为 Θ(min{R,√((Γ+log(1/δ))/n)})；当 ε≤c₀R（c₀>0 为通用常数）时，副本复杂度为 Θ((Γ+log(1/δ))/ε²)。

Conclusion: 在参考态迹距离球的约束下，集体测量的量子态层析最优期望风险、置信半径与副本复杂度均由 Γ 与 R、n、δ 的上述表达式精确控制，达到通用常数意义下的最优。

Abstract: We study quantum state tomography when the unknown state lies in a trace-distance ball of radius $R$ around a known reference state $ρ$. For arbitrary collective measurements, we determine the minimax expected risk and confidence radius up to universal constants, uniformly over the dimension, center spectrum, radius, and sample size. Write $Γ=d(\mathrm{Tr}\sqrtρ)^2+d^2R$. For $d\ge2$, $0<R\le1$, and $n\ge1$ copies, the expected risk is $Θ(\min\{R,\sqrt{Γ/n}\})$. At failure probability $0<δ\le1/3$, the minimax confidence radius is \[
  Θ\!\left(\min\left\{R,\sqrt{\frac{Γ+\log(1/δ)}{n}}\right\}\right). \] For $\varepsilon\le c_0R$ with a universal $c_0>0$, the copy complexity is $Θ((Γ+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon^2)$. For the lower bound we use a weighted trace-norm witness to retain all spectral scales in a square-root perturbation family. For the upper bound, we give a direct spectral and concentration analysis of a modified existing purification-based procedure.

</details>


### [155] [On State Distinguishing Without Inverse Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05389)
*Emma Wang,Joseph Carolan,Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究仅有前向访问（无逆操作）的量子态区分问题：证明即便两个态均已知，前向量子查询也需要 Ω(ε^{-2}) 次；同时给出连续时间变体下仅需 O(ε^{-1}) 前向演化时间的维度无关算法，利用量子 Zeno 动力学实现二次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准量子态区分中，已知需要 Θ(ε^{-2}) 份拷贝；若可访问酉态制备 oracle 及其逆，则可降至 Θ(ε^{-1}) 次查询。然而许多物理过程不可逆，只能前向访问。Tang 与 Wright 已证明在已知态与未知近邻态区分时前向查询仍需 Ω(ε^{-2})；本文旨在加强该下界使其覆盖两态均已知的态区分场景，并探讨连续时间类比下是否仍能保留二次优势。

Method: 下界方面，采用直接的 hybrid 论证，避免使用此前工作中的 advanced compressed oracle 技术，证明前向查询下界为 Ω(ε^{-2})。算法方面，针对态区分的连续时间变体，设计维度无关算法，借助量子 Zeno 动力学将演化限制在已知的二维子空间内，从而仅需 O(ε^{-1}) 总前向演化时间。

Result: 证明了即使两个态均已知，前向查询区分仍需 Ω(ε^{-2}) 次，强化了 Tang–Wright 的结果；给出了连续时间态区分变体的维度无关算法，前向演化时间仅 O(ε^{-1})。

Conclusion: 在离散前向查询模型中，无逆访问会丧失二次加速；但在连续时间类比下，借助量子 Zeno 动力学，二次优势仍可在仅有前向演化时保持。

Abstract: In the quantum state distinguishing problem, an algorithm is given copies of a state $|ψ\rangle$ or $|φ\rangle$ separated by trace distance $\varepsilon$, and tasked with deciding which is the case. It is a standard result that $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ copies are required, while having access to a unitary state preparation oracle and its inverse improves this to $Θ(\varepsilon^{-1})$ queries. We study the intermediate case with forward-only access, capturing scenarios where the oracle is a physical process that cannot be reversed. Tang and Wright recently showed that distinguishing a known state from an \emph{unknown} close state requires $Ω(\varepsilon^{-2})$ forward queries in high dimension; we strengthen this result by proving the same bound even when both states are known, as in the state distinguishing setting. Moreover, our lower bound uses a direct hybrid argument, avoiding advanced compressed oracle techniques used in the prior work. In contrast, we give a dimension-independent algorithm solving a continuous-time variant of state distinguishing with only $O(\varepsilon^{-1})$ total forward evolution time. The algorithm uses quantum Zeno dynamics to confine evolution to a known two-dimensional subspace. Thus, a quadratic advantage survives without inverse access for a natural continuous-time analogue of state distinguishing.

</details>


### [156] [Performance Analysis of Multiparty GHZ-State Quantum Conference Key Agreement over Optical Fiber Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05448)
*Dipanjan Bera,Ramniwas Meena,Neel Kanth Kundu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将GHZ态N方QCKA的光纤链路建模为含暗计数的擦除-泡利信道，推导错误率闭式解并嵌入有限密钥SKR框架，用Brent方法优化参数估计概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GHZ态是QCKA的天然资源，但光纤传输中的损耗、偏振退相干和探测器不完美会破坏其性能，需在有限密钥条件下定量刻画信道影响并优化密钥生成。

Method: 将双体擦除-泡利信道扩展以纳入探测器暗计数，对每条光链路建模；分析该信道对共享GHZ态的影响，推导比特错误率与相位错误率的闭式表达式；将错误率纳入有限密钥SKR框架，用Brent无导数法优化参数估计概率。

Result: 局域比特错误率仅依赖透射率与暗计数，而全局相位错误率在N-1条链路上累积，随距离和用户数增长更快；光纤距离与网络规模增大会降低可达到的SKR；优化参数估计概率可持续提升SKR并延长最大安全距离，相对固定分配基线更优，与渐近极限对比量化了残余有限密钥惩罚。

Conclusion: 所提模型与优化方法可定量评估有限密钥下GHZ基QCKA性能，参数估计概率优化是提升SKR与安全距离的有效手段。

Abstract: Quantum conference key agreement (QCKA) enables multiple spatially separated users to establish a common key with composable information-theoretic security by exploiting multipartite quantum correlations. Greenberger--Horne--Zeilinger (GHZ) states are a natural resource for this task, but their distribution over optical fiber is degraded by transmission loss, polarization decoherence, and detector imperfections. We investigate the finite-key performance of an $N$-party GHZ-based QCKA system using the $N$-BB84 protocol, where each optical link is modeled by extending the bipartite erasure--Pauli channel to incorporate detector dark counts. We characterize the impact of this quantum channel on the shared GHZ state and derive closed-form expressions for the error rates. The analytical results reveal that the local bit-error rate depends only on transmissivity and dark counts, while the global phase-error rate compounds over all $N-1$ links and therefore degrades faster with both distance and the number of parties. These error rates are integrated into a finite-key secret-key-rate (SKR) framework, in which we optimize the parameter-estimation probability via Brent's derivative-free method. Numerical results show that increasing fiber distance and network size reduce the achievable SKR. Furthermore, the optimized parameter-estimation probability consistently increases the SKR and extends the maximum secure distance relative to a fixed-allocation baseline, with comparison to the asymptotic limit quantifying the residual finite-key penalty.

</details>


### [157] [Game of Nons: Non-Gaussian Nonunitary Environments with Nonlinear Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05454)
*Artem Karasev,Alexander Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个微扰框架，用于处理二次耦合到具有二次哈密顿量和线性 Lindblad 算符的玻色环境中的系统，可处理非高斯、非平稳初态的少模与多模环境，通过双算子空间线性演化与特征函数表示给出多时刻关联函数，并递归构造任意有限阶 TCL 生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放系统微扰方法多要求环境初态为高斯或平稳态，难以处理非高斯、非平稳初态及多模情形；需要一种能系统计算高阶多时刻环境关联函数并构造 TCL 生成元的通用框架。
