Method: 结合双算子空间中的线性演化与特征函数表示，将有序多时刻环境关联函数表示为初始特征函数的各阶导数；这些关联函数用于递归构造任意有限阶的时间卷积无（TCL）生成元。

Result: 以通过双光子相互作用耦合到阻尼模的量子比特为例进行说明：具有相同一阶和二阶场矩的初始 Fock 态与热态，通过其四阶矩产生不同的二阶生成元。

Conclusion: 该方法为具有二次哈密顿量和线性 Lindblad 算符的玻色环境中系统提供了通用的微扰处理手段，能够揭示非高斯性（如四阶矩差异）对生成元的贡献。

Abstract: We develop a perturbative framework for systems coupled quadratically to bosonic environments with quadratic Hamiltonians and linear Lindblad operators. The method applies to both single-mode and multimode environments whose initial states may be non-Gaussian and nonstationary. By combining linear evolution in a doubled operator space with a characteristic-function representation, we express ordered multitime environmental correlation functions in terms of derivatives of the initial characteristic function. These correlation functions enter a recursive construction of the time-convolutionless generator at arbitrary finite order. We illustrate the method with a qubit coupled to a damped mode via a two-photon interaction. Initial Fock and thermal states with identical first and second field moments produce different second-order generators through their fourth moments.

</details>


### [158] [Optimization Geometry of QAOA and Variational Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05524)
*Vojtěch Novák,Ivan Zelinka,Silvie Illésová,Swagatam Das,Martin Beseda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将VQE与QAOA的目标函数几何特征（采样的局部极小数量与局部搜索端点质量差异）与优化器选择联系起来，发现局部极小数量本身并不能决定全局搜索是否有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法（VQA）将哈密顿量、拟设与参数化的选择转化为经典非凸优化问题，但目标函数的性质如何影响优化器行为尚缺乏可视化与系统刻画，需要理解何时全局进化求解器优于局部搜索。

Method: 区分目标函数的两个性质：沿采样方向遇到的局部极小数量，以及局部搜索端点之间的质量差异。以BFGS代表局部优化器、jSO代表自适应差分进化，并让BFGS在同一函数评估预算下多起点运行以构成更强基线。在VQE与QAOA上考察阻挫、电路深度、参数绑定、混合局域性与非线性重参数化对目标函数的影响。

Result: 增加独立电路深度会提高采样的局部极小数量，但并未使全局搜索更有效；参数绑定则同时产生更多重复局部结构与更大的局部搜索端点质量差异，在此情形下自适应差分进化优于函数评估匹配的多起点BFGS。

Conclusion: 局部极小数量本身不足以判断全局搜索是否有利，关键在于不同盆地是否导向相近质量的解还是显著不同的目标值。研究提供了连接模型构造、目标函数几何、经验诊断与优化器选择的实用工作流。

Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms turn choices of Hamiltonian, ansatz, and parameterization into a classical nonconvex optimization problem. We study how this objective function can be visualized and characterized in ways that help explain optimizer behavior. We distinguish two properties of the objective: the number of local minima encountered along sampled directions and the differences in quality among local-search endpoints. We then ask how a local optimizer, represented by BFGS, compares with adaptive differential evolution, represented by jSO. Rather than comparing jSO with a single local run, we allow BFGS multiple starts within the same function-evaluation budget. This gives local search repeated opportunities to explore different basins and provides a stronger baseline for asking when a global evolutionary solver is useful. We study these questions using VQE and QAOA, focusing on how frustra- tion, circuit depth, parameter tying, mixed locality, and nonlinear repa- rameterization change the Hamiltonian expectation-value objective seen by the classical optimizer. Increasing independent circuit depth raises the sampled local-minimum count without making global search more effective. Parameter tying, by contrast, produces both more repeated local structure and much larger differences in quality among local-search endpoints, and in this regime adaptive differential evolution outperforms function-evaluation-matched multistart BFGS. The comparison shows that the number of local minima alone does not determine whether global search is advantageous: the important distinction is whether different basins lead to similarly good solutions or to substantially different objective values. These results provide a practical workflow for connecting model construction, objective- function geometry, empirical diagnostics, and optimizer choice.

</details>


### [159] [Optimal and Verifiable Quantum Advantages in Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05585)
*Ryan Anselm,Michelle Ding,Dar Gilboa,Sabee Grewal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在通信复杂度上建立了搜索问题的最优量子-经典分离，提出"远洋傅里叶钓鱼"总搜索问题：具有$n$量子比特单向量子协议，但任意随机双向协议需$Ω(2^n)$比特；其可多项式验证变体亦保持此分离。进一步构造可高效验证问题族，实现每固定$d\ge2$的$n$对$Ω_d(n^d)$量子单向与随机单向分离，并在$d=2$时以无魔法（稳定子态+Clifford测量）达到最优二次量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子-经典通信复杂度分离多依赖特定结构或缺乏高效可验证性，且难以在近期量子设备上实现无条件、可验证的优势。本文旨在构造具有最优分离度的搜索问题，兼顾可高效验证性与实验可实现性。

Method: 引入Pelagic Fourier Fishing总搜索问题及其可多项式验证变体；利用$d$阶Clifford层级中的单个酉变换制备Alice消息、Bob执行Clifford测量，构造高效可验证总搜索问题族，并分析其通信复杂度上下界。

Result: Pelagic Fourier Fishing存在$n$量子比特单向量子协议，而任意随机双向协议需$Ω(2^n)$比特；其可验证变体同样保持$n$对$Ω(2^n)$的分离。所构造问题族实现$n$对$Ω_d(n^d)$分离，且在该限制下渐近最优；$d=2$时使用稳定子态与Clifford测量，无魔法即达最优二次量子优势。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了通信复杂度中搜索问题的最优量子-经典分离，并提供了可高效验证、无条件且无魔法（$d=2$情形）的优势方案，可适配近期量子优势实验。

Abstract: We establish optimal quantum-classical separations in communication complexity for search problems. We introduce a total search problem called Pelagic Fourier Fishing and show that it admits an $n$-qubit quantum one-way protocol, whereas every randomized two-way protocol requires $Ω(2^n)$ bits of communication. We then introduce a variant of this problem whose solutions can be verified in polynomial time. This variant also admits an $n$-qubit quantum one-way protocol, while every randomized one-way protocol requires $Ω(2^n)$ bits of communication.
  We also construct a family of efficiently verifiable total search problems achieving an $n$ versus $Ω_d(n^d)$ separation between quantum one-way and randomized one-way communication for every fixed $d \ge 2$. In the quantum protocol, Alice prepares her message using a single unitary from the $d$th level of the Clifford hierarchy, and Bob performs a Clifford measurement. This separation is asymptotically optimal under this restriction on Alice's message. For $d = 2$, Alice's message is a stabilizer state and Bob's measurement is Clifford, so the protocol uses no magic, yet achieves an optimal quadratic quantum advantage.
  Finally, we discuss how these separations can be adapted to near-term quantum advantage experiments in which the demonstrated advantage is both unconditional and efficiently verifiable.

</details>


### [160] [Autonomous Quantum Memories from Classical Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05589)
*Nadav Drechsler,Yuval Oreg,Dorit Aharonov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了受Pauli噪声影响的CSS量子记忆与经典记忆之间的一般等价性，通过异步（Lindbladian）与同步（SPCA）两类概率过程将二者对应，并首次严格证明带Toom规则的4D环面码在局部Pauli噪声下是量子记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机有两方面：一是容错量子计算需要能抵御真实噪声的量子记忆；二是关于自主多体系统中量子记忆何时能够涌现这一基本问题。

Method: 在异步设定下，作者识别出一类基于syndrome的Lindbladian，使量子记忆问题等价于连续时间经典马尔可夫动力学中的记忆问题；在同步设定下，引入基于syndrome的概率元胞自动机（SPCA），建立其经典记忆与局部量子信道下CSS量子记忆的一一对应。进而将SPCA中的经典记忆通过Floquet Lindbladian动力学提升为量子记忆。

Result: 给出首个严格证明：带Toom规则的4D环面码在局部Pauli噪声下、误差低于某个常数阈值时是量子记忆，且对相应的同步PCA型量子动力学与Floquet Lindbladian均成立。该框架也复现了已知结果（如4D环面码在热噪声下是量子记忆）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了关联经典与量子记忆的一般框架，揭示了完全自主量子记忆的条件，并为在低维构造可证明的自主量子记忆提供指导；能否用时间无关的Lindbladian实现这一提升仍为开放问题。

Abstract: The study of quantum memories out of thermal equilibrium is motivated both by the need for memories that withstand realistic noise in fault-tolerant quantum computers and by the fundamental question of when quantum memory can emerge in autonomous many-body systems. We establish a general equivalence between CSS-based quantum memories subject to Pauli noise and classical memories via two associated probabilistic processes. In the asynchronous setting, where the quantum dynamics is generated by a Lindbladian, we identify a broad class of syndrome-based Lindbladians for which the quantum memory problem is equivalent to memory in continuous-time classical Markov dynamics. In the synchronous setting, we introduce syndrome-based probabilistic cellular automata (SPCAs) and establish a one-to-one correspondence between their classical memories and CSS-based quantum memories under local quantum channels. This framework provides a systematic route for deriving and constructing quantum memories from classical probabilistic processes, and recovers known results, including the fact that the 4D toric code is a quantum memory in the presence of thermal noise.
  As an application, we give the first rigorous proof that the 4D toric code with Toom's rule is a quantum memory in the presence of local Pauli noise, provided the error is below a constant threshold. We prove this both for the corresponding synchronous, PCA-type quantum dynamics and for a Floquet Lindbladian. More generally, we show that classical memory in an SPCA can be lifted to a quantum memory under Floquet Lindbladian dynamics; whether this can be done with a time-independent Lindbladian remains open. Our results provide a general framework for relating classical and quantum memory, shed light on the conditions for fully autonomous quantum memories, and may guide the construction of provable autonomous quantum memories in low dimensions.

</details>


### [161] [Erasure of entanglement-locked energy in multi-cell quantum batteries through decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05592)
*Haniyeh Hajizade,Vahid Ameri,Alidad Askari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子电池充电过程中被"锁"在双体关联中的能量（关联遍历能），发现其本质由真正的量子纠缠所携带而非相干集体动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泵浦充电会使量子电池相当一部分储能被锁定在电池元胞之间生长的双体关联内，这部分能量无法通过任何局域单胞操作获取，只能靠集体操作回收。作者希望厘清这种锁定能量的物理来源，区分真正的纠缠与单纯相干集体动力学。

Method: 追踪两个耦合三能级元胞的充电轨迹，将关联遍历能与对数负性对比；对纯双胞电池推导精确、与基无关的闭式表达式；并考察退相干、退相位、冷阻尼和热占居等环境效应的影响。

Result: 关联遍历能与对数负性高度同步，表明锁定能量由真正的双体纠缠承载；等强度的相干非纠缠驱动完全不产生锁定。纯双胞电池的闭式解表明锁定能量只依赖于约化胞的排序本征值和胞能级：锁定比例在Schmidt单纯形的可分离顶点为零，在对称最大纠缠态处为1，且在局域幺正变换下不变。退相干与退相位不释放而是抹除该能量；冷阻尼下效应保持不变，热占居则抑制之。

Conclusion: 结果为量子电池中的关联提供了资源论解读，厘清了真正纠缠与相干集体动力学的界限，并指出实现关联充电与集体提取协议的实验可行条件。

Abstract: Charging a quantum battery through a pumped charger can lock a substantial part of its stored energy inside the bipartite correlations that grow between the constituent cells, and this \emph{correlation ergotropy}, being the energy that no local single-cell operation can reach and that only collective operations can recover, is the object of the present study. Following the charging trajectory of two coupled three-level cells, we find that the correlation ergotropy closely tracks the logarithmic negativity, which shows that the locked energy is carried by genuine bipartite entanglement rather than by coherent collective dynamics alone, and that an equal-strength coherent non-entangling drive produces no such locking at all. For a pure two-cell battery we establish an exact, basis-independent closed form in which the locked energy depends only on the sorted eigenvalues of the reduced cell and the cell energy levels, revealing that the locked fraction rises from zero at the separable corners of the Schmidt simplex to unity at the symmetric maximally entangled state and remains invariant under local unitaries. Decoherence and dephasing, which destroy the correlations, do \emph{not} release this energy but erase it, so extraction must be collective and must take place while the correlations persist, while cold damping leaves the effect intact and thermal occupation suppresses it. These results give a resource-theoretic reading of correlations in quantum batteries, sharpen the line between genuine entanglement and coherent collective dynamics, and point to experimentally accessible conditions for correlated-charge and collective-extraction protocols.

</details>


### [162] [A solvable quantum many-body problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05593)
*John Lekner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一类以粒子间距离平方和的开方R为指数的多体波函数，证明它是势能正比于1/R时N体薛定谔方程的严格解，并给出具有确定角动量的平移不变转动本征态集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找多体薛定谔方程（N粒子体系）的严格解析解，特别是针对形如1/R的势（R为粒子间距离平方和的开方）这类具有高度对称性的相互作用，以便获得可解析处理的多体本征态。

Method: 构造指数型多体试探波函数 exp(-αR)，其中R²为所有粒子间距离平方之和；将其代入N粒子薛定谔方程，配合正比于1/R的势能项进行求解验证，并在此基础上按角动量量子数分类，构造平移不变的转动本征态。

Result: 该指数型波函数被证明是上述1/R势下N体薛定谔方程的精确解；同时得到一组满足平移不变性、具有确定角动量的转动本征态。

Conclusion: 该工作为1/R型多体势提供了解析可解的本征态，所构造的平移不变角动量本征态可作为进一步研究多体关联、集体激发及少体/多体精确解的出发点。

Abstract: A many-body wavefunction exponential in R, where the square of R is the sum of the squares of the interparticle distances, is shown to be the solution of Schrodingers equation for a potential proportional to the reciprocal of R, for N particles. A set of translationally invariant rotational eigenstates of angular momentum is found.

</details>


### [163] [Frame-dependent coherence of Gaussian quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05623)
*Nicolae Cotfas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用所有正则相干态构成的紧框架替代Hermite-Gauss基描述连续变量高斯态，导出了混合高斯态框架依赖相干性的显式公式，并构造了快速收敛的离散变量量子态序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hilbert空间通常用正交归一基描述，但更一般的描述可用紧框架给出。连续变量高斯态的相干性通常借助Hermite-Gauss基刻画，作者希望寻找一种更简洁的描述方式。使用正则相干态紧框架来刻画高斯态比Hermite-Gauss基更简单，从而有望获得混合高斯态相干性的解析表达。

Method: 采用所有正则相干态构成的紧框架来描述连续变量高斯态，并据此推导框架依赖相干性。

Result: 给出了混合高斯态框架依赖相干性作为协方差矩阵行列式与迹的函数的显式公式；对每个混合高斯态构造了离散变量量子态序列，当Hilbert空间维度足够大（15或以上）时，该序列在纯度和相干性上快速收敛于原态。离散情形下，相干性相对于所有离散相干态构成的紧框架来度量。

Conclusion: 以相干态紧框架代替Hermite-Gauss基可更简洁地描述高斯态，并给出混合高斯态相干性的显式解析式；所构造的离散变量量子态序列能快速收敛到连续变量混合高斯态，表明连续与离散相干性描述之间存在有效联系。

Abstract: A Hilbert space is usually described using an orthonormal basis, but a more general description can be defined using a tight frame. In the case of continuous-variable Gaussian states, the coherence is usually described using the Hermite-Gauss basis. We present an alternative description using the tight frame of all canonical coherent states. The description of Gaussian states in terms of coherent states is simpler than in terms of Hermite-Gauss basis, and this allows us to obtain an explicit formula describing the frame-dependent coherence of a mixed Gaussian state as a function of the determinant and the trace of covariance matrix. For each mixed Gaussian state, we construct a sequence of discrete-variable quantum states rapidly convergent to it (as concern the purity and coherence) when the dimension of Hilbert space becomes large enough (15 or more). In the discrete case, the coherence is measured with respect to the tight frame of all discrete coherent states.

</details>


### [164] [Ground state energy estimation and preparation by the truncated power method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05627)
*Savero Lukianto Chandra,Daochen Wang,Carolyn Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别出GLHP的一个经典可解新区间：当真实基态的ℓ₁范数多项式有界时，经典截断幂方法（TPM）可在量子算法多项式可比的复杂度内求解GLHP，额外代价仅为‖ψ‖₁²因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GLHP在小误差下是BQP完全问题，在大误差下经典可解。作者希望找到另一个经典可处理的区间，为量子优势的边界划定更精细的界限，并探索去量子化（dequantization）的可能性。

Method: 采用Kirby等人提出的经典截断幂方法（Truncated Power Method, TPM），并分析其运行时间与量子GLHP算法在各项相关参数上的复杂度关系。

Result: TPM的运行时间与量子GLHP算法在除‖ψ‖₁²之外的所有相关参数上均多项式可比。即当‖ψ‖₁²多项式有界时，TPM可去量子化GLHP的量子算法。

Conclusion: ℓ₁范数多项式有界构成GLHP的一个经典可解区间；在此条件下，经典TPM可实现与量子算法多项式等价的求解，表明该区间量子优势消失。

Abstract: The Guided Local Hamiltonian Problem (GLHP) is the problem of ground state energy estimation and preparation given a guiding state having good overlap with the true ground state. The problem is known to be BQP-complete for small error but is classically tractable for larger error. In this work, we identify an orthogonal regime in which the problem is classically tractable, namely when the true ground state $ψ$ has polynomially bounded $\ell_1$-norm. In particular, we show that the Truncated Power Method (TPM), a classical algorithm introduced by Kirby et al. in the quantum context, has a runtime that is polynomially comparable to quantum algorithms for GLHP in all relevant parameters, except for an extra factor of $\|ψ\|_1^2$. In other words, if $\|ψ\|_1^2$ is polynomially bounded, then TPM dequantizes quantum algorithms for GLHP.

</details>


### [165] [Transformer-Based Time-Series Inference of Lindblad Dynamics in Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05647)
*Julian Guam,Jianqing Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于原始时间序列的Transformer模型，直接输入Pauli期望值全轨迹，用于推断开放量子系统中的未知耗散率与哈密顿量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当系统哈密顿量与耗散率部分未知或显含时间依赖时，传统解析反演与系统辨识方法变得难以处理；已有Transformer方法多依赖手工统计特征且在理想、受限条件下工作。

Method: 引入raw time-series Transformer，直接接收⟨σ_x(t)⟩、⟨σ_y(t)⟩、⟨σ_z(t)⟩完整轨迹，利用自注意力机制进行时序建模；架构进一步扩展以联合学习未知哈密顿量参数、处理多耗散通道，并在真实测量噪声下稳健运行。

Result: 在所有测试场景中，模型均取得一致的高重建精度，同时消除了人工特征工程。

Conclusion: 该工作为现实开放量子系统中的量子环境感知提供了一个可扩展、稳健且通用的框架。

Abstract: The Lindblad master equation is the standard framework for describing the non-unitary evolution of open quantum systems, where environmental interactions induce dissipation and decoherence. When both the system Hamiltonian and the dissipation rates are partially unknown or explicitly time-dependent, traditional analytical inversion and system-identification techniques become intractable. Recent works have demonstrated that Transformer-based models can infer unknown dissipation rates from observable time series, yet these approaches typically rely on hand-crafted statistical features under idealized and highly restricted conditions. Here we advance the paradigm by introducing a raw time-series Transformer that directly ingests the full trajectories of Pauli expectation values $\langleσ_x(t)\rangle$, $\langleσ_y(t)\rangle$, and $\langleσ_z(t)\rangle$, thereby fully exploiting the self-attention mechanism for temporal modeling. The architecture is further extended to jointly learn unknown Hamiltonian parameters, handle multiple dissipation channels, and operate robustly under realistic measurement noise. Across all tested scenarios the model achieves consistently high reconstruction accuracy while eliminating manual feature engineering. This provides a scalable, robust, and versatile framework for quantum environment sensing in realistic open quantum systems.

</details>


### [166] [Universal Improvement of Channel Fidelity via Entanglement Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05669)
*Xinan Chen,Eric Chitambar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种普适的纠缠辅助编解码策略，可将多个非相同量子信道的使用组合成一个最坏情况保真度更高的信道，并推广至经典信道与一般量子资源理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中退相干使量子信道的多次使用非完全相同，逐一编码解码难以保证性能，需在非相同信道条件下仍能提升传输质量。

Method: 对n个非相同信道进行纠缠辅助的编码与解码，构造一个组合信道；分析其普适性、最优性与唯一性，并将方法扩展到经典信道（借助共享随机性）及一般量子资源理论（给出充分与必要条件）。

Result: 所得信道的最坏情况保真度高于任一给定信道；对退极化信道达到最优，且该策略本质上是唯一普适的方案；经典情形下共享随机性可实现正确传输概率的普适增强；资源理论中给出了普适提升可能性的充要条件。

Conclusion: 存在一个普适且最优（对退极化信道）的纠缠辅助方案，可提升非相同量子信道的性能，并可推广至经典信道与一般量子资源理论。

Abstract: Communication using quantum channels generally requires encoding and decoding on many identical uses of a quantum channel. In practical settings, decoherence may severely limit our ability to do so, potentially rendering each channel use nonidentical. Given $n$ nonidentical channels, we present an entanglement-assisted encoding and decoding strategy that yields a channel with higher worst-case fidelity than all of the $n$ given channels. The strategy is universal, in the sense that the improvement holds regardless of what channels are given, as long as they satisfy mild assumptions on their initial entanglement fidelities. Our protocol is optimal when the given channels are depolarizing channels, and is essentially the unique one that is universal. This idea can also be extended to classical channels, where shared randomness between the sender and the receiver allows universal enhancement of the probability of correct transmission. We then consider general quantum resource theories, providing a necessary condition and a sufficient condition for the possibility of such universal improvement of quantum resources.

</details>


### [167] [Momentum-Space Path Integral Approach to Non-Hermitian Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05702)
*Zhuo-Ting Cai,Wei Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在动量空间中建立了非厄米对称性破缺的量子-经典对应，通过构造相应的迹公式与量子化条件，揭示了本征值实/复性质由半经典轨道的对称性决定，并阐明轨道拓扑决定量子化条件的自然表述形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前的非厄米对称性破缺量子-经典对应仅基于坐标空间路径积分，缺乏动量空间的对偶表述，适用性受限，需补全该框架以拓展其应用范围。

Method: 在动量空间中构造对应的迹公式与量子化条件，分析半经典轨道的对称性与拓扑性质（可收缩轨道与非可收缩轨道），并区分坐标空间绕转与布里渊区绕转轨道；以有限非厄米晶格中布洛赫振荡的边界诱导谱跃迁作为应用实例。

Result: 单个本征值的实或复性质由相关半经典轨道的对称性决定，与坐标空间路径积分方法一致；对于相空间中可收缩周期轨道，坐标与动量空间量子化条件等价；对于非可收缩轨道，沿绕转方向的量子化条件仍成立，但其对偶形式需加入边界项修正。实空间绕转与布里渊区绕转轨道分别自然选择坐标空间与动量空间量子化。在有限非厄米晶格中，跨越布里渊区的布洛赫振荡轨道保持相关对称性并给出实能级，而边界反射轨道形成对称相关对并产生复共轭本征值。

Conclusion: 本工作补全了非厄米对称性破缺的量子-经典对应框架，将其适用性拓展至更广泛的非厄米问题。

Abstract: A quantum-classical correspondence for non-Hermitian symmetry breaking has recently been established using coordinate-space path integrals, providing a semiclassical understanding of spectral transitions at the level of individual eigenstates. Here we develop its dual formulation in momentum space by constructing the corresponding trace formula and quantization condition. We show that the real or complex nature of individual eigenvalues is determined by the symmetry properties of the associated semiclassical orbits, as in the coordinate-space path-integral approach. Moreover, we demonstrate that the topology of semiclassical orbits determines the natural formulation of the quantization condition: the coordinate- and momentum-space formulations are equivalent for contractible periodic orbits in phase space, whereas for noncontractible orbits, the quantization condition along the winding direction remains valid, but its dual form must be corrected by a boundary term. In particular, real-space-winding and Brillouin-zone-winding orbits naturally select coordinate- and momentum-space quantization, respectively. As a nontrivial application, we investigate the boundary-induced spectral transition of Bloch oscillations in a finite non-Hermitian lattice, where Bloch-oscillation orbits winding across the Brillouin zone preserve the relevant symmetry and yield real energy levels, whereas boundary-reflected orbits form symmetry-related pairs and give rise to complex-conjugate eigenvalues. Our work completes the quantum-classical correspondence framework for non-Hermitian symmetry breaking, extending its applicability to a broader class of non-Hermitian problems.

</details>


### [168] [An efficient Hamiltonian-based quantum algorithm for characters of the symmetric group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05752)
*Dikshant Rathore,Leo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了制备对称群 S_n 特征标态（character state）的替代算法，利用哈密顿量演化序列替代量子傅里叶变换，显著降低门复杂度，并将其推广为量子特征标变换（QCT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作（arXiv:2501.12579）表明，采样自按特征标值加权的不可约表示叠加态在合理复杂性假设下是经典困难的，但可用基于 S_n 上 QFT 的高效量子算法制备。该 QFT 子程序门复杂度高达 Õ(n^3)，因此需要更简单、门复杂度更低的制备方案。

Method: 作者提出用一系列哈密顿量演化来制备特征标态，并给出基于可重构量子比特、仅使用一维最近邻门的高效实现。门数估计来自领头阶、依赖状态的 Trotter 误差分析；最坏情况界则基于 QSVT 的哈密顿量模拟方法。方法还被推广为量子特征标变换（QCT）的显式算法。

Result: 在固定目标态误差下，足够的总门数随所选列而变化：n-循环列为 O(n)，接近猜想的经典困难参数区域为 Õ(n^{1.75})，最坏情况可证明为 Õ(n^{2.5})，而先前 QFT 方法为 Õ(n^3)。数值基准测试表明 Trotter 协议的门数估计偏保守。QCT 算法使用 Õ(n^{2.5}) 门和 O(n) 个量子比特。

Conclusion: 该工作为对称群特征标态及量子特征标变换提供了门复杂度更优的算法，并讨论了其在对称轨形共形场论纠缠熵中的应用。

Abstract: Consider a quantum state vector proportional to any given column of the character table of the symmetric group ($S_n$), corresponding to a superposition over the irreps weighted by the character values. It was recently argued that sampling from this state is classically hard under reasonable complexity-theoretic assumptions, while there exists an efficient quantum algorithm for preparing this character state using the quantum Fourier transform (QFT) over $S_n$ [arXiv:2501.12579]. We give an alternative, simpler algorithm to prepare this character state with a sequence of Hamiltonian evolutions, as well as an efficient implementation with reconfigurable qubits that uses only nearest-neighbor gates in 1D. The gate complexity, accounting for Hamiltonian simulation errors, depends on the choice of the column: For fixed target state error, the estimated sufficient total gate count ranges from as few as $O(n)$ gates for the $n$-cycle column, to $\widetilde O(n^{1.75})$ gates near the conjecturally classically hard parameter regime, to provably $\widetilde O(n^{2.5})$ gates in the worst case. In contrast, the QFT subroutine in the prior approach uses $\widetilde O(n^3)$ gates. Our gate count estimates come from leading-order state-dependent Trotter error analysis, and the proven worst-case bound relies on a QSVT-based Hamiltonian simulation method; numerical benchmarks of the Trotter-based protocol suggest that the estimates are conservative. Furthermore, we generalize our protocol to give an explicit algorithm for the quantum character transform (QCT) over $S_n$ using $\widetilde O(n^{2.5})$ gates and $O(n)$ qubits. An application to entanglement entropy of symmetric orbifold conformal field theories is also discussed.

</details>


### [169] [Certified Randomness Generation against Quantum Adversaries via Energy Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05766)
*Thomas A. Hahn,Efrat Gerchkovitz,Ester Yehuda,Rotem Arnon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于物理能量约束的prepare-and-measure型量子随机数发生器（QRNG），针对同时持有经典与量子边信息的对手，证明了一个近乎紧致的单轮冯·诺依曼熵下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QRNG安全性依赖非信号假设（设备无关QRNG）或后量子计算假设（计算有界单证明者协议）。能量约束模型实现更简单、速率更优，但此前工作要么在安全性证明中仅假设对手是经典的，要么仅通过条件最小熵给出较弱的认证随机性界。本文旨在为能量约束QRNG建立针对一般量子攻击的严格安全性证明基础。

Method: 在能量约束的prepare-and-measure框架下，采用单轮冯·诺依曼熵（而非条件最小熵）作为认证随机性的度量，针对同时拥有经典和量子边信息的对手，推导其近乎紧致的下界，并进一步分析该熵界的鲁棒性。

Result: 证明了针对经典与量子边信息对手的单轮冯·诺依曼熵的近乎紧致下界。结果表明，在量子情形下使用冯·诺依曼熵替代最小熵可带来显著提升，在许多情况下几乎与经典界相匹配；同时所提熵界具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 单轮冯·诺依曼熵是刻画QRNG密钥率的核心量，本文的下界为该能量约束框架下针对一般量子攻击的严格安全性证明提供了关键要素。

Abstract: For quantum random number generators (QRNGs), strong security guarantees can be obtained under various assumptions. The most well-known assumptions are the non-signalling assumption (needed for device-independent (DI) QRNG) and post-quantum computational assumptions (needed for computationally bounded single-prover QRNG protocols). In this work, we study a third model: a simple prepare-and-measure QRNG whose security relies on physically motivated energy constraints. This physically motivated set-up allows for easier implementations of QRNGs and can supply better rates.
  So far, previous works that studied the energy-constrained model either assumed that the adversary is classical in their security proof or supplied relatively weak bounds on the certified randomness, by bounding the conditional min-entropy. In this work, we prove a new, essentially tight, lower bound on the single-round von Neumann entropy against adversaries holding both classical and quantum side-information. Our work reveals that working with the von Neumann entropy, rather than the min-entropy, in the quantum case can lead to a major improvement, nearly matching the classical bound in many cases. We further study the robustness of our entropy bound. Since the single-round von Neumann entropy is the main quantity of interest when characterizing the key rate of QRNGs, our bound establishes the main ingredient for rigorous security proofs against general quantum attacks in this framework.

</details>


### [170] [Quantum Complexity of Solving Linear Equations on Higher-Order Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05806)
*Caesnan M. G. Leditto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在特定稀疏oracle模型与参数承诺下，制备Hodge拉普拉斯线性系统的归一化最小范数解是BQP-hard的，并结合高效量子算法给出该问题的BQP-完全性，从而为高阶网络分析中的量子优势提供最坏情形复杂性基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶网络（HONs）中的单纯模型可用Hodge拉普拉斯线性系统统一刻画（如统计排序、耦合振子稳定性）。但边、三角形及高维单纯形对应变量规模巨大，求解代价高。已有量子算法试图缓解该瓶颈，然而仅与特定经典算法比较，无法判定此类线性系统问题是否具备可证明量子优势的复杂性基础。因此需要给出严格的最坏情形复杂性刻画。

Method: 作者在指定的稀疏oracle模型与参数承诺下，构造一系列归约，将任意多项式时间有界错误量子计算映射为Hodge拉普拉斯线性系统；每次归约都能高效恢复前一个线性系统的归一化最小范数解。再结合一个从近似解态出发求解相关判定问题的高效量子算法，完成复杂性闭合。

Result: 证明制备Hodge拉普拉斯线性系统归一化最小范数解的问题在给定承诺下是BQP-hard的；结合高效量子算法，该问题为BQP-完全。结果表明：即使矩阵被限制为Hodge拉普拉斯，量子线性系统问题（QLSP）的BQP-hardness依然保持。

Conclusion: 该工作为评估高阶网络分析中的量子优势提供了最坏情形复杂性基础，并将QLSP的困难性推广到Hodge拉普拉斯这一受限但重要的矩阵类。

Abstract: Higher-order networks (HONs) represent interactions among groups of objects, supporting the study of collective behaviour that pairwise models can miss. In simplicial models of these networks, Hodge Laplacian linear systems provide a common mathematical framework for analysis, for example, for solving statistical ranking problems and investigating long-term stability in coupled oscillator systems. The large number of variables associated with edges, triangles, and higher-dimensional simplices can make these systems costly to solve. Recent quantum algorithms address this bottleneck. However, comparisons with particular classical algorithms do not decision output whether solving these linear system problems establishes a quantum complexity foundation for provable quantum advantage. We prove that preparing a quantum state encoding the normalized minimum-norm solution of Hodge Laplacian linear systems is $\mathsf{BQP}$-hard under the specified sparse oracle model and parameter promises. Our sequence of reductions maps an arbitrary polynomial-time bounded-error quantum computation to such linear systems. Each reduction allows efficient recovery of the normalized minimum-norm solution of the preceding linear system. Together with an efficient quantum algorithm for the associated decision problem from the approximate solution state, we show that this problem is $\mathsf{BQP}$-complete. These results provide a worst-case complexity foundation for evaluating quantum advantage in HON analysis and show that the $\mathsf{BQP}$-hardness of the quantum linear system problem (QLSP) persists even when the matrices are restricted to Hodge Laplacians.

</details>


### [171] [Spectral Analysis Beyond the Spectral Gap: Rapid Mixing of Quantum Gibbs Samplers in One Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05848)
*Hongrui Chen,Zhiyan Ding*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一维非对易局域自旋系统的量子Gibbs采样器在任意固定正温度下具有快速混合性，混合时间从先前的O(n+log(1/ε))改进为O(log⁴(n)+log(1/ε))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前关于量子Gibbs采样器混合时间的结果O(n+log(1/ε))仅适用于高温或弱相互作用区域。作者希望突破这些限制，在一般的一维非对易局域自旋系统的所有固定正温度下建立快速混合性，并解释为何谱间隙会显著低估平衡化速度。

Method: 作者基于CKG2023提出的KMS细致平衡Gibbs采样器，通过超越谱间隙的谱分析框架证明快速混合。关键思想是：远离平衡的态必须在低权重Pauli算子张成空间之外有显著分量，在该区域可应用更强的耗散估计。由此产生两阶段弛豫机制，并将衰减估计转化为夹心Rényi-2散度下的指数收敛。

Result: 对于n个格点的系统，采样器从任意初态收敛到迹范数误差ε的时间为O(log⁴(n)+log(1/ε))。衰减估计等价于夹心Rényi-2散度下的指数收敛，结合谱间隙还可推出速率Ω(log⁻⁴(n))的修正对数Sobolev不等式。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了超越高温和弱相互作用区域的一维量子Gibbs采样器的快速混合性，提供了一个通过超越谱间隙的谱分析证明快速混合的通用框架，并揭示了谱间隙可能低估平衡速度的机制：先双指数收敛、后近平衡处指数收敛的两阶段弛豫过程。

Abstract: We prove rapid mixing of a quantum Gibbs sampler for general one-dimensional non-commuting local spin systems at every fixed positive temperature. In particular, for a system of $n$ sites, we show that the Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) detailed-balanced Gibbs sampler of~\cite{CKG2023} converges from any initial state to trace-norm error $ε$ in time $O(\log^4(n)+\log(1/ε))$. This improves the previous $O(n+\log(1/ε))$ bound and establishes rapid mixing beyond high-temperature and weak-interaction regimes. Our proof explains why the slowest decay rate, captured by the spectral gap, can substantially underestimate the speed of equilibration. The key insight is that a state far from equilibrium must have a substantial component outside the span of low-weight Pauli operators, where stronger dissipation estimates apply. This yields two stages of relaxation: a double-exponential convergence from a bad initial state, followed by exponential convergence near equilibrium. We turn this mechanism into a framework for proving rapid mixing through spectral analysis beyond the gap. Our decay estimate is equivalent to exponential convergence in sandwiched Rényi-2 divergence. Together with the spectral gap, it also implies a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality with rate $Ω(\log^{-4}(n))$.

</details>


### [172] [Classical observable coalescence as a discriminating diagnostic for spectral degeneracies in non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05877)
*P. K. Nkaimbi,C. A. Mba,A. B. Moubissi,M. R. Kamsap,L. C. Fai,M. E. Ateuafack,M. N. Jipdi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出“经典可观测凝聚”作为从物理构型直接识别例外点(EP)的诊断方法，并在非厄米Ising链中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 例外点通常需通过本征矢凝聚来判定，而缺乏哈密顿谱的经典平台无法直接获取该特征，因此需要一种无需谱信息的经典诊断手段来识别非厄米临界性。

Method: 对具有耦合J与交错虚场γ的非厄米Ising链的海森堡动力学做平均场约化，得到两支对称相关平衡分支；通过Jordan-Wigner与Bogoliubov-de Gennes精确处理验证本征矢凝聚，并提出耦合增益-损耗电路实现方案。

Result: 平衡分支在EP超曲面|γ|=|J|边界融合，呈现√(J²-γ²)分支点结构及双叶黎曼拓扑；γ=0,η=0处退化为diabolic point且无可观测凝聚，表明该诊断具判别性而非仅提示性；精确处理确认同一阈值处本征矢凝聚。

Conclusion: 经典可观测可作为非厄米临界性的实验可及特征，所提电路实现为在经典平台探测EP提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are conventionally identified through eigenvector coalescence, inaccessible in classical platforms lacking a Hamiltonian spectrum. We introduce classical observable coalescence as a diagnostic identifying EPs directly from physical configurations, and demonstrate it in a known non-Hermitian Ising chain with coupling $J$ and staggered imaginary field $γ$, chosen for verifiability against established quantum results. A mean-field reduction of the Heisenberg dynamics yields equilibrium branches organising into two symmetry-related families whose fusion at the boundary of the EP supersurface $|γ|=|J|$ exhibits the characteristic $\sqrt{J^2-γ^2}$ branch-point structure and two-sheeted Riemann topology. Crucially, the degeneracy at $γ=0,η=0 $ is shown to be a diabolic point, producing no observable coalescence, establishing the diagnostic as discriminating rather than merely suggestive. An exact Jordan-Wigner and Bogoliubov-de Gennes treatment confirms eigenvector coalescence at the same threshold, and a coupled gain-loss circuit realisation is proposed, establishing classical observables as experimentally accessible signatures of non-Hermitian criticality.

</details>


### [173] [Fusion-assisted decoding of non-Abelian topological order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05901)
*Rohith Sajith,Yabo Li,Zijian Song,Tsung-Cheng Lu,Carolyn Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非阿贝尔任意子码的解码问题，提出通过精细化综合征集合（含分辨融合结果的测量）系统性提升最大似然解码器性能，并以 \(D_4\) 量子双重模型为例给出显式解码器与阈值数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然解码器在阿贝尔任意子的环面码/表面码中已被充分理解并能达到信息论解码阈值，但非阿贝尔任意子码虽在容错非Clifford门和高效制备方面日益重要，其解码面临非阿贝尔任意子无法确定性湮灭、逻辑算符不能用阿贝尔同调分类等新挑战。

Method: 作者证明精细化综合征集合——包括能够分辨非阿贝尔任意子融合结果的测量——可系统性改进最大似然解码器性能；具体通过为电荷噪声下的 \(D_4\) 量子双重模型构造显式解码器，并借助到经典统计力学模型的映射获得阈值数值结果。

Result: 对 \(D_4\) 量子双重模型在电荷噪声下构造了显式解码器，并通过经典统计力学映射得到阈值数值结果，表明存在非零噪声阈值，低于该阈值时单轮测量即可恢复整个逻辑量子比特（qudit）。

Conclusion: 精细化综合征测量能够提升非阿贝尔任意子码的最大似然解码性能，且 \(D_4\) 量子双重模型在电荷噪声下具有非零阈值并支持单轮测量恢复整个逻辑qudit。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) decoders perform quantum error correction by measuring a set of syndromes, classically computing an optimal recovery based on these syndromes and the noise model, and then performing that recovery. ML decoders are well understood for the toric code and generalize straightforwardly to other surface codes with Abelian anyons, where they can saturate information-theoretic decoding thresholds. On the other hand, codes with non-Abelian anyons have become increasingly relevant because they can support transversal or constant-depth non-Clifford gates and admit efficient preparation protocols in modern quantum devices. However, they present new challenges for decoding: non-Abelian anyons cannot in general be deterministically annihilated, and the logical operators cannot be classified by Abelian homology. In this work, we show that refined syndrome sets, including measurements that resolve non-Abelian anyon fusion outcomes, systematically improve the performance of ML decoders. We demonstrate this concretely by constructing explicit decoders for the $D_4$ quantum double model under charge noise, and obtaining numerical results for thresholds via mappings to classical statistical-mechanics models. We show that there is a nonzero noise threshold below which the entire logical qudit can be recovered with a single round of measurements.

</details>


### [174] [Robust Diamond Quantum Sensing in Double-Quantum 4-Ramsey Interferometry by Dual-Feedback Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05907)
*Yuta Araki,Takeharu Sekiguchi,Yuji Hatano,Naota Sekiguchi,Zehan Li,Chikara Shinei,Masashi Miyakawa,Takashi Taniguchi,Tokuyuki Teraji,Hiroshi Abe,Shinobu Onoda,Takeshi Ohshima,Mutsuko Hatano,Takayuki Iwasaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过双反馈系统补偿双量子4-Ramsey干涉中的共模与差模频移，实现了高灵敏、长期稳定的金刚石量子磁强计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测极弱磁场信号需要长时间信号累积以提升信噪比，但热漂移和环境磁噪声导致工作频率偏离最优值，严重制约传感系统的长期稳定性。

Method: 基于含失谐哈密顿量推导信号对共模与差模失谐的解析解，明确DQ 4-Ramsey传感的鲁棒性极限；据此引入同步频率调制与双反馈方案，独立跟踪并补偿频移，同时保持最优磁场响应。

Result: 系统可在2.8 K温度变化下稳定连续运行；在5–100 Hz频段维持7.0 pT/Hz^1/2灵敏度达4小时，噪声底达64 fT/Hz^1/2；长时间连续传感中呈现预期的1/T^1/2标度关系，并避免了幅度低估与波形失真。

Conclusion: 该工作为长期DQ 4-Ramsey金刚石磁强计建立了鲁棒方案，并展示了其在弱生物磁信号检测中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We developed a highly sensitive and long-term stable diamond quantum magnetometer by integrating a dual-feedback system that compensates for common- and differential-mode frequency shifts in double-quantum (DQ) 4-Ramsey interferometry. Detecting extremely weak magnetic-field signals requires extensive signal accumulation to enhance the signal-to-noise ratio. However, deviations from the optimal operating frequencies caused by thermal drift or environmental magnetic noise severely hinder the long-term stability of such a sensing system. To overcome the limitation, we first derived analytical solutions for the signal response to common- and differential-mode detunings based on a detuning-inclusive Hamiltonian to theoretically clarify the robustness limit in DQ 4-Ramsey sensing. Guided by this theoretical model, we introduced synchronized frequency modulation and a dual-feedback scheme to independently track and compensate for these frequency shifts while maintaining an optimal magnetic-field response. The proposed method enabled stable continuous operation against temperature variations of 2.8 K. By maintaining a magnetic field sensitivity of 7.0 pT/Hz^1/2 in the frequency range of 5-100 Hz for a prolonged duration of 4 hours, we achieved an extremely low noise floor of 64 fT/Hz^1/2. During prolonged continuous sensing, the system exhibited the expected 1/T^1/2 scaling with integration time T, and prevented both the underestimation of amplitude and waveform distortion. These results establish a robust approach for long-term DQ 4-Ramsey diamond magnetometry and demonstrate its potential for detecting weak biomagnetic signals.

</details>


### [175] [Evaluating the performance of QEC primitives on quantum processors at large width and depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05928)
*J. A. Montanez-Barrera; Kristel Michielsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LR-QAOA的少样本基准，用算法信号r的深度衰减刻画硬件有效错误率λeff，用于评估QEC相关原语与硬件代际。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QEC依赖重复奇偶校验提取、MCM、复位、前馈与调度等原语，但这些原语通常被孤立评估，或需通过资源开销高昂的实验验证，缺乏低成本、可比的整体基准。

Method: 以固定线性参数的QAOA（LR-QAOA）结合MCM实现基准：依据所选码的校验结构构造QAOA哈密顿量，使电路复现校验提取的连通性与MCM模式，并以近似比r作为直接算法信号；QAOA层数类比QEC的重复校验轮数；由r随深度的衰减定义有效硬件错误，并映射为等效双比特退极化率λeff；在IBM、IQM、Quantinuum共10台QPU上比较直接实现与MCM中介实现，电路最多含2950个MCM。

Result: 在Quantinuum Helios-1与H2-1上，分别对表面码、三角色码与双变量自行车qLDPC哈密顿量运行码结构LR-QAOA，数据比特规模达81、91、48，MCM操作最多480个；在IBM ibm_phoenix上实现表面码结构，并将LR-QAOA响应与逻辑存储实验对比，发现基准结果与QPU不同区域的逻辑性能存在相关性。

Conclusion: 该基准构造简单、资源需求低，可在完整逻辑存储实验之前用于比较硬件代际与QEC实现，为QEC原语评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) relies on repeated parity extraction, mid-circuit measurement (MCM), reset, feed-forward, and scheduling, yet these primitives are usually assessed either in isolation or through resource-demanding experiments. We introduce a few-sample benchmark of QEC-relevant primitives based on an MCM implementation of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) with a fixed set of linear parameters (LR-QAOA). For a chosen code, the QAOA Hamiltonian is constructed from its check structure, such that the resulting circuit mimics the syndrome-extraction connectivity and MCM pattern while producing a direct algorithmic signal, the approximation-ratio r. The LR-QAOA depth, defined by the number of QAOA layers, plays a role analogous to the number of repeated syndrome-extraction rounds in a QEC experiment. From QPU executions, the decay of r with depth defines an effective hardware error, which we map to an equivalent two-qubit depolarizing rate λeff. We compare direct and MCM-mediated implementations across 10 QPUs from IBM, IQM, and Quantinuum, with circuits containing up to 2950 MCM operations. On Quantinuum's Helios-1 and H2-1, we run code-structured LR-QAOA benchmarks for surface-code, triangular color-code, and bivariate-bicycle qLDPC Hamiltonians up to 81, 91, and 48 data qubits, respectively, using up to 480 MCM operations. On IBM ibm_phoenix, we implement the surface-code structure and compare the LR-QAOA response with logical-memory experiments, observing a correlation between the benchmark and the logical performance across different regions of the QPU. Its construction and low resource requirements provide a practical benchmark for comparing hardware generations and QEC implementations before full logical-memory experiments are performed.

</details>


### [176] [Fast mixing of the SYK model at high temperatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05953)
*Yiyi Cai,Yongtao Zhan,Alexander Zlokapa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明SYK模型在足够高的常数温度下（以高概率对无序）存在谱隙与系统规模无关的量子Gibbs采样器，从而可在量子计算机上多项式时间制备SYK Gibbs态至任意逆多项式迹距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型是全对全无序相互作用的强关联费米子系统，其动力学难以用Lieb-Robinson界和团簇展开分析；此前关于SYK热化的非严格计算仅局限于局域关联函数。

Method: 引入针对费米子的伪Lindblad算子（pseudo-Lindbladian）方法，并采用基于与经典动力学类比的简化SYK分析。

Result: 在对无序取高概率意义下，SYK模型在足够高的常数温度下具有与系统规模无关谱隙的量子Gibbs采样器，可在量子计算机上多项式时间制备其Gibbs态，且精度达到任意逆多项式迹距离。

Conclusion: 该工作为SYK热化提供了严格依据，突破了此前仅限局域关联函数的局限，并建立了费米子体系中量子Gibbs采样的新分析框架。

Abstract: The Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model is a strongly interacting fermionic system. Its dynamics are difficult to analyze with Lieb-Robinson bounds and cluster expansions due to its all-to-all disordered interactions. We prove that, with high probability over the disorder, the SYK model admits a quantum Gibbs sampler with a system-size-independent spectral gap at sufficiently high constant temperatures. This yields polynomial-time preparation of SYK Gibbs states on a quantum computer. While prior non-rigorous computations that suggested SYK thermalization were limited to studying local correlators, we emphasize that our result holds up to arbitrary inverse polynomial trace distance. Our proof introduces the pseudo-Lindbladian approach to fermions and uses an especially simple SYK analysis based on a comparison to classical dynamics.

</details>


### [177] [Joint GKP Encoding for GHZ States Transmission over Bosonic Quantum MIMO Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05992)
*Seid Koudia,Selssabil Kadid,Symeon Chatzinotas,Mérouane Debbah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限能量GKP编码下GHZ态在非幺正QuMIMO信道中的传输，采用联合晶格整形与经典伴随式译码的收发机方案，并比较三种CSI条件下的复Givens晶格优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模玻色信道存在相干混模、模式相关衰减与高斯噪声，传统乘积式GKP处理难以适配各向异性噪声；作者希望揭示奇异帧几何（而非仅衰减信息）在联合GKP处理中的关键作用。

Method: 建立耦合模模型描述相干混模、模式相关衰减与高斯噪声；收发机由可编程无源网格、量子极限MMSE增益、本地GKP恢复及联合经典伴随式译码组成；基于瞬时CSI、当前奇异值加信道统计、以及仅统计信息三种条件，优化复Givens晶格。

Result: 仿真表明联合晶格整形优于乘积式GKP处理。在相干基漂移下，完整瞬时CSI能更好地保持GHZ态；而奇异值条件化设计与纯统计设计表现相近，原因在于二者均无法解析当前奇异帧。

Conclusion: 奇异帧几何是超越衰减知识的关键资源，联合GKP处理通过使逻辑晶格与接收机适配各向异性玻色噪声，而非仅反转多模混模，从而实现更优的GHZ态保持。

Abstract: We study transmission of a Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) state over nonunitary quantum multiple-input multiple-output (QuMIMO) channels using finite-energy Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) encoding. A coupled-mode model describes coherent mixing, mode-dependent attenuation, and Gaussian noise. The transceiver combines programmable passive meshes, quantum-limited MMSE gain, local GKP recovery, and joint classical syndrome decoding. We optimize complex-Givens lattices using instantaneous channel-state information (CSI), current singular values with channel statistics, or statistics alone. Simulations show that joint lattice shaping outperforms product GKP processing. Under coherent-basis drift, full instantaneous CSI improves GHZ-state preservation, whereas singular-value conditioned and fully statistical designs perform similarly because neither resolves the current singular frames. These findings identify singular-frame geometry as a critical resource beyond attenuation knowledge where joint GKP processing adapts the logical lattice and receiver to anisotropic bosonic noise rather than merely inverting multimode mixing.

</details>


### [178] [Free fermion models with an operator entanglement growing as $O(\sqrt{t})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05995)
*Federico Tonetto,Jérôme Dubail*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究含随机性的一维守恒费米子系统中算子空间纠缠熵（OSEE）的时间演化，发现随机性使局域算子的OSEE从对数增长变为 $\mathcal O(\sqrt t)$ 增长，并揭示了不同随机类型下的普适行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知无随机性的自由费米子系统中，局域算子的OSEE至多对数增长。作者希望探究引入随机性（时空依赖随机演化）后，OSEE的增长行为如何改变，以及演化算子自身的OSEE是否也受随机性影响。

Method: 研究两类模型：布朗哈密顿量与砖墙匹配门电路。通过将量子模型映射到对称简单排他过程（SSEP），在自平均假设（经数值验证）下解析计算两个算子的平均熵。还考察了保持空间平移不变的随机时间演化和淬火无序两种其他随机类型。

Result: 含随机性时，含Jordan-Wigner弦的局域自旋算子的OSEE对所有Rényi指数均以 $\mathcal O(\sqrt t)$ 增长；演化算子的OSEE同样以 $\mathcal O(\sqrt t)$ 增长，而无随机性时通常预期为线性增长。保持空间平移不变的随机演化下OSEE也以 $\sqrt{t}$ 增长。淬火无序下，哈密顿演化中局域算子和演化算子的熵以 $\mathcal O(\log\log t)$ 增长，而砖墙匹配门电路中熵在所有时刻保持有界。

Conclusion: 随机性显著改变了算子空间纠缠熵的增长规律：从无随机性时的对数或线性增长转变为 $\sqrt t$ 或 $\log\log t$ 增长，且具体行为依赖于随机类型与模型（哈密顿量或电路）。SSEP映射为解析计算提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We study the time evolution of the Operator Space Entanglement Entropy (OSEE) of one-dimensional number-conserving free fermions subject to a random space- and time-dependent evolution. It is known that, in free-fermion systems without randomness, the OSEE of \emph{local} operators grows at most \emph{logarithmically} in time. Here we show that, in the presence of randomness, the OSEE of local spin operators involving a Jordan-Wigner string grows as $\mathcal O(\sqrt t)$, for all values of the Rényi index. We establish this in two models: a Brownian Hamiltonian and a brick-wall matchgate circuit. We also consider the OSEE of the evolution operator, and we show that it also grows as $\mathcal O(\sqrt{t})$, in contrast with the linear growth generically expected without randomness. We analytically compute the average entropies of the two operators by mapping the quantum models to the symmetric simple exclusion process, relying on an assumption of self-averaging which we support numerically. We also consider two other types of randomness: random evolution in time that keeps spatial translation invariance, and quenched disorder. In the former case, we find that the OSEE also grows as $\sqrt{t}$. In the latter case, we find that the entropy grows as $\mathcal O( \log \log t)$ for Hamiltonian evolution both for local operators with a Jordan-Wigner string and for the evolution operator, whereas it remains bounded at all times in the brick-wall matchgate circuit.

</details>


### [179] [Metastability in Quantum Dynamics for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06000)
*Yuchen Lu,Zherui Chen,Tongyang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以量子朗之万动力学（QLD）为例，研究相干演化与耗散如何共同塑造亚稳态，给出谱分离判据并说明调控耗散结构可加速趋向目标态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优化动力学中，系统可能在中间态附近长时间停留（亚稳态平台），延迟到达全局极小值附近的目标态；需理解相干隧穿与耗散如何共同决定这种亚稳行为。

Method: 以量子朗之万动力学为具体模型，将目标函数编码于势能，井间相干转移与可调耗散相互作用；精确求解双态模型，得到以隧穿频率、耗散强度和阻尼不平衡表示的谱分离判据；并推广到多态模型分析。

Result: 双态模型可精确求解并给出谱分离判据；多态情形下，足够弱的井间隧穿能将慢布居转移与井内快弛豫分离；并建立改变耗散结构以缩短接近指定目标态时间的条件。

Conclusion: 相干演化与耗散的相互作用是量子优化中亚稳态的一般机制；通过设计耗散结构可在保持目标与精度不变、计入控制时间的前提下加速收敛，双井与多井势的数值实验验证了该改进。

Abstract: Metastability separates apparent equilibration from true convergence: a dynamical system can remain near an intermediate state long before reaching its stationary state. In quantum dynamics used for optimization, such a plateau can delay progress toward a target concentrated near the global minimizers of a nonconvex potential. In this paper, we initiate the study of the general mechanisms by which coherent evolution and dissipation shape metastability, using quantum Langevin dynamics (QLD) as a concrete example [CLW+25], where the objective function is encoded in the potential and coherent transfer between wells interacts with tunable dissipation. To quantify this interaction, we solve a two-state model precisely and derive a spectral-separation criterion in terms of tunneling frequency, dissipation strength, and damping imbalance. Extending the analysis to multi-state models, we show that sufficiently weak tunneling between wells can separate slow population transfer from fast relaxation within each well. In addition, we establish conditions under which changing the structure of dissipation shortens the time needed to approach a prescribed target state. Numerical experiments on double-well and multi-well potentials demonstrate these improvements while keeping the target and accuracy fixed and accounting for the time spent on control.

</details>


### [180] [Polynomial neural surrogates for designing photonic quantum experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06032)
*Rohit Chaurasiya,Xuemei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种物理启发的多项式神经代理模型，替代昂贵的量子光学模拟器 PyTheus，实现量子态预测与逆向实验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PyTheus 等量子光学模拟器计算开销大，反复调用会严重限制对目标量子态实验配置的搜索效率，因此需要高效替代模型。

Method: 构建多项式激活的神经代理模型，其激活函数设计灵感来自图完美匹配与量子态振幅之间的关系；分别针对四、六、八光子系统训练独立代理模型，并与标准多层感知机（MLP）对比。

Result: 代理模型在预测精度上优于标准 MLP，且可训练参数更少；用于 GHZ、W 与线性簇态的逆向设计时，较大系统上比直接用 PyTheus 优化更快。

Conclusion: 将底层物理机制融入神经代理模型，可为量子实验设计提供高效途径。

Abstract: Physics simulators can support the discovery of quantum experiments by predicting the states generated by experimental configurations. When these simulators are computationally expensive, repeated simulator calls can limit the search for experiments that generate a desired quantum state. Here, we develop a physics-inspired polynomial neural surrogate for PyTheus, a graph-based quantum-optics simulator, to predict quantum states and use it to design quantum experiments. Its polynomial activations are motivated by the relation between graph perfect matchings and the resulting state amplitudes. We train separate surrogate models for four-, six-, and eight-photon systems and show that they achieve higher prediction accuracy with fewer trainable parameters than standard multilayer perceptrons. We then use the trained surrogates for inverse design of GHZ, W, and linear-cluster states. For the larger systems, the surrogates also enable faster inverse design than direct optimization with PyTheus. These results suggest that incorporating the underlying physics into neural surrogates can provide an efficient approach to quantum experiment design.

</details>


### [181] [Materialised symmetries of 2D translationally invariant codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06036)
*Andrew Li,Stephen D. Bartlett,Andrew C. Doherty,Campbell K. McLauchlan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维平移不变（TI）CSS码的对称性结构，将无限晶格上的对称空间分解为平移不变子空间，给出平面波形式的显式基，并说明如何通过限制到与周期边界兼容的基元来获得矩形周期晶格上的对称基，进而用于匹配类解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期qLDPC码（如二元循环码）作为表面码与颜色码的高性能替代方案受到关注。其核心对象"物化对称性"可用于构造基于匹配的解码器，这类解码器推广了环面码的最小权完美匹配解码器并具渐近性能保证。然而，对称性的数学结构、其在平移下以及在有限尺寸晶格限制下的性质尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者将无限晶格上二维CSS TI码的对称空间分解为平移不变子空间，从而以类平面波形式写出对称性的显式基；随后说明如何调整该无限晶格基，使得任意对应矩形周期晶格的基可通过限制到与周期边界兼容的基元而得到；并分析对称空间随矩形尺寸的变化。

Result: 给出了无限晶格对称空间的平移不变子空间分解与平面波式显式基；建立了矩形周期晶格对称基的限制构造方法；阐明了对称空间随矩形维度的变化规律；并通过实例表明，在扭曲边界上往往无需重新计算分解即可直接确定对称基。

Conclusion: 该框架为二维TI qLDPC码的对称性提供了系统描述，并适用于匹配类解码器；文中以gross码等为例展示了对称性的应用。

Abstract: There has been significant recent interest in near-term qLDPC codes as high-performance alternatives to surface and color codes. One such class of codes is 2-dimensional translationally invariant (TI) codes, such as bivariate bicycle codes, which share similar properties to topological codes. Fundamental objects in the study of such codes are the materialised symmetries, which can be used for the construction of matching-based decoders. These decoders generalise the minimum-weight perfect matching decoder for toric codes and provide similar asymptotic performance guarantees that heuristic decoders such as BP and its variants lack. Despite this, the mathematical structure of symmetries along with their properties under translation and restriction to finite-sized lattices has not been well-studied. We describe a decomposition of symmetry spaces of 2D CSS TI codes on infinite lattices into translation-invariant subspaces which allow us to write an explicit basis of symmetries in a plane-wave-like form. We then describe how to adapt this infinite lattice basis so that any corresponding rectangular periodic lattice basis can be obtained by restriction to basis elements compatible with the periodic boundaries. This allows us to describe how the symmetry space varies with different rectangular dimensions. We illustrate via examples that it is often straightforward in practice to determine a basis of symmetries on twisted boundaries as well, without needing to recompute the decomposition. We comment on the applications of this framework to matching-based decoders and provide examples of symmetries of such codes as the gross code.

</details>


### [182] [Comparing thresholds of XZZX and rotated surface codes under spatio-temporal correlated noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06059)
*Dhruv Koul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估了XZZX码与旋转表面码在相关及时空复杂噪声模型下的性能，采用MWPF解码器直接处理综合征超图，揭示了偏置定制码对时空相关噪声几何结构的高度敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算研究通常假设独立同分布噪声，而相关噪声与时空噪声等复杂错误模型研究不足；标准基于图的解码器难以处理偏置定制码中相关错误产生的超边。

Method: 采用原生处理综合征超图、无需人工边分解的最小权重奇偶因子（MWPF）解码器，在强Z偏置电路级相关噪声下对XZZX码与旋转表面码进行仿真，并扫描不同偏置比。

Result: 在强Z偏置电路级相关噪声（η=100）下，旋转码阈值降至约0.36%，XZZX码降至约0.70%；XZZX码在不同偏置比下呈现非单调阈值依赖，在中等偏置（η≈10）处出现阈值极小值。

Conclusion: XZZX码在极端偏置下能有效吸收高密度Z型超边，但中等偏置时横向X、Y错误桥接对角串、破坏其一维解耦对称性，导致阈值下降；使用超图感知解码器至关重要，偏置定制码性能对时空相关噪声的几何结构高度敏感。

Abstract: While independent and identically distributed noise is typically assumed in quantum computing research, complex error models such as correlated and spatio-temporal errors remain underexplored. In this paper, the performance of $\mathrm{XZZX}$ and rotated surface codes is evaluated under these complex noise models. Standard graph-based decoders struggle with the hyperedges generated by correlated errors on bias-tailored codes. To address this, we employ the Minimum Weight Parity Factor (MWPF) decoder, which natively processes syndrome hypergraphs without artificial edge decomposition. Our simulations show that under strong Z-biased circuit-level correlated noise ($η=100$), the threshold of the rotated code degrades to $\approx 0.36\%$, while the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code drops to $\approx 0.70\%$. Furthermore, a distinct non-monotonic threshold dependence is observed for the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code across different bias ratios. While the $\mathrm{XZZX}$ code effectively absorbs high-density Z-type hyperedges at extreme biases, it exhibits a threshold minimum at moderate biases ($η\approx 10$) because transverse X and Y errors actively bridge the diagonal strings, breaking the code's one-dimensional decoupling symmetry. These findings demonstrate that utilizing hypergraph-aware decoders is critical, as the performance of bias-tailored codes is highly sensitive to the geometric structure of spatio-temporal correlated noise.

</details>


### [183] [Quantum data loading from the learned shared structure of real signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06076)
*Pablo Herrero Gómez,Antonio Jimeno Morenilla,David Muñoz-Hernández,Higinio Mora Mora*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种"量子原生"数据加载器：一次性学习整个数据集的低维表示，再用固定电路以少量数值为每个信号制备量子态，从而大幅降低量子态制备成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经典数据制备量子态的开销可能超过其后续计算本身，而现有加载器大多为每个输入单独定制电路，导致参数与门成本随信号数量增长。作者希望利用真实数据集信号间共享的结构，实现"一次学习、重复使用"的高效加载。

Method: 采用量子原生加载器，学习数据集的低维描述（latent description），据此生成每个信号的少量数值参数，并由一个固定的量子电路完成所有信号的态制备；在五个公开数据集的七种视角上，与最强结构化加载器在相同门成本下比较每信号所需数值数量，并进行预注册盲复现实验，考察达到全信号准确率90%所需随机子集规模随信号规模（扩大16倍）的变化。

Result: 在相同门成本下，该方法达到最强结构化加载器的精度目标，而每信号所需数值数量少数倍；其参数可由随机子集推断，盲复现中达到全信号准确率10%以内所需子集规模随信号扩大16倍保持恒定（在预设边界内），而结构化加载器所需子集持续增大。该方法会拒绝其无法表示的信号，因此覆盖的案例少于该基线，且不涵盖心电图信号。

Conclusion: 数据集的共享结构可被一次学习并复用，使固定电路的量子态加载在保持精度的同时显著减少每信号参数，并具备良好的可扩展性与可复现性；代价是对不可表示信号采取拒绝策略，适用覆盖范围有所缩减。

Abstract: Preparing quantum states from classical data can cost more than the computation they serve; most loaders tailor a circuit to each input. Here we show that the signals of a real dataset share structure that can be learned once and reused. Our quantum-native loader learns a low-dimensional description of a dataset and prepares every signal with one fixed circuit set by a few numbers. Across seven views of five public datasets it meets the targets of the strongest structured loader at equal gate cost with several times fewer numbers per signal. These numbers can be inferred from a random subset: in a preregistered blind replication the subset needed to come within ten per cent of full-signal accuracy stayed constant within a prespecified margin as signals grew sixteenfold, whereas the structured loader needed ever more. It declines what it cannot represent, covering fewer cases than that baseline and no electrocardiogram.

</details>


### [184] [Gaussian Universality and Its Breakdown in Tensor-Network Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06080)
*Shi-Tuan Wang,Zidu Liu,Li-Wei Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文解析张量网络学习模型（以矩阵乘积态为核心）中高斯过程普适性的成立与失效条件，指出其不仅依赖参数量，还受架构尺度、可观测量局域性与谱性质控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程极限在描述过参数化机器学习模型方面十分有效，但其在结构化张量网络架构中的有效性尚不明确，因此需要明确其成立与失效的精确条件。

Method: 提出一种基于矩（moment-based）的解析方法，针对矩阵乘积态学习模型，分析局部与全局可观测量在不同极限下的高阶矩偏差，并给出有限尺寸界。

Result: 在大键维极限下，无论局部还是全局可观测量，学习模型均收敛为高斯过程，且具有高阶矩偏差的显式有限尺寸界；在大物理维极限下，高斯普适性不再成立：局域可观测量模型仍保持高斯过程行为，而全局情形则出现持续的非高斯修正。

Conclusion: 张量网络学习中的高斯过程行为不仅由参数数量决定，还受架构缩放方式、可观测量的局域性以及谱性质共同控制。

Abstract: Gaussian-process limits are powerful in describing overparameterized machine learning models, yet their validity in structured tensor-network architectures remains unclear. Here we analytically present a moment-based approach that identifies precise conditions for the emergence and breakdown of Gaussian universality in tensor-network learning models, with a focus on matrix product states. We prove that in the large bond dimension limit, the learning models with both local and global observables converge to Gaussian processes, with explicit finite-size bounds on higher-order moment deviations. Whereas in the large physical dimension limit, the Gaussian universality no longer persists: while the models with local observables retain Gaussian-process behavior, those global cases exhibit persistent non-Gaussian corrections. Our results reveal that Gaussian-process behavior in tensor-network learning is controlled not only by parameter number, but also by architectural scaling, observable locality, and the spectral properties.

</details>


### [185] [Collective Quantum State Preparation Is Nonadditive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06113)
*Lin Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang,Shenggen Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究加权Pauli控制下的多拷贝量子态制备问题，推导出精确的单量子比特制备代价，并证明其严格非可加性：四脉冲集体协议制备两个T态的代价低于两个最优单拷贝协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备是量子计算与控制的基本操作，但其资源代价通常只针对单一目标态或在独立拷贝构造下进行分析。本文旨在研究多拷贝情形下加权Pauli控制（Pauli积旋转e^{iφP}按旋转角|φ|计费，与P的权重无关）下的态制备代价，探索集体控制能否带来超越独立拷贝的优势。

Method: 在加权Pauli控制模型下，将Pauli积旋转e^{iφP}的代价定义为其旋转角|φ|，与Pauli积P的权重无关。推导单量子比特态制备的精确代价，并构造四脉冲集体协议制备两个T态。进一步刻画Bloch球面上具有集体节省效应的有限区域，包括始终保持为乘积态的轨迹。同时通过维度无关的Rényi熵速度极限建立下界。

Result: 单量子比特制备代价被精确求出，并证明严格非可加：四脉冲集体协议制备两个T态的代价低于两个最优单拷贝协议。识别出Bloch球面上具有集体节省的有限区域，其中包含始终保持乘积态的轨迹，表明瞬态纠缠并非该优势的唯一机制。Rényi熵速度极限给出对每一致相干目标的广延下界，以及在有限制备速率以下的指数保真度惩罚。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了态合成的多拷贝几何：即使输出之间不相关，也可从集体控制中获益。加权Pauli控制下的多拷贝态制备具有非可加性，集体协议能够突破单拷贝最优方案的限制。

Abstract: Quantum state preparation is a basic primitive of quantum computation and control, but its resource cost is usually analyzed for one target state or under an independent-copy construction. Here we study the many-copy problem under weighted Pauli control, where a Pauli-product rotation $e^{iφP}$ is charged by its rotation angle $|φ|$, independently of the weight of $P$. We derive the exact single-qubit preparation cost and show that it is strictly nonadditive: a four-pulse collective protocol prepares two $T$ states at lower cost than two optimal one-copy protocols. We further identify finite regions of the Bloch sphere with collective saving, including trajectories that remain product throughout, showing that transient entanglement is not the only mechanism behind the advantage. Complementarily, a dimension-independent Rényi-entropy speed limit gives an extensive lower bound for every coherent target and an exponential fidelity penalty below a finite preparation rate. These results establish a many-copy geometry of state synthesis in which uncorrelated outputs can nevertheless benefit from collective control.

</details>


### [186] [Geometric Measure of Entanglement of Rank-2 Mixed Multiqubit States and its Estimation on a Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06127)
*N. A. Susulovska,Kh. P. Gnatenko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 基于几何纠缠度与自旋关联的关系，推导了二维Hilbert子空间中rank-2混合态纠缠度的解析表达式，并通过量子计算（AerSimulator与IBM量子计算机）构造协议进行检测与误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究混合态几何纠缠度通常缺乏简洁解析表达，且实验检测困难；本文借助几何纠缠度与自旋关联的关系，以简化多量子比特rank-2混合态纠缠的刻画与测量。

Method: 利用几何纠缠度与自旋关联的关联，推导二维Hilbert子空间中由任意多量子比特纯态构成的rank-2混合态纠缠度解析表达式；分析其对混合纯态参数的依赖；针对任意多量子比特态与薛定谔猫态的等概率混合类，构造基于自旋关联的几何纠缠度检测量子协议；在AerSimulator和ibm_marrakesh量子计算机上量化纠缠并分析量子计算误差。

Result: 仿真结果重现了解析依赖关系；量子设备测量结果在估计误差范围内与理论预测吻合良好；同时给出了量子计算误差的影响分析。

Conclusion: 几何纠缠度与自旋关联的关系可有效用于rank-2多量子比特混合态纠缠的解析刻画与量子协议检测，且在当前含噪量子硬件上具有可行性。

Abstract: The geometric measure of entanglement of mixed states is investigated based on its relation to spin correlations. We derive an analytical expression for the entanglement of rank-2 mixed states composed of arbitrary multiqubit pure states within a two-dimensional Hilbert subspace. The dependence of the entanglement of rank-2 mixed multiqubit states on the parameters characterizing arbitrary pure states in the mixture is studied both analytically and using quantum computing. For a particular class of mixed states, namely, equal-probability mixtures of an arbitrary multiqubit state and the Schrödinger cat state, we construct quantum protocols for detecting the geometric measure of entanglement based on its relation to spin correlations. We quantify the entanglement using both the AerSimulator and the ibm_marrakesh quantum computer. The effects of quantum-computing errors are also analyzed. The simulation results reproduce the analytical dependencies, while measurements on the quantum device demonstrate good agreement with theoretical predictions within estimated error bounds.

</details>


### [187] [When Equivalent Quantum Circuits Lose Synthesis Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06142)
*Boshuai Ye,Peng Liang,Arif Ali Khan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"综合可用性"（synthesis availability）概念，系统研究量子编译器在序列化、编译器中转换与OpenQASM交换过程中高层操作信息的丢失问题，并提出Verified机制进行恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子编译需将QFT、多控X门等高层操作综合为门级电路，但在序列化、OpenQASM交换、编译器转换等表示变化中，高层操作可能被消除，导致接收方编译器无法应用所请求的综合方法。仅保持功能等价并不足以保证后续综合选择所需的高层结构。

Method: 通过控制实验、代码仓库分析、项目测试提取的电路以及Munich Quantum Toolkit（MQT）流水线，在Qiskit、TKET、Cirq中研究综合可用性；并提出Verified机制：在表示变化前记录高层操作，仅当校验确认记录与交付电路仍匹配时才重建这些操作。

Result: 综合可用性在反序列化能恢复高层操作时可在序列化后保留，仅在接收编译器支持该操作时可在编译器转换后保留；在所评估的OpenQASM路径下均不保留。直接OpenQASM 3往返后，全部360个交付条件下所请求综合均失效，尽管编译成功。在MQT流水线中，OpenQASM 2交换使八量子比特Grover电路的双量子比特门数增加37.2%。Verified在全部830次恢复运行中恢复所请求综合，各操作族中位校验时间为2.0至638 ms；校验拒绝所有过期记录，但也拒绝部分有效记录。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当后续综合选择依赖所保留的高层结构时，仅保持功能等价是不充分的。

Abstract: Quantum compilers synthesize high-level operations, such as the quantum Fourier transform and multi-controlled X gates, into gate-level circuits. Across compilation stages, a circuit may be serialized, exchanged as OpenQASM, converted between compilers, or lowered to gates. These representation changes can preserve computation while removing the high-level operation itself, leaving the receiving compiler unable to apply a requested synthesis method. We call this loss synthesis availability. We study synthesis availability in Qiskit, TKET, and Cirq through controlled experiments, repository analysis, circuits captured from project tests, and a Munich Quantum Toolkit pipeline. Synthesis availability survives serialization when deserialization restores the high-level operation and survives compiler conversion only when the receiving compiler supports that operation. It does not survive the evaluated OpenQASM routes. After a direct OpenQASM 3 round trip, requested synthesis has no effect in all 360 delivered conditions even though compilation succeeds. In the MQT pipeline, OpenQASM 2 interchange increases the two-qubit gate count by 37.2 percent for an eight-qubit Grover circuit. We also introduce Verified, which records high-level operations before a representation change and reconstructs them only when a check confirms that the record still matches the delivered circuit. Verified restores requested synthesis in all 830 recovery runs, with median verification times from 2.0 to 638 ms per operation family. The check rejects every stale record but also some valid records. These results show that preserving functional equivalence alone is insufficient when later synthesis choices depend on retained high-level structure.

</details>


### [188] [What Does It Cost to Simulate a Quantum Sentence Classifier? An Energy and Compute Perspective on Near-Term QNLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06176)
*Kishlay Kashyap,Sandipan Ganguly*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在SST-2二分类上系统测量VQC的模拟器计算成本：27种配置下，VQC仅6/27单种子优于匹配的经典基线，多种子复跑后仅1种保持稳定优势，且训练耗时约为经典拟合的886–21,127倍（中位数3,158倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近邻量子自然语言处理（QNLP）实验多依赖经典模拟器运行，模拟器开销构成该领域实际算力负担，但现有准确率表格未体现这一成本；作者希望以可复现的可行性测量方式，将模拟器代价与准确率一并量化。

Method: 在二分类SST-2情感分类任务上使用变分量子分类器（VQC），基于PennyLane态矢量模拟器，设置27种配置的受控网格：3种平衡训练集规模（N=200、500、1000）×3种量子比特数（4、6、8）×3种电路深度。每个VQC与相同PCA降维输入上的逻辑回归对比；另以全TF-IDF逻辑回归作为未压缩参照。记录墙钟训练时间，并用CodeCarbon估算能耗与CO2。

Result: 在27组单种子对比中，VQC仅在6组超过其匹配基线；在另外两个随机种子复跑后，6组中仅1组保持大于配对种子间波动的正向平均优势，且固定验证集上的配对检验无法确立该优势。VQC训练墙钟时间为匹配经典拟合的886–21,127倍（中位数3,158倍）；量子比特数从4增至8约使模拟器时间翻倍，在所测范围内单步成本可由参数数量的线性函数较好近似。CodeCarbon的能耗与CO2估计为次要信息：其隐含功率近乎恒定（约41 W），相较运行时间增量信息有限，故未据此构建能耗比。

Conclusion: 该研究为范围受限的可行性测量（单一数据集、表示、拟设、模拟器与CPU环境），并非对QNLP的总体判断；其价值在于以可复现方式揭示模拟器成本，提示准确率表应同时报告此类计算开销。

Abstract: Near-term quantum natural language processing (QNLP) experiments often run on classical simulators, so simulator cost is part of the field's practical compute burden, yet accuracy tables do not show it. We measure that cost for a variational quantum classifier (VQC) on binary SST-2 sentiment classification, using PennyLane's state-vector simulator over a controlled grid of 27 configurations: three balanced training-set sizes (N = 200, 500, 1000), three qubit counts (4, 6, 8), and three circuit depths. Each VQC is compared with logistic regression on the same PCA-reduced input; full TF-IDF logistic regression gives an uncompressed reference. The VQC beats its matched baseline in 6 of 27 single-seed comparisons. After reruns at two further seeds, only 1 of these 6 keeps a positive mean advantage larger than its paired seed-to-seed variability, and paired tests on the fixed validation set do not establish it. VQC training is 886-21,127 times slower in measured wall-clock time than the matched classical fit (median 3,158 times); going from 4 to 8 qubits roughly doubles simulator time, and within the tested range per-step cost is well approximated by a linear function of the parameter count. CodeCarbon energy and CO2 estimates are secondary: they imply an almost constant power of about 41 W, so they add little beyond runtime, and we do not build an energy ratio from them. The study is narrow (one dataset, representation, ansatz, simulator, and CPU environment) and is a reproducible feasibility measurement, not a general verdict on QNLP.

</details>


### [189] [The superb supersinglet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06183)
*Gabriele Cobucci,Armin Tavakoli,Alexander Bernal,Shishir Khandelwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高维全反对称态（supersinglets）的纠缠特性，利用凸优化证明其噪声鲁棒性，开发基于集体自旋与随机测量的纠缠见证方法，并将其确立为高维梯度传感的天然资源，实现海森堡标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维多体系统可承载比特系统无法实现的量子纠缠形式，其中全反对称态（supersinglets）具有任意集体局域酉变换下的不变性，但其纠缠性质与量子信息应用价值尚待系统探索。

Method: 采用凸优化方法分析supersinglets的纠缠噪声鲁棒性；构建基于集体自旋测量和随机测量的纠缠见证方法，用于认证纠缠深度与维度；将supersinglets作为高维梯度传感资源进行理论分析。

Result: supersinglets的核心纠缠特性对噪声极为鲁棒，在特定松弛条件下显著超越若干典型纠缠态；所发展的见证方法可同时认证纠缠深度与维度；supersinglets可实现高维梯度传感的海森堡标度。

Conclusion: supersinglets是一类兼具显著对称性与强纠缠的多体纠缠态，具有异常鲁棒和通用的特性，在量子传感方面展现出明确的应用潜力。

Abstract: Multipartite systems of high-dimensional particles can host forms of quantum entanglement inaccessible to qubit systems. A particularly fascinating class is formed by the fully antisymmetric states, also known as supersinglets, which possess a remarkable symmetry: they are invariant under arbitrary collective local unitaries. Here, we explore their entanglement properties and establish their quantum information applications. Using convex optimisation, we show that central entanglement features of supersinglets are remarkably robust to noise, substantially surpassing the robustness of several paradigmatic entangled states under certain relaxations. We then develop witness methods, based on collective-spin or randomised measurements, that certify both the depth and dimensionality of their entanglement. Finally, we identify supersinglets as a natural resource for high-dimensional gradient sensing and prove that they enable Heisenberg scaling. Together, these results reveal supersinglets as an exceptionally robust and versatile class of multipartite entangled states, combining striking symmetry and strong entanglement with concrete potential for quantum sensing.

</details>


### [190] [Improved Adaptive Estimation of Quantum Partition Functions with Heisenberg Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06197)
*Yufei Wang,Daniel Stilck França,Samuel Slezak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出量子算法，估计n量子比特哈密顿量的对数配分函数到加性误差ε并达到海森堡标度，同时最小化热态纯化制备调用次数，最优可达Õ(n/ε)次查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计算子配分函数在统计物理与量子多体问题中具有基础意义，量子算法有望实现超越经典方法的标度优势；但热态纯化制备等原语代价高昂，需要在保持海森堡精度标度的同时显著降低其调用次数。

Method: 输入为H的块编码、制备给定逆温度下热场双态的酉算子及其逆与受控版本，以及自适应缓变冷却调度。假设0⪯H⪯hI，h=O(n)，块编码归一化α=Θ(h)。对常数逆温度，构造长度O(√n)的调度并通过重叠估计生成。核心采用递归倍增恒等式，将每个调度增量表达为热场双态重叠对数与短虚时间步的加权和，后者用量子奇异值变换结合方差缩减技术实现。当热场双态可按温度寄存器受控制备时，采用量子逆二项式对数估计器与量子均值估计进一步降低查询数。

Result: 给定调度与经典选定的温度，估计对数配分函数需Õ(n^{5/4}/ε)次期望态制备与块编码调用，比非自适应策略提升n^{1/4}。若热场双态可受控于温度寄存器制备，两类查询计数均可降至Õ(n/ε)。进一步证明后者在块编码查询数上至多差多对数因子意义下是最优的，并讨论了一维配分函数估计的端到端复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作通过递归倍增恒等式与自适应冷却调度，在保持海森堡标度的同时降低了热态纯化制备开销，并给出了块编码查询下界，为配分函数估计的量子算法提供了接近最优的查询复杂度刻画。

Abstract: We give quantum algorithms that estimate the log partition function of an $n$-qubit quantum Hamiltonian to additive error $ε$ with Heisenberg scaling, while minimizing calls to thermal-state purification preparation. The input is a block encoding of $H$, unitaries preparing thermofield-double states at chosen inverse temperatures, with their inverses and controlled versions, and an adaptive slowly-varying cooling schedule. The system-size bounds below assume $0\preceq H\preceq hI$ with $h=O(n)$ and block-encoding normalization $α=Θ(h)$. For constant inverse temperature, we show that such a schedule of length $O(\sqrt n)$ always exists and can be generated from overlap estimates with $\widetilde O(\sqrt n)$ further preparations.
  Given such a schedule and classically chosen temperatures, we estimate the log partition function with $\widetilde O(n^{5/4}/ε)$ expected calls to state preparation and to the block encoding, improving by $n^{1/4}$ on non-adaptive strategies. To achieve our result, we use a recursive-doubling identity that expresses each schedule increment as a weighted sum of logarithms of thermofield-double overlaps and of short imaginary-time steps implemented by quantum singular value transformation combined with previously developed variance reduction techniques. If thermofield doubles can be prepared controlled on a temperature register, a quantum inverse-binomial log estimator combined with quantum mean estimation reduces both query counts to $\widetilde O(n/ε)$. We show that the latter scaling is optimal in block-encoding queries up to polylogarithmic factors and discuss end-to-end complexities for one-dimensional partition-function estimation.

</details>


### [191] [Casimir force screening by a quantum Boltzmann plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06202)
*Petr Brandyshev,Maksim Koniushenko,Yury Budkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了理想导体板间量子玻尔兹曼等离子体的有限温场论描述，给出卡西米尔力的领头量子修正，并在静态长波极限下与林哈德响应及朗道轨道抗磁性对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典卡西米尔屏蔽图像中，纵向模被德拜屏蔽、横向电磁模不受经典可动电荷影响，量子效应（有限热德布罗意波长）的修正尚缺乏系统场论刻画。

Method: 有限温场论方法：用虚时世界线描述带电粒子量子运动，电磁场取高斯近似，在两块理想导体边界间、均匀介质背景中填充双分量量子玻尔兹曼等离子体，构建配分函数并分离纵、横响应与量子诱导源项。

Result: 配分函数含纵向、横向响应及无经典对应的量子诱导源项；经典极限回到卡西米尔屏蔽图像；有限热德布罗意波长修正非零松原响应并给出力的领头量子修正；静态长波极限下纵向介电函数与简并电子气林哈德响应在 k_TF↔κ_D、k_F↔λ_dB^{-1} 对应下同构，横向响应重现高温朗道轨道抗磁化率领头项。

Conclusion: 量子玻尔兹曼等离子体的卡西米尔相互作用可由世界线—高斯场论统一描述，其量子修正与简并电子气的林哈德响应和朗道抗磁性存在形式对应。

Abstract: We develop a finite-temperature field-theoretical description of the Casimir interaction between two perfectly conducting boundaries with the intervening gap filled by a two-component quantum Boltzmann plasma embedded in a homogeneous dielectric background. The quantum motion of the charged particles is represented by imaginary-time worldlines, while the electromagnetic field is treated in the Gaussian approximation. The resulting partition function contains distinct longitudinal and transverse response contributions and an additional quantum-induced source term with no classical counterpart. In the classical limit the theory reduces to the usual Casimir-screening picture: the static longitudinal mode is Debye screened, whereas the transverse electromagnetic sector is unaffected by classical mobile charges. Finite thermal de Broglie wavelengths modify the nonzero Matsubara response and produce the leading quantum correction to the force. In the static long-wavelength limit, the longitudinal dielectric function has, through the leading quantum correction, the same expansion structure as the Lindhard response of a degenerate electron gas under the correspondence $k_{TF}\leftrightarrowκ_D$ and $k_F\leftrightarrowλ_{dB}^{-1}$, while the static transverse response reproduces the leading high-temperature Landau orbital diamagnetic susceptibility.

</details>


### [192] [Unitary RQL Equals RQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06231)
*Quinten Tupker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在标准门集Γ={H,T,CNOT}下，单边有界误差可消除中间测量，即RQLΓ=RQULΓ，同时保持多项式时间、对数空间与no-instance上零接受率；并推广至含精确逆的更广有限门集，建立特定代数门集的独立性，另证QMAL1,G=QUMAL1,G。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间测量可使量子计算丢弃信息并复用工作空间，但若推迟全部测量则需存储整个测量历史，难以保持对数空间。Fefferman与Remscrim已证明双边有界误差下可消除测量，并公开询问单边误差是否同样成立[FR21]。

Method: 将Girish、Raz与Zhan的密度矩阵加倍方法[GRZ21]推广至一般信道，涵盖重置操作与经典存储；针对精确逆的有限门集（含带有超越元的合适门）证明测量消除；对指定的代数门集族建立门集独立性；另用独立的历史检查构造证明QMAL1,G=QUMAL1,G。

Result: 在Γ={H,T,CNOT}下得到RQLΓ=RQULΓ，保持多项式时间、对数空间及no-instance精确零接受；测量消除推广至更广含精确逆的有限门集；得到指定代数门集族的门集独立性，以及QMAL1,G=QUMAL1,G并保持完美完备性。

Conclusion: 正面回答了FR21关于单边有界误差下测量消除的问题，将密度矩阵加倍技术从特定门集拓展至一般信道与更广门集，并表明对数空间量子验证中的测量亦可被消除且保持完美完备性。

Abstract: Intermediate measurements let quantum computations discard information and reuse their workspace. Deferring all measurements can require storing their entire history, which need not preserve logarithmic space. Fefferman and Remscrim proved that measurements can nevertheless be eliminated with two-sided bounded error, and asked whether the same holds with one-sided error [FR21]. We prove RQLΓ = RQULΓ for the standard gate set Γ = {H, T, CNOT}, preserving polynomial time, logarithmic space, and exactly zero acceptance on no-instances. Our proof extends the density-matrix doubling method of Girish, Raz and Zhan [ GRZ21 ] to general channels, including resets and classical memory. We also prove measurement elimination for broader finite gate sets with exact inverses, including suitable gates with transcendental entries, and establish gate-set independence for a specified family of algebraic gate sets. A separate history-checking construction proves QMAL1,G = QUMAL1,G under explicit gate assumptions: measurements can also be eliminated from logarithmic-space quantum verification while preserving perfect completeness

</details>


### [193] [Probing Quantum Anomalous Hall Transport Under Microwave Irradiation Using a Topological Circulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06245)
*Athul Ashok,Frank Jin,Nick Du,Luis A. Martinez,Jenny Zhou,Sean O'Kelley,Zachary J. -R. Espley,Gang Qiu,Kang L. Wang,Dong-Xia Qu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用量子反常霍尔拓扑环行器研究微波光子对边缘磁等离激元输运的扰动，发现频率选择性响应，并借助非厄米奇异点增强微波探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘磁等离激元（EMP）可用于探测拓扑量子材料中的手性电荷动力学与非互易微波输运，但其传播的小扰动在常规微波散射测量中信号微弱，检测困难。

Method: 采用量子反常霍尔拓扑环行器进行泵浦-探测微波测量，研究微波辐照对EMP透射的扰动；并利用耦合EMP-谐振器系统的非厄米模式杂化，在奇异点（EP）附近探测响应。

Result: 观察到强频率选择性响应：若干泵浦频率下EMP透射被显著改变，而在包括4 GHz与7 GHz在内的特定频率几乎不变；在EP附近，泵浦引起的透射幅度变化约为远离EP时的两倍，表明扰动响应被增强。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了手性EMP输运、微波光子诱导扰动与非厄米动力学之间的相互作用，并表明拓扑环行器可作为增强微波光谱与传感的潜在平台。

Abstract: Edge magnetoplasmons (EMPs) provide a platform for probing chiral charge dynamics and nonreciprocal microwave transport in topological quantum materials. However, detecting small perturbations to EMP propagation remains challenging because their signatures in conventional microwave scattering measurements can be weak. Here, we investigate microwave-photon-induced perturbations of EMP transport using a quantum anomalous Hall topological circulator. Pump--probe measurements reveal a strongly frequency-selective response: while microwave irradiation substantially modifies the EMP transmission at several pump frequencies, the transmission remains nearly unchanged at selected frequencies, including 4 and 7 GHz. We further exploit the non-Hermitian mode hybridization of the coupled EMP-resonator system to probe the response near an exceptional point (EP). The pump-induced change in transmission magnitude near the EP is approximately twice that observed away from the EP, demonstrating an enhanced microwave response to perturbations. These results reveal the interplay among chiral EMP transport, microwave-photon-induced perturbations, and non-Hermitian dynamics, and demonstrate the potential of topological circulators as a promising platform for enhanced microwave spectroscopy and sensing.

</details>


### [194] [High-Fidelity Remote Graph State Preparation for Blind Quantum Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06247)
*Jiawei Cai,Rex Fleur,Benedikt Tissot,Wolfgang Löffler,Tzula B. Propp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出远程图态制备（RGSP）框架，利用单个高维光子qudit直接制备任意图态，减少或消除服务器端纠缠门需求，实现拓扑无关的保真度和更高效的盲量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量型量子计算（MBQC）依赖纠缠图态，但现有远程态制备（RSP）协议只能制备可分离态，需在远程服务器上追加纠缠门操作，导致量子比特开销大、服务器端操作复杂。因此需要一种能直接从客户端资源远程制备纠缠图态、减少服务器端纠缠操作的方法。

Method: 提出RGSP框架，将多个量子比特及其图连接关系编码到单光子qudit的结构化相位分布中，直接从单个高维光子qudit制备任意图态。针对累积光纤相位漂移，采用"最高权重优先"模式重排序策略提升保真度；并基于RGSP实现任意拓扑上减少SWAP的通用盲量子计算。

Result: 在合理噪声模型下，所得态保真度与图拓扑无关；在累积光纤相位漂移下，"最高权重优先"模式重排序策略显著提升保真度；RGSP支持任意拓扑上减少SWAP的通用盲量子计算，例如六比特量子傅里叶变换的RSP比特开销从标准砖墙拓扑的420个降至48个。

Conclusion: RGSP是一种资源高效、拓扑无关的原语，为量子安全云计算提供新方案。

Abstract: Measurement-based quantum computation (MBQC) relies on entangled graph states, yet existing remote state preparation (RSP) protocols prepare only separable states, requiring subsequent entangling gates on the remote server. Here, we introduce Remote Graph State Preparation (RGSP), a framework that prepares arbitrary graph states directly from a single high-dimensional photonic qudit. By encoding multiple qubits and their graph connectivity into the photon's structured phase profile, RGSP can reduce or, for small computations, completely eliminate the need for server-side entangling operations among qubits.
