We show that under a reasonable noise model the resulting state fidelity is independent of the graph topology. Under cumulative fiber phase drift, we demonstrate that this fidelity is significantly enhanced by a ``highest-weight-first'' mode reordering strategy. Finally, we show that RGSP enables reduced-SWAP universal blind quantum computing on arbitrary topologies, reducing qubit overhead e.g., for a six-qubit Quantum Fourier Transform from $420$ RSP qubits with a standard brickwork topology to $48$ RSP qubits. These results establish RGSP as a resource-efficient, topology-invariant primitive for quantum-secured cloud computing.

</details>


### [195] [Suppressing Frequency Collisions in a Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06264)
*Kuljeet Kaur,Xuexin Xu,Mohammad H. Ansari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将量子比特频率分配中的双体/三体碰撞机制映射为 Max-k-Cut 问题，提出 cluster-stitching 分解协议以处理大规模格点，并在此框架下基准测试 DC-QAOA、标准 QAOA 与模拟退火。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子处理器的频率拥挤（量子比特跃迁间距过近）会通过串扰、泄漏和 spectator 依赖误差限制性能，因此需要硬件感知的频率规划来抑制主导性碰撞惩罚。

Method: 提出基于哈密顿量的框架，将双体与三体碰撞机制映射为量子比特频率分配上的 Max-k-Cut 问题；为处理更大格点，引入 cluster-stitching 协议，将大格点分解为重叠子图并将局部频率分配协调为全局一致配置；在多种量子比特架构上测试，并对比 digitized counterdiabatic QAOA (DC-QAOA)、标准 QAOA 与模拟退火。

Result: 该框架在多种架构上抑制了主导性碰撞惩罚，并揭示了硬件残余串扰；在陡峭碰撞惩罚附近，counterdiabatic 项可改善 QAOA 优化，而在所测试实例上模拟退火取得最低代价。

Conclusion: 该硬件感知优化将微观相互作用物理与处理器级频率规划相连接，为可扩展的软硬件协同设计基准测试提供了系统化求解器。

Abstract: Frequency crowding, the close spacing of qubit transitions, limits superconducting quantum processors through crosstalk, leakage, and spectator dependent errors. We introduce a Hamiltonian based framework that maps two- and three-body collision mechanisms onto a Max-k-Cut problem on qubit frequency allocation. In order to make larger lattices tractable, we introduce a cluster-stitching protocol that decomposes larger lattices into overlapping subgraphs and reconciles their local frequency assignments into globally consistent configurations. We tested our proposed framework across multiple qubit architectures to suppress dominant collision penalties by revealing a hardware residual crosstalk. We benchmark digitized counterdiabatic QAOA (DC-QAOA), standard QAOA, and simulated annealing. Counterdiabatic terms improve QAOA optimization near steep collision penalties, while simulated annealing achieves the lowest costs on the tested instances. These results indicate that our hardware-aware optimization connects microscopic interaction physics to processor-level frequency planning. These results enable a systematic solver for benchmarking of scalable software hardware co design.

</details>


### [196] [Adiabatic tracking and optimal schedules for classical and quantum Gibbs-state preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06268)
*Pedro C. S. Costa,Mauro E. S. Morales,Dong An,Kaur Kristjuhan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为经典与量子优化中的Gibbs态制备建立离散绝热框架，推导有限时间非齐次可逆Markov链的追踪定理，给出显式步复杂度界及自适应调度，并借助温度依赖Szegedy量子游走证明量子绝热代价可实现关于谱隙的二次加速（O(δ_min^{-1/2}) vs 经典O(δ_min^{-1})）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态制备是经典与量子优化算法的核心子问题。现有绝热/退火方法多依赖连续时间近似，缺乏对有限时间、离散、非齐次Markov动力学的严格误差控制；同时量子绝热加速中谱隙放大如何转化为实际调度与复杂度优势尚不清晰。作者旨在建立一个统一、离散且严格的绝热框架来刻画上述问题。

Method: 1) 对有限时间非齐次可逆Markov链推导追踪定理，将演化分布到瞬时稳态的距离分解为两部分：既有误差经Markov动力学的收缩，以及稳态逐步变化产生的新误差。2) 对Gibbs路径给出显式步复杂度界，并构造由Markov链谱隙δ与能量涨落尺度V(β)=sqrt(Var_{π_β}(E))控制的自适应调度；弱阻尼下调度形式为dotβ ∝ δ(β)/V(β)。3) 将离散量子绝热定理应用于温度依赖的Szegedy量子游走，分析其本征相位隙Δ_w=Θ(sqrt δ)以及进入定理的有限差分项，研究谱隙放大向复杂度优势的转化条件。

Result: 追踪定理给出分布的瞬时偏差界并导出显式步复杂度与谱隙/涨落自适应调度。量子游走本征相位隙满足Δ_w=Θ(sqrt δ)。分析表明该平方根谱放大仅在调度恰当地适配谱隙时，才能转化为关于谱隙的二次改善；在相应正则条件下，量子绝热代价为O(δ_min^{-1/2})，而经典松弛尺度为O(δ_min^{-1})。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Gibbs态制备的离散绝热统一框架：经典侧得到严格的追踪误差界与自适应调度，量子侧通过Szegedy游走的平方根谱放大，在合适调度与正则条件下实现关于最小谱隙的二次加速。加速并非自动成立，而依赖于谱隙自适应调度这一关键条件。

Abstract: We develop a discrete adiabatic framework for Gibbs-state preparation in classical and quantum optimisation algorithms. For finite time-inhomogeneous reversible Markov chains, we derive a tracking theorem that bounds the distance from the evolving distribution to the instantaneous stationary state by combining two effects: contraction of previously accumulated error by the Markov dynamics and the new error generated as the stationary distribution changes from one step to the next. For Gibbs paths, this leads to explicit step-complexity bounds and to gap- and fluctuation-adapted schedules controlled by the Markov-chain spectral gap and the energy-fluctuation scale $V(β)=\sqrt{\operatorname{Var}_{π_β}(E)}$. In the weak-damping regime, the resulting schedule takes the form $\dotβ\proptoδ(β)/V(β)$. We then apply the discrete quantum adiabatic theorem to temperature-dependent Szegedy quantum walks. The quantum-walk eigenphase gap satisfies $Δ_w=Θ(\sqrtδ)$, where $δ$ is the spectral gap of the underlying Markov chain. By analysing the finite-difference terms entering the theorem, we show that this square-root spectral amplification translates into a quadratic improvement in the gap dependence only for suitably gap-adapted schedules. Under the corresponding regularity conditions, the quantum adiabatic cost scales as $O(δ_{\min}^{-1/2})$, compared with the classical $O(δ_{\min}^{-1})$ relaxation scale.

</details>


### [197] [Two-Sided Bounds on Ground State Properties in Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06272)
*Eloïc Vallée,Ariel Kelman,Julius Mildenberger,Jordi Tura,Patrick Emonts*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于半定规划（SDP）层级的方法，为晶格规范理论的基态能量提供越来越紧的下界，并将该界传播到Wilson圈、介子弦等可观测量的严格界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格规范理论常通过蒙特卡洛或变分法求解，但蒙特卡洛受符号问题限制，变分法（量子和经典）只能给出基态能量的上界，且精度依赖所选拟设。

Method: 构建半定规划层级框架以生成逐步改进的基态能量下界，并将这些界限推广至Wilson圈和介子弦等扩展可观测量。

Result: 在含动态费米子物质的(1+1)维Z2规范理论及(2+1)维纯规范版本上验证：(1+1)维97... 一维129格点系统认证区间相对宽度0.04%；二维97格点（7×8晶格）相对宽度2.08%；并对多格方Wilson圈与介子弦幅度给出严格界限。

Conclusion: 该方法既可作为多体物理的计算显微镜，也可用于认证量子模拟器的结果。

Abstract: Gauge theories describe fundamental interactions in the standard model of particle physics and build effective theories in condensed matter physics. Being notoriously hard to simulate, these theories are commonly regularized as lattice gauge theories which can be evaluated with high precision through Monte Carlo algorithms or variational methods. However, Monte Carlo algorithms are not applicable in all regimes due to the sign problem; while variational methods, quantum and classical alike, only give upper-bounds on the ground-state energy. Their precision crucially depends on the chosen ansatz. Here, we present a framework based on a hierarchy of semidefinite programs to yield increasingly good lower-bounds on the ground-state energy and propagate these bounds to extended observables like Wilson loops and mesonic strings. We demonstrate the numerical capabilities by applying the algorithm to a (1+1)-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_2$ theory with dynamic fermionic matter and its pure-gauge version in (2+1)-dimensions. In both cases, we obtain certified intervals for the ground-state energy. For a one-dimensional system with 129 sites, the certified interval has a relative spread of $0.04\%$. For a two-dimensional system with 97 sites (corresponding to a $7 \times 8$ lattice), the relative spread is $2.08\%$. Beyond energy estimates, the proposed method provides rigorous bounds on both short- and long-range observables such as Wilson loops over multiple plaquettes and the magnitude of the mesonic string. The method can be both used as a computational microscope into many-body physics and a way to certify results of quantum simulators.

</details>


### [198] [Robust subspace designs and the power of a unique small quantum witness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06302)
*Simon Apers,Roman Edenhofer,Benjamin Mathieu-Bloise,Partha Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"鲁棒子空间设计"概念，给出多项式规模的随机构造及超多项式规模的显式构造，并将其应用于量子空间有界Valiant-Vazirani定理、零化度测试等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guruswami-Xing引入子空间设计、Guruswami-Kopparty给出显式构造，其要求族中每个子空间与任意固定维数子空间交小。本文希望定量加强这一要求，用于隔离量子见证子空间、处理零化度测试等量子空间有界问题。

Method: 引入鲁棒子空间设计：要求族中仅有少量子空间包含接近任意固定维数子空间的方向。给出多项式规模的随机构造，以及超多项式规模的显式构造。将该构造用于空间有界量子Merlin-Arthur协议中隔离唯一见证。

Result: 得到量子空间有界版Valiant-Vazirani定理：可隔离具有完美完备性且接受间隙位于其完美接受子空间之外的QMA协议的唯一见证；给出良态零化度测试到空间有界QMA的随机归约；并借助普通子空间设计以更简证明恢复Allender-Beals-Ogihara的C=L包含结果。

Conclusion: 鲁棒子空间设计是子空间设计的定量强化，具有多项式规模随机构造与超多项式规模显式构造，并在量子空间有界复杂度与零化度测试中获得应用，同时为经典C=L包含提供更简证明。

Abstract: The concept of subspace designs was introduced by Guruswami and Xing (STOC'13), and explicit constructions were given by Guruswami and Kopparty (FOCS'13). These are families of subspaces that have small intersection with any given subspace of a fixed dimension. We introduce \emph{robust subspace designs}. Informally, these are a quantitative extension in which we demand that not too many subspaces of the family contain directions that lie close to any given subspace of a fixed dimension. We give a probabilistic construction of such a robust subspace design of polynomial size, as well as a non-trivial explicit construction of superpolynomial size.
  Our main application of this new concept is a quantum space-bounded variant of the Valiant-Vazirani theorem (Theor.Comput.Sci.'86), which shows that restricting $\mathsf{NP}$-complete problems to instances with at most one accepting witness preserves hardness under randomized reductions. For quantum witnesses, the analogous quantity is the dimension of an accepting witness subspace. We use our probabilistic construction of robust subspace designs to isolate a unique witness for space-bounded quantum Merlin-Arthur protocols with perfect completeness and an acceptance gap outside their perfectly accepting subspace.
  As further applications, we give a randomized reduction of well-conditioned nullity testing to space-bounded quantum Merlin-Arthur protocols with perfect completeness. Using a similar idea, we find that ordinary subspace designs allow us to recover the classical $\mathsf{C_= L}$ containment of Allender, Beals, and Ogihara (STOC'96) for general nullity testing through a simpler proof.

</details>


### [199] [Quantum Leakage from Shamir Sharing with Correlated Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06350)
*Isaac Barouch Essayag,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究Shamir秘密分享中设备用量子资源生成单量子比特时的泄漏问题，证明在满足算子混合条件的链和有限量子记忆生成的资源下，任意两个秘密的泄漏态迹距离指数小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shamir秘密分享在量子场景下，各设备利用其份额从与秘密和分享随机性无关的初始量子资源产生量子比特，可能导致秘密信息泄漏。需要量化这种泄漏并给出安全性保证。

Method: 将设备边缘的任意扩展作为敌手边信息；对独立设备-参考对确定最优指数基；通过选定边缘与乘积态比较，将独立对界的界转化为相关资源的安全性保证；证明统一适用于大于份额数的素数域。

Result: 证明在重构需要足够大比例份额时，任意两个秘密的泄漏态间迹距离指数小；对固定平稳链满足上算子混合条件及有限量子记忆原语转移信道生成的资源成立。重构需全部份额时，独立设备-参考对的最优指数基：量子比特为2√2/π，经典比特为2/π，均允许任意有限参考。

Conclusion: 给出了Shamir秘密分享量子泄漏的指数小界限与最优指数基，并借助边缘比较将独立对结果推广到相关资源的安全性保证。

Abstract: We study leakage from Shamir secret sharing when each device uses its share to produce one qubit from an initial quantum resource independent of the secret and sharing randomness. We prove exponentially small trace distance between the leakage states of any two secrets, when reconstruction requires a sufficiently large fraction of the shares, for fixed stationary chains satisfying an upper operator mixing condition and for resources generated by fixed finite quantum memories with primitive transfer channels. These guarantees permit arbitrary extensions of the device marginal as adversary side information and hold uniformly over prime fields larger than the number of shares. For independent device--reference pairs, which provide the quantitative input to the argument, we determine the optimal exponential base when reconstruction requires all shares: $2\sqrt2/π$ for a qubit and $2/π$ for a classical bit, in both cases allowing arbitrary finite references. A comparison of selected marginals with product states converts the bounds for independent device--reference pairs into security guarantees for the correlated resources.

</details>


### [200] [Adaptive complete quantum search with unknown number of solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06364)
*Raj Alexandru Guţoiu,Bogdan-Călin Ciobanu,Pantelimon George Popescu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究解数量未知时找出所有解的数据库搜索问题，统一了经典、Grover与广义定点量子搜索，提出自适应贝叶斯策略以最大化单位时间期望成功概率，并给出渐近最优性、策略偏好与最优切换点的理论分析及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Grover算法仅适用于标记元素数量已知的情形，而在解数量未知时如何找出全部解仍具挑战性；已有经典采样、Grover搜索与广义定点量子搜索等不同方法，但缺乏统一的搜索模型与策略优化分析。

Method: 将经典采样、Grover与广义定点量子搜索的不同实例统一为自适应贝叶斯方法，在每次搜索实例中以最大化单位执行时间的期望成功概率为准则进行决策；采用积分近似解数量概率分布，确定经典采样与量子搜索之间的最优切换点，并将结果与Grover类最优得分进行比较。

Result: 当解数量已知且远小于数据库规模时，所提方法在考虑搜索模型内被证明渐近最优；对任意刻画可能解数量的概率分布，当经典验证时间不超过量子振幅放大时间的25%时，混合经典-Grover方法优于广义定点搜索；在该区间之外，给出了定点搜索相对最佳Grover类得分所能取得最大改进的上界，并确定了经典采样与量子搜索的最优过渡点；数值仿真验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为解数量未知时寻找全部解的数据库搜索提供了统一的自适应贝叶斯优化框架，明确了经典、Grover与定点量子搜索的适用区间及最优切换条件，并通过数值实验佐证了理论分析。

Abstract: Unstructured database search is a fundamental problem that requires finding one or more elements satisfying a specific property within a set of candidates. While Grover's algorithm provides a quadratic speedup for finding a marked element when the number of solutions is known, finding all solutions when their number is unknown presents additional challenges. In this paper, we thoroughly analyze this problem by considering all the different regimes of combinations of classical, Grover and generalized fixed-point quantum search instances. We unify these into an adaptive Bayesian approach that maximizes the expected success probability per unit execution time at each searching instance. For a problem with a known number of solutions, much lower than the database size, we prove that our proposed approach is asymptotically optimal within the considered search model. Furthermore, we prove that for any probability distribution characterizing the likely number of solutions, in regimes where the classical verification time is at most 25% of the quantum amplitude amplification time, hybrid classical and Grover approaches are preferred to generalized fixed-point. Outside of this regime, we derive an upper bound on the maximal improvement achievable by fixed-point search compared to the best Grover-based score. Additionally, we determine the optimal transition point between classical sampling and quantum searching using an integral approximation of the probability distribution of the number of solutions. The analytical results are also supported by numerical simulations of the proposed algorithm and by comparisons between the generalized fixed-point and Grover-based search policies.

</details>


### [201] [Value of spatially multiplexed SNSPDs with photon-number-resolving capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06388)
*Fabio Auriemma,Mikkel Ejrnaes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出组合与贝叶斯框架，量化多路复用SNSPD探测系统中提升本地光子数分辨能力所获得的信息增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明单个SNSPD可突破传统开/关探测机制而具备光子数分辨能力，因此需要将原有二值器件公式推广到具有任意可分辨态数的多路复用SNSPD系统，以评估多态光子数分辨探测阵列的信息价值。

Method: 推导任意可分辨态数多路复用SNSPD系统的概率响应张量；通过二项损失卷积纳入有限效率，并单独评估暗计数影响；由响应张量导出广义N光子探测器保真度、贝叶斯光子数重建及其重建保真度；对2、3、4态多路复用系统在不同探测器数目下进行实例化研究。

Result: 增加本地光子数分辨能力可系统性提升探测器保真度与贝叶斯光子数重建性能，并显著放宽纯二值多路复用阵列中探测器数目与可分辨光子数之间的二次缩放关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为多态光子数分辨探测阵列提供了定量的信息价值评估。

Abstract: We develop a combinatorial and Bayesian framework to quantify the information gained by increasing the local photon-number resolution of multiplexed detection systems based on superconducting nanowire single-photon detectors (SNSPDs). Motivated by recent studies that attribute photon-number resolving capabilities to a single SNSPD overcoming the typical on/off detection mechanism, we generalize the existing formulation for the binary devices by deriving the probability response tensor of multiplexed systems based on SNSPDs with an arbitrary number of resolvable states. We include finite efficiency through binomial loss convolution and, separately, assess the impact of dark counts. From this response tensor we derive a generalized N-photon detector fidelity, a Bayesian photon-number reconstruction, and the corresponding reconstruction fidelity. We particularize this formulation to study 2-, 3- and 4-state multiplexed systems with different numbers of detectors and show that increasing local photon-number resolution systematically improves both detector fidelity and Bayesian photon-number reconstruction. This substantially relaxes the quadratic scaling between detector number and resolvable photon-number required by purely binary multiplexed arrays. These results provide a quantitative value-of-information assessment for multi-state photon-number-resolving detector arrays.

</details>


### [202] [When is the Clifford hierarchy generalized semi-Clifford?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06402)
*Rongbiao Thomas Wang,Bobby Zixuan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Clifford层级中的半Clifford与广义半Clifford结构随层级k和量子比特数n的变化，刻画了其成立的参数范围，并提出广义半Clifford门与nc-广义半Clifford门的新猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford层级刻画量子门的能力与可模拟性，理解其半Clifford结构有助于判定门集的可经典模拟与结构分类；此前对一般k、n下该结构的成立范围缺乏完整刻画。

Method: 通过分析Clifford层级C_k(n)的代数结构，构造反例并给出归约，借助有限域Q(i)上的门表示与置换控制比特形式，论证半Clifford、广义半Clifford及nc-广义半Clifford门的刻画。

Result: 证明C_k(n)为半Clifford当且仅当k≤2、n≤2或k=3且n≤6；当k≤4或n≤3时为广义半Clifford，并在k,n≥5时构造反例。对n=4,k≥5，证明Q(i)上除标量外的门均为广义半Clifford，并将一般情形归约到单一层级。nc-广义半Clifford门恰为两侧Clifford门下的∑_x|π(x)⟩⟨x|⊗V_x形式。

Conclusion: 提出猜想：C_k(n)为广义半Clifford当且仅当k≤4或n≤4；并猜想k≥3时每个C_k(n)中门都具nc-广义半Clifford形式，且V_x∈C_{k-3}(n-n_c)，推广了Zeng、Chen与Chuang的猜想。

Abstract: We study semi-Clifford and generalized semi-Clifford structures in the Clifford hierarchy as a function of hierarchy level $k$ and qubit number $n$. We show that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is semi-Clifford if and only if $k\le2$, $n\le2$, or $k=3$ and $n\le6$. We also establish that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is generalized semi-Clifford when $k\le4$ or $n\le3$ and construct counterexamples when $k,n \geq 5$. For $n=4$ and $k\ge5$, we show that every gate defined over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ up to a scalar is generalized semi-Clifford, reduce the general case to a single level, and propose a strategy to prove our conjecture that $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ is generalized semi-Clifford if and only if $k\le4$ or $n\le4$. Lastly, we go beyond this structure by introducing $n_c$-generalized semi-Clifford gates, which we show are exactly the gates of the form $\sum_x|π(x)\rangle\langle x|\otimes V_x$ up to Clifford gates on both sides, where $π$ permutes the computational basis states of $n_c$ control qubits. We conjecture that for $k\ge3$ every gate in $\mathcal{C}_k(n)$ has this form for some $n_c\ge1$ with every $V_x\in\mathcal{C}_{k-3}(n-n_c)$, refining the conjecture of Zeng, Chen, and Chuang.

</details>


### [203] [A Practical Introduction to VQE: Methods, Challenges, and Applications in Physics and Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06448)
*Jannis Ehrlich,Felix Rupprecht,Konstantin Lamp,Elias Walter,Alejandro D. Somoza,Manuel Enns,Christian Elsässer,Birger Horstmann,Daniel F. Urban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述变分量子特征求解器（VQE）的核心组成——问题编码、拟设构造、经典优化与测量策略，并结合噪声、误差缓解及硬件约束讨论实际性能影响因素，面向新手提供入门指引与决策参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQE已成为近中期量子硬件上处理电子结构与材料科学问题的核心混合量子-经典方法，但物理、化学与量子信息领域的研究者在实践中面临大量算法选择，且缺乏面向新手的系统性入门指导。

Method: 采用结构化综述方式，梳理VQE的算法组件；结合作者早期实践经验，分析真实环境下的噪声特征、可用误差缓解技术及硬件相关线路设计考量，并以物理与量子化学示例加以说明。

Result: 提供了VQE各环节选择的结构化概览，识别出实际工作流程中的常见挑战与对新研究者尤为关键的要点。

Conclusion: 本文为进入VQE量子模拟领域的研究者提供易用的入门切入点，并随方法与硬件演进支持其做出有依据的决策。

Abstract: The Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) has become a central hybrid quantum-classical method for addressing electronic structure and materials science problems on near-term quantum hardware. As researchers across physics, chemistry, and quantum information engage with VQE, they encounter a wide range of algorithmic choices, including problem encoding, ansatz construction, classical optimization, and measurement strategies to choose from. This article provides a structured overview of these components and discusses practical factors that influence VQE performance in real settings, such as noise characteristics, available error-mitigation techniques, and hardware-dependent circuit considerations. Drawing partly on our early experiences, we highlight aspects that may be especially relevant for newcomers and outline common challenges observed in practical workflows. Our aim is to offer an accessible entry point for researchers approaching VQE-based quantum simulation and to support informed decision-making as the method and underlying hardware continue to evolve, which we support by discussion of examples from physics and quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [204] [A programmable quantum photonic platform integrating coherent emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06451)
*Hanna Salamon,Mikkel T. Mikkelsen,Zhe Liu,Peter Lodahl,Severin Krüger,Arne Ludwig,Leonardo Midolo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个可编程量子光子平台，能够在低温下同时控制高相干量子点单光子发射器与可重构光学元件，实现了纯单光子产生、双光子干涉和谐振透射/反射测量等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子量子技术需要将高质量量子光源与可编程光子电路集成，以实现片上量子信息处理。半导体量子点是有前景的量子光源平台，但其与低温兼容的可编程光子电路的单片集成仍具挑战。

Method: 通过系统级集成，在单芯片上实现对有源组件的独立电学和光学寻址，使其可同时运行，同时保持相干的非经典光发射。

Result: 实现了多种量子光子功能，包括纯单光子产生、双光子干涉，以及集成发射器的谐振透射和反射测量。

Conclusion: 该成果标志着从孤立量子光学器件迈向可编程量子光子硬件平台的一步，该平台能够将相干量子发射器与大规模光子功能相结合。

Abstract: Photonic quantum technologies require the integration of high-quality quantum light sources with programmable photonic circuitry to enable on-chip quantum information processing. Semiconductor quantum dots have emerged as a leading platform for quantum light generation, yet their monolithic integration with cryogenic-compatible, programmable photonic circuitry remains challenging. Here we demonstrate a programmable quantum photonic platform that enables simultaneous control of highly coherent quantum dot-based single photon emitters and reconfigurable optical elements at cryogenic temperatures. System-level integration enables individual electrical and optical addressing of active components on a single chip, allowing their concurrent operation while preserving coherent non-classical light emission. We demonstrate multiple quantum-photonic functionalities, including pure single-photon generation, two-photon interference, and resonant transmission and reflection measurements of integrated emitters. These results mark a step from isolated quantum-optical devices towards programmable quantum-photonic hardware platforms capable of combining coherent quantum emitters with large-scale photonic functionality.

</details>


### [205] [The right entanglement for perfect nonlocality and zero-error communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06512)
*Laura Mančinska*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构造了Flagged Magic-Square博弈，证明其完美策略需要并自检验Schmidt系数为(1,√2,√2,√2,√2)的非最大纠缠态，且最大纠缠策略胜率有界远离1。进一步给出博弈到信道的归约，得到一个经典信道：纠缠可单次零错误传输10条消息，而任意有限维最大纠缠资源至多传输9条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有自检验与伪心灵感应博弈多集中于最大纠缠态，尚不清楚能否用非局域博弈锁定并自检验特定的非最大纠缠态；同时，最大纠缠态是否在经典信道零错误通信中普遍最优也缺乏反例。

Method: 在标准Magic-Square博弈上为每位玩家增设一个"flag"输出和两个额外问题，构造Flagged Magic-Square博弈；给出直接代数证明说明最大纠缠策略胜率有界远离1；证明该博弈自检验上述非最大纠缠态；建立一般性的博弈到信道归约，将博弈模型中的分离迁移到经典信道的一次性零错误通信。

Result: Flagged Magic-Square博弈存在使用Schmidt系数正比于(1,√2,√2,√2,√2)的完美策略，最大纠缠策略胜率有界远离1且与维度无关，并自检验该非最大纠缠态。经归约得到经典信道：纠缠可实现单次使用零错误传输10条消息，任意有限维最大纠缠资源至多9条。

Conclusion: 通过在刚性测量几何上添加合适约束可强制特定Schmidt比例，为基于其他刚性伪心灵感应博弈的自检验提供模块化路径。最大纠缠态并非经典含噪信道零错误通信中普遍最优的纠缠资源。

Abstract: We identify a structural mechanism by which perfect play in a nonlocal game requires and self-tests a specific non-maximally entangled state. To this end, we construct a Flagged Magic-Square game by extending the standard Magic-Square game with an additional ``flag'' outcome and two additional questions for each player. The game admits a perfect strategy using a state with Schmidt coefficients proportional to $(1,\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2},\sqrt{2})$. We give a direct algebraic proof that maximally entangled strategies have winning probability bounded away from one, independently of dimension. Moreover, the game self-tests this state, providing an example of a two-party pseudo-telepathy game that certifies a specific non-maximally entangled state. Our construction illustrates how adding suitable constraints to a rigid measurement geometry can enforce specific Schmidt ratios and provides a modular route to further self-tests based on other rigid pseudo-telepathy games.
  We establish a general game-to-channel reduction that transfers separations in the game model to one-shot zero-error communication over classical channels. Applied to the Flagged Magic-Square game, it yields a classical channel for which entanglement permits the zero-error transmission of ten messages in a single use, whereas any finite-dimensional maximally entangled resource permits at most nine. Thus, maximally entangled states are not a universally optimal entanglement resource for zero-error communication over classical noisy channels.

</details>


### [206] [Causal memory and structural complexity of a quantum space series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06519)
*Thomas J. Elliott*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子计算力学的完全量子扩展，用因果生成所需记忆刻画量子过程的复杂度，证明存在等距生成的最小记忆模型，并揭示记忆熵即纠缠熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典复杂度科学通过因果生成所需记忆来刻画结构，但缺乏对量子过程复杂度的操作化定义。作者希望建立量化内在量子结构的操作框架，回答“什么使一个量子过程比另一个更复杂”。

Method: 将计算力学扩展到量子空间序列（不与生成器返回交互的量子输出序列）。针对允许有限维、本原因果模型的平稳有记忆量子空间序列，构造可由等距方式生成输出的记忆最小模型（无需丢弃环境），得到典范矩阵乘积态表示。

Result: 典范矩阵乘积态同时确定最小记忆维度与最小记忆熵；记忆熵恰为过去-未来二分下的纠缠熵。在该类中进一步证明基本熵率消失、无因果不对称性、以及无穷量子马尔可夫阶。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量化内在量子结构的操作框架，将纠缠赋予“因果生成的不可约记忆”这一操作解释，揭示了因果生成、复杂度、记忆、熵与纠缠之间的深层联系。

Abstract: What makes one quantum process more complex than another? We adopt an operational approach inspired by classical complexity science, which characterises structure through the memory required for causal generation. We develop a fully-quantum extension of computational mechanics for quantum space series: Sequences of quantum outputs that do not return to interact with their generator. For stationary, memoryful quantum space series admitting finite-dimensional, primitive causal models, we show that a memory-minimal model can be chosen to generate the outputs isometrically, without a discarded environment. The resulting canonical matrix product state representation determines both the minimum memory dimension and the minimum memory entropy. The latter is precisely the entanglement entropy across the past-future bipartition, giving entanglement an operational interpretation as irreducible memory for causal generation. Within this class, we further establish a vanishing fundamental entropy rate, the absence of causal asymmetry, and infinite quantum Markov order. These results establish an operational framework for quantifying intrinsic quantum structure, revealing deep connections between causal generation, complexity, memory, entropy, and entanglement.

</details>


### [207] [Classical Simulation of Lossy Gaussian Boson Sampling beyond Square-Root Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06526)
*Youngrong Lim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究光子损失下高斯玻色采样（GBS）的经典可模拟性，证明“用经典光替代输入”的方法存在精度标度极限，并提出替代探测环节的新算法突破该极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子损失使GBS更易被经典模拟。既有标准方法用经典光替代损失输入，在固定压缩与精度下可保持准确，但平均存活光子数增长不超过√N。作者希望确定这一标度是否为该类替代方案的根本极限，并寻找突破途径。

Method: 首先证明极限：对显式干涉仪，任何具有非负Glauber–Sudarshan P表示的态（无论是否高斯、是否关联），都无法在超过√N标度后保持光子数分布误差很小。其次提出改进方法：不替代输入而近似探测。采样器在两次探测之间精确演化量子态，每次探测后用具有相同协方差的高斯态替代条件态。

Result: 新方法的全变差误差为O(Nη³)，多项式期望代价，其中η为光子存活概率；因此在固定精度下平均存活光子数可增长至N²/³。另一对构造在所有输入模式等压缩时可达N³/⁴。

Conclusion: 经典光替代输入存在√N的固有精度标度极限；通过改为近似探测环节，可突破该极限，使可经典模拟的损失GBS在更大存活光子数范围内保持精度。

Abstract: Photon loss makes Gaussian boson sampling easier to simulate classically. A standard approach replaces the lossy input by classical light, and it stays accurate while the mean number of surviving photons grows no faster than the square root of the number $N$ of squeezed inputs at fixed squeezing and accuracy. We prove that this scale is a limit of replacement by classical light. For explicit interferometers, no state with a nonnegative Glauber--Sudarshan $P$ representation, Gaussian or not and however correlated, keeps the error in the photon count distribution small beyond this scale. We then surpass this limit by approximating the detection instead of the input. The sampler evolves the state exactly between detections and, after each detection, replaces the conditional state by a Gaussian with the same covariance. For every interferometer its total variation error is $O(Nη^3)$ at polynomial expected cost, where $η$ is the photon survival probability. The mean surviving photon number can therefore grow as $N^{2/3}$ at fixed accuracy. A separate pair construction reaches $N^{3/4}$ when every input mode is equally squeezed.

</details>


### [208] [Closed-Form Arbitrary-Gap Rényi Nonadditivity Above Any Positive Order via Direct Products of Free Groups and High-Girth Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06533)
*Guocheng Zhen,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了最小输出Rényi熵可加性违反的显式有限维构造，可在任意给定正Rényi阶数区间上一致地获得任意大的可加性间隙，并改进了已有构造的量子比特数参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道最小输出Rényi熵的可加性问题是量子信息论中的核心问题。已有工作（如Shou-Gorshkov）给出了违反可加性的构造，但所用资源（输入/输出量子比特数）较大。本文旨在给出显式、有限维且资源更少的构造，使得在任意正Rényi阶数范围内都能一致地产生大的可加性间隙。

Method: 采用具有实代数元素的量子信道的闭式有限维构造；基于自由群直积与固定域高围长图的有限实现。对任意p0>0和g>0，构造单个信道与单个Bell输入，使可加性间隙在所有p∈[p0,∞]上同时大于g。令r=⌈2g⌉，构造使用15r个输出量子比特与O(r2^{30r}+r^2(1+p0^{-1}))个输入量子比特。在von Neumann阶数情形下给出单独的特化构造。

Result: 构造在使用15r个输出量子比特时，对每个目标间隙均少于Shou-Gorshkov所述参数所需的输出量子比特；在固定正截断且间隙增大时，输入量子比特的渐近上界也更小。在von Neumann阶数下，给出显式信道，其间隙大于1/1024，使用37608898个输入量子比特和8个输出量子比特。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了显式有限维的量子信道构造，统一地在任意正Rényi阶数上实现任意大的最小输出Rényi熵可加性违反，并在输出量子比特数和渐近输入量子比特界上优于已有构造，给出了可加性破坏的更强证据。

Abstract: We give a closed-form finite-dimensional construction of quantum channels with real algebraic entries exhibiting arbitrarily large violations of minimum-output Rényi entropy additivity uniformly above any prescribed positive Rényi order, using a different finite realization based on direct products of free groups and fixed-field high-girth graphs. More precisely, for every $p_0>0$ and $g>0$, we construct one channel and one Bell input for which the additivity gap is greater than $g$ simultaneously for all $p\in[p_0,\infty]$. With $r=\lceil2g\rceil$, the construction uses $15r$ output qubits and $O(r2^{30r}+r^2(1+p_0^{-1}))$ input qubits. Compared with the stated parameters of Shou and Gorshkov \cite{ShouGorshkov2026}, our construction uses fewer output qubits for every target gap and has a smaller asymptotic input-qubit bound as the gap grows with the positive cutoff fixed. At the von Neumann order, a separate specialization gives an explicit channel with gap greater than $1/1024$ using $37608898$ input qubits and $8$ output qubits.

</details>


### [209] [The Mermin-Peres Magic Square Game With Three or More Players](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06534)
*Itamar Lorian,J. E. Avron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Mermin-Peres魔方博弈推广到N个玩家（用N维超立方体替代方阵），证明最优经典策略胜率恒为8/9，而量子策略在N≥3时胜率仅介于1/3与3/4之间，量子优势因monogamy（单配性）而消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Mermin-Peres魔方博弈是2玩家情形，展示量子策略（胜率1）优于经典策略（胜率8/9）。作者希望探究该量子优势是否能推广到多玩家情形，以及多体纠缠约束如何影响博弈表现。

Method: 将魔方博弈中的方阵替换为N维超立方体，构造N玩家博弈；分析最优经典策略的胜率；并将N=2的量子策略自然推广到N≥3，计算其获胜概率。

Result: 最优经典策略胜率对所有N恒为8/9；对N≥3，量子策略胜率介于1/3与3/4之间。量子优势在N≥3时消失。

Conclusion: 多玩家情形下量子策略不再优于经典策略，这一量子优势的丧失源于monogamy（纠缠单配性）：多体系统中纠缠的分配受限制，无法像2玩家情形那样充分利用量子关联。

Abstract: We present a generalization of the Mermin-Peres magic square game to $N$ players, in which the square is replaced by an $N$-dimensional hypercube. We show that the winning probability of the optimal classical strategy remains $8/9$ for all $N$, while for $N \ge 3$, a generalization of the quantum strategy for $N = 2$ achieves a winning probability between $1/3$ and $3/4$. This loss of quantum advantage for $N \ge 3$ is a consequence of monogamy.

</details>


### [210] [Symmetry and AI-assisted discovery of magic-state factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06535)
*Shubham P. Jain,Adam Wills,Shraddha Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出对称性与AI辅助搜索方法，在距离≥3的魔法态蒸馏工厂领域发现699个工厂类别（564个为新的），包括最低开销指数的纯T工厂与含纠缠输出的工厂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔法态蒸馏是容错量子计算中的主要资源开销，工厂成本强烈依赖失效率，而失效率随输入魔法态数量增长。距离二搜索已借助对称性限制方法实现，但距离三及以上在中等等输入数量下仍难以处理。

Method: 提出统一的二元矩阵表述，同时涵盖三正交码与直接电路搜索；证明距离至少三等价于非零且两两不同的校验子，从而将校验子选择与兼容输出门搜索分离；利用群对称性与语言模型智能体限制校验子搜索，随后进行确定性求解与独立验证。

Result: 搜索得到699个工厂类别（含564个新类别），覆盖纯T态工厂以及输出为T、CS、CCZ组合的纠缠输出工厂。纯T工厂[[63,11,3]]和[[850,128,6]]分别在至多100与1000输入下达到已知最低开销指数，γ=1.589与γ=1.057；[[1715,287,6]]工厂γ=0.998，是已知最小的γ<1纯T工厂。

Conclusion: 提供搜索简报与活动笔记的上下文目录，供读者训练自己的智能体并按需定制搜索；由此开始构建面向量子社区的活跃开源魔法态蒸馏协议库，并附带方法与数据，服务于实际容错量子计算场景。

Abstract: Magic-state distillation is a major resource cost in fault-tolerant quantum computing. The cost of a magic-state factory depends strongly on its failure rate, which grows with the number of input magic states. Although symmetry-restricted methods have recently made distance two searches tractable, distance three and above have remained elusive at moderate input counts. We develop symmetry- and AI-assisted methods to search this regime. We present a unified binary-matrix formulation encompassing both triorthogonal-code and direct circuit searches. We show that distance at least three is equivalent to nonzero, pairwise distinct syndromes, separating the choice of syndromes from the search for compatible output gates. We restrict the syndrome search using group symmetry and language-model agents, followed by deterministic solving and independent verification. Our searches yield 699 factory classes, including 564 new ones. These include factories for pure-T states and factories with entangled outputs comprising combinations of T, CS, and CCZ magic states. The pure-T factories [[63, 11, 3]] and [[850, 128, 6]] achieve the lowest overhead exponents we know among protocols with at most 100 and 1000 inputs, respectively, with $γ= 1.589$ and $γ= 1.057$. Our [[1715, 287, 6]] factory, with $γ= 0.998$, is the smallest known pure-T factory with $γ< 1$. We also provide a context directory of search briefs and campaign notes with which readers can train their own agents and tailor the search to their requirements. With these results, we begin constructing an active, open-source repository of magic-state distillation protocols for the quantum community, supplemented by our methods and data, for the practical fault-tolerant quantum computing regime.

</details>


### [211] [Products of unitaries as continuous evolutions at finite precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06541)
*Michael Jarret*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将连续酉演化与有限维单位步长酉乘积相互对应的离散化方法，借此把离散绝热定理归约到连续绝热定理，并给出 O(1/T) 的算子范数误差界，应用于量子线性系统求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散绝热定理的证明通常较为繁琐，而连续绝热理论体系成熟。作者希望建立连续酉演化与离散酉乘积之间的双向对应关系，从而无需离散绝热定理即可将连续模拟与连续绝热界直接迁移到离散乘积上。

Method: 对受调控哈密顿量在合适有限划分上取截断样本，细步长下给出逼近乘积；反之通过"舍入时钟"把 T 个单位步样本的乘积识别为连续演化（相差两个半步相位）。在每步内利用抵消技巧，结合有界一阶、二阶导数、有界谱宽及特征值差一致远离 2π 非零整数倍等假设，导出算子范数误差 O(1/T)。并为具有这些导数界的酉行走构造固定公共自由谱弧上的 Hermitian 对数路径以满足比较假设。

Result: 连续模拟保证可迁移至相位修正后的乘积，连续绝热界也能迁移到乘积本身，误差为显式可加的 O(1/T)，且各绝热定理假设均得以保留。对条件数为 κ 的正定量子线性系统，该比较与连续绝热理论给出重标度量子化行走在泄漏 δ 下 O(κ/δ) 步的复杂度，与先前通过离散绝热定理得到的标度一致。

Conclusion: 所构造的离散酉乘积不需要离散绝热定理即可获得与之一致的绝热界与复杂度，为连续与离散绝热分析之间建立了简洁的桥梁。

Abstract: I present a short approach to discretizing continuous unitary evolution. It gives a correspondence in both directions between continuous unitary evolutions and products of unitaries in finite dimensions, and I use it to reduce discrete adiabatic theorems to continuous ones. At fine steps, holding truncated samples of a regulated Hamiltonian on a suitable finite partition gives an approximating product. Conversely, rounding the clock identifies a product of $T$ unit-step samples with a continuous evolution, up to two half-step phases. I bound its operator-norm distance from the original evolution by $O(1/T)$ through cancellation within each time step. This assumes bounded first and second derivatives, bounded spectral width, and eigenvalue differences bounded away from nonzero multiples of $2π$, uniformly in $T$. For a unitary walk with these derivative bounds, a fixed common free spectral arc supplies a Hermitian logarithm path satisfying the comparison hypotheses. Continuous simulation guarantees then transfer to the phase-corrected product, and continuous adiabatic bounds to the product itself, with an explicit additive $O(1/T)$ error and each adiabatic theorem's hypotheses retained. For positive definite quantum linear systems of condition number $κ$, this comparison and continuous adiabatic theory give a rescaled qubitized walk with $O(κ/δ)$ steps for leakage $δ$, matching the scaling previously obtained through a discrete adiabatic theorem. These products need no discrete adiabatic theorem.

</details>


### [212] [Mid-circuit measurements and feedforward implement every unitary exactly in constant depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06551)
*Chenfeng Cao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究带前馈的中途测量对精确n量子比特酉合成最优最坏情况量子深度的影响，将深度从Θ(n)降至O(1)，并给出深度-宽度权衡的构造与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意单/双量子比特门与无限制辅助比特空间下，精确n量子比特酉合成的最坏情况量子深度原本为Θ(n)；而带前馈的中途测量、重置能否突破该限制及所需代价尚不明确，因此本文探讨测量与前馈对深度的作用及深度-宽度权衡。

Method: 采用确定性构造：使用单量子比特测量、O(1)轮宇称前馈与O(n4^n log(n+2))总量子比特（辅助比特起止均为|0⟩）；仅对Nehoran-Yuen近似的矩阵误差进行隐形传态，由精确制备的辅助态编码该误差并经相干干涉将其作用加回；在算子范数误差2^{-n}时仅需一步振幅放大。此外构造相干扇出电路达到相同常数深度，并分析仅用单/双量子比特门时的深度与门数。

Result: 带前馈中途测量将最优最坏情况量子深度从Θ(n)降至O(1)；无前馈的测量与重置仍受因果锥界限制。相干扇出电路达到同样常数深度；仅用单/双量子比特门的电路深度为Θ(n)、门数为O(4^n log^2(n+2))。一般目标在深度h下至少需Ω(4^n/(h+1))量子比特，即使在无限制经典控制下亦然；本文深度-宽度权衡在1≤h≤2^n范围内与该下界相差O(n^2)因子。

Conclusion: 带前馈的中途测量可将精确n量子比特酉合成的最坏情况深度降至常数，代价为大量辅助比特；深度-宽度权衡接近通用下界（差O(n^2)因子），而相干扇出同样可实现常数深度。

Abstract: Mid-circuit measurements with feedforward reduce the optimal worst-case quantum depth for exact $n$-qubit unitary synthesis from $Θ(n)$ to $O(1)$, using arbitrary one- and two-qubit gates and unrestricted ancillary space. Measurements and resets without feedforward remain subject to the circuit causal-cone bound. Our deterministic construction uses single-qubit measurements, $O(1)$ rounds of parity feedforward, and $O(n4^n\log(n+2))$ total qubits, with all ancillas starting and ending in $|0\rangle$. Classical processing is not counted in quantum depth. We teleport only the matrix error of an approximation by Nehoran and Yuen. An exactly prepared ancillary state encodes this error, whose action is added back by coherent interference. At operator-norm error $2^{-n}$, one step of amplitude amplification suffices. Coherent fanout circuits achieve the same constant-depth bound, while circuits using only one- and two-qubit gates attain depth $Θ(n)$ with $O(4^n\log^2(n+2))$ gates. Generic targets require $Ω(4^n/(h+1))$ qubits at depth $h$, even under unrestricted classical control. Our depth-width trade-offs meet this bound within a factor $O(n^2)$ for $1\le h\le2^n$.

</details>


### [213] [CT-bMPS: Approximate Tensor Network Contraction with Boundary MPS on Contraction Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06574)
*Hidetaka Manabe,Feng Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 CT-bMPS，一种针对一般闭合张量网络的可扩展近似收缩方法，将收缩树每条边上的边界张量及其互补环境表示为矩阵乘积态，从而在多项式代价下实现任意收缩树下的环境感知局部压缩，并在多类基准上优于现有通用近似收缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般闭合张量网络的精确收缩代价随网络规模指数增长，现有通用近似收缩方法在精度与可扩展性上存在局限，且难以灵活利用环境信息进行压缩。

Method: 提出收缩树边界矩阵乘积态（CT-bMPS）：在收缩树的每条边上，将边界张量与其互补环境同时表示为矩阵乘积态；基于约化转移矩阵进行局部压缩，从而沿任意收缩树融入环境信息，同时保持时间和内存代价关于网络规模与键维数多项式增长；并采用优化收缩树与环境更新策略。

Result: 在 Ising 配分函数、随机张量网络、kicked Ising 动力学及量子纠错解码等模型与应用上进行了基准测试，相比现有通用近似收缩方法，精度与计算效率均有提升，并能处理更大、更复杂的网络；优化收缩树与环境更新可在不增加键维数的情况下显著提高精度。

Conclusion: CT-bMPS 是一种通用且可扩展的近似框架，具备灵活收缩顺序与基于环境的压缩能力，适用于广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce contraction tree boundary matrix product states (CT-bMPS), a scalable approximate contraction method for general closed tensor networks. CT-bMPS represents the boundary tensor and its complementary environment at each edge of a contraction tree as matrix product states. This representation allows local compression based on a reduced transition matrix to incorporate environment information along arbitrary contraction trees, while keeping the time and memory costs polynomial in the network size and bond dimension. We benchmark the method on a variety of models and applications, including Ising partition functions, random tensor networks, kicked Ising dynamics, and quantum error correction decoding. These benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy and computational efficiency over existing general-purpose approximate contraction methods, together with the ability to handle larger and more complex networks. Optimized contraction trees and environment updates substantially improve accuracy without increasing the bond dimension. These results establish CT-bMPS as a general and scalable approximation framework with flexible contraction orders and environment-based compression for a wide range of applications.

</details>


### [214] [Shaping double descent in quantum extreme learning through model design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06581)
*Sabri Meyer,Ege Yilmaz,Aurelien Lucchi,Francesco Tacchino,Francesco Scala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为线性回归在相关特征下的双下降风险曲线给出了解析刻画，并将其应用于量子极端学习机（QELM），揭示了编码强度、输入重上传和测量次数三个设计旋钮对输出协方差的隐式正则化作用及插值阈值稳定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双下降现象在独立特征下已被充分理解，但相关特征情形其曲线形状由特征协方差谱决定，现有分析通常只能给出隐式方程，缺乏显式解析刻画；同时量子模型（如QELM）中的双下降与设计参数关系尚不清晰。

Method: 首先针对经典线性回归，通过特征总体协方差的条件数与相关强度的谱测度两个量，解析刻画完整的风险曲线，补足相关特征下双下降的显式理论；随后将该理论推广到量子模型，重点分析QELM中编码强度、输入重上传与测量次数三个可调设计参数对输出协方差的隐式正则化影响，并推导插值阈值保持稳定的条件。

Result: 获得了经典线性回归在相关特征下完整的双下降风险曲线解析表达式；证明QELM的三个设计旋钮各自正则化输出协方差，并给出插值阈值稳定的条件，其中有限测量散粒噪声可被利用来保证该稳定性；数值实验验证了所有理论预测。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了相关特征下双下降显式分析的空白，并为QELM提供了具体设计原则，使其能够在超越插值区域后仍具备良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Increasing capacity can induce a machine learning model to generalize worse until it perfectly interpolates the training data, and better once it moves beyond interpolation. This inversion, known as \emph{double descent}, is well understood for independent features. For correlated features, its shape is governed by the spectrum of the feature covariance, but existing analyses typically yield only implicit equations. We first close this gap for classical linear regression, analytically characterizing the entire risk curve through two quantities, namely the condition number of the feature population covariance and a spectral measure of correlation strength. Then, we apply this theory on quantum models, focusing on quantum extreme learning machines (QELMs). We show that three tunable design knobs, \textit{i.e.,} encoding strength, input reuploads, and measurement shots, each regularize the QELM output covariance, and derive conditions under which the interpolation threshold remains stable. Finite measurement shot noise can be leveraged to guarantee this stability. Numerical experiments confirm each prediction, yielding concrete design principles for QELMs that generalize well beyond interpolation.

</details>


### [215] [Information causality does not single out the set of quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06604)
*Marco Pompili,Mirjam Weilenmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文否定了"对任意 n 的信息因果性(IC)能唯一刻画量子关联集"这一猜想：构造出既不包含也不被包含于量子关联、且违反量子界的 IC 满足集，表明所有已知形式的 IC 都fails于刻画二体量子关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息因果性(IC)被提出作为限制 Bell 场景中非局域关联的物理原理，其能否像信息论原理那样唯一刻画出量子关联集一直是开放问题。作者旨在判定：对任意输入数 m 和输出数 d，满足 IC 的关联集是否恰好等于量子关联集。

Method: 通过构造满足全部兼容协议的 IC 的二体关联集，其中构造过程依赖于为 GPT(广义概率理论)建立的一种熵形式的 IC 证明。

Result: 1) 对任意有限数据库长度 n≥2，存在满足 IC(对所有兼容协议)的二体关联集，且在 n+1 输入、每方 2 输出的 Bell 场景中表现出后量子关联；该关联集既非量子关联集的超集也非其子集。2) 构造出 3 输入、2 输出 Bell 场景中满足 IC(对所有 n∈ℕ)且包含后量子点的关联集。

Conclusion: 所有已知的 IC 表述都无法完整刻画二体量子关联集，即便在 3 输入、2 输出的 Bell 场景中亦然；因此 IC 不能作为唯一刻画量子关联的物理原理。

Abstract: Information causality (IC) was introduced as a physical principle that imposes constraints on the non-local correlations between two parties (Alice and Bob) in a Bell scenario. Specifically, it states that the information that Bob can gain about an $n$-bit database held by Alice is bounded by the information she communicates to him. To date, the question whether IC for any $n \in \mathbb{N}$ singles out the set of quantum correlations remains open, meaning that it is unknown whether for any number of inputs $m$ and outputs $d$ of the two parties the set of correlations that satisfy IC exactly coincides with the quantum correlations. In this work, we answer this question in the negative. Firstly, we show that for any finite database length $n \geq 2$, there is a bipartite set of correlations that satisfies IC for all compatible protocols and that exhibits post-quantum correlations in the Bell scenario with $n+1$ inputs and $2$ outcomes per party. This result relies on constructing an entropic proof of IC for GPTs that may be of independent interest. We further show that in the Bell scenario with $n+1$ inputs and $2$ outcomes per party, our constructed sets of correlations lead to neither a superset nor a subset of the quantum correlations. Secondly, we construct a set of correlations in the Bell scenario with $3$ inputs and $2$ outputs per party that satisfies IC (for all database sizes $n \in \mathbb{N}$) and that includes post-quantum points. Consequently, all known formulations of information causality fail to fully characterize the bipartite set of quantum correlations, already in the $3$-input, $2$-outcome Bell scenario.

</details>


### [216] [Staying Sober: A Shot-Free Predictor of Logical Error Rates for Concatenated Quantum Error Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06606)
*Sayam Sethi,Aditi Awasthi,Maxwell Poster,Joshua Viszlai,Jonathan Mark Baker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种无需采样的预测器，通过码的稳定子、最小权逻辑算子（奇数 d 时含权 d+1 逻辑算子）直接计算 LER 多项式首项单项式，可逐层用于级联码，计算时间对码参数多项式、对层数线性；理论证明非级联码精确，级联码近似递归精确方法，并在 37 种配置上验证高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Monte Carlo 估计 QEC 码 LER 需 O(p^{-⌈d/2⌉}/ε²) 次采样，在大码距、低错误率尤其是距离随层数 d^ℓ 增长的级联码下不可行。fail fast 方法虽按权重分采样降本，但仅适用于非级联码，且未建模级联各层间传递的软信息。

Method: 构造预测器：输入稳定子、最小权逻辑算子及奇数 d 时的权 d+1 逻辑算子，无需采样，在每层级联上计算 LER 多项式首项 c p^o，时间对码参数多项式、对 ℓ 线性（固定预算）。对级联码，另构建递归方法在指数时间内精确求首项，预测器为其近似。

Result: 证明在预算未耗尽时，对任意非级联稳定子码、任意逻辑可观测量及任意逐比特错误率，预测精确；对级联码，预测器近似递归精确方法。预测与 level 1 精确枚举在所有评估码上一致，对 iceberg 码可达三层。48 核心小时内评估比较 37 种码配置、四种噪声偏置，其中 148 个情形中的 113 个在 p=10^-3 下 Monte Carlo 需超过 10^12 次采样。

Conclusion: 该预测器以解析方式高效估计 LER 首项，避免高成本采样，适用于级联码软信息传递建模，可大规模比较码与噪声配置，为低错误率下 QEC 码评估提供可行途径。

Abstract: Estimating the logical error rate (LER) of a quantum error-correcting (QEC) code by Monte Carlo sampling takes $O(p^{-\lceil d/2 \rceil}/\varepsilon^2)$ samples at physical error rate $p$, code distance $d$ and relative error $\varepsilon$, which becomes intractable at large distances and low error rates, and especially for concatenated codes, whose distance grows as $d^{\ell}$ with the number of levels $\ell$. The fail fast method reduces this cost by sampling the failures of each weight separately, but it only works for unconcatenated codes, and does not model the soft information that the levels of a concatenated code pass to each other. We propose a predictor that takes the stabilizers, the minimum-weight logical operators and, for odd $d$, the logical operators of weight $d + 1$ of a code, and computes, without sampling, the leading monomial $c\,p^{o}$ of the LER when expressed as a polynomial in $p$, at every level of concatenation, in time polynomial in the code parameters and linear in $\ell$ for fixed budgets. We prove that our prediction is exact for any unconcatenated stabilizer code, for every logical observable and arbitrary per-qubit error rates, whenever no budget is exhausted. Furthermore, for concatenated codes, we construct a recursive approach that computes the leading monomial exactly in exponential time, and our predictor approximates this approach. Our predictions match exact enumeration at level 1 on every code we evaluate, and up to three levels for the iceberg code. Using our predictor, we evaluate and compare $37$ code configurations under four noise biases in $48$ core-hours, where Monte Carlo sampling would need more than $10^{12}$ shots at $p = 10^{-3}$ for $113$ of these $148$ cases.

</details>


### [217] [Quantum codes are quantized codes: Stabilizer codes from quantization over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06607)
*Michael Zurel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出有限域上几何量子化的离散类比，将其作用于特定经典纠错码即可得到量子纠错码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码理论中已存在量子-经典对应关系，但此前缺乏物理解释；本文旨在为该对应关系提供物理诠释。

Method: 构造有限域上几何量子化的离散类比（discrete analogue of geometric quantization），并将其应用于特定经典纠错码。

Result: 将离散几何量子化方法作用于某些经典纠错码后，成功得到相应的量子纠错码。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了离散几何量子化与编码理论之间的桥梁，为已知的量子-经典对应提供了物理层面的解释。

Abstract: We present a discrete analogue of geometric quantization over finite fields which, when applied to certain classical error-correcting codes, yields quantum error-correcting codes. This provides a physical interpretation for a previously known quantum-classical correspondence in coding theory.

</details>


### [218] [Quantum Krylov Linear Solving Beyond Global Conditioning via Spectral Compression and Directional Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06620)
*Sijia Yu,Yifan Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究时间演化量子Krylov线性求解器（QKS）在"消失特征值仍与解相关"这一困难情形下的复杂度，证明归一化态复杂度不必继承全局条件数的发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局条件数依赖常被用来估计归一化量子线性系统求解的难度，但这一估计可能严重高估实际难度。已有超越条件数的方法多假设全局病态方向与目标解相关性有限；本文关注更困难的区域：一个趋于零的特征值仍与解相关且必须被保留，此时全局条件数发散，需重新刻画真正的难度来源。

Method: 在时间演化量子Krylov线性求解（QKS）框架下，识别出一类特定区域：与解相关的谱被压缩到一个紧致的约化空间中，病态性集中在一个与解方向对齐的"软方向"上。通过分析归一化对沿该方向发散放大的消除作用，将归一化态难度归因于"逆放大是否改变解方向"，并进一步在有限投影系统误差下证明该稳定性可传递到真实QKS输出。

Result: （1）解相关信息存在紧凑且稳定的QKS表示，当相关特征值趋于零时，子空间维度、演化时间与重构开销均有界；（2）归一化态难度取决于逆放大是否改变解方向，而非仅由小特征值决定，即使约化系统任意病态，只要放大与解对齐，仍可保持方向稳定；（3）该稳定性在有限投影误差下持续并传递至实际QKS输出，给出不依赖保留小特征值逆的端到端复杂度界；并构造显式分离族，表明完整逆放大判据可能发散而对应QKS量保持有界。

Conclusion: 归一化量子线性求解的复杂度不应被全局条件数支配；在解相关病态被压缩且放大方向与解对齐的QKS区域中，复杂度可保持有界，不随保留的小特征值发散，从而揭示了比条件数更精确的难度刻画。

Abstract: Global condition-number dependence can substantially overestimate the difficulty of preparing normalized quantum linear-system solutions. Many existing beyond-conditioning approaches are favorable when globally ill-conditioned directions have limited relevance to the target solution. Here we study a harder regime for time-evolution quantum Krylov linear solving (QKS), where a vanishing eigenvalue remains solution-relevant and must be retained. We identify a QKS regime in which the solution-relevant spectrum is compressed into a compact reduced space where the ill-conditioning is concentrated along a soft direction aligned with the solution, so that normalization removes the divergent amplification along that direction and leaves only a bounded transverse response. Consequently, the normalized-state complexity need not inherit the divergence of the global condition number. Our contributions are threefold: (1) We show that the solution-relevant information admits a compact and stable QKS representation, with bounded subspace dimension, evolution time, and reconstruction overhead as the relevant eigenvalue vanishes. (2) We show that normalized-state difficulty is governed by whether inverse amplification changes the solution direction, rather than by the small eigenvalue alone; even an arbitrarily ill-conditioned reduced system can remain directionally stable when the amplification is solution-aligned. (3) We prove that this stability persists under finite projected-system errors and transfers to the actual QKS output, yielding an end-to-end complexity bound without inverse dependence on the retained small eigenvalue. An explicit separation family further shows that a full inverse-amplification criterion can diverge while the corresponding QKS quantities remain bounded.

</details>


### [219] [Large Language Model-Guided Discovery of Weight-Five Bivariate Bicycle Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06623)
*Juan Cruz-Benito*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在LLM引导的程序演化工作流基础上，系统研究权重为五的二元自行车（BB）与扰动二元自行车（PBB）码，生成并认证了包含1142个不同码方案的目录，并给出结构分类与精确距离证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在前期的LLM引导程序演化工作流基础上，进一步将该方法推广到约束更强的权重五BB与PBB码族，验证LLM引导发现的适用性，并提供可复现的结构与精确距离分析。

Method: 采用LLM引导的程序演化工作流生成权重五BB/PBB码方案；进行后搜索比较与认证，包括连通CSS实现认证、Lin--Pryadko存档构造导入比较、精确距离证据与显式双变量表示及分量归约验证；开展基无关连通性分析；通过代数方法将连通CSS类划分为3阶、7阶与15阶分圆核分层；并设计三个随机搜索对照，以相同的每格与编码维度采样数均匀无放回抽取6444个CSS码方案作为比较。

Result: 目录含1142个不同码方案，其中1081个为LLM生成程序贡献的非基线方案；认证连通CSS实现[[96,4,10]]、[[140,6,10]]、[[180,4,14]]，并认证7个导入的Lin--Pryadko存档构造；连通性分析显示1142个条目中409个为不连通，且具有精确距离证书的类中73.1%含重复连通分量；最强精确连通PBB参数点为[[216,4,10]]，由两个不同分量类达到；LLM引导campaign中936个正距离CSS方案有816个（87.18%）达到d≥5，而随机对照为72.50--74.26%。

Conclusion: LLM引导campaign的认证产出率高于随机搜索，但随机对照覆盖更多连通类；结果将LLM引导发现拓展至更受限的码族，并为其最强候选提供了可复现的结构与精确距离说明。

Abstract: Building on our earlier program-evolution workflow guided by large language models (LLMs), we study weight-five bivariate bicycle (BB) and perturbed bivariate bicycle (PBB) codes. The resulting catalogue contains 1,142 distinct code proposals, including 1,081 nonbaseline proposals attributable to LLM-generated programs. Across the catalogue, we certify connected Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) realizations [[96,4,10]], [[140,6,10]], and [[180,4,14]]. A post-search comparison certifies seven imported Lin--Pryadko archive constructions. For leading parameter triples also represented in that archive, we provide exact distance evidence, explicit bivariate presentations, and verified component reductions. A basis-independent connectivity analysis identifies 409 of the 1,142 catalogue entries as disconnected and shows that 73.1\% of the classes with exact distance certificates contain repeated connected components. Algebraic analysis organizes the connected CSS classes into order-3, order-7, and order-15 cyclotomic-kernel strata. The strongest exact connected PBB parameter point is [[216,4,10]], attained by two distinct component classes. Among the 936 distinct CSS proposals from the LLM-guided campaign with a recorded positive distance, 816 (87.18\%) are certified at $d\geq5$. For comparison, three random-search controls each sample 6,444 CSS code proposals uniformly without replacement, using the same per-lattice and encoded-dimension sample counts as the LLM-guided campaign. In these controls, 4,672--4,785 proposals (72.50--74.26\%) meet the same criterion. The LLM-guided campaign has the higher certified yield, while the random controls cover more connected classes. Together, these results extend LLM-guided discovery to a more constrained code family and provide a reproducible structural and exact-distance account of its strongest candidates.

</details>


### [220] [Destroying and Preserving Measurement Incompatibility over a Quantum Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06642)
*Eric Chitambar,Yujie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用zonotope与zonoid的数学框架，完整刻画了二值测量的兼容性，给出了量子比特信道破坏不兼容性的充要条件，发现了不兼容性破坏信道的超激活现象，并证明了保不兼容性信道在同维情形下必为酉变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道对量子测量不兼容性的破坏与保持是量子信息中的基本问题，但此前缺乏系统性刻画。作者希望借助zonotope/zonoid的几何框架，统一研究破坏不兼容性与保持不兼容性两类信道，并揭示其非平凡性质。

Method: 采用zonotope与zonoid的数学框架刻画二值测量的兼容性；以此推导量子比特unital信道为不兼容性破坏信道的充要条件（对非unital信道给出充分条件）；通过构造与平行使用两个信道分析超激活现象；并利用信道维度关系证明保不兼容性信道在同维情形下等价于酉变换。

Result: 给出了qubit unital信道为不兼容性破坏信道的充要条件，并对非unital信道证明其为充分条件；证明了两个不兼容性破坏的qubit信道在并行使用时不再破坏不兼容性的超激活现象；将结果转化为量子steering：两个两量子比特Werner态副本可在噪声阈值1/2下被steer，而单个副本无法做到；证明了输入输出维度相同的信道保持一般不兼容性当且仅当其为酉变换。

Conclusion: zonoid框架为研究信道对测量不兼容性的作用提供了有效工具；不兼容性破坏性并非在并行组合下封闭，存在超激活效应，并在量子steering中具有可观测意义；保不兼容性信道在同维情形下被完全刻画为酉变换，而在输出维度更大时该结论不成立，作者据此给出了通用保不兼容性信道的猜想形式。

Abstract: A quantum channel is called incompatibility-breaking if it always destroys the incompatibility of quantum measurements. On the other extreme are incompatibility-preserving channels, which never destroy incompatibility. This work studies both types of channels through the mathematical framework of zonotopes and zonoids, an approach that provides a complete characterization of compatibility for dichotomic measurements and is particularly powerful in the qubit setting. Using the language of zonoids, we present necessary and sufficient conditions for when a qubit unital channel is incompatibility-breaking, which are also sufficient for nonunital channels. As one of our main results, we demonstrate the superactivation phenomenon of two incompatibility-breaking qubit channels that are no longer incompatibility-breaking when used in parallel. Translating this result into the task of quantum steering, it says that two copies of a two-qubit Werner state can be steered at noise threshold $1/2$, even though this is impossible using just one copy. For the problem of incompatibility preservation, we prove that a quantum channel with matching input and output dimensions will preserve general incompatibility if and only if it is a unitary transformation. When the output dimension is larger, this result no longer holds, and we offer a conjectured form of a general incompatibility-preserving channel.

</details>


### [221] [Kramers' Theorem Leaves a Topological Footprint in Classical Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06661)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Kai-Wen Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出用经典记录（而非量子态）来读取时间反演Θ的符号Θ²=±1，并给出基于符号签名（signature）拓扑整数的可观测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演Θ在半整数自旋上平方为-1，使每个态与其时间反演正交；此符号以往只能从量子对象中读出，而平衡态会丢失该信息。作者希望从经典记录中保留并读取这一符号。

Method: 对能分辨手性的探测器，测量“一段记录后接另一段记录的时间反演”的概率，用这些概率构造矩阵以刻画时间反演的上同调类Θ²=±1；矩阵的对角化/签名给出承载该符号的拓扑整数。

Result: 该矩阵的signature携带此符号，为拓扑整数，并在“发射与吸收”等量损耗下保持不变；在量子处理器上用可编程发射器约10³次测量即可读出。

Conclusion: 时间反演符号可作为拓扑不变量从经典记录中稳健提取，并对对称损耗免疫，为在量子处理器上高效读出该符号提供可行路径。

Abstract: Time reversal $Θ$ squares to $-1$ on a half-integer spin, so every state is orthogonal to its time reverse. This sign has been read only from quantum objects. The equilibrium state loses it, but the classical record keeps it. For a detector that resolves handedness, the probabilities that one stretch of record is followed by the time reverse of another measure the cohomology class of time reversal, $Θ^2=\pm1$. Their matrix carries this sign in its signature, a topological integer that survives equal emission and absorption losses. A programmed emitter on quantum processors reads it in about $10^3$ shots.

</details>


### [222] [Quantum Gibbs State Preparation via Relative Decoding: From Fast-Mixing Sources to Broader Target Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06667)
*Zhong-Xia Shang,Yufei Wang,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于同态多项式转导（DQI/HDQI 的推广）的相对解码方法，可将量子 Gibbs 态的制备保证（如快速混合、有隙相干制备路径）从源哈密顿量 H_A 转移到目标哈密顿量 H_B，并证明新保证由相对距离 d_rel（而非普通距离 d_ord）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于量子 Gibbs 态可证明制备保证（快速混合、有隙相干制备路径）的结果仅适用于受限的哈密顿量族，且通常需对每个新族重新推导。作者希望建立一个通用的转导机制，使制备保证能从一个已知可制备的源哈密顿量自动转移到新目标哈密顿量，从而扩大可高效制备 TFD 的哈密顿量类别。

Method: 将每个哈密顿量写成 m 个 Pauli 项之和，把乘积正比于恒等式的项子集（即关系）视为二元线性码：源为 K_A，目标为 K_B，Pauli 标签矩阵作为奇偶校验矩阵（如 DQI）。从 H_A 的规范热场双态（TFD）出发，通过 Bell 变换、可逆标签映射以及商码 K_B/K_A 的相干解码器，制备 H_B 的近似 TFD 及其 Gibbs 态。解码器只解析目标中源所不具备的关系，故可达逆温度由相对距离 d_rel（此类关系中最少的项数）决定，而非普通距离 d_ord（目标任意关系中最少项数，即 DQI/HDQI 均匀精确解码半径的上界）。

Result: 证明线性 d_rel 加上在线性半径处的高效解码可保证常数逆温度。作为示例，从一个已知可在任意有限温度下制备 TFD 的最近邻自旋链出发，使用稀疏经典奇偶校验矩阵可得到有界度、几何非局域、非对易的目标哈密顿量，其 d_ord=3 而 d_rel=Θ(m)。据作者所知，这给出了一类新的可高效制备的 TFD。

Conclusion: 相对解码建立了从源哈密顿量到目标哈密顿量的制备保证转导框架；关键在于用相对距离 d_rel 取代普通距离 d_ord 作为可达逆温度的控制量，d_rel 可远大于 d_ord。通过稀疏经典奇偶校验矩阵，可从可制备的最近邻自旋链构造出 d_ord=3、d_rel=Θ(m) 的有界度非局域非对易目标，从而得到一类新的可高效制备 TFD。

Abstract: Provable guarantees for preparing quantum Gibbs states, such as rapid mixing or gapped coherent preparation paths, are known only for restricted Hamiltonian families and usually must be re-derived for each new family. Building on homomorphic polynomial transduction, a generalization of decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) and Hamiltonian DQI (HDQI), we show how relative decoding transfers such a guarantee from a source Hamiltonian $H_A$ to a target $H_B$. Writing each Hamiltonian as a sum of $m$ Pauli terms, the subsets of terms whose product is proportional to the identity, its relations, form a binary linear code, $K_A$ for the source and $K_B$ for the target, with the Pauli-label matrix as parity-check matrix as in DQI. Starting from the canonical thermofield double (TFD) of $H_A$, a Bell transform, a reversible label map, and a coherent decoder for the quotient code $K_B/K_A$ prepare an approximate TFD of $H_B$, and hence its Gibbs state. Because this decoder only resolves target relations absent from the source, the reachable inverse temperature is set by the relative distance $d_{\rm rel}$, the fewest terms in any such relation. It can far exceed the ordinary distance $d_{\rm ord}$, the fewest terms in any target relation, which bounds the uniform exact decoding radius of DQI and HDQI. We show that linear $d_{\rm rel}$ with efficient decoding at linear radius certifies a constant inverse temperature. As an example, starting from a nearest-neighbor spin chain whose TFD is known to be preparable at every finite temperature, a sparse classical parity-check matrix yields bounded-degree, geometrically nonlocal, noncommuting targets with $d_{\rm ord}=3$ and $d_{\rm rel}=Θ(m)$. To our knowledge, this gives a new class of efficiently preparable TFDs.

</details>


### [223] [Climbing the Fusion Tree: Center Ordering and Decoding Hierarchy in Non-Abelian Mixed States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06692)
*Pablo Sala,Vlad Temkin,Cenke Xu,Daniel Podolsky,Ehud Altman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出研究非阿贝尔对称群强-弱自发对称破缺（sw-SSB）的可操作框架，将其映射为量子双模型D(G)在纯荷噪声下的解码任务，并推导出统计力学层级模型，预测强对称仅破缺到中心Z(G)乘以弱对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强-弱自发对称破缺此前主要在阿贝尔对称情形下研究，缺乏针对非阿贝尔对称群的一般性、可操作框架，也缺少与非阿贝尔拓扑码解码器构造的联系。

Method: 将sw-SSB表述为区分两个强对称态的解码任务（两态相差一对插入的不可约表示），并受强对称退相干破坏；对有限群G，该任务对偶于纯荷噪声下区分量子双D(G)的逻辑态；从嵌套区域上融合测量结果推导最优解码的统计力学层级模型，并进行张量网络计算（S_3、SU(2)）。

Result: 不可约表示作为冻结无序并满足类Nishimori条件；由于局域不可约表示无法决定全局融合，强G×G对称仅非局域实现，Z(G)平凡荷的表示可隐藏在噪声生成的表示中。S_3（中心平凡）第一层级无相变，所有表示保真度关联衰减到非零值；SU(2)呈Z_2相变，符合Nishimori-Ising普适类，自旋-1/2表示的保真度关联变为长程，阈值从第一层级到第二层级上升16%。

Conclusion: 强对称自发破缺到Z(G)乘以弱对称；非零错误率下唯一相变是中心的序化，其阈值随层级升高不下降，该结论对非幂零群在任意有限层级成立。结果为研究非阿贝尔sw-SSB及构造非阿贝尔拓扑码解码器提供系统框架。

Abstract: We develop an operational approach to strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (sw-SSB) of a non-Abelian symmetry group G. We formulate it as the decoding task of distinguishing two strongly symmetric states that differ by a pair of inserted irreps, after both are corrupted by strongly symmetric decoherence. For finite G, this task is dual to distinguishing logical states of the quantum double D(G) under pure-charge noise. We derive a hierarchy of statistical mechanics models describing optimal decoding from the fusion outcomes measured on successively larger nested regions. As for maximum-likelihood decoders of Abelian codes the measured irreps act as quenched disorder obeying a Nishimori-like condition. Strikingly, because local irreps do not determine how they fuse globally, the strong $G\times G$ symmetry is realized only nonlocally, and irreps trivially charged under Z(G) (the center of G) can hide inside noise-generated ones. As a result, the strong symmetry spontaneously breaks down to Z(G), times the weak symmetry. The only transition at nonzero error rate is then an ordering of the center, whose threshold cannot decrease as the hierarchy is ascended. This conclusion holds at any finite level of the hierarchy for non-nilpotent groups. We confirm these predictions with tensor-network calculations for $S_3$ and SU(2). At the first level, the $S_3$ model, whose center is trivial, shows no transition, and the fidelity correlations of all irreps provably decay to non-zero values. The SU(2) model exhibits a $Z_2$ transition consistent with the Nishimori-Ising universality class, where the fidelity correlations of spin-$1/2$ irreps become long-range. The threshold rises from the first to the second level of the hierarchy by $16\%$. Our results provide a systematic framework for studying non-Abelian sw-SSB and for constructing decoders for non Abelian topological codes.

</details>


### [224] [Measurement-free Preparation of Surface-Code States with Digital-Analog Counterdiabatic Drivings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06697)
*Shubham Kumar Balaganchi A. Bhargava,Paolo A. Erdman,Anne-Maria Visuri,Jose D. Martın-Guerrero,Yolanda Vives-Gilabert,Enrique Solano,Narendra N. Hegade*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种数字化-模拟混合方法，用于在超导体系中制备表面码基态流形，利用绝热规范势的结构性质将稳定子局域反绝热项映射为固定角度双比特XY门、单比特旋转与模拟交换相互作用构成的数字化-模拟模块，在短演化时间下显著提升基态制备效果，并将纠缠深度降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近邻量子纠错中，逻辑态初始化需要从天然的单体与双体控制中生成四体稳定子关联，而用浅电路制备稳定子码态是一个重要基本操作。现有裸绝热演化在短时间下制备精度不足，因此需要一种适配超导硬件、可降低纠缠深度的制备方案。

Method: 基于Sels-Polkovnikov绝热规范势框架，利用绝热规范势的结构性质，将稳定子局域反绝热项映射到超导布局上的数字化-模拟模块；这些模块由固定角度双比特XY门、单比特旋转和模拟交换相互作用演化组成，生成的 dressed 模块产生高阶算符，作为变分拟设来确定反绝热项。方法在棋盘格 plaquette 稳定子网格上进行基准测试，覆盖至4×4规模，其中方形网格对应旋转表面码，矩形情形用于探测扩展性，并进一步推广到n×m稳定子晶格。

Result: 对所有测试网格，方法均能生成主导的四体反绝热基；在短演化时间下，相比裸绝热演化显著提升了基态制备效果。推广到n×m稳定子晶格后，数字化-模拟合成可将纠缠深度降低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果在稳定子几何、反绝热规范势结构与数字化-模拟控制之间建立了直接联系，为近邻超导架构上无测量制备稳定子码态提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Preparing stabilizer-code states with shallow circuits is an important primitive for near-term quantum error correction in superconducting circuits, where logical-state initialization requires four-body stabilizer correlations from native one- and two-body controls. We introduce a digital-analog method for the preparation of surface-code ground-state manifolds. The method exploits a structural property of the adiabatic gauge potential, mapping stabilizer-local counterdiabatic terms to digital-analog blocks on the superconducting layout using fixed-angle two-qubit \(XY\) gates, single-qubit rotations, and analog exchange-interaction evolutions. The dressed blocks generate higher-order operators used as the variational ansatz to determine the counterdiabatic terms within the Sels-Polkovnikov adiabatic-gauge-potential framework. We benchmark checkerboard plaquette-stabilizer grids up to \(4\times4\), the square-grid instances correspond to rotated surface codes, while the rectangular cases probe the scaling of the method. For all grids, the proposed method generates the dominant four-body counterdiabatic basis and, at short evolution times substantially improves the ground-state preparation as compared with bare adiabatic evolution. We further generalize the construction to \(n\times m\) stabilizer lattices and show that digital-analog synthesis can reduce the entangling depth by an order of magnitude. Together, these results establish a direct connection between stabilizer geometry, the structure of the counterdiabatic gauge potential, and digital-analog control for measurement-free preparation of stabilizer-code states on near-term superconducting architecture.

</details>


### [225] [Random-Oracle Unitary Synthesis is Impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06700)
*Andrew Huang,Akshar Ramkumar,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子复杂度中的酉合成问题，提出"平均情形"酉合成：给定随机布尔函数的叠加访问，能否高效实现Haar随机酉。作者证明超多项式查询下界，表明该模型下酉合成不可行；同时否定回答了Brakerski与Yuen关于ROM-PRU模型可扩展伪随机酉的问题，并构造出O(N)-design。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 酉合成是量子复杂度的核心开放问题：若能在叠加下评估任意经典函数，是否可高效实现任意酉变换？受Brakerski与Yuen（CRYPTO 2026）工作启发，作者希望从"平均情形"角度重新审视该问题，并解决其提出的ROM-PRU模型下可扩展伪随机酉是否可实现的问题。

Method: 作者定义"平均情形"酉合成问题：目标是借助对随机布尔函数的访问，高效实现Haar随机酉。通过量子查询复杂度分析，证明该任务存在超多项式查询下界。针对ROM-PRU模型，作者进一步分析酉design的可达性，给出下界与构造性上界。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 在平均情形酉合成模型中证明超多项式查询下界，说明酉合成不可行；(2) 在ROM-PRU模型中，否定回答了Brakerski与Yuen的问题——对任意γ>0，无法高效实现n量子比特上的Ω(N^{1+γ})酉design（N=2^n）；(3) 正面构造：在同一模型中用poly(n)查询构造出O(N)-design，优于此前最优的O(√N)-design。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，即使在平均情形与随机函数访问的宽松设定下，通用酉合成仍受超多项式查询下界限制；同时明确了ROM-PRU模型中可扩展PRU的不可实现性，并显著提升了酉design的查询效率上界（O(N)-design）。

Abstract: A major open problem in quantum complexity is the question of unitary synthesis---namely, is it possible to efficiently implement any unitary, given the ability to evaluate any classical function in superposition? Inspired by a recent work by Brakerski and Yuen (CRYPTO 2026), we propose an ``average-case'' unitary synthesis problem, which asks whether it is possible to efficiently implement a Haar random unitary given access to a random Boolean function. We then demonstrate a superpolynomial query lower bound, establishing that unitary synthesis in this model is impossible.
  Along with our main result, we settle in the negative a question Brakerski and Yuen pose, of whether scalable pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs) can be implemented in the ROM-PRU model. In particular, we show that in the ROM-PRU model, no $Ω(N^{1+γ})$ unitary design on $n$ qubits, where $N=2^n$, can be efficiently achieved for any $γ> 0$. On the other hand, we construct an $O(N)$-design in $\text{poly}(n)$ queries in the same model, surpassing the previously best known results which constructed $O(\sqrt{N})$-designs.

</details>


### [226] [Optimal compression with quantum retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06702)
*Shyam Dhamapurkar,Mohit Garg,Manaswi Paraashar,Jaikumar Radhakrishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究将Hamming重量至多n的m位串压缩为s位串，使得可用至多t次量子查询无误地恢复任意原始比特；给出自适应查询下达到最优（差对数因子）的压缩方案，以及非自适应查询下空间复杂度接近最优的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子查询模型下研究带局部可恢复性的数据压缩，即压缩后仍需支持对原始串任意比特的高效无误读取，探索自适应与非自适应查询两种设定下压缩空间的最优性。

Method: 将输入串编码为较短串y的标准量子oracle形式；允许至多t次量子查询（自适应或非自适应）来恢复x的任意比特x_i且要求零错误；分别设计自适应查询编码方案与非自适应查询编码方案并分析其空间复杂度。

Result: 自适应查询下可实现至多相差对数因子的最优压缩；非自适应查询下方案空间对m的依赖达到最优（差对数因子），对n的依赖相对最优最多差二次方因子。

Conclusion: 量子查询模型下带逐比特可恢复性的压缩在自适应查询时可达近似最优，非自适应查询时亦能在m上近似最优、n上损失至多平方。

Abstract: We consider the following data compression problem. Given a string $x \in \{0,1\}^m$ of Hamming weight at most $n$, compress it into a shorter string $y \in \{0,1\}^s$ so that any bit $x_i$ of $x$ can be retrieved without any error using at most $t$ quantum queries to the standard oracle encoding of $y$. If queries are allowed to be adaptive we show how optimal compression up to a logarithmic factor can be achieved. If the queries are required to be made non-adaptively, we show schemes whose space is optimal in its dependence on $m$ except for a logarithmic factor, and is at most quadratically worse when compared to the optimum in its dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [227] [Out-of-control Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06709)
*Weiyuan Gong,Muzhou Ma,Sitan Chen,Jordan Cotler,Hsin-Yuan Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在极简访问模型下的哈密顿量学习：证明在统一态制备/测量下可重构任意图上一般2-局域哈密顿量全部参数，并在计算基制备/测量下对仅含Pauli X/Z相互作用的最邻近哈密顿量在1D/2D矩形晶格上可重构全部参数（至不可避免的规范自由度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具有最强可证明保证的哈密顿量学习算法依赖快速、任意的单比特门与任意基测量等量子控制能力，超越近期待用模拟量子模拟器的能力。作者受模拟原子与离子平台启发，研究在最简访问模型下能否学习多体系统哈密顿量。

Method: 研究两种受限访问设定：(1) 统一态制备与测量：每次实验可将每个量子比特旋转到相同态，进行短时演化，并在相同基下测量每个量子比特；(2) 计算基态制备与测量：制备与测量限于计算基。针对这些设定设计协议，引入求解具有大量参数的结构化多项式系统的新技术，用于参数重构。

Result: (1) 对任意相互作用图上的通用2-局域哈密顿量，惊奇地证明可从统一态制备/测量实验中重构所有参数；(2) 对仅含Pauli X/Z相互作用的最邻近哈密顿量（涵盖当代Rydberg原子平台），在1D与2D矩形晶格上证明可从计算基实验中重构所有参数，直至不可避免的规范自由度。

Conclusion: 即使在最严苛的实验约束下，也能从量子多体系统动力学中学习到大量信息；所提协议为求解大规模参数化结构化多项式系统提供了新方法。

Abstract: Learning the Hamiltonian of a many-body system from its dynamics is a central task in quantum science, yet the algorithms with the strongest provable guarantees assume some level of quantum control--fast, arbitrary single-qubit gates interleaved with time evolution, and measurements in arbitrary bases--that is beyond the capabilities of near-term analog quantum simulators. Motivated by analog atom- and ion-based platforms, we study Hamiltonian learning under minimal access models.
  Uniform state preparation and measurements: We first consider the setting where in every experiment, one can rotate each qubit to the same state, perform short-time evolution, and measure every qubit in the same basis. Surprisingly, we show that for generic 2-local Hamiltonians on any interaction graph, all of the parameters can be reconstructed from such experiments.
  Computational basis state preparation and measurements: We then consider a similarly constrained setting, but where state preparation and measurement are restricted to the computational basis. For nearest-neighbor Hamiltonians with only Pauli $X/Z$ interactions, a class which captures contemporary Rydberg atom platforms, we show that over 1D and 2D rectangular lattices, all of the parameters can be reconstructed from such experiments up to unavoidable gauges.
  Our protocols introduce new techniques for solving structured polynomial systems over an extensive number of parameters. Taken together, our results suggest that one can learn a great deal from the dynamics of quantum many-body systems even under the most stringent experimental constraints.

</details>


### [228] [Tight Query Lower Bounds for Quantum Sampling, with an Application to Certified Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06721)
*Keshav Bhateja,Mehdi Esmaili,Atul Mantri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了线性交叉熵基准(XEB)高分能对不可信量子设备证明什么。在量子查询模型中证明：诚实采样器的理想分数构成了高效设备与可证随机性的Tsirelson界。超越理想分数一个常数需Ω(N^{1/3})次查询（Haar与Fourier预言机系综），且该界紧致；同时证明了多项式查询设备输出具有n-O(log n)比特的平滑最小熵，几乎最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机电路采样实验是量子计算优势的主要展示，其验证依赖线性交叉熵基准(XEB)。然而，高XEB分数究竟能对不可信量子设备"证明"什么，缺乏严格的理论刻画。本文旨在从量子查询复杂性角度，给出理想分数作为上界（Tsirelson型界）的严格证明，并量化其可证随机性。

Method: 在量子查询模型下，针对Haar随机态与Fourier采样两类预言机系综展开分析。核心是构造"进展度量"(progress measure)，用以界定相对于理想分数的超出量，且每次查询该度量仅微增。对Fourier采样，度量取净化随机函数中去配对相干振幅的范数，结合多项式方法与压缩预言机技术；对Haar随机态，度量取Dirichlet正交多项式展开中平均阶数的平方根，并由单次观测效应的精确恒等式控制。同时给出碰撞分布采样的查询复杂度并构造相干算法。

Result: 第一，超越理想分数一个常数需Ω(N^{1/3})次查询（Haar与Fourier系综，N为输出数），该界紧致，证明了此前关于Haar随机态的猜想并推广至Fourier采样。第二，对Fourier采样及经规范态制备预言机访问的Haar随机态，多项式查询且分数在理想分数o(1)内的设备，其n比特输出具有n-O(log n)比特平滑最小熵，即使对手与设备纠缠并事后获知预言机，该结果几乎最优。推论：碰撞分布采样查询复杂度为Θ(N^{1/3})，同一算法的相干版本可近似制备均匀振幅模的量子Hadamard积。

Conclusion: 理想XEB分数充当量子查询模型下的Tsirelson界，为高效设备可达到的分数设定了上限，并由此建立了近乎最优的可证随机性。所提出的进展度量方法（结合多项式方法、压缩预言机与Dirichlet正交多项式）为量子优势实验的基准验证提供了严格的复杂性理论基础。

Abstract: Random circuit sampling experiments, the leading demonstrations of quantum computational advantage, are tested with the linear cross-entropy benchmark (XEB), which scores outcomes by their ideal probabilities. We study what a high XEB score certifies about an untrusted quantum device. In the quantum query model, we show that the honest sampler's ideal score acts as a Tsirelson bound for efficient devices and certified randomness. First, exceeding the ideal score by a constant requires $Ω(N^{1/3})$ queries for the Haar and Fourier oracle ensembles with $N$ outcomes, and this bound is tight; this proves an earlier conjecture for Haar-random states and extends it to Fourier sampling. Second, for Fourier sampling and for Haar-random states accessed through the canonical state-preparation oracle, the $n$-bit output of a device that makes polynomially many queries and scores within $o(1)$ of the ideal score has $n-O(\log n)$ bits of smooth min-entropy, even against an adversary who is entangled with the device and later learns the oracle; this is nearly optimal. As a corollary, sampling the collision distribution, which is proportional to the square of the ideal distribution, has query complexity $Θ(N^{1/3})$, and a coherent version of the same algorithm approximately prepares quantum Hadamard products of states with uniform amplitude magnitudes. Both lower bounds rest on progress measures that bound the excess over the ideal score and increase only slightly with each query. For Fourier sampling the measure is the norm of the coherent amplitudes for removing pairs in a purification of the random function, combining the polynomial method with the compressed oracle technique; for Haar-random states it is the square root of the average level in an expansion in Dirichlet orthogonal polynomials, controlled by an exact identity for the effect of one observation.

</details>


### [229] [Certifying Measurement Incompatibility under Bounded Classical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06722)
*Oxana Shaya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出“并集极大泄漏”（union maximal leakage），用于量化用经典通信解释所有观测概率所需的信息量，并证明其可作为测量不相容性与贝尔非局域性的判据，同时给出面向发送方的私密随机性认证与可组合安全提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量不相容是通信与态分辨中量子优势的来源，也是贝尔非局域性的必要条件。然而，经典通信会影响接收方结果，因此在认证测量不相容时必须将其纳入考虑；需要一个能从观测概率直接计算、且能同时刻画经典通信与共享资源影响的量化工具。

Method: 引入并集极大泄漏（union maximal leakage），通过线性规划从观测量中计算，用以刻画对所有观测概率给出共同经典解释所需的信息量。分析在至多 k 比特消息先于接收方独立测量选择的情形下，各消息对应相容测量的并集极大泄漏不超过 k，并证明该界在任意与输入无关的非信号共享资源下成立。进一步将该量用于判定用相容测量加任意非信号辅助重现观测所需的最小经典通信量，并结合贝尔非局域性条件分析违反的可能性。对二元接收方结果，推导关于发送方的条件熵界，并在含设备记忆的序列协议中建立可组合安全的随机性提取。

Result: 若消息至多 k 比特，则各消息相容测量的并集极大泄漏至多 k，且该界在输入无关的非信号共享资源下成立；在量子理论中，违反该界还意味着初始共享态是纠缠的。并集极大泄漏确定了用相容测量与任意非信号辅助重现观测所需的最小经典通信量。对固定共享量子态与接收方测量，当且仅当某个消息取值下接收方测量相对于该态上的二元发送方测量呈贝尔非局域时，才可能违反。对二元接收方结果，违反还可认证对发送方私密的随机性，即使接收方测量依赖发送方消息且发送方保留量子边信息；该隐私由显式条件熵界量化，并可在含设备记忆的序列协议中实现可组合安全的随机性提取。

Conclusion: 并集极大泄漏为在经典通信存在下认证测量不相容与贝尔非局域性提供了可计算且紧致的框架，并给出对发送方私密的随机性量化与可组合安全提取方案，适用于含设备记忆的序列协议。

Abstract: Measurement incompatibility enables quantum advantages in communication and state discrimination. It is also necessary for Bell nonlocality, which is used in device-independent protocols for quantum key distribution and private randomness generation. Classical communication can influence the receiver's outcome and must therefore be accounted for when certifying measurement incompatibility. We introduce union maximal leakage, which quantifies the information needed for a common classical explanation of all observed probabilities and is computed from them by a linear program. If a message of at most $k$ bits precedes the receiver's independent measurement choice, compatible measurements for each message give union maximal leakage at most $k$. This bound holds with any input-independent nonsignalling shared resource. In quantum theory, a violation also implies that the initially shared state is entangled. Conversely, union maximal leakage determines the minimum classical communication needed to reproduce the observations with compatible measurements and arbitrary nonsignalling assistance. For a fixed shared quantum state and receiver measurements, a violation is possible exactly when, for some message value, the receiver's measurements are Bell-nonlocal against two-outcome sender measurements on that state. For binary receiver outcomes, we show that a violation also certifies randomness private from the sender, even when the receiver's measurement depends on the sender's message and the sender retains quantum side information. We quantify this privacy through an explicit conditional entropy bound and establish composably secure randomness extraction in sequential protocols with device memory.

</details>


### [230] [Approximate cloning of structured pure states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06723)
*Yaroslav Herasymenko,Junqiao Lin,Philip Verduyn Lunel,Dmitry Grinko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化量子态族的近似克隆样本复杂度，构造了费米子高斯态/Slater态与玻色子高斯态的最优及显式克隆信道，将复杂度从指数级降至多项式级，并给出相位态独立构造及框架外解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子不可克隆定理表明，对任意纯态的N→N+1近似克隆，所需副本数N与希尔伯特空间维数成正比，对多数感兴趣的系统而言过于庞大。因此作者试图探究：若输入态被承诺属于具有结构的特定族，克隆所需样本复杂度能否显著降低。

Method: 采用表示论框架统一处理费米子与玻色子高斯态克隆，该框架推广了Werner及Chiribella-Yang的工作，并给出克隆信道的幺正实现。对相位态则独立构造最优克隆保真度信道。此外，通过在高N极限下对框架势(frame potential)进行受控鞍点评估，分析克隆复杂度随族流形维数的线性关系。

Result: 对费米子(包括n模费米子高斯态与Slater态)，构造出最优克隆信道，将复杂度从关于n的指数级降至多项式级；对玻色子高斯态，构造显式克隆信道证明多项式复杂度，且不限制态的能量，但该信道一般不最优。这些结果由单一表示论框架导出。同时，鞍点计算解释了多族克隆复杂度与族流形维数的线性关系，并同样适用于层析(tomography)复杂度的类似标度。对相位态给出最优克隆保真度信道的独立构造。

Conclusion: 结构化量子态族可显著降低近似克隆的样本复杂度：费米子与玻色子高斯态达到多项式复杂度，相位态亦获最优构造。所提表示论框架统一并推广了先前结果，且鞍点评估揭示了克隆与层析复杂度对流形维数的线性标度规律，补充了近期关于稳定子态克隆的研究。

Abstract: The no-cloning theorem is a cornerstone result in quantum mechanics that forbids copying of general quantum information. More quantitatively, even given $N$ copies of an unknown state, any channel producing an $(N+1)$-copy state incurs a nonzero trace-distance error on some inputs.
