Turning this around, one can ask about the sample complexity of approximate $N\rightarrow N+1$ cloning: for a desired small error $ε$, what $N$ suffices? For arbitrary pure states on a Hilbert space $H$, the answer is $N\simeq \dim H/ε$ - astronomically large for most $H$ of interest. What if the input is promised to lie in a structured family? We examine a range of families fundamental to many-body physics and quantum information: $n$-mode fermionic Gaussian and Slater states, $n$-mode bosonic Gaussian states, and qudit phase states. For fermions, we construct optimal cloning channels, reducing the complexity from exponential to polynomial in $n$. For bosonic Gaussian states, we construct an explicit cloner that certifies polynomial complexity, without any constraint on the energy of the state; this channel, however, is not optimal in general. The fermionic and bosonic results follow from a single representation-theoretic framework, which generalizes previous work by Werner and by Chiribella and Yang; it also yields the cloners' unitary implementation. Furthermore, we explain why for many families the cloning complexity is linear in the dimension of the family manifold, via a controlled saddle-point evaluation of the frame potential at high $N$. The same calculation accounts for the analogous scaling of tomography complexity. The above framework does not cover phase states; for these we give an independent construction of a channel with optimal cloning fidelity. Our work complements recent results on cloning stabilizer states.

</details>


### [231] [Random Order in Quantum Streaming: Replenishment and Robust Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06727)
*Nadezhda Voronova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究随机顺序如何改变量子内存在流式计算中的作用，提出"补充（replenishment）"概念，并构造了在均匀随机顺序下一趟量子算法仅需多对数空间、但在经典随机顺序或量子"更新先于查询"设置下无条件需要多项式空间的分离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常流式计算中量子内存一旦被早期查询消耗便不可复用，但随机顺序可能让后续经典输入"补充"已消耗的量子态。作者希望刻画这种随机顺序对量子内存价值的影响，并厘清量子流式算法相对经典算法在空间上的优势边界。

Method: 构造基于Hidden Matching的人工问题（重复坐标数据+在线匹配请求）；强化Gilboa-Jain-McClean关于Multiple Hidden Matching的可消耗性界；将Kallaugher的三角形计数算法适配到每条边等频重复的均匀随机流，重建量子草图并重采样经典估计器；将Assadi-Sundaresan的鲁棒Noisy Gap Cycle框架推广到量子流式，并使用Block Hidden XOR的量子通信下界。

Result: 该人工问题在均匀随机顺序下存在使用多对数空间的一趟量子算法，但经典随机顺序下以及量子"所有更新先于请求"设置下均无条件需要多项式空间。下界方面：无预先纠缠时，支持r个独立匹配请求的量子编码需Ω(r)量子比特（r≤N^{1/2-δ}，δ∈(0,1/2)），顺序请求时线性界延伸到r=Θ(√N)。三角形计数算法在合适参数区间改进了期望空间界。Block Hidden XOR下界给出随机边序下Ω(n)空间下界，影响若干图问题。

Conclusion: 随机顺序能够实现小型量子表示的"补充"，从而赋能某些任务的量子优势；但对其他任务，显著的空间需求依然存在。这揭示了随机顺序在量子流式计算中的双重作用。

Abstract: How can random order change the role of quantum memory in streaming? Later classical input can restore the usefulness of a quantum state consumed by earlier queries. We call this replenishment.
  We construct an artificial problem based on Hidden Matching, with repeated coordinate data and online matching requests. It admits a one-pass quantum algorithm using polylogarithmic space in uniformly random order, but unconditionally requires polynomial space both classically in random order and quantumly when all updates precede the requests.
  To prove the quantum lower bound, we strengthen the consumability bounds of Gilboa, Jain, and McClean for Multiple Hidden Matching. Without prior entanglement, any quantum encoding supporting $r$ independent matching requests requires $Ω(r)$ qubits for $r\le N^{1/2-δ}$ and every fixed $δ\in(0,1/2)$, even with simultaneous revelation and arbitrary joint decoding. For sequential requests, the linear bound extends through $r=Θ(\sqrt N)$.
  We also adapt Kallaugher's triangle-counting algorithm to uniformly random streams in which every edge is repeated equally often. Rebuilding the quantum sketch and resampling the classical estimator improve its expected-space bound in suitable parameter regimes.
  Finally, we extend the robust Noisy Gap Cycle framework of Assadi and Sundaresan to quantum streaming. A quantum communication lower bound for Block Hidden XOR yields an $Ω(n)$ space lower bound in random edge order for large enough cycles, with consequences for several graph problems.
  Thus random order can enable replenishment of small quantum representations, while substantial space requirements persist for other tasks.

</details>


### [232] [Qubit CSS codes with Universal Transversal Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06730)
*Arpit Dua,Adam Holmes,Michael J. Gullans,Victor V. Albert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 深度一（depth-one）分层逻辑门可绕过Eastin-Knill定理限制，在纠错码上生成通用逻辑门集，无需魔态蒸馏等额外组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eastin-Knill定理排除了在单量子比特纠错码上实现通用横向逻辑门集，但该定理一次仅约束一个比特划分。作者希望突破这一限制，寻找无需魔态蒸馏、码切换等昂贵组件的通用逻辑门方案。

Method: 让每个深度一少比特门层各自遵循其自身的比特划分，多层组合即可生成通用逻辑门集；每个逻辑门生成元为单个深度一层后接同一稳定子码的纠错。通过将纠缠电路作用于一个允许深度一非Clifford门的低距离"种子"码及合适的"核"码来构造此类码。

Result: 构造了47个具有深度一生成集的码，每层至少容忍一个故障门，其中包括一个[[37,1,7]]码和一个[[59,1,9]]码，两者校验权重不超过8。

Conclusion: 深度一分层门方法可绕过Eastin-Knill定理，实现通用逻辑门集，且无需魔态蒸馏、码切换、规范固定、晶格手术等组件。

Abstract: The Eastin-Knill theorem rules out a universal set of transversal logical gates on any code that detects single-qubit errors. The theorem constrains one partition of the qubits at a time. We show that depth-one layers of few-qubit gates, each respecting its own partition, can together generate a universal logical gate set on an error-correcting code. Each logical gate generator is then a single depth-one layer followed by error correction of the same stabilizer code, with no magic-state distillation, code switching, gauge fixing, lattice surgery, or other gadgets. We show that such codes can be found by applying an entangling circuit to a lower-distance "seed" code admitting a depth-one non-Clifford gate and an appropriate "kernel" code. We construct 47 codes with depth-one generating sets that tolerate at least one faulty gate per layer, including a [[37,1,7]] and a [[59,1,9]] code, both of check weight at most eight.

</details>


### [233] [Streaming Regev's Quantum Factoring Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06733)
*Katherine Van Kirk,Craig Gidney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了 Regev 因式分解算法中相位估计控制量子比特无法"回收流式化"的问题，通过叠加掩码与 Fibonacci 幂相位估计两项改进，将空间降至 n+M(n)+O(√n)，用 Luo 等人的原地乘法器可仅为 4n+O(√n) 个量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Regev 因式分解算法的主要实用障碍是空间占用大，此前被认为无法与"量子比特回收"（qubit recycling）兼容，即无法将相位估计中的控制比特流式处理而非全部同时存储。Ragavan 与 Vaikuntanathan 用 Fibonacci 累加器将比特数从 O(n^1.5) 降到 11.32n，但仍需额外编码转换与反计算。

Method: 在 Ragavan–Vaikuntanathan 的 Fibonacci 累加器构造基础上做两点改进：(1) 采用叠加掩码（superposition masking）阻止无用信息进入累加器，从而省去反计算（uncomputation）；(2) 用酉算子的 Fibonacci 幂而非 2 的幂执行相位估计，避免原构造所需的多种编码转换。

Result: 改进后相位估计量子比特在电路中仅被使用一次，因而可被流式回收。空间占用降至 n+M(n)+O(√n)，其中 M(n) 为原地乘法所需空间。例如配合 Luo 等人（2026）的原地乘法器，Regev 算法可用 4n+O(√n) 个量子比特分解 n 位整数。

Conclusion: 该构造虽仍不及 Shor 算法主流实现的 n/2+εn 比特，但已是迄今 Regev 算法空间最低的实例化方案，表明 Regev 算法在空间优化方面仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: A major practical obstacle to Regev's factoring algorithm is its large space usage. In particular, the algorithm was believed to be incompatible with "qubit recycling," which streams the control qubits in phase estimation rather than storing them all simultaneously. We show how to fix this streaming problem. We build on the construction of Ragavan and Vaikuntanathan, which used Fibonacci accumulators to reduce the qubit count from $O(n^{1.5})$ to $11.32n$. We make two key improvements. First, we use superposition masking to prevent unwanted information from entering the accumulators, removing the need for uncomputation. Second, we show how to perform phase estimation using Fibonacci powers of a unitary operation rather than powers of two. This avoids various encoding conversions required by the construction of Ragavan and Vaikuntanathan. After these improvements, the phase-estimation qubits are used only once in the circuit, allowing them to be streamed.
  Our improvements reduce the space usage of Regev's factoring algorithm to $n+M(n)+O(\sqrt{n})$, where $M(n)$ is the space required for an in-place multiplication. For example, using the in-place multiplier of Luo et al. (2026), Regev's algorithm can factor an $n$-bit integer using $4n+O(\sqrt{n})$ qubits. Although our construction still falls short of the $n/2+εn$ qubits sufficient for leading implementations of Shor's algorithm, it is the lowest-space instantiation of Regev's algorithm to date and suggests that Regev's algorithm is still far from fully optimized.

</details>


### [234] [Characterizing Photonic Partial Distinguishability Through Gram Matrix Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06736)
*Marcin Kotowski,Marco Robbio,Oliver Reardon-Smith,Benoit Seron,Leonardo Novo,Ernesto F. Galvão,Michał Oszmaniec*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"Gram矩阵层析"方法，可对独立制备的（近）纯内部态光子重建其Gram矩阵，从而预测对内部自由度不敏感的实验统计，样本复杂度与内部希尔伯特空间维度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可区分性影响多光子干涉，是Boson Sampling等大规模光子实验及多光子量子技术应用的核心。然而，导致该行为的内部自由度难以表征：常无法直接访问，其维度甚至物理本质也可能先验未知。因此需要一种操作性强、鲁棒的表征手段。

Method: 提出Gram矩阵层析：针对纯或近纯内部态的独立制备光子。对纯态而言，Gram矩阵包含预测不敏感于内部自由度的实验统计所需的全部信息。利用可测量的Bargmann不变量（编码重叠模长与集体相位），以二次数量重建该矩阵（至物理无关的相位选择）。获取方式有两种互补途径：定向傅里叶干涉仪，以及在单个随机干涉仪中测量的两体与三体光子数关联。

Result: 推导了显式误差界，控制任意后续对内部态不敏感实验中预测分布与实际结果分布之间的全变差距离；该保证计及有限采样与纯度偏差。协议总样本复杂度与内部希尔伯特空间维度无关，在对内部态重叠的温和假设下为多项式级。

Conclusion: 该协议仅使用标准干涉测量，将部分可区分光子的表征转化为稳健且具有操作意义的刻画，为多光子干涉实验提供了可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Partial distinguishability affects multiphoton interference, which is central to large-scale photonic experiments such as Boson Sampling and to applications of multiphoton states across quantum technologies. Characterizing the internal degrees of freedom responsible for this behavior is challenging: in many scenarios they cannot be accessed directly and even their dimension or physical nature may be unknown \emph{a priori}. Here we introduce Gram-matrix tomography for independently prepared photons with pure or nearly pure internal states. For pure states, the Gram matrix contains all information required to predict the statistics of experiments that are insensitive to the internal degrees of freedom. We reconstruct this matrix, up to physically irrelevant phase choices, from a quadratic number of measurable Bargmann invariants (quantities encoding overlap magnitudes and collective phases). We access these quantities using two complementary approaches: targeted Fourier interferometers and two- and three-body photon-number correlations measured in a single random interferometer. We derive explicit error bounds that control the total-variation distance between predicted and actual outcome distributions for any subsequent experiment that is insensitive to the internal states. These guarantees account for finite sampling and deviations from purity. Crucially, the total sample complexity of the protocol is independent of the internal Hilbert-space dimension and polynomial under mild assumptions on overlaps of internal states. Our protocol uses standard interferometric measurements and turns them into a robust and operationally grounded characterization of partially distinguishable photons.

</details>


### [235] [Anti-symmetrization is cheap and useful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06737)
*Adam Bene Watts,John Bostanci,Matthias C. Caro,Daniel Grier,Sihan Liu,Gaurav Mahajan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究未知混合量子态的反称化任务，提出了两种反称化算法，并将其应用于单向态生成器归约、Schur采样及纯度放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反称化是量子信息处理中的基础操作，但针对未知混合态的实用反称化方法尚不完善。作者希望设计出样本复杂度低、操作简单、甚至能在流式设置下运行的反称化流程，并借此改进若干下游量子信息任务。

Method: 给定秩为 $r$ 的 qudit 态 $\rho$ 的独立同分布副本，目标制备正比于 $\Pi_{\mathrm{anti}}^r (|\varphi_1\rangle\otimes\ldots\otimes|\varphi_r\rangle)$ 的态。第一种算法仅使用受控-SWAP 与常数层单量子比特门，消耗 $\widetilde{O}(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 个副本，且适用于小工作内存的流式场景；第二种非流式算法借助 Schur-Weyl 对偶工具，消耗 $O(r/\lambda_{\min})$ 个副本。

Result: 流式反称化算法给出了从带隙混合态单向态生成器到纯态单向态生成器的归约；非流式算法作为构建模块，用于弱 Schur 采样与酉 Schur 采样，在副本数 $n$ 与局域维度 $d$ 的某些区间上优于已有方法，并给出了 qudit 态的最优纯度放大算法。

Conclusion: 反称化可在低样本复杂度与简单量子操作下实现，且作为通用原语能有效提升单向态生成器归约、Schur 采样和纯度放大等任务的性能。

Abstract: We consider the computational task of anti-symmetrization of an unknown mixed quantum state: Given iid copies of a rank-$r$ qudit state $ρ=\sum_{i=1}^r λ_i |\varphi_i\rangle\!\langle\varphi_i|$, prepare a copy of the state proportional to $Π_{\mathrm{anti}}^r (|\varphi_1\rangle\otimes \ldots\otimes|\varphi_r\rangle)$, with $Π_{\mathrm{anti}}^r$ the anti-symmetric subspace projector. We first give a simple algorithm for anti-symmetrization that uses $\widetilde{O}(r / λ_{\min})$ copies of $ρ$, where $λ_{\min}$ is the minimum non-zero eigenvalue of $ρ$. This algorithm works even in the streaming setting with small working memory, and it uses only simple quantum operations like controlled-SWAPs and constantly many layers of single-qubit gates. As an application of this streaming anti-symmetrization procedure, we give a reduction from gapped mixed-state one-way state generators to pure-state one-way state generators. Then, using tools from Schur-Weyl duality, we give and analyze a second (non-streaming) anti-symmetrization procedure that uses $O(r / λ_{\min})$ copies of $ρ$, which then serves as a building block in new algorithms for weak Schur sampling and unitary Schur sampling, outperforming existing approaches in some regimes of number of copies $n$ and local dimension $d$, as well as in an algorithm for optimal purity amplification for qudit states.

</details>


### [236] [Free entropy and quantum minimum description length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06741)
*Patrick Hayden,Alexander Maloney,Jinzhao Wang,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在有限维、有限分辨率下定义"物理自由熵"，证明其（相差一个显式常数）直接刻画了在仅有已知特征值、未知特征基的情形下编程多份量子态所需的最小量子内存，即"量子最小描述长度"，并讨论其与量子熵的差异及潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Voiculescu自由熵是Shannon熵的非交换类比，用于计数逼近算子的矩阵微观态，但此前在量子理论中缺乏物理意义。作者希望赋予其物理内涵，并将其与量子信息中的资源度量联系起来。

Method: 定义有限维、有限分辨率下的"物理自由熵"，构造一个变体的Schumacher压缩任务：待压缩态的启发式信息为已知特征值但未知特征基；以最小量子内存（量子最小描述长度）为最优代价，分析物理自由熵与该代价之间的定量关系。

Result: 物理自由熵可直接刻画（在相差一个显式常数意义下）编程多份该状态所需的最小量子内存，即量子最小描述长度；同时阐明自由熵区别于各类量子熵（如密度算子的量子熵）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为自由熵赋予明确的量子物理意义，将其确立为量子最小描述长度的度量，并指出其在量子物理中的潜在应用前景。

Abstract: In free probability, Voiculescu's free entropy counts matrix microstates that approximate an operator. It is a non-commutative analog of the Shannon entropy, distinct from any quantum entropy of a density operator, but it has had no physical meaning in quantum theory. We define a finite-dimensional physical free entropy at finite resolution and show that it directly characterizes, up to an explicit constant, the minimal quantum memory needed to program many copies of a state with known eigenvalues and unknown eigenbasis. This task is a variant of Schumacher compression, and we call its optimal cost the quantum minimum description length. We briefly discuss how free entropy differs from quantum entropies and its potential applications in quantum physics.

</details>


### [237] [Quantum minimum description of density matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06742)
*Patrick Hayden,Alexander Maloney,Jinzhao Wang,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究 Schumacher 压缩的变体：在已知谱、未知本征基且不保留纯化的情况下，压缩密度矩阵多副本所需的最小内存代价，并给出其加性常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Schumacher 压缩假设本征基已知；当本征基未知且不要求保留纯化时，压缩代价的精确刻画（含加性常数）此前不明确，且与通用无损编码开销的关系有待建立。

Method: 可达性：将 Werner 克隆映射推广到 GL(d, C) 不可约表示，并用最高权差与行间隙控制有限迹距离界。逆命题：在谱间隙假设下，对不可约群轨道使用 Koashi--Imoto 不可压缩性的定量形式。另提供 Lean 形式化证书。

Result: 对固定维度和互异正特征值，得到了该压缩任务的精确代价（通过其加性常数刻画）；结果与通用无损编码开销建立联系，并在配套快报中阐明其与自由熵的关系。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了未知本征基下密度矩阵压缩的最小内存代价，连接了量子最小描述长度与通用编码开销，并给出可机器验证的 Lean 证明证书。

Abstract: We study a variant of Schumacher compression. The task asks for the minimal memory cost for compressing many copies of a density matrix with known spectrum and unknown eigenbasis, without preserving its purification. For fixed dimension and distinct positive eigenvalues, we obtain the cost through its additive constant. Achievability uses a generalization of Werner's cloning map to $\mathrm{GL}(d,\mathbb C)$ irreducible representations, with finite trace-distance bounds controlled by highest-weight differences and row gaps. The matching converse follows from a quantitative form of Koashi--Imoto incompressibility for irreducible group orbits under a spectral-gap assumption. We also identify the relation between this quantum minimum description length and universal lossless coding overhead, and its connection to free entropy is explained in a companion letter. We provide a Lean certificate for our proofs.

</details>


### [238] [Efficient Logic with Ultra-High-Rate Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06749)
*Nishad Maskara,Rohan Mehta,Zhiyang He,Varun Menon,J. Pablo Bonilla Ataides,Mikhail D. Lukin,Hengyun Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对超高码率量子LDPC码，提出利用群对称性将逻辑量子比特划分为轨道，并通过并行手术实现可编程的逻辑Pauli乘积测量；引入chain-map convolution技术以高效提升时空距离，构造了低时空开销的并行测量gadget，并支持培养魔态注入与子群限制下的可编程性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码有望降低容错的量子比特开销，近期超高码率构造（编码率超过1/2）进一步推动了这一方向。然而，这类超高码率码缺乏快速、并行的逻辑计算方案，其逻辑操作的时空开销成为实现高效容错量子架构的瓶颈，因此需要开发适用于超高码率码的低开销逻辑计算与测量方法。

Method: 首先利用码的群对称性将逻辑量子比特划分为轨道；随后采用并行手术方法实现群不变的逻辑Pauli乘积测量集合，这些测量具有高度可编程性，可在满足每条测量在整条轨道上重复的条件下任意耦合逻辑轨道。为降低空间与时间开销，提出chain-map convolution技术，利用群结构高效地同时提升协议的空間距离与时间距离。进一步通过子群限制扩展可编程性。

Result: 针对[[256, 68, 12]]、[[576, 148, 18]] pair-partition码构造了保距的并行测量gadget，并为[[1152, 580, 12]]码率1/2的码构造了相应gadget，空间开销仅约2.5倍，每轮需3-4轮syndrome extraction。相同方法亦支持将培养的魔态并行注入高码率码中。子群限制揭示了逻辑控制与容错所需时空资源之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计低时空开销的qLDPC逻辑操作提供了新方法，表明结构化并行性在降低开销中起关键作用，并为更高效的容错量子架构开辟了道路。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes offer a promising route to reducing the qubit overhead of fault tolerance, with recent ultra-high-rate constructions achieving encoding rates above one half. In this work, we develop fast and parallel logical computation methods for ultra-high-rate codes. We first utilize the group symmetries of the codes to partition the logical qubits into orbits, and then apply parallel surgery methods to implement group-invariant collections of logical Pauli-product measurements. These measurements are highly programmable, as we may arbitrarily couple logical orbits as long as each measurement is repeated across the whole orbit(s). To reduce the space and time overheads, we develop a technique called chain-map convolution, which uses the group structure to boost both the spatial and temporal distances of our protocol in a highly efficient manner. As case studies, we construct distance-preserving parallel measurement gadgets for the [[256, 68, 12]], [[576, 148, 18]] pair-partition codes, and a [[1152, 580, 12]] rate-$1/2$ code, with only around $2.5\times$ space overhead and 3-4 rounds of syndrome extraction each. The same methods also enable parallel injection of cultivated magic states into high-rate codes. Finally, we extend programmability through subgroup restrictions, revealing a tradeoff between logical control and the spacetime resources required for fault tolerance and highlighting the crucial role structured parallelism plays in reducing overhead. These results provide new methods for designing qLDPC logical operations with low spacetime overhead, opening a route toward more efficient fault-tolerant quantum architectures.

</details>


### [239] [Discovering New Problems for Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06753)
*Rhik Mazumder,Pradeep Niroula*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为解码量子干涉（DQI）建立"健全性规则"，用码参数、解码保证与经典基线筛选候选问题，证明在最坏Hamming解码下DQI相对Prange的裕度上限为(√2−1)/2，并给出实值目标的性能律，据此发现两个候选应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI借助经典纠错码求解优化问题，但难以判断哪些问题能带来量子优势，缺乏系统的筛选准则与理论边界。

Method: 发展健全性规则，从码参数、解码保证和经典基线三方面筛查候选问题；针对计数满足约束的标准目标，分析最坏情况Hamming解码下DQI保证与Prange completion的差距上限；推导实值目标的性能律（目标函数同时决定解质量与解码器需纠正的错误）；并用AI智能体搜索在上述规则指导下寻找候选应用。

Result: 证明最坏情况Hamming解码下DQI保证相对Prange completion的超出量至多为(√2−1)/2，最优多项式相交（OPI）渐近达到该裕度上限，故该设定下无候选能超过OPI。实值目标性能律表明目标同时固定解质量与待纠正错误。AI搜索发现两个候选应用：McEliece型公钥上的alternant max-agreement，以及由离散对数构造的背包型问题multiplicative polynomial intersection（余弦目标配公钥解码器处理带符号错误）。二者均给出解码保证及与Prange重启的有限长度比较；在较大测试实例上，DQI的期望质量超过经典求解器在固定预算内达到的水平。

Conclusion: DQI的可证明优势受严格裕度上限约束，OPI在该框架下最优；健全性规则与性能律可有效指导候选问题发现，两个新应用显示出相对经典基线的有限规模优势。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) uses classical error correction to solve optimization problems, but it is hard to tell which problems could give it a quantum advantage. We develop soundness rules that screen candidate problems by their code parameters, decoding guarantees and classical baselines. For the standard objective of counting satisfied constraints, we prove that under worst-case Hamming decoding DQI's guarantee can exceed Prange completion by at most $(\sqrt{2}-1)/2$. Optimal polynomial intersection (OPI) attains this margin cap asymptotically, so no candidate in this setting can exceed OPI's margin. We also derive a performance law for real-valued objectives: the objective fixes both the solution quality and which errors the decoder must correct. An AI-agent search guided by these rules found two candidate applications. Alternant max-agreement on McEliece-type public keys tests what a secret decoder is worth to DQI against classical solvers that see only the public instance. Multiplicative polynomial intersection, a knapsack-type problem built from discrete logarithms, pairs a cosine objective with a public decoder for signed errors. For both we prove decoding guarantees and finite-length comparisons with Prange restarts, and on the larger instances we tested, DQI's expected quality exceeds what our classical solvers reach within fixed budgets.

</details>


### [240] [Finite-bath projected ensembles in dual-unitary circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06754)
*Rahul Arvind,Nicholas Hunter-Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在三条逆多项式假设下，证明双幺正电路中多项式规模浴即可使投影系综形成近似k-设计，并建立随机性涌现的微观输运图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明双幺正砖墙电路经部分测量后，在浴尺寸趋于无穷时投影系综构成量子态设计，但有限浴尺寸下的收敛速率尚未被严格建立，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 考虑可解双幺正砖墙电路演化后对部分系统测量，在未测量子系统上诱导出投影系综；引入三条逆多项式假设——局域置换混合、后向递归运动的有界性、以及测量边界处的收缩性；并将局部置换算符的偏离解释为缺陷，发展出投影系综随机性涌现的微观输运图像，与量子行走理论建立联系。

Result: 在三条逆多项式假设下，证明多项式规模（polynomial size）的浴即足以形成近似k-设计，从而确立了有限浴尺寸下投影系综收敛速率的严格界。

Conclusion: 投影系综的随机性可通过缺陷输运的微观机制理解，且多项式浴尺寸足以实现近似k-设计；该框架将投影系综、量子行走与置换混合理论统一起来。

Abstract: We study the emergent randomness in projected ensembles generated by dual-unitary circuits. Specifically, we consider evolution by solvable dual-unitary brickwork circuits followed by measurements on part of the system, which induce an ensemble of states on the unmeasured subsystem. Previous work has established that this projected ensemble of states forms a quantum state design in the limit where the bath size is large, but the rate of convergence at finite bath size has not been rigorously established. Under three inverse-polynomial assumptions, local permutation mixing, a bound on recurrent backward motion, and contraction at the measured boundary, we fill this gap and prove that a bath of polynomial size suffices to form approximate $k$-designs. In establishing our results, we develop a microscopic transport interpretation of emergent randomness in the projected ensemble, in which deviations from local permutation operators appear as defects, and make connections with the theory of quantum walks.

</details>


### [241] [The Grothendieck Game and Self-Testing Continuous Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06755)
*Alexander Kulpe,Giulio Malavolta,Simon Schmidt,Michael Walter*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了Majorana算符连续测量族的鲁棒自检验，并进一步将其推广至连续pin群的旋量表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自检验（self-testing）可仅凭观测到的关联即认证量子态与测量，而无需描述产生这些关联的装置。此前尚缺乏针对由Majorana算符生成的连续测量族的鲁棒自检验方案，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明Grothendieck博弈（一种双人非局域博弈，参与者接收球面上的方向并返回二元答案）是Majorana可观测量的一种自检验。即任何接近最优的表现都可认证整个连续参数化的量子测量族；并进一步将该认证扩展到Majorana可观测量的乘积。

Result: 建立了Majorana可观测量的自检验，覆盖由Majorana算符生成的完整连续测量族；并进一步获得连续pin群旋量表示（即Majorana可观测量的乘积）的鲁棒自检验。

Conclusion: Grothendieck博弈可作为Majorana可观测量的自检验工具，从而实现对该连续测量族的鲁棒认证，并将认证范围推广至连续pin群的旋量表示。

Abstract: Self-testing certifies quantum states and measurements from observed correlations alone, without requiring a description of the devices that produce them. In this work we establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first robust self-test of the full continuous family of measurements generated by Majorana operators. We achieve this by showing that the Grothendieck game, a two-player nonlocal game in which the players receive directions on a sphere and return binary answers, is a self-test of Majorana observables. That is, any near-optimal performance certifies an entire continuously parameterized family of quantum measurements. We further extend this certification to products of Majorana observables, obtaining a robust self-test of the spin representation of the continuous pin group.

</details>


### [242] [Nonadditivity in fermionic quantum Shannon theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06758)
*Farzin Salek,Amir-Reza Negari,Graeme Smith,Jens Eisert,Debbie Leung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在费米子系统的宇称超选择与正则反对易关系约束下，精确求解了两模高斯态及其信道的一系列纠缠与容量问题，证明了纠缠形成、最小输出熵和Holevo信息的非可加性，并给出无辅助经典与量子容量及强逆定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子系统的物理约束（宇称超选择、正则反对易关系）会改变纠缠与信道容量的标准结论。本文旨在回答Shor与Hastings所提四个可加性问题的费米子对应版本，并刻画相应的有限块长行为。

Method: 针对两模高斯态τ及其相关高斯信道，在所有物理纯态分解与信道输入（包括高斯系综和经典码）上进行优化，计算任意块长的纠缠形成、最小输出熵和Holevo信息，并推广到任意Rényi阶α∈(0,∞]。

Result: 得到Entanglement of formation E_F^{phys}(τ^{⊗m})=⌈m/2⌉log2，两拷贝等同于单拷贝，违反可加性与强超可加性，而正则化使其减半；信道的最小输出熵与Holevo信息在任意块长下均非可加。单次使用不传递经典信息，两次使用可完美传输1比特；无辅助经典容量与零误差经典容量均为每模1/2比特，且具强逆。无辅助量子容量与零误差量子容量均为每模1/2逻辑比特，并具强逆（纠缠保真度在超容量速率下消失）。对某噪声高斯族确定了单、双拷贝形成值并证明满秩下严格非可加性；τ在局域保宇称测量下的Bell违背首次出现于三拷贝。

Conclusion: 费米子物理约束导致纠缠形成与信道容量呈现显著的非可加及非超可加行为，经典与量子容量均被限制为每模半比特，且满足强逆定理；最优可由高斯系综与经典码达到。

Abstract: We study collective state preparation and channel encoding in fermionic systems under parity superselection and composition governed by the canonical anticommutation relations. For a two-mode Gaussian state $τ$, we obtain the exact entanglement of formation, $E_{\mathrm F}^{\mathrm{phys}}(τ^{\otimes m})=\lceil m/2\rceil\log2$ for every $m\geq1$. Two copies have the same formation value as one, violating additivity and strong superadditivity, while regularization halves the single-copy value. For an associated Gaussian channel, we determine the minimum output entropy and Holevo information at every blocklength and prove nonadditivity of both. Together, these results give exact answers to the fermionic counterparts of the four additivity questions associated with Shor and Hastings. One physical use carries no classical information, whereas two uses transmit one bit perfectly. The unassisted classical and zero-error classical capacities both equal one half bit per mode. A sharp finite-block bound gives a strong converse: the decoding success probability vanishes at every rate above capacity. These optimizations cover all physical pure-state decompositions and channel inputs, with Gaussian ensembles and classical codes attaining the optima. The formation and minimum output entropy formulas hold for every Rényi order $α\in(0,\infty]$. We also determine the unassisted quantum and zero-error quantum capacities, both equal to one half logical qubit per mode, and prove a strong converse: entanglement fidelity vanishes at every rate above capacity. For a noisy Gaussian family, we determine the one- and two-copy formation values and prove strict nonadditivity at full rank. For $τ$, Bell violation under local parity-preserving measurements first appears at three copies.

</details>


### [243] [A classical oracle separation between QMA and QMA(2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06760)
*Sabee Grewal,Saachi Mutreja,Kabir Tomer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个经典 oracle，相对于该 oracle 证明 QMA ≠ QMA(2)，其分离基于图上带符号边约束定义的可分离哈密顿量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 QMA 与 QMA(2) 的分离问题，即在允许验证者接收两个不可纠缠的量子见证（merlin）时，其计算能力是否严格强于标准 QMA。此前该问题缺乏相对化分离证据，本文旨在给出经典 oracle 下的分离。

Method: 构造基于图上有符号边约束的可分离哈密顿量问题：YES 实例存在零能量的乘积态（可分离态），NO 实例通过反对称子空间构造，存在纠缠的零能量态但无低能量乘积态。QMA 下界证明采用压缩 oracle（compressed-oracle）技术，追踪量子计算在少量查询 YES 实例时所获取的信息，关键步骤是将部分 oracle 记录的叠加态映射到 NO 实例族，同时不显著改变验证者的行为。

Result: 存在一个经典 oracle，使得相对于该 oracle 有 QMA ≠ QMA(2)。

Conclusion: 通过可分离哈密顿量问题与压缩 oracle 技术，本文给出了 QMA 与 QMA(2) 在经典 oracle 下的相对化分离，表明允许两个非纠缠见证的验证者模型在该 oracle 下严格强于标准 QMA。

Abstract: We exhibit a classical oracle relative to which QMA \neq QMA(2). The separation is based on a separable Hamiltonian problem defined by signed edge constraints on a graph. The YES instances have zero-energy product states, while the NO instances, constructed using the antisymmetric subspace, have entangled zero-energy states but no low-energy product states. For the QMA lower bound, we use compressed-oracle techniques to track the information learned by a quantum computation making few queries to a YES instance. The key step is to map the resulting superpositions of partial oracle records to the NO family without significantly changing the verifier's behavior.

</details>


### [244] [Hypercontractivity and entropy decay for infinite-dimensional quantum Gibbs samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06764)
*Simon Becker,Cambyse Rouzé,Robert Salzmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为一类无穷维量子Gibbs采样器推导了相对熵指数衰减速率，并给出超收缩性与对数Sobolev不等式的微扰稳定性理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无穷维量子Gibbs采样器的收敛性（相对熵指数衰减）尚缺乏统一处理，尤其涉及多模玻色系统与库仑气体等复杂相互作用时。

Method: 建立超收缩性估计与对数Sobolev不等式的微扰稳定性理论；选取合适的耗散耦合。

Result: 对任意跳跃与排斥密度相互作用的全多模Bose-Hubbard采样器得到指数熵衰减；对谐振约束量子库仑气体与分子系统，证明任意固定有限秩低能截断相互作用下均指数熵衰减；构造出具有正谱隙但无任何p>2的有限时间L²到L^p界的周期性充电模型。

Conclusion: 微扰稳定性框架可推广指数相对熵衰减至广泛无穷维量子Gibbs采样器；同时谱隙不足以保证有限时间超收缩性。

Abstract: We derive rates of exponential relative-entropy decay for a broad class of infinite-dimensional quantum Gibbs samplers. A key technical ingredient is a perturbative stability theory for hypercontractive estimates and logarithmic Sobolev inequalities. For suitably chosen dissipative couplings, we obtain exponential entropy decay for full multimode Bose-Hubbard samplers with arbitrary hopping and repulsive density interactions. For harmonically confined quantum Coulomb gases and molecular systems, we prove exponential entropy decay for every fixed finite-rank low-energy truncation of the interaction. Finally, we exhibit a periodic charging model with a positive spectral gap but no finite-time $L^2$-to-$L^p$ bound for any $p>2$.

</details>


### [245] [Fermions and the Tsirelson Problem in Real Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06767)
*Timothee Hoffreumon,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了实量子理论（RQT）与普通量子理论（QT）的不可区分性在两个自然扩展下依然成立：允许用对易可观测量（而非张量积可观测量）表示分离测量，以及引入超选择规则（如费米子信息论情形）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已表明RQT无法与普通QT在实验上区分。本文旨在探究这种不可区分性是否在两种自然推广下依然保持：一是允许分离测量由对易可观测量而非张量积可观测量表示；二是引入超选择规则（如费米子信息论中那样）。

Method: 第一部分，给出RQT中Tsirelson问题的肯定回答，证明对易测量与张量积测量生成相同的有限维关联，指出标准复数证明无法直接迁移到实数域，因此先在RQT中建立等价性；第二部分，构建超选择理论的局域实量子表述，将RQT框架扩展到在实数上表示超选择，并给出其费米子版本。

Result: 证明了对易与张量积测量在RQT中生成相同的有限维关联（Tsirelson问题的肯定回答），并在两种扩展下均保持了与普通QT的不可区分性；所构造的费米子实量子版本在预测上与费米子信息论实验不可区分，且该构造可推广至所有超选择QT。

Conclusion: RQT与普通QT的不可区分性在允许对易测量以及引入超选择规则两种扩展下依然成立；复数域下的标准证明无法平移到实数域这一障碍在超选择情形尤为关键，因此需先在RQT中建立等价性再扩展至超选择RQT。

Abstract: In a previous work, we showed that real quantum theory (RQT) cannot be experimentally distinguished from ordinary quantum theory (QT). In this paper, we investigate whether this indistinguishability survives two natural extensions of the setting: allowing separated measurements to be represented by commuting rather than tensor-product observables, and imposing superselection rules, as in fermionic information theory. We show that it does in both cases.
  Our first result is a positive answer to the Tsirelson problem in RQT: commuting and tensor-product measurements generate the same finite-dimensional correlations. This is surprisingly not a trivial consequence of the standard complex proof, as it does not carry over to the reals. The proof obstruction becomes particularly relevant in the presence of superselection rules, which is why we first establish the equivalence in RQT before extending the framework to superselected RQT.
  Our second result is a local, real quantum formulation of superselected theories. We extend the RQT framework to accommodate a representation of superselection over the reals and show, in particular, that it admits a fermionic version whose predictions are experimentally indistinguishable from those of fermionic information theory. More generally, the construction extends to all superselected QTs.

</details>


### [246] [Complexity separations for optimal matchgate-Clifford synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06768)
*Berta Casas,Diego García-Martín,Yuxuan Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 matchgate-Clifford 群中的最优精确综合问题：给定目标酉算子与门集合，输出用最少门数实现该目标的 n 比特线路，并证明该问题的复杂度强烈依赖于门集合的连通图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: matchgate-Clifford 群在随机费米子协议与 matchgate 综合中具有重要意义，而其伴随作用同构于 2n 个 Majorana 模的幺模带符号置换群。理解该群中可精确、门最优综合的条件与复杂度，对费米子线路编译与综合理论具有基础价值。

Method: 将问题形式化为：给定目标酉算子与门集合，寻找使用最少操作的 n 比特线路。以不同连通图上的 Majorana 编织（braid）作为门集合，分别研究路径图、完全图与树形图情形下的最优精确综合，并给出显式门最优编译器；对树形图情形进行判定版本的复杂度归约。

Result: 对于路径图与完全图，证明该问题可在 O(n^2) 时间内经典求解，并给出显式的门最优编译器；对于连通图为树的情形，证明最优综合问题的判定版本是 NP-complete 的。在最长至 n=80 比特的链上，将最优编译器与 Qiskit、Tket 基准对比，在总门数上分别取得 ×2.57 与 ×2.28 的常数倍改进。

Conclusion: matchgate-Clifford 群中精确最优综合的计算复杂度取决于门集合的连通性：路径图与完全图可高效求解，而树形图导致 NP-complete。所提最优编译器在实际基准上优于现有工具，表明针对特定门集合结构设计专用最优综合算法兼具理论意义与实践价值。

Abstract: Clifford and matchgate circuits are canonical families of classically simulable quantum circuits. Their intersection, the matchgate-Clifford group, plays an important role in randomized fermionic protocols and in matchgate synthesis. Its adjoint action is isomorphic to the group of unit-determinant signed permutations of $2n$ Majorana modes, and we study optimal exact synthesis in this group. That is, given a target unitary and a gate set, output an $n$-qubit circuit implementing the target using the fewest operations. We show that the complexity of this problem strongly depends on the gate set. In particular, we study gate sets consisting of Majorana braids with different connectivity graphs. For path and complete graphs, we prove that the problem is classically solvable in $\mathcal{O}\left(n^2\right)$ time, and we provide explicit gate-optimal compilers. In addition, we prove that when the connectivity graph is a tree, the decision version of the optimal synthesis problem becomes NP-complete. Finally, we benchmark our optimal compiler on chains of up to $n=80$ qubits against those of \texttt{Qiskit} and \texttt{Tket}, obtaining circuits with constant factor improvements $\times2.57$ and $\times2.28$ in the total number of gates, respectively.

</details>


### [247] [Fast convergence of propagation algorithms for open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06769)
*Giorgio Facelli,Hamza Fawzi,Omar Fawzi,Cambyse Rouzé,Sam Slezak,Daniel Stilck França*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了传播算法快速收敛的一般性条件，表明收缩性可控制局域相互作用产生的增长，从而实现高效经典模拟，并应用于含噪时间演化与Gibbs态中局域观测量的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统中局域观测量的经典模拟效率问题，尤其是传播算法在何种条件下能快速收敛，尚需统一的机制性刻画。

Method: 针对自旋与费米子系统，给出适用足够收缩演化的快速收敛一般性条件；应用于任意相互作用图上的局域哈密顿量含噪时间演化，推导关于相互作用强度u、噪声强度λ及相互作用结构的效率时间窗口t_max；并将同一框架用于Gibbs态，通过稳态为Gibbs态的伪Lindbladian分析高温下的快速收敛。

Result: 当噪声强度λ相对相互作用图度数足够大时动力学可高效模拟；无噪声极限λ=0下将可高效模拟时间尺度从log(1/u)扩展至t_max∼1/u；对弱相互作用系统，Gibbs态局域观测量计算可适用至逆温度β∼log(1/u)。

Conclusion: 收缩性为局域相互作用下的高效经典模拟提供了统一机制，既改进并匹配了近期无噪声时间尺度的结果，也给出了高温Gibbs态可高效计算的条件。

Abstract: We establish general conditions for the rapid convergence of propagation algorithms for computing expectation values of local observables in quantum many-body systems, covering both spin and fermionic systems. Our results apply to evolutions that are sufficiently contractive, providing a general mechanism by which contractivity controls the growth generated by local interactions and enables efficient classical simulation. As a first application, we consider noisy time evolution generated by local Hamiltonians on arbitrary interaction graphs. We show that the dynamics can be efficiently simulated when the noise strength $λ$ is sufficiently large compared to the degree of the interaction graph. More generally, for systems with an interaction strength $u$, our bounds determine a time horizon $t_{\text{max}}$ as a function of the interaction strength $u$, the noise strength $λ$, and the interaction structure below which the algorithm is efficient. In the noiseless limit, $λ=0$, our analysis extends the efficiently simulable time scale from $\log(1/u)$ proved in Facelli, Fawzi, and Fawzi (2026) to $t_{\text{max}} \sim 1/u$ matching the recent improvement by Zhao, Marvian and Tong (2026). As a second application, we apply the same framework to the computation of local observables in Gibbs states of local Hamiltonians. We consider a pseudo-Lindbladian whose steady state is the Gibbs state and show that, at sufficiently high temperature, its propagation algorithm converges rapidly. In particular, for weakly-interacting systems with interaction strength $u$, our result applies up to inverse temperatures $β\sim\log(1/u)$.

</details>


### [248] [Engineering logical gates with irrep surface codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06775)
*Yabo Li,Zijian Song,Vieri Mattei,Naren Manjunath,Apoorv Tiwari,Tyler D. Ellison*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"不可约表示表面码"(ISC)，一种能横向实现指定逻辑门集合的二维拓扑量子纠错码，并给出与常规表面码计算融合的格点手术协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算通常依赖固定的逻辑原语集合，实现其他所需操作往往需要巨大资源开销。作者希望有一种系统方法，为指定的逻辑操作定制拓扑码，并将其集成到常规计算中。

Method: 引入irrep surface codes（ISC）：设计出的横向门只需满足两个条件——(i)生成有限群（受Eastin-Knill定理约束，至多相差全局相位）；(ii)不可约，即任何与门集合对易的算符必正比于恒等。该框架可容纳任意有限对角门群与任意可逆经典门（可嵌入更大的不可约有限门群），并支持超出Clifford层级任何有限层的逻辑门。为将操作纳入表面码计算，进一步开发格点手术协议，在表面码与满足两种充分条件之一的ISC之间转移编码信息。

Result: ISC可横向实现任意有限对角门群、任意可逆经典门，以及超出Clifford层级所有有限层的逻辑门；所设计的格点手术协议可在表面码与ISC之间转移编码信息。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同建立了一套系统方法，用于为所需逻辑操作定制拓扑码并将其集成进常规计算流程。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation typically relies on a fixed set of logical primitives, while realizing other desired operations can require substantial resources. We introduce irrep surface codes (ISCs), two-dimensional topological quantum error-correcting codes engineered to transversally implement a prescribed set of logical gates. The engineered gates only need to satisfy two conditions: (i) they generate a finite group, which, up to global phases, is forced by the Eastin-Knill theorem, and (ii) they are irreducible, meaning that any operator commuting with the set of gates must be proportional to the identity. This accommodates arbitrary finite groups of diagonal gates and arbitrary reversible classical gates, since each can be embedded into a larger irreducible finite group of gates. It also enables logical gates outside every finite level of the Clifford hierarchy. To incorporate these operations into surface-code computations, we further develop lattice-surgery protocols to transfer the encoded information between surface codes and ISCs obeying either of two sufficient conditions. Together, these results establish a systematic method for tailoring topological codes to desired logical operations and integrating them into conventional computations.

</details>


### [249] [Informed Trading model with quantum walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06779)
*H. W. Shawn Liew,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Kyle模型框架下，分别用经典随机游走和连续时间量子游走刻画资产未来价值，解析近似并数值求解风险中性做市商与知情交易者之间的市场均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Kyle模型仅针对单一资产的知情交易者价格冲击，本文希望通过引入不同的资产价值动态（经典随机游走与量子游走）来拓展该框架，比较两种情形下的均衡特征。

Method: 在Kyle模型设定下，将资产未来价值分别建模为经典随机游走与连续时间量子游走，对风险中性做市商与知情交易者之间的均衡进行解析近似与数值识别。

Result: 经典均衡中，知情交易者采取线性策略，做市商定价规则近似logistic曲线，知情交易者利润随时间t次线性增长；量子均衡中，知情交易者策略呈多个簇，定价规则出现相应多次跳跃，利润随时间t线性增长。

Conclusion: 资产价值的量子动态会显著改变Kyle模型均衡结构：策略与定价的非线性增强，但知情交易者利润增长反而由次线性变为线性。

Abstract: The seminal Kyle model offers a framework to understand the price impact of an informed trader in a market model for a single asset. We study variants in which the asset's future value is modeled by a classical random walk and by a continuous-time quantum walk, respectively. With these assumptions, we analytically approximate and numerically identify the market equilibrium between risk-neutral market maker and informed trader. In the classical equilibrium, the informed trader possesses a linear strategy, whereas the market maker's pricing rule resembles a logistic curve. The informed trader's profit is also identified to grow sub-linearly with time t. In the quantum equilibrium, the informed trader's strategy forms multiple clusters and the pricing rule exhibits multiple jumps correspondingly. Despite the increasing non-linearity of the equilibrium, the informed trader's profit grows linearly with time t.

</details>


### [250] [Pretty good shadow tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06781)
*Sabee Grewal,Angelos Pelecanos,Jack Spilecki,Ewin Tang,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个影子层析算法，使用 $O(\log(m)\log(m/\delta)/\varepsilon^2)$ 份量子态副本，即可同时估计 $m$ 个可观测量在未知量子态上的期望值，均达到 $\pm\varepsilon$ 精度。方法基于平均情形下的测量（pretty good measurement）加提升技术，再借助极小极大原理得到最坏情形的存在性保证，并指出进一步的改进受到本质性障碍的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子影子层析问题是：给定未知量子态 $\rho$ 和 $m$ 个可观测量 $O_1,\ldots,O_m$，需要估计所有 $\mathrm{tr}(O_i\rho)$。经典对应问题只需 $O(\log(m)/\varepsilon^2)$ 样本，而此前量子算法存在额外开销。论文旨在缩小量子与经典复杂度之间的差距，并理解剩余 $\log(m)$ 因子的来源。

Method: 核心思路是在平均情形（average-case）下求解：对从已知先验中随机抽取的量子态，采用 pretty good measurement，仅需 $O(\log(m)/\varepsilon^2)$ 份副本即可对先验中除极小比例外的大部分态给出常数误差保证。通过提升（boosting）将该平均情形保证转化为平均情形的影子层析算法，再利用极小极大原理（minimax principle），以非构造性方式导出具有相同最坏情形保证的测量存在性。

Result: 算法使用 $O(\log(m)\log(m/\delta)/\varepsilon^2)$ 份输入态副本，对所有 $i\in[m]$ 输出 $\mathrm{tr}(O_i\rho)$ 的估计，误差不超过 $\pm\varepsilon$，失败概率至多 $\delta$。同时论文识别出该方法的改进障碍：pretty good measurement 能够在先验随机性上放大成功率，但无法在测量随机性上放大成功率，该问题似乎是固有的。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了复杂度为 $O(\log(m)\log(m/\delta)/\varepsilon^2)$ 的影子层析算法，改进了量子影子层析的副本复杂度。论文进一步指出，因测量随机性无法被放大这一本质性障碍，导致量子问题与经典对应问题之间仍存在 $\log(m)$ 因子的差距，这为后续研究指明了限制与方向。

Abstract: We give a shadow tomography algorithm that uses $O(\log(m) \log(m/δ) / \varepsilon^2)$ copies of the input state. Specifically, given a quantum state $ρ$ and observables $O_1,\ldots,O_m$, the algorithm outputs estimates of $\mathrm{tr}(O_iρ)$ for all $i\in[m]$ that are all accurate to within $\pm\varepsilon$, except with probability at most $δ$. The key idea is to work in the average-case setting: for a random state drawn from a known prior, a pretty good measurement on $O(\log(m)/\varepsilon^2)$ copies gives a constant-error guarantee on all but a small fraction of the prior. Boosting this guarantee gives an average-case shadow tomography algorithm, and the minimax principle then (non-constructively) implies the existence of a measurement with the same worst-case guarantee.
  We also identify an obstruction to further improvement with this approach. While pretty good measurements amplify success over the randomness of the prior, they do not amplify success over the randomness of measurement. This issue appears to be inherent, and gives rise to the remaining $\log(m)$ factor separating the complexity of the quantum problem from its classical counterpart.

</details>


### [251] [Quantum-optimized phase closure for robust interferometric imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06786)
*Zhenning Liu,Emil T. Khabiboulline,Lorcan O. Conlon,Brittany McClinton,Aqil Sajjad,Saikat Guha,Jayadev Rajagopal,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Daniel Gottesman,Stephen T. Ridgway*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明利用多光子联合测量可更高效、更精确地测量闭合相位，并给出大阵列中光子成本由单光子协议的Ω(m²)降至Õ(m)的高效协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地基光学干涉成像依赖闭合相位来消除望远镜相关的大气相位噪声，但传统单光子顺序测量在光子利用效率和精度上受限，尤其在大规模阵列中成本随望远镜数增长过快。

Method: 首先分析闭合相位信息在未知望远镜相位噪声下存活所需的最少光子数，建立三光子联合测量方案；随后针对大阵列，在全局均方误差准则下估计所有双谱（其相位即闭合相位），利用可见度物理约束构造至多三光子联合测量的高效协议，并用多参数量子度量衡分析高精度区域。

Result: 至少需要来自同一大气时间相位稳定窗口的三个光子才能使闭合相位信息在噪声下存活；三光子联合测量在三望远镜最小情形已优于单光子；m望远镜阵列虽有Θ(m²)独立三角形双谱，但总信息量随m近线性增长，高效协议光子成本为Õ(m)，而任何顺序单光子协议需Ω(m²)；当每稳定窗口探测光子数与阵列尺寸线性增长时，所需曝光时间在忽略对数因子下与m无关；高精度区域集体测量也有类似优势。

Conclusion: 量子信息处理技术可显著提升光学干涉测量的灵敏度与鲁棒性，为大规模地基干涉成像提供更高效的光子利用与测量方案。

Abstract: Closure phase enables imaging with ground-based interferometric arrays by canceling telescope-dependent phase errors due to atmospheric fluctuations. It can be obtained by measuring fringe phases on individual baselines forming a triangle and summing them. We show that closure phases can be measured more accurately with fewer photons by performing joint measurements on multiple photons. We first establish that at least three photons from the same atmospheric temporal phase-stability window are required for closure-phase information to survive the unknown telescope-dependent phase noise, and show that joint three-photon measurements already provide an advantage over single-photon measurements in the minimal three-telescope setting. We then turn to large arrays and consider estimating all bispectra---complex quantities whose phases are the closure phases---under a global mean-square error criterion. Although an $m$-telescope array contains $Θ(m^2)$ independent triangle bispectra, we show that physical constraints on the visibilities make the total information content grow only nearly linearly with $m$ at fixed accuracy. Exploiting this structure, we construct an efficient protocol using at most three-photon joint measurements with photon cost $\tilde{O}(m)$, whereas any sequential single-photon protocol requires $Ω(m^2)$ photons. When the number of detected photons per temporal stability window scales linearly with the array size, this allows the required exposure time to remain independent of $m$ up to logarithmic factors. We further analyze the complementary high-precision regime using multiparameter quantum metrology and find a similar advantage for collective measurements. Overall, our results show that quantum information processing techniques can substantially improve the sensitivity and robustness of optical interferometry.

</details>


### [252] [Computational entanglement distillation beyond pure states: efficient protocols and fundamental limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06787)
*Takeru Utsumi,Jacopo Rizzo,Ryuji Takagi,Lorenzo Leone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限资源下的纠缠蒸馏，证明Rényi相干信息在两种资源约束（样本高效、计算高效）下刻画混合态蒸馏的最优速率，并赋予其新的操作意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠信息论依赖任意多输入副本与无界复杂操作，但现实资源受限。有限资源纠缠理论中，何种量刻画纠缠操作这一问题此前仅对理想化的纯态情形有较好探索，混合态情形尚待研究。

Method: 从混合态出发研究纠缠蒸馏，在两种不同的资源约束下分析最优速率。首先刻画样本高效区域（允许无界计算能力），构造达到该速率的通用协议；再推进到计算高效前沿，分析已知输入态下任意高效协议的界，并通过额外多项式约束下的通用协议实现该速率。

Result: 识别出Rényi相干信息为最坏情形下决定最优速率的量。在多项式秩约束下，通用协议无需知道输入态即可达到该速率，且该速率在通用协议中最优：构造出可蒸馏纠缠近乎扩张、但任何通用样本高效协议速率不超过其Rényi相干信息的态。对任意高效协议（即使已知输入态）同样成立，另有通用协议在边际秩多项式约束下高效达到该速率。

Conclusion: Rényi相干信息在两种计算场景中获得新的操作意义：有限副本下的通用蒸馏，以及即使已知输入态的高效蒸馏，统一刻画了混合态纠缠蒸馏的最优速率。

Abstract: The information-theoretic approach to entanglement typically relies on arbitrarily many input copies and unboundedly complex operations; however, the available resources are constrained in reality. This motivates the study of entanglement theory with limited resources. One of its central questions is which quantities characterize entanglement manipulations, and so far, only the idealized case of pure states has been well explored. In this work, we address entanglement distillation from mixed states and identify the Rényi coherent information as the quantity determining the optimal rate in the worst case under two distinct resource constraints. We first characterize the sample-efficient regime, in which unbounded computational power is allowed. Our universal protocol achieves this rate without knowledge of the input state under certain polynomial rank constraints. This rate is optimal among universal protocols: we construct states whose distillable entanglement is nearly extensive, yet from which no universal sample-efficient protocol attains a rate above their Rényi coherent information. We then push this characterization to the computationally efficient frontier: the same bound holds for any efficient protocol even when the input state is known, and another universal protocol achieves this rate efficiently under additional polynomial constraints on the marginal ranks. Overall, our results give the Rényi coherent information a novel operational meaning in two computational settings: universal distillation with limited copies and efficient distillation even with knowledge of the input state.

</details>


### [253] [Characterizing Quantum Advantage for Generalizations of the Boolean Hidden Matching Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06789)
*Mark Bun,Joao F. Doriguello,John Kallaugher,Nadezhda Voronova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 f-Boolean Hidden Partition 问题的一路通信复杂度，用 f 的符号次数 d 刻画经典与量子通信界，证明了 Doriguello 和 Montanaro (TQC 2020) 的猜想，并完整刻画了具有多项式量子优势的函数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题推广了 Boolean Hidden Matching 与 Hidden Hypermatching（f 为奇偶函数的情形）。此前工作仅给出 d≤1 的经典上界、d≤2 的量子上界，以及部分大符号次数函数的 Polly 下界，缺乏对一般 f 的紧界刻画，且存在关于符号次数的猜想待证。

Method: 以 f 的符号次数 d 为参数，建立 f-Boolean Hidden Partition 的经典与量子一路通信复杂度界：分析随机经典协议的下界与上界，并给出量子协议的上界与下界。

Result: 对任意符号次数 d≥2 的 f，随机经典通信复杂度为 Θ(n^{1-1/d})；量子通信复杂度介于 Ω(n^{1-2/d}) 与 O(n^{1-1/⌈d/2⌉} log n) 之间。d≤1 时经典为对数上界，d=2 时给出一族新的指数级分离。

Conclusion: 完全刻画了具有多项式量子优势的函数 f 恰为 d≥2 的情形，验证并推广了 Doriguello-Montanaro 猜想，同时通过 d=2 给出无限族指数分离，深化了对通信复杂度中量子优势来源的理解。

Abstract: We study the one-way communication complexity of the $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition problem. Here, Alice is given an $n$-bit string and Bob is given $Ω(n)$ disjoint blocks of its indices together with a string of labels. Under the promise that the evaluations of $f$ on these blocks either agree with all of the labels or disagree with all of them, Bob must determine which is the case using a single message from Alice. This problem generalizes the Boolean Hidden Matching and Hidden Hypermatching problems, which capture the special case where $f$ is the parity function.
  We establish classical and quantum communication bounds for $f$-Boolean Hidden Partition in terms of the sign degree $d$ of $f$, proving a conjecture of Doriguello and Montanaro (TQC 2020). Their prior work gave logarithmic communication upper bounds for classical protocols when $d \le 1$ and quantum protocols when $d \le 2$, as well as polynomial lower bounds for certain structured functions with larger sign degree. We show that for every $f$ of sign degree $d \ge 2$, the randomized classical communication complexity of this problem is $Θ(n^{1-1/d})$ while its quantum communication complexity lies between $Ω(n^{1-2/d})$ and $\bO{n^{1-1/\lceil d/2 \rceil} \log n}$. This completely characterizes the functions $f$ admitting polynomial quantum advantage as those for which $d \ge 2$, with a new infinite family of exponential separations given by the case $d = 2$.

</details>


### [254] [Local universality of non-local quantum resources in rotationally invariant scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06791)
*Silas R. Beane,Roland C. Farrell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了任意自旋J下弹性散射S矩阵产生的纠缠与魔力的闭式解，并证明在领头阶上量子资源生成能力仅依赖于与最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离平方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作仅针对J=1/2,1,3/2的旋转不变S矩阵给出纠缠能力的表达式，缺乏对任意J的普适描述，且不清楚不同量子资源生成能力之间是否存在共同结构。

Method: 以旋转不变S矩阵为对象，用散射相移参数化S矩阵，对任意自旋J求解纠缠能力的闭式表达式；同时计算非局部魔力等量子资源，并与最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离进行比较分析。

Result: 得到所有J下纠缠能力的闭式解；证明包括纠缠能力和非局部魔力在内的一类广泛非局部量子资源，其领头阶仅依赖于与最近非纠缠门的投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离平方，资源种类只改变整体归一化系数。

Conclusion: 散射的领头阶资源生成能力具有统一的几何起源，即投影Hilbert-Schmidt距离，与相互作用细节无关，体现了不同量子资源在领头阶的普适性。

Abstract: Elastic scattering can generate entanglement and non-local magic between the spins of the two outgoing particles. The amount of entanglement and magic produced is encoded in the S-matrix; a unitary quantum gate that acts on the joint Hilbert space of the two spin-J particles. Previous work derived the entanglement power in terms of the scattering phase shifts for rotationally invariant S-matrices with J=1/2,1,3/2. In this paper, a closed form solution is derived for all J. We further prove that a broad class of non-local quantum resources depend to leading order only on the squared projective Hilbert-Schmidt distance from the nearest non-entangling gate. This holds for the entanglement power and non-local magic power, and the choice of resource simply changes the overall normalization. This universality establishes a common geometric origin for the leading resource-generating capacity of scattering, independent of the details of the interaction.

</details>


### [255] [Error Mitigation From a Single Unmodified Error-Corrected Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06792)
*Maor Shutman,Dorit Aharonov,Itai Arad,Yosi Atia,Eyal Bairey,Zvika Brakerski,Barak A. Katzir,Netanel H. Lindner,Omri Golan,Ilya Gurwich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出SOLEM框架，完全在后处理阶段利用症状数据进行逻辑错误缓解，无需修改纠错电路，兼具外部LEM的易实现性和SALEM的低运行开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有症状感知逻辑错误缓解（SALEM）虽能指数级降低运行开销，但依赖基于症状实时处理的自适应电路操作，实现复杂；而外部LEM方法虽无需症状数据但开销大。因此需要一种兼具低开销与易实现的方案。

Method: SOLEM框架：在给定纠错电路不作任何修改的前提下，完全在后处理中利用症状数据，以内生的症状条件逻辑错误信道替代刻意的电路修改。在该框架内开发了无偏与启发式估计器，包括离线版概率错误消除（PEC）、零噪声外推（ZNE）以及全局回归估计器。

Result: SOLEM的完全离线特性带来三点优势：(1)可用比实时解码器更强的解码器后处理症状；(2)可从同一测量数据构造多个估计器并利用经验统计误差等指标优化；(3)逻辑错误表征仅需在后处理进行，表征数据可在电路执行期间采集，降低对硬件漂移的敏感性。其运行开销比外部LEM指数级更低，与SALEM相当。

Conclusion: SOLEM是一种可"内建"于纠错电路中的逻辑错误缓解框架，实现上比外部LEM更简单，同时在运行开销上兼具指数级优势和与SALEM相当的性能，为容错量子计算提供了一种高效且易于落地的错误缓解途径。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that syndrome information obtained during error-corrected quantum computation can be utilized for logical error mitigation (LEM), yielding exponential reductions in runtime overhead compared to external'' LEM methods, which do not make use of syndrome data. However, these syndrome-aware LEM methods (SALEM) generally require adaptive circuit operations based on real-time processing of syndromes. Here, we introduce syndrome-based offline logical error mitigation (SOLEM), a framework that utilizes syndrome data entirely in post-processing, without any modification to the given error-corrected circuit. In this sense, SOLEM is built into'' error-corrected circuits, and is in fact simpler to implement than external LEM protocols. The basic idea of SOLEM is to use naturally occurring syndrome-conditioned logical error channels in place of deliberate circuit modifications. We develop unbiased and heuristic estimators within this framework, including offline logical versions of probabilistic error cancellation (PEC) and zero-noise extrapolation (ZNE), as well as a global-regression estimator. SOLEM's fully offline nature leads to three significant advantages. First, SOLEM can post-process syndromes with a more powerful decoder than the one used in real time. Second, multiple estimators can be constructed from the same measured data and optimized using empirical statistical errors and related performance metrics. Third, logical error characterization is needed only in post-processing, allowing characterization data to be collected during circuit execution and reducing sensitivity to hardware drift. Remarkably, SOLEM estimators can achieve these advantages while attaining a runtime overhead which is exponentially lower than that of external LEM and is comparable to that of corresponding SALEM estimators.

</details>


### [256] [Testing bosonic and fermionic Gaussian states with a constant number of copies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06794)
*Jop Briët,Davi Castro-Silva,Michael Walter,Freek Witteveen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯量子态的检验问题，证明恒定数量的态副本即可判定给定态是否为高斯态或与任意高斯态重叠有界，突破了此前需副本数随模式数（系统规模）增长的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色子与费米子高斯量子态在量子物理与量子信息处理中居于核心地位。此前对该检验问题的分析所导出的检验方案，需要态副本数随模式数（系统规模）增长，效率受限，因此作者希望降低副本需求。

Method: 基于高斯态相同副本的对称性进行推导；并借助梯度流以及此前针对球面上Kac随机游走研究过的谱界，来界定到高斯态集合的距离。

Result: 证明恒定数量的态副本即足以完成检验，即判定给定态是否为高斯态，或与任意高斯态的重叠有界。

Conclusion: 该检验问题的样本复杂度可由随系统规模增长降为常数，为高斯态检验提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Bosonic and fermionic Gaussian quantum states are families of quantum states that play a central role in quantum physics and quantum information processing. We study the problem of testing whether a given state is either Gaussian, or has bounded overlap with any Gaussian state. Previous analyses of this testing problem led to tests that require a number of copies of the state that scales with the number of modes (the system size). In this work we show that a constant number of copies of the state suffices. We derive these results based on the symmetries of identical copies of Gaussian states, and by bounding the distance to the set of Gaussian states through a gradient flow and a spectral bound studied previously for the Kac random walk on a sphere.

</details>


### [257] [From Quantum Tunneling to Classical Optimization through Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06797)
*Jiayu Shen,Dylan Herman,Guneykan Ozgul,Akshay Seshadri,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Shree Hari Sureshbabu,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Niraj Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用瞬子分析证明：由路径积分蒙特卡洛（PIMC）导出的经典优化算法PISGD可继承量子隧穿机制，其逃脱局部极小的时间按bounce作用量而非势垒高度指数缩放，与量子隧穿一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化中常见瓶颈是解被困于局部极小，被势垒与更优解隔开。经典噪声驱动优化器（如SGD）的逃脱时间随势垒高度指数增长；量子隧穿对高而薄的势垒可更快逃脱。作者想探究这种量子隧穿的指数缩放是否专属于真实量子动力学算法。

Method: 采用基于瞬子的分析，将Kramers逃脱率理论推广到路径空间，分析由PIMC实例化的经典优化算法PISGD，并在可分离与非可分离势上数值实现PISGD以验证缩放规律。

Result: 理论上得到PISGD与量子隧穿相同的指数缩放（以bounce作用量、即两盆间Agmon距离的两倍表示，而非势垒高度）；数值实验在可分离与非可分离势上均确认该缩放。

Conclusion: PISGD只保留非相干隧穿，缺乏多条隧穿路径间的相干性；因此分析不排除量子隧穿类算法相对经典算法在一般情况下存在量子优势的可能。

Abstract: We use an instanton-based analysis to show that Path Integral Stochastic Gradient Descent (PISGD), a classical optimization algorithm instantiated from path integral Monte Carlo (PIMC), can inherit the quantum tunneling mechanism. Quantum tunneling is the phenomenon in which a quantum particle passes through a potential barrier that a classical trajectory cannot cross in the same way. This mechanism can contribute to the performance of quantum optimization algorithms, since a common bottleneck in optimization is that the solution becomes trapped in a local minimum, separated from better solutions by a potential barrier. Classical noise-driven optimizers, such as stochastic gradient descent (SGD), have escape times that scale exponentially with the potential barrier height. For quantum tunneling, the escape time is instead exponential in the bounce action, which equals twice the Agmon distance between the two basins. When the barrier is tall but thin, quantum tunneling can allow escaping local minima faster than such classical methods. Remarkably, this scaling is not exclusive to algorithms with genuine quantum dynamics. For PISGD applied to classical nonconvex global optimization, we adapt Kramers' escape-rate theory to path space and obtain the same exponential scaling as quantum tunneling, in terms of the bounce action rather than the barrier height. We confirm this scaling numerically by implementing PISGD for both separable and nonseparable potentials. That said, PISGD retains only incoherent tunneling, since coherence among multiple tunneling paths is absent. Our analysis therefore does not rule out a potential quantum advantage for quantum-tunneling-based algorithms over classical ones in general.

</details>


### [258] [Bell nonlocality does not imply violation of nonlocal games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06799)
*Ricardo Faleiro,Lucas E. A. Porto,Marco Túlio Quintino,Flavien Hirsch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：按原始定义（即采用确定性谓词）的非局域博弈无法完备地刻画贝尔非局域性，存在不违反任何此类博弈的非局域关联，且量子力学可产生这类"博弈经典"的非局域关联；该现象在几乎所有二分场景中普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域博弈长期以来被视为推理贝尔非局域性的概念清晰工具，并被普遍用作非局域性的判据。作者质疑其作为非局域性完备见证的资格，希望澄清"博弈定义"与"非局域性"之间的本质差距。

Method: 从非局域博弈的原始定义出发，即采用确定性谓词（deterministic predicate）描述获胜条件，证明存在非局域关联（nonlocal behaviours）不违反任何此类博弈。进一步通过量子力学构造，给出可产生这些"博弈经典"非局域关联的量子态与测量方案，并推广到一般二分场景。

Result: 证明存在不违反任何确定性谓词非局域博弈的非局域关联，且量子力学能够产生这些关联。该现象在每一个满足 X,Y≥2 且 A,B≥4 的二分场景 (X,Y,A,B) 中均成立，即具有普遍性。

Conclusion: 原始定义下的非局域博弈（确定性谓词）不是非局域性的完备见证，其刻画能力弱于贝尔非局域性本身；这揭示了两者间的结构性区别，并提示需扩展博弈定义（如非确定性或更一般的谓词）以完备刻画非局域性。

Abstract: Nonlocal games offer a conceptually clear way to reason about Bell nonlocality. However, we show that as originally defined, and most commonly used, i.e. with a deterministic predicate, they cannot be complete witnesses of nonlocality. Concretely, we prove the existence of nonlocal behaviours which do not violate any such nonlocal game. Furthermore, we also show that quantum mechanics can produce these game-classical nonlocal behaviours. We prove that this phenomenon exists in every bipartite scenario $(X,Y,A,B)$ with $X,Y\geq2$ and $A,B\geq4$.

</details>


### [259] [Tight bounds for hybrid quantum-classical query algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06803)
*Andris Ambainis,András Gilyén,Martins Kokainis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究混合量子-经典查询模型：计算由至多做 $q$ 次 oracle 查询的量子子程序构成，子程序之间所有量子比特被测量并丢弃。作者证明若干问题的紧致上下界，并提出证明混合量子算法下界的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典算法（量子子程序间测量并丢弃量子比特）日益常见，但其下界证明此前多为 ad-hoc，缺乏系统方法。作者旨在为该模型建立匹配的上下界，并发展通用的下界证明技术。

Method: 在查询模型中分析混合量子-经典算法。对相位估计、振幅估计给出统一框架：分析测量记录的�率分布，构造并界定刻画其可区分性的进度度量。对 AND-OR 树使用更精细的论证，结合两个进度度量分别追踪算法可经典和量子获取的信息。另推导区分由态制备酉矩阵给定的两个分布的下界。

Result: （i）无偏相位估计精度 $\epsilon$ 需 $\Theta(\frac{1}{q\epsilon^2})$ 次查询；（ii）无偏振幅估计精度 $\epsilon$ 需 $\Theta(\frac{p(1-p)}{q\epsilon^2})$；（iii）$N$ 项搜索需 $\Theta(\frac{N}{q})$；（iv）两层 AND-OR 树（$m$ 个 OR 的 AND，每个 OR 有 $n$ 个输入）需 $\Theta(\frac{nm}{q})$。上述界对从 1（对应经典复杂度）到 $Q(f)$（无限制量子查询复杂度）的所有 $q$ 均最优（常数因子内）。态制备酉矩阵区分两分布的下界紧致至对数因子。

Conclusion: 本文为混合量子-经典查询模型中的多个核心问题建立了匹配的最优上下界，覆盖从经典到全量子复杂度的全部 $q$ 范围，并提供了系统的下界证明方法（测量记录上的进度度量及经典/量子信息结合的论证），弥补了此前此类下界证明多为 ad-hoc 的不足。

Abstract: We study hybrid quantum-classical algorithms in the query model. The computation consists of quantum subroutines that make at most $q$ oracle queries; between subroutines, all qubits are measured and discarded. We prove matching upper and lower bounds for several problems in this model:
  (i) (Unbiased) Phase estimation up to a precision $ε$ requires $Θ(\frac{1}{q ε^2})$ queries;
  (ii) (Unbiased) Amplitude estimation up to a precision $ε$ requires $Θ(\frac{p(1-p)}{q ε^2})$ queries;
  (iii) Search among $N$ items requires $Θ(\frac{N}{q})$ queries;
  (iv) Two level AND-OR tree (AND of $m$ ORs, with $n$ inputs to each OR) requires $Θ(\frac{nm}{q})$ queries.
  All of these bounds are optimal up to a constant factor, for all $q$ from 1, corresponding to the classical complexity, to $Q(f)$, the unrestricted quantum query complexity of the respective problem. We also derive a hybrid lower bound for distinguishing two distributions specified by a state-preparation unitary; this bound is tight up to a logarithmic factor.
  Besides specific lower bounds, an important contribution is developing methods for proving lower bounds on hybrid quantum algorithms (which have been very ad-hoc up to now). The first two bounds follow from a common framework: we analyze probability distributions over measurement transcripts and bound a progress measure that captures their distinguishability. The AND-OR bound requires a more delicate argument, combining two progress measures that track the information available to the algorithm classically and in quantum form, respectively.

</details>


### [260] [Polynomial-time classical algorithms for mean-field models up to the glass transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06807)
*Alexander Schmidhuber,Alexander Zlokapa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明经典算法可在多项式时间内估计Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev模型在所有常数温度下的局部可观测量，并给出严格的量子空腔方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SYK模型是强关联费米子系统，高度量子化：高斯态远离热态，表示热态需要大的多项式规模量子电路。此前仅证明经典算法可在足够高温下以拟多项式时间估计局部热期望值，本文旨在突破温度限制。

Method: 发展了一套完全严格的量子空腔方法（quantum cavity method），并将其技术推广至经典系统。

Result: 经典算法可在多项式时间内估计所有常数温度下的局部可观测量；同时解决了关于经典自旋玻璃在相变前热期望值计算的一个开放问题。

Conclusion: 量子空腔方法有望在优化、采样、推断和学习等领域获得更多应用；作为示例，给出了从任意常数温度的吉布斯态学习SYK哈密顿量的多项式时间与样本复杂度量子算法。

Abstract: The Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model is a strongly interacting fermionic system that has been well-studied in condensed matter and high energy physics. It is highly quantum: Gaussian states are far from the thermal state (Hastings and O'Donnell, STOC'22) and representing the thermal state requires large polynomial-size quantum circuits (Anschuetz et al., QIP'25). Very recently, it was nonetheless proven that classical algorithms can estimate local thermal expectations at sufficiently high temperature in quasipolynomial time (Zlokapa, FOCS'26). We show that classical algorithms can in fact estimate local observables at all constant temperatures in polynomial time. Our techniques also extend straightforwardly to classical systems: we resolve an open question about computing thermal expectations of a classical spin glass up to its phase transition (Bencs et al., STOC'26). Our proof develops a fully rigorous quantum cavity method. Due to the success of the classical cavity method in optimization, sampling, inference and learning, we expect the quantum cavity method to find further applications of independent interest. As an example, we give a quantum algorithm that learns SYK Hamiltonians from the Gibbs state at any constant temperature with polynomial time and sample complexity.

</details>


### [261] [Quantum 1-PCA with Pauli Measurements in Nearly Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06808)
*Dutch Hansen,Jerry Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子1-PCA问题：在未知n量子比特混合态下恢复其主导特征向量，仅使用非自适应单量子比特测量，并给出带谱间隙与噪声容限的样本与时间复杂度保证，推广了此前仅针对纯态的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子1-PCA的目标是从未知量子态的副本中获取其最大特征向量的经典描述。既有工作（Grewal等）已在纯态假设（η=0）下实现近线性复杂度，但实际中状态常存在设定偏差（misspecification）。本文旨在证明在非纯混合态、即存在噪声的情况下，相同量级的样本与时间效率依然可以保持，从而解决状态设定偏差带来的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 作者采用非自适应、单量子比特Pauli基测量。设n量子比特态顶层特征值为λ、谱间隙至少Δ，定义噪声量η=max(1-λ, ε)。算法先递归构造低维子空间，使该子空间近似保持目标特征向量；为实现对Hilbert空间维数近乎线性的运行时间，提出一种新颖的结构化Pauli采样方案，支持对指数多个投影Pauli矩阵进行快速批量计算。

Result: 对顶层特征值为λ、谱间隙≥Δ的n量子比特态，算法以高概率恢复主导特征向量至保真度≥1-ε，所需副本数为Õ(2^n·η²/(Δ³ε³))，时间复杂度为Õ((2^n/Δε)·poly(η/Δε))。当谱间隙为常数且精度与噪声水平相当（ε=Ω(η)）时，运行时间与副本复杂度均为Õ(2^n/ε)，所有测量均为非自适应单量子比特Pauli基测量。

Conclusion: 本文在存在状态设定偏差（混合态）的情况下，达到了与纯态情形（η=0）相同的样本与时间速率，仅相差多对数因子，推广了Grewal等人的结果。核心贡献是递归低维子空间构造与结构化Pauli采样方案，使算法在保持测量简单性的同时获得对Hilbert空间维数的近线性依赖。

Abstract: We consider the problem of quantum 1-PCA: given copies of an unknown $n$-qubit mixed state, recover a classical description of its leading eigenvector. Our goal is to do so using non-adaptive and single-qubit measurements. For an $n$-qubit state with top eigenvalue $λ$ and spectral gap at least $Δ> 0$, we give an algorithm that recovers the leading eigenvector to fidelity at least $1 - \varepsilon$ with high probability using $\tilde{O}\left( {2^n \cdot η^2} / {Δ^3 \varepsilon^3}\right)$ copies and $\tilde{O}((2^n/Δ\varepsilon) \cdot \operatorname{poly}(η/Δ\varepsilon))$ time, where $η= \max (1 - λ, \varepsilon)$. All of our measurements are non-adaptively chosen, and performed in single-qubit Pauli bases. When the spectral gap is constant and the desired accuracy is comparable to the noise level, i.e. $\varepsilon = Ω(η)$, our runtime and copy complexity become $\tilde{O} (2^n / \varepsilon)$. This generalizes the guarantees of Grewal et al. [arXiv:2601.04444], who achieved similar rates, but under the assumption $η= 0$, i.e., that the state was pure. Our results show that the same rates hold in the presence of state misspecification, up to polylogarithmic factors. From a technical perspective, our algorithm works by recursively constructing low-dimensional subspaces that approximately preserve the target eigenvector. To achieve nearly linear runtime dependence on the dimension of the Hilbert space, we develop a novel structured Pauli sampling scheme that enables fast batched computation of exponentially many projected Pauli matrices.

</details>


### [262] [Finding Gaussian Structure in Bosonic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06810)
*Alvan Arulandu,Sitan Chen,Ziyun Chen,Jerry Li,Eric Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了纯玻色高斯态的不可知层析（agnostic tomography）问题，给出了高保真与低保真两种情形下的高效协议，并证明了非高斯测量的必要性及计算复杂度的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在给定任意n模玻色态副本的情况下，需要输出一个纯高斯态，使其与目标态的不可信度（infidelity）不超过最优值opt加ε。现有方法难以在不可知设定下同时保证容忍性与效率，因此需要设计高效协议并厘清非高斯测量与计算复杂度的必要性。

Method: 协议遵循统一范式：首先迭代使用一般高斯测量结合经典鲁棒统计技术获得良好的热启动估计；随后利用非高斯测量，通过凸与非凸优化方法对热启动进行精炼。针对低保真情形（opt低于某通用常数）与任意opt分别设计了相应算法。

Result: 当opt低于某通用常数时，协议运行时间与样本复杂度关于n、1/ε及log log E（E为最近纯高斯态能量）强多项式；对任意opt，副本数与运行时间为(n+1)^{poly(1/ε)}·poly(1+log log E)。作为推论，首次得到真正容忍的高斯性检验协议，可区分opt>c+ε与opt<c-ε（任意阈值c∈(0,1)）。同时证明除非NP⊆BQP，否则poly(n,1/ε)运行时间不可能实现。

Conclusion: 非高斯测量对于达到所获强不可知保证是必要的，且这些保证可证明优于经典高斯鲁棒估计所能达到的水平。

Abstract: We study agnostic tomography of pure bosonic Gaussian states: given copies of an arbitrary $n$-mode bosonic state $ρ$, the goal is to output a pure Gaussian state whose infidelity with $ρ$ is at most $\mathrm{opt} + ε$, where $\mathrm{opt}$ is the minimum infidelity achievable by any pure Gaussian state.
  We give efficient protocols achieving this in both the high and low fidelity regimes. When $\mathrm{opt}$ is below some universal constant, our protocol has runtime and copy complexity which is strongly polynomial in $n, 1/ε$ and $\log \log E$, where $E$ is the energy of the closest pure Gaussian state. For arbitrary $\mathrm{opt}$, our protocol uses $(n+1)^{\mathrm{poly}(1/ε)} \mathrm{poly}\left(1+\log\log(E)\right)$ copies and runtime. As a corollary, we obtain the first truly tolerant Gaussianity testing protocol for distinguishing whether $\mathrm{opt} > c + ε$ or $\mathrm{opt} < c - ε$, for any threshold $c\in(0,1)$. We also prove $\mathrm{poly}(n,1/ε)$ runtime is impossible, unless $\mathrm{NP}\subseteq\mathrm{BQP}$.
  Our protocols follow a shared paradigm: first, we iteratively use general Gaussian measurements combined with techniques from classical robust statistics to obtain a good warm start estimate, then we leverage non-Gaussian measurements to refine this warm start using convex and non-convex optimization methods. Interestingly, we prove that non-Gaussian measurements are necessary to match the strong agnostic guarantees we obtain, and in fact these guarantees are provably superior to what is possible for robustly estimating classical Gaussians.

</details>


### [263] [Polynomial-time simulation of non-Clifford quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06811)
*Serban Cercelescu,Mark Koch,Arthur Pesah*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明一大类非Clifford量子纠错电路的任意中间态都是三阶相位多项式态，并给出精确的多项式时间模拟算法与开源实现merlin。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Clifford量子纠错电路（如magic state distillation、cultivation、code switching等）难以经典模拟，缺乏对其内部状态的统一理论刻画与高效的精确模拟方法。

Method: 证明这类电路在随机Pauli噪声下的每个中间态均为三阶相位多项式态；引入对角Clifford-and-Pauli（DCP）稳定子形式体系，证明三阶相位多项式态恰为该体系下的稳定子态；刻画其表示并实现多项式时间更新，从而构造精确多项式时间模拟算法；提供开源实现merlin。

Result: DCP形式体系精确刻画三阶相位多项式态；表示可多项式时间更新，得到精确多项式时间模拟算法；在distillation、cultivation、code switching电路上基准测试显示，Bravyi-Haah distillation中随逻辑输出数增长具有更优的时间与内存扩展性，且可模拟其他测试模拟器均无法处理的code switching电路。

Conclusion: 三阶相位多项式态严格推广稳定子态，为分析此类非Clifford纠错电路的内部状态与magic state提供了统一框架，并实现了优于现有非Clifford模拟器的精确模拟。

Abstract: We show that every intermediate state of a broad class of non-Clifford quantum error-correction circuits is a third-order phase-polynomial state, even under stochastic Pauli noise. The class includes magic state distillation and cultivation, code switching and gauge fixing, transversal non-Clifford gates with syndrome extraction, and injection of diagonal magic states. Third-order phase-polynomial states strictly generalize stabilizer states, and we prove that they are exactly the stabilizer states of the diagonal-Clifford-and-Pauli (DCP) stabilizer formalism that we introduce. We characterize these states and show that their representations can be updated in polynomial time. This yields an exact polynomial-time simulation algorithm for these circuits and, more broadly, a framework for reasoning about their internal states and about magic states in general. We provide an open-source implementation, \texttt{merlin}, and benchmark it against existing non-Clifford simulators on distillation, cultivation, and code switching circuits. The benchmarks demonstrate improved runtime and memory scaling as the number of logical outputs grows in Bravyi-Haah distillation, and simulation of a code switching circuit beyond the reach of all other tested simulators.

</details>


### [264] [Protecting Quantum Computers against Untrusted Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06812)
*Shiv Akshar Yadavalli,Joel Rajakumar,Alexander Schuckert,Michael J. Gullans*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出受限量子计算模型 1/2BQP_1：量子服务器提供随机计算基输入（执行后才揭示）与单个指定输出比特，允许任意电路与系统规模，并猜想经典客户端无法据此高效分解 RSA 模数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开可用的容错量子计算机需在保留科学效用的同时限制其密码分析能力，因此需要一种既能支撑多体关联、OTOC、配分函数等科学应用，又能抵御经典客户端分解 RSA 的受限计算接口。

Method: 定义模型 1/2BQP_1：随机计算基输入在执行后揭示，仅输出一个指定比特。证明一比特读出使标准分解算法（Shor、Ekera-Hastad、Kitaev、Regev）的量子阶段在单次算术预言机调用附近的广泛电路族中可经典模拟；再考察以相干方式实现有理重构以释放因子比特的绕过攻击，随机输入使已知对数深度并行化技术失效。

Result: 1/2BQP_1 涵盖 DQC1 并保留其在无限温度多时关联、OTOC 与归一化配分函数中的应用；给出基于量子自旋动力学经典预测的区分候选；安全性归约到分解的经典困难性以及对有理重构的猜想性电路下界。

Conclusion: 该受限模型在保留科学效用的同时限制了密码分析能力，其安全性不仅依赖分解的经典困难性，还依赖有理重构的猜想性电路下界，为容错量子计算的可信部署提供了新框架。

Abstract: Publicly accessible fault-tolerant quantum computers must preserve scientific utility while limiting cryptanalytic power. We propose the restricted model of computation, 1/2BQP_1: a quantum server provides random computational-basis inputs, revealed only after execution, and one designated output bit. This interface permits arbitrary circuits and system sizes. We conjecture that a classical client making polynomially many adaptive requests cannot efficiently factor RSA moduli. The model subsumes the well-known one-clean-qubit model DQC1, retaining its applications to infinite-temperature multi-time correlations, out-of-time-order correlators, and suitably normalized partition functions. We propose a candidate for separating 1/2BQP_1 from DQC1 based on testing classical predictions of quantum spin dynamics. We argue security in two steps. First, we show that one-bit readout makes the quantum stages of standard factoring algorithms classically simulable, even when their preparation and postprocessing are redesigned within a broad family around a single arithmetic-oracle call. This covers the quantum stages of Shor, Ekera-Hastad, Kitaev, and Regev factoring constructions. Second, we examine the workaround of coherently implementing rational reconstruction to release a factor bit. Random inputs obstruct this straightforward attack: known techniques accommodate logarithmic-depth classical circuits, but rational reconstruction has resisted such parallelization for decades. Therefore, we show that security of our protocol is built on not only classical hardness for factoring, but also conjectured circuit lowerbounds for rational reconstruction.

</details>


### [265] [CV-QAOA: Efficient Low-Depth Quantum Optimization of Continuous Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06815)
*Sriram Bharadwaj,Di Luo,Leo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高维连续优化中的连续变量量子近似优化算法(CV-QAOA),给出其在多类代价函数上的严格性能保证,并引入RSW问题以超越可去量子化(dequantizable)区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维连续优化问题中,量子算法相对经典算法的优势边界尚不清晰,尤其是哪些问题族能体现真正的量子加速、哪些可被经典算法高效去量子化,缺乏严格的理论刻画。

Method: 提出带变分优化初始态的CV-QAOA,在高深度极限下恢复QHD的收敛保证;对严格凸二次函数、旋转双井(RDW)函数、旋转方井(RSW)函数进行查询复杂度分析与证明,并结合经典算法下界对比及数值模拟。

Result: d步CV-QAOA用2d次量子查询最小化d维严格凸二次函数;1步CV-QAOA仅用2次查询求解RDW(优于QHD的Õ(d³)),但该协议可高效去量子化,梯度对齐线搜索以O(d)查询接近Ω(d/log d)下界;CV-QAOA经绝热演化以d^{o(1)}查询求解RSW,而经典算法需Ω(d²/log d)下界,并有Θ(d²log d)查询的显式经典算法。

Conclusion: CV-QAOA在结构可去量子化的问题上并无本质量子优势,但在全局平坦的RSW类问题上展现出显著超越经典的查询复杂度;在偏转波纹弹簧与Easom函数上的数值模拟表明其在更一般问题上具有潜力。

Abstract: We study a Continuous-Variable Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (CV-QAOA) for high-dimensional continuous optimization. Our formulation extends an earlier CV-QAOA proposal with a variationally optimized initial state and recovers the convergence guarantees of Quantum Hamiltonian Descent (QHD) in the high-depth limit. We prove rigorous performance guarantees of CV-QAOA on several families of cost functions. First, we show $d$-step CV-QAOA minimizes any $d$-dimensional strictly convex quadratic function with $2d$ quantum queries to the cost function. We then analyze a family of nonconvex "Rotated Double Well" (RDW) functions with $2^d$ local minima introduced by arXiv:2311.00811. While prior work showed QHD reaches its global minimum with $\tilde O(d^3)$ queries, we prove that 1-step CV-QAOA solves RDW with just two quantum queries. Although general-purpose classical solvers need superpolynomial time for RDW and structure-awareness can reduce the cost to polynomial time, we show that the 1-step CV-QAOA protocol can be efficiently dequantized, and that a gradient-aligned line search succeeds with $O(d)$ queries, nearly matching the information-theoretic $Ω(d/\log d)$ query lower bound. To move beyond the dequantizable regime, we introduce a ``Rotated Square Well'' (RSW) problem, whose globally flat landscape suppresses useful local gradient information. For this family, we show that an adiabatic evolution simulated by CV-QAOA can reach the global minimum using $d^{o(1)}$ queries. On the other hand, any classical algorithm that learn the hidden rotation in RSW provably requires $Ω(d^2/\log d)$ queries, a bound we nearly match with an explicit $Θ(d^2\log d)$-query classical algorithm.Numerical simulations on deflected corrugated spring and Easom functions illustrate the promising performance of CV-QAOA on more general problems.

</details>


### [266] [The complete phase diagram of detailed-balanced Gibbs samplers for the mean-field Heisenberg model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06818)
*Levente Raikovich,József Mák,Csaba Czabán,András Gilyén*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究平均场Heisenberg模型上KMS-细致平衡Gibbs采样Lindbladians的谱隙，证明其相图与GNS-细致平衡Davies生成元的相图一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期发现的KMS-细致平衡Gibbs采样Lindbladians是量子Gibbs采样的重要工具，但其谱隙（决定混合/收敛速度）在平均场Heisenberg模型中的行为尚未被完整刻画。已有工作（Basso et al. 2026）仅针对GNS-细致平衡Davies生成元部分解决了临界温度处的谱隙标度问题，因此需要更完整地确定这些自然Lindbladian族的谱隙相图。

Method: 针对n个自旋的平均场Heisenberg模型，分析新近发现的KMS-细致平衡Gibbs采样Lindbladians族的谱隙；在Basso et al. (2026)工作的基础上进行推广与延伸，完整刻画临界温度处的谱隙标度。

Result: 这些Lindbladians族的谱隙具有相同的相图：在临界温度以上（无序相）谱隙为常数，在临界温度处按1/√n标度，在临界温度以下（有序相）按1/n标度。

Conclusion: KMS-细致平衡Gibbs采样Lindbladians与GNS-细致平衡Davies生成元共享同一谱隙相图，且本文完整解决了临界温度处的谱隙标度，扩展了Basso et al. (2026)的结果。

Abstract: We study the spectral gap of recently discovered families of KMS-detailed-balanced Gibbs-sampler Lindbladians for the mean-field Heisenberg model on $n$ spins. We show that the spectral gaps of these natural families of Lindbladians admit the same phase diagram: the gap is constant above the critical temperature (i.e., in the disordered phase), scales as $1/\sqrt{n}$ at the critical temperature, and as $1/n$ below the critical temperature (i.e., in the ordered phase). Our result builds on and extends the recent work of Basso et al.~(2026) which characterized this phase diagram for the GNS-detailed-balanced Davies generator, but only partially resolved the gap scaling at the critical temperature.

</details>


### [267] [Improved bounds on stabilizer extent and Clifford rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06819)
*Pulkit Sinha,Benjamin Lovitz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明稳定子秩≤k的纯态其稳定子范围至多2^{O(√(k log(k+1)))}，并给出Clifford秩-k算子平方Clifford系数范数的类比界，进而解决Mehraban-Tamasbi的定量猜想，导出多个下界、去随机化否证与层析算法改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子秩与Clifford秩是量化非稳定子性（magic）与量子优势的核心复杂度度量。此前稳定子秩与稳定子范围/保真度之间的定量关系仅有猜想，且张量幂近似稳定子秩下界仅对特定T态成立，非稳定子比特态缺乏通用下界；同时量子相位的精确表示、伪随机性否证与层析算法复杂度均受限于此。

Method: 建立稳定子秩到稳定子范围的定量不等式：证明任意稳定子秩≤k的纯态，其稳定子范围≤2^{O(√(k log(k+1)))}；对Clifford秩-k算子证明平方Clifford系数范数的类比界（Clifford秩–范数不等式），并据此推导稳定子保真度下界，进而转用到张量幂近似稳定子秩、二次相位表示、伪随机集合与层析算法分析。
